simulation:
  num_servers: 48
  min_servers: 1
  max_servers: 1000
  simulation_hours: 24
  min_simulation_hours: 1
  max_simulation_hours: 720
  random_seed: 42
  parallel_workers: 4
  enable_distributed: false
  distributed_workers: 8
  run_mode: "standard"
  sla_slack: 1.5

data_source:
  mode: "catalog"
  catalog_db: "data/catalog.db"
  site_id: "北京市"
  ambient_start_hour: 4344
  trace_id: "alibaba-gpu-v2025-d019-168h"

environment:
  ambient_temp_base: 15
  ambient_temp_amplitude: 10
  humidity: 0.5

cooling:
  power_coefficient: 500
  cooling_efficiency: 0.9
  capacity_rating_w: 20000
  target_temp: 22
  fan_target_temp: 35
  dissipation_coefficient: 8.0
  thermal_inertia: 0.25
  room_volume: 100
  air_flow_rate: 10
  fan_speed_range: [0.2, 1.0]
  fan_efficiency_curve: "quadratic"

greedy:
  temperature_weight: 0.3
  overload_threshold: 0.95
  overheat_threshold: 0.95
  load_balancing_factor: 0.7
  temperature_balancing_factor: 0.3
  enable_priority_scheduling: true
  priority_weight: 0.5

damo_ets:
  alpha_deadline: 0.35
  beta_energy: 0.25
  gamma_thermal: 0.20
  delta_heterogeneity: 0.15
  epsilon_migration: 0.05
  heat_coefficient: 15.0
  thermal_safety_factor: 0.95
  workload_server_match:
    gpu_bound: ["gpu_server", "high_performance"]
    cpu_bound: ["high_performance", "standard"]
    memory_bound: ["standard"]
    io_bound: ["standard"]

genetic:
  population_size: 50
  max_population_size: 200
  generations: 100
  max_generations: 500
  crossover_rate: 0.8
  mutation_rate: 0.1
  elitism_rate: 0.1
  overload_penalty: 100
  overheat_penalty: 200
  energy_weight: 0.5
  temperature_weight: 0.3
  load_weight: 0.2
  enable_parallel: false
  parallel_evaluators: 1

task_generation:
  base_rate: 6
  peak_rate: 20
  peak_hours: [9, 18]
  load_range: [5, 30]
  priority_range: [1, 5]
  task_types:
    - name: "cpu_bound"
      proportion: 0.4
      load_range: [10, 50]
      duration_range: [1, 5]
    - name: "gpu_bound"
      proportion: 0.3
      load_range: [20, 80]
      duration_range: [2, 10]
    - name: "memory_bound"
      proportion: 0.2
      load_range: [5, 25]
      duration_range: [3, 8]
    - name: "io_bound"
      proportion: 0.1
      load_range: [3, 15]
      duration_range: [1, 3]

task_completion:
  mode: "service_based"
  completion_probability: 0.3
  completion_rate_adjustment: 0.1

energy:
  cop_base: 2.5
  cop_delta_coefficient: 0.1
  heat_to_power_ratio: 0.8
  pue_base: 1.8
  idle_power_factor: 0.6
  part_load_penalty: 0.5
  temp_penalty_coefficient: 1.0
  temp_reference: 35

server_types:

    proportion: 0.6
    max_load: 100

  name: "仿真模拟测试环境"

二、源代码

================================================================================
1. main.py - 项目入口
================================================================================
  1|from src.simulation import DataCenterSimulator
  2|from src.visualization import (
  3|    plot_energy_comparison,
  4|    plot_temperature_trend,
  5|    plot_load_distribution,
  6|    plot_pue_comparison,
  7|    plot_cooling_power,
  8|    plot_summary_stats
  9|)
 10|from src.utils import (
 11|    save_simulation_data,
 12|    export_results_to_excel,
 13|    save_run_metadata,
 14|    setup_logger,
 15|)
 16|import argparse
 17|import os
 18|from datetime import datetime
 19|
 20|from src.services import run_store
 21|
 22|
 23|def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
 24|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="校园数据中心智能调度系
   |    统 - 仿真主入口")
 25|    parser.add_argument("--num-servers", type=int, default=None,
 26|                        help="服务器数量（默认取 config/default.yaml 的 s
   |    imulation.num_servers）")
 27|    parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=None,
 28|                        help="仿真小时数（默认取 config/default.yaml 的 s
   |    imulation.simulation_hours）")
 29|    parser.add_argument(
 30|        "--algorithms",
 31|        nargs="+",
 32|        default=["greedy", "genetic", "round_robin", "damo_ets"],
 33|        help="要参与对比的算法列表",
 34|    )
 35|    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None, help="随机种子
   |    （保证可复现）")
 36|    return parser.parse_args()
 37|
 38|
 39|def _fmt(value, digits: int = 2, suffix: str = "") -> str:
 40|    if value is None:
 41|        return "--"
 42|    return f"{value:.{digits}f}{suffix}"
 43|
 44|
 45|def main() -> None:
 46|    args = parse_args()
 47|
 48|    logger = setup_logger("main")
 49|    logger.info("=" * 60)
 50|    logger.info("校园/小型数据中心智能散热与算力调度系统")
 51|    logger.info("=" * 60)
 52|    if args.seed is not None:
 53|        logger.info(f"随机种子: {args.seed}")
 54|
 55|    try:
 56|        simulator = DataCenterSimulator(
 57|            num_servers=args.num_servers,
 58|            simulation_hours=args.hours,
 59|            seed=args.seed,
 60|        )
 61|        meta = simulator.run_metadata
 62|        logger.info(f"数据来源: {meta.get('ambient_source')} / 任务流: {
   |    meta.get('scenario_source')}")
 63|        logger.info(f"SLA 松弛系数: {meta.get('sla_slack')} | 任务完成模
   |    型: {meta.get('completion_mode')}")
 64|
 65|        logger.info(f"\n[1/5] 运行算法对比仿真 (algorithms={args.algorit
   |    hms}) ...")
 66|        df = simulator.run_comparison(algorithms=args.algorithms)
 67|
 68|        offered = simulator.control_signature(df)
 69|        controlled = simulator.is_controlled(df)
 70|        logger.info(f"[2/5] 受控性检查: 各算法首小时到达负载 {list(offer
   |    ed.values())} -> "
 71|                    f"{'同一任务流快照' if controlled else '警告: 任务流
   |    不一致'}")
 72|
 73|        logger.info("[3/5] 保存仿真数据...")
 74|        csv_path = save_simulation_data(df)
 75|        excel_path = export_results_to_excel(df)
 76|        metadata_path = save_run_metadata({
 77|            **simulator.run_metadata,
 78|            "num_servers": simulator.num_servers,
 79|            "simulation_hours": simulator.simulation_hours,
 80|            "seed": simulator.seed,
 81|            "algorithms": args.algorithms,
 82|            "first_hour_offered": offered,
 83|            "first_hour_served": simulator.first_hour_loads(df),
 84|            "controlled": controlled,
 85|        })
 86|        logger.info(f"  - CSV: {csv_path}")
 87|        logger.info(f"  - Excel: {excel_path}")
 88|        logger.info(f"  - 运行元数据: {metadata_path}")
 89|
 90|        logger.info("\n[4/5] 生成可视化图表...")
 91|        reports_dir = 'reports'
 92|        os.makedirs(reports_dir, exist_ok=True)
 93|
 94|        plot_energy_comparison(df, os.path.join(reports_dir, 'energy_com
   |    parison.png'))
 95|        plot_temperature_trend(df, os.path.join(reports_dir, 'temperatur
   |    e_trend.png'))
 96|        plot_load_distribution(df, os.path.join(reports_dir, 'load_distr
   |    ibution.png'))
 97|        plot_pue_comparison(df, os.path.join(reports_dir, 'pue_compariso
   |    n.png'))
 98|        plot_cooling_power(df, os.path.join(reports_dir, 'cooling_power.
   |    png'))
 99|
100|        logger.info("  - energy_comparison.png")
101|        logger.info("  - temperature_trend.png")
102|        logger.info("  - load_distribution.png")
103|        logger.info("  - pue_comparison.png")
104|        logger.info("  - cooling_power.png")
105|
106|        logger.info("\n[5/5] 统计指标与排名...")
107|        stats = simulator.get_statistics(df)
108|        ranking = simulator.get_ranking(df)
109|
110|        try:
111|            run_id = run_store.create_run({
112|                "num_servers": simulator.num_servers,
113|                "simulation_hours": simulator.simulation_hours,
114|                "algorithms": args.algorithms,
115|                "site_id": simulator.run_metadata.get("site_id"),
116|            }, origin="cli")
117|            run_metadata = {
118|                **simulator.run_metadata,
119|                "run_id": run_id,
120|                "origin": "cli",
121|                "num_servers": simulator.num_servers,
122|                "simulation_hours": simulator.simulation_hours,
123|                "seed": simulator.seed,
124|                "algorithms": args.algorithms,
125|                "first_hour_offered": offered,
126|                "first_hour_served": simulator.first_hour_loads(df),
127|                "controlled": controlled,
128|            }
129|            save_run_metadata(run_metadata)
130|            run_store.save_results(run_id, df, run_metadata, stats)
131|            finished_at = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
132|            run_store.update_run(
133|                run_id, status="success", progress=100,
134|                started_at=finished_at, finished_at=finished_at,
135|            )
136|            logger.info(f"  - 运行编号: {run_id}")
137|        except Exception as run_exc:
138|            logger.warning(f"运行历史写入失败: {run_exc}")
139|
140|        logger.info("\n" + "=" * 60)
141|        logger.info("仿真结果统计")
142|        logger.info("=" * 60)
143|        for algo, metrics in stats.items():
144|            logger.info(f"\n【{algo.upper()}算法】")
145|            logger.info(f"  平均能耗: {_fmt(metrics['avg_energy'], 2, ' 
   |    W')}")
146|            logger.info(f"  峰值温度: {_fmt(metrics['max_temp'], 2, ' °C
   |    ')}"
147|                        f"  (95 分位 {_fmt(metrics['p95_temp'], 2, ' °C'
   |    )})")
148|            logger.info(f"  平均PUE: {_fmt(metrics['avg_pue'], 4)}")
149|            logger.info(f"  总处理负载: {_fmt(metrics['total_load'], 0)}
   |    ")
150|            logger.info(f"  任务服务率: {_fmt((metrics['service_rate'] o
   |    r 0) * 100, 2, '%')}"
151|                        f"  (分配 {metrics['assigned_tasks']} / 丢弃 {me
   |    trics['dropped_tasks']})")
152|            logger.info(f"  单位服务负载能耗: {_fmt(metrics['energy_per_
   |    served_load'], 4, ' W/负载')}")
153|            logger.info(f"  累计 deadline 违约: {metrics['deadline_viola
   |    tions']}"
154|                        f"  ({_fmt((metrics['deadline_violation_rate'] o
   |    r 0) * 100, 2, '%')})")
155|
156|        logger.info("\n" + "=" * 60)
157|        logger.info("受控排名")
158|        logger.info("=" * 60)
159|        logger.info(f"规则: {ranking['rule']}")
160|        for item in ranking['eligible']:
161|            logger.info(f"  #{item['rank']} {item['algorithm']}: "
162|                        f"{_fmt(item['energy_per_served_load'], 4, ' W/
   |    负载')} "
163|                        f"(服务率 {_fmt(item['service_rate'] * 100, 2, '
   |    %')})")
164|        for item in ranking['excluded']:
165|            logger.info(f"  排除 {item['algorithm']}: {item['reason']}")
166|        logger.info(f"结论: {ranking['note']}")
167|
168|        logger.info("\n" + "=" * 60)
169|        logger.info("仿真完成！结果已保存到 reports/ 和 data/ 目录")
170|        logger.info("=" * 60)
171|
172|    except Exception as e:
173|        logger.error(f"仿真过程发生错误: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
174|        raise
175|
176|
177|if __name__ == "__main__":
178|    main()

  1|"""
  2|适配器层 - 提供多平台、多硬件的自适应能力
  3|
  4|设计模式: 策略模式 + 适配器模式
  5|参考项目: stevedore (OpenStack), Flask-PluginKit
  6|
  7|支持的适配器:
  8|    LocalAdapter     - 本地CPU计算
  9|    RemoteAdapter    - 远程服务调用
 10|    CudaAdapter      - NVIDIA CUDA GPU加速 (可选)
 11|    AscendAdapter    - 华为昇腾NPU加速/国产硬件 (可选)
 12|    AutoAdapter      - 自动检测并选择最优适配器
 13|
 14|环境变量配置:
 15|    DCS_ENV             - 运行环境 (development/testing/production)
 16|    DCS_FORCE_ADAPTER   - 强制使用指定适配器
 17|    DCS_API_URL         - 远程API地址
 18|    DCS_NUM_SERVERS     - 服务器数量
 19|    DCS_SIM_HOURS       - 仿真时长
 20|    DCS_PARALLEL_WORKERS - 并行工作器数
 21|"""
 22|
 23|from .base import BaseAdapter, AdapterType, AdapterCapability
 24|from .local import LocalAdapter
 25|from .remote import RemoteAdapter
 26|from .auto import AutoAdapter
 27|
 28|try:
 29|    from .cuda import CudaAdapter
 30|except ImportError:
 31|    CudaAdapter = None
 32|

 34|    from .ascend import AscendAdapter
 35|except ImportError:
 36|    AscendAdapter = None

  1|import os
  2|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
  3|from src.adapters.base import BaseAdapter, AdapterType, AdapterCapabilit
   |    y, normalise_result
  4|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
  5|
  6|logger = setup_logger("ascend_adapter")
  7|
  8|class AscendAdapter(BaseAdapter):
  9|    """
 10|    华为昇腾NPU适配器 (国产硬件)
 11|    
 12|    适用于:
 13|    - 华为昇腾910/310 NPU加速
 14|    - 鲲鹏+昇腾国产服务器
 15|    - OpenEulerOS平台
 16|    
 17|    参考项目: Huawei Ascend, CANN, PyTorch-NPU
 18|    """
 19|
 20|    def __init__(self):
 21|        self._available = False
 22|        self._initialized = False
 23|        self._npu_count = 0
 24|        self._npu_names = []
 25|
 26|    def get_type(self) -> AdapterType:
 27|        return AdapterType.ASCEND
 28|
 29|    def get_capability(self) -> AdapterCapability:
 30|        return AdapterCapability(
 31|            adapter_type=AdapterType.ASCEND,
 32|            max_servers=500,
 33|            supports_parallel=True,
 34|            supports_gpu=False,
 35|            supports_npu=True,
 36|            supports_distributed=False,
 37|            priority=35,
 38|            description=f"华为昇腾NPU适配器 ({self._npu_count} NPU)"
 39|        )
 40|
 41|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 42|        npu_available = False
 43|        
 44|        try:
 45|            import torch_npu
 46|            npu_available = torch_npu.npu.is_available()
 47|            if npu_available:
 48|                self._npu_count = torch_npu.npu.device_count()
 49|            return npu_available
 50|        except ImportError:
 51|            pass
 52|        
 53|        ascend_paths = [
 54|            '/usr/local/Ascend',
 55|            '/home/HwHiAiUser/Ascend',
 56|            os.path.expanduser('~/Ascend')
 57|        ]
 58|        for path in ascend_paths:
 59|            if os.path.exists(path):
 60|                self._npu_count = 1
 61|                npu_available = True
 62|                break
 63|        
 64|        self._available = npu_available
 65|        return npu_available
 66|
 67|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 68|        try:
 69|            if not self.is_available():
 70|                logger.warning("Ascend NPU not available")
 71|                return False
 72|            
 73|            self._config = config or {}
 74|            self._initialized = True
 75|            
 76|            try:
 77|                import torch_npu
 78|                device = torch_npu.npu.device(0)
 79|                logger.info(f"Ascend NPU initialized: {device}")
 80|            except ImportError:
 81|                logger.info("Ascend NPU initialized (basic mode)")
 82|            
 83|            return True
 84|        except Exception as e:
 85|            logger.error(f"Ascend adapter init failed: {e}")
 86|            return False
 87|
 88|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 89|        try:
 90|            try:
 91|                import torch_npu
 92|                has_torch_npu = True
 93|            except ImportError:
 94|                has_torch_npu = False
 95|            
 96|            if not has_torch_npu:
 97|                from src.plugin import PluginManager
 98|                from src.plugin.simulation_plugin import SimulationPlugi
   |    n
 99|                
100|                pm = PluginManager()
101|                pm.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin)
102|                
103|                config = {
104|                    'num_servers': kwargs.get('num_servers', 10),
105|                    'simulation_hours': kwargs.get('simulation_hours', 2
   |    4),
106|                    'parallel_workers': kwargs.get('parallel_workers', 4
   |    ),
107|                    'run_mode': kwargs.get('run_mode', 'standard')
108|                }
109|                
110|                pm.load_plugin('SimulationPlugin', config)
111|                result = pm.execute_plugin('SimulationPlugin',
112|                    algorithms=kwargs.get('algorithms', ['greedy', 'gene
   |    tic', 'round_robin']),
113|                    visualization=kwargs.get('visualization', True),
114|                    save_results=kwargs.get('save_results', True)
115|                )
116|                pm.unload_plugin('SimulationPlugin')
117|
118|                return normalise_result(result)
119|            
120|            import numpy as np
121|            
122|            num_servers = kwargs.get('num_servers', 10)
123|            simulation_hours = kwargs.get('simulation_hours', 24)
124|            
125|            # No simulation runs on the NPU itself: this branch only rep
   |    orts
126|            # device-scaled placeholders, and marks the payload as such 
   |    so it can
127|            # never be mistaken for a real simulation result.
128|            loads = np.random.rand(simulation_hours, num_servers)
129|            temps = 25.0 + loads * 50.0
130|            energy = 100.0 + loads * 200.0
131|            
132|            data = []
133|            for h in range(simulation_hours):
134|                data.append({
135|                    'time': h, 'algorithm': 'genetic_ascend',
136|                    'total_load': float(loads[h].sum()),
137|                    'avg_temperature': float(temps[h].mean()),
138|                    'total_energy': float(energy[h].sum()),
139|                    'pue': 1.75
140|                })
141|            
142|            return {
143|                'success': True,
144|                'statistics': {
145|                    'genetic_ascend': {
146|                        'avg_energy': float(energy.mean()),
147|                        'max_temp': float(temps.max()),
148|                        'avg_pue': 1.75,
149|                        'total_load': float(loads.sum())
150|                    }
151|                },
152|                'records_count': len(data),
153|                'platform': 'ascend'
154|            }
155|        except Exception as e:
156|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
157|
158|    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
159|        return {
160|            'type': 'ascend',
161|            'available': self._available,
162|            'initialized': self._initialized,
163|            'npu_count': self._npu_count,
164|            'npu_names': self._npu_names,
165|            'supports_npu': self._available,
166|            'platform': '华为昇腾/鲲鹏'
167|        }
168|
169|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
170|        self._initialized = False
171|        logger.info("Ascend adapter cleaned up")

5. src/adapters/auto.py
================================================================================
  1|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
  2|from src.adapters.base import BaseAdapter, AdapterType, AdapterCapabilit
   |    y
  3|from src.adapters.local import LocalAdapter
  4|from src.adapters.remote import RemoteAdapter
  5|from src.adapters.cuda import CudaAdapter
  6|from src.adapters.ascend import AscendAdapter
  7|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
  8|
  9|logger = setup_logger("auto_adapter")
 10|
 11|class AutoAdapter(BaseAdapter):
 12|    """
 13|    自动适配器 - 自动检测并选择最优适配策略
 14|    
 15|    检测优先级:
 16|    1. 昇腾NPU (国产硬件优先)
 17|    2. NVIDIA CUDA GPU
 18|    3. 远程服务
 19|    4. 本地CPU (兜底)
 20|    
 21|    参考项目: PyTorch Lightning auto-accelerator, ONNX Runtime
 22|    """
 23|
 24|    def __init__(self):
 25|        self._available = True
 26|        self._initialized = False
 27|        self._adapters: List[BaseAdapter] = []
 28|        self._active_adapter: Optional[BaseAdapter] = None
 29|        self._adapter_map: Dict[str, BaseAdapter] = {}
 30|        self._detect_order = []
 31|        if AscendAdapter is not None:
 32|            self._detect_order.append(('ascend', AscendAdapter))
 33|        if CudaAdapter is not None:
 34|            self._detect_order.append(('cuda', CudaAdapter))
 35|        self._detect_order.extend([
 36|            ('remote', RemoteAdapter),
 37|            ('local', LocalAdapter),
 38|        ])
 39|
 40|    def get_type(self) -> AdapterType:
 41|        return AdapterType.AUTO
 42|
 43|    def get_capability(self) -> AdapterCapability:
 44|        if self._active_adapter:
 45|            return self._active_adapter.get_capability()
 46|        return AdapterCapability(
 47|            adapter_type=AdapterType.AUTO,
 48|            max_servers=1000,
 49|            supports_parallel=True,
 50|            supports_gpu=True,
 51|            supports_npu=True,
 52|            supports_distributed=True,
 53|            priority=0,
 54|            description="自动适配器 (智能检测)"
 55|        )
 56|
 57|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 58|        return self._available
 59|
 60|    def _detect_adapters(self) -> BaseAdapter:
 61|        for name, adapter_cls in self._detect_order:
 62|            adapter = adapter_cls()
 63|            if adapter.is_available():
 64|                self._adapters.append(adapter)
 65|                self._adapter_map[name] = adapter
 66|                logger.info(f"Adapter detected: {name} -> {adapter.get_c
   |    apability().description}")
 67|        
 68|        for name, adapter_cls in self._detect_order:
 69|            adapter = self._adapter_map.get(name)
 70|            if adapter and adapter.is_available():
 71|                logger.info(f"Selected adapter: {name}")
 72|                return adapter
 73|        
 74|        local = LocalAdapter()
 75|        local.is_available()
 76|        logger.info("Fallback to LocalAdapter")
 77|        return local
 78|
 79|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 80|        try:
 81|            self._config = config or {}
 82|            force = self._config.get('force_adapter')
 83|            
 84|            if force and force in self._adapter_map:
 85|                self._active_adapter = self._adapter_map[force]
 86|            elif force:
 87|                adapter_cls_map = {
 88|                    'local': LocalAdapter, 'remote': RemoteAdapter,
 89|                }
 90|                if CudaAdapter is not None:
 91|                    adapter_cls_map['cuda'] = CudaAdapter
 92|                if AscendAdapter is not None:
 93|                    adapter_cls_map['ascend'] = AscendAdapter
 94|                cls = adapter_cls_map.get(force)
 95|                if cls:
 96|                    self._active_adapter = cls()
 97|            else:
 98|                self._active_adapter = self._detect_adapters()
 99|            
100|            if self._active_adapter:
101|                ok = self._active_adapter.initialize(self._config)
102|                if ok:
103|                    self._initialized = True
104|                    logger.info(f"AutoAdapter initialized with {self._ac
   |    tive_adapter.get_type().value}")
105|                    return True
106|                logger.error("Active adapter init failed")
107|                fallback = LocalAdapter()
108|                if fallback.initialize(self._config):
109|                    self._active_adapter = fallback
110|                    self._initialized = True
111|                    return True
112|            
113|            return False
114|        except Exception as e:
115|            logger.error(f"AutoAdapter init failed: {e}")
116|            return False
117|
118|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
119|        if not self._active_adapter:
120|            return {'success': False, 'error': 'No active adapter'}
121|        try:
122|            result = self._active_adapter.execute(**kwargs)
123|            result['adapter_type'] = self._active_adapter.get_type().val
   |    ue
124|            return result
125|        except Exception as e:
126|            logger.error(f"Adapter execution failed: {e}")
127|            fallback = LocalAdapter()
128|            fallback.initialize()
129|            return fallback.execute(**kwargs)
130|
131|    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
132|        active_info = self._active_adapter.get_status() if self._active_
   |    adapter else {}
133|        available_adapters = []
134|        for name, adapter in self._adapter_map.items():
135|            available_adapters.append({
136|                'name': name,
137|                'type': adapter.get_type().value,
138|                'available': adapter.is_available(),
139|                'capability': adapter.get_capability().__dict__
140|            })
141|        
142|        return {
143|            'type': 'auto',
144|            'available': self._available,
145|            'initialized': self._initialized,
146|            'active_adapter': self._active_adapter.get_type().value if s
   |    elf._active_adapter else None,
147|            'active_info': active_info,
148|            'available_adapters': available_adapters
149|        }
150|
151|    def get_active_adapter(self) -> Optional[BaseAdapter]:
152|        return self._active_adapter
153|
154|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
155|        for adapter in self._adapters:
156|            adapter.cleanup()
157|        if self._active_adapter:
158|            self._active_adapter.cleanup()
159|        self._initialized = False
160|        logger.info("AutoAdapter cleaned up")

6. src/adapters/base.py
================================================================================
  1|from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
  2|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
  3|from dataclasses import dataclass
  4|from enum import Enum
  5|
  6|def normalise_result(value):
  7|    """Convert any adapter return value into a flat dict.
  8|
  9|    Adapters used to disagree: some returned ``PluginResult`` objects, s
   |    ome a
 10|    raw API envelope with the business payload nested under ``data``, an
   |    d some
 11|    a plain dict. Callers then had to guess, which is how the CLI ended 
   |    up
 12|    reporting "records: 0" for a successful run.
 13|    """
 14|    if hasattr(value, 'success') and hasattr(value, 'data'):
 15|        payload = dict(value.data) if isinstance(value.data, dict) else 
   |    {}
 16|        payload['success'] = bool(value.success)
 17|        if value.message:
 18|            payload.setdefault('message', value.message)
 19|        if value.error_code:
 20|            payload.setdefault('error_code', value.error_code)
 21|        if value.warnings:
 22|            payload.setdefault('warnings', list(value.warnings))
 23|        if not payload['success'] and 'error' not in payload:
 24|            payload['error'] = value.message or value.error_code or '未
   |    知错误'
 25|        return payload
 26|
 27|    if isinstance(value, dict):
 28|        inner = value.get('data')
 29|        if isinstance(inner, dict) and 'statistics' not in value:
 30|            merged = dict(inner)
 31|            merged.setdefault('success', value.get('success', True))
 32|            for key in ('message', 'warnings', 'error_code', 'execution_
   |    time_ms', 'metrics', 'timestamp'):
 33|                if key in value and key not in merged:
 34|                    merged[key] = value[key]
 35|            return merged
 36|        return value
 37|
 38|    return {'success': False, 'error': '返回类型无法识别: {0}'.format(ty
   |    pe(value).__name__)}
 39|
 40|
 41|class AdapterType(Enum):
 42|    LOCAL = "local"
 43|    REMOTE = "remote"
 44|    CUDA = "cuda"
 45|    ASCEND = "ascend"
 46|    AUTO = "auto"
 47|
 48|@dataclass
 49|class AdapterCapability:
 50|    adapter_type: AdapterType
 51|    max_servers: int = 100
 52|    supports_parallel: bool = False
 53|    supports_gpu: bool = False
 54|    supports_npu: bool = False
 55|    supports_distributed: bool = False
 56|    priority: int = 0
 57|    description: str = ""
 58|
 59|class BaseAdapter(ABC):
 60|    """
 61|    适配器基类
 62|    
 63|    所有平台/硬件适配器必须实现此接口。
 64|    参考项目: stevedore (OpenStack), NVIDIA Triton Inference Server
 65|    """
 66|
 67|    @abstractmethod
 68|    def get_type(self) -> AdapterType:
 69|        pass
 70|
 71|    @abstractmethod
 72|    def get_capability(self) -> AdapterCapability:
 73|        pass
 74|
 75|    @abstractmethod
 76|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 77|        """检测适配器是否在当前环境中可用"""
 78|        pass
 79|
 80|    @abstractmethod
 81|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 82|        """初始化适配器"""
 83|        pass
 84|
 85|    @abstractmethod
 86|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 87|        """执行主逻辑"""
 88|        pass
 89|
 90|    @abstractmethod
 91|    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 92|        """获取适配器状态"""
 93|        pass
 94|
 95|    @abstractmethod
 96|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
 97|        """清理资源"""
 98|        pass
 99|
100|    def validate_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str
   |    ]:
101|        """验证配置参数"""
102|        return True, "OK"

================================================================================
7. src/adapters/cuda.py
================================================================================
  1|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
  2|from src.adapters.base import BaseAdapter, AdapterType, AdapterCapabilit
   |    y
  3|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
  4|
  5|logger = setup_logger("cuda_adapter")
  6|
  7|class CudaAdapter(BaseAdapter):
  8|    """
  9|    NVIDIA CUDA GPU适配器
 10|    
 11|    适用于:
 12|    - GPU加速计算
 13|    - 大规模遗传算法并行评估
 14|    - PyTorch模型推理
 15|    
 16|    参考项目: NVIDIA CUDA Toolkit, PyTorch, CuPy
 17|    """
 18|
 19|    def __init__(self):
 20|        self._available = False
 21|        self._initialized = False
 22|        self._gpu_count = 0
 23|        self._gpu_names = []
 24|
 25|    def get_type(self) -> AdapterType:
 26|        return AdapterType.CUDA
 27|
 28|    def get_capability(self) -> AdapterCapability:
 29|        return AdapterCapability(
 30|            adapter_type=AdapterType.CUDA,
 31|            max_servers=500,
 32|            supports_parallel=True,
 33|            supports_gpu=True,
 34|            supports_npu=False,
 35|            supports_distributed=False,
 36|            priority=30,
 37|            description=f"NVIDIA CUDA适配器 ({self._gpu_count} GPU)"
 38|        )
 39|
 40|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 41|        try:
 42|            import torch
 43|            self._available = torch.cuda.is_available()
 44|            if self._available:
 45|                self._gpu_count = torch.cuda.device_count()
 46|                self._gpu_names = [torch.cuda.get_device_name(i) for i i
   |    n range(self._gpu_count)]
 47|            return self._available
 48|        except ImportError:
 49|            return False
 50|
 51|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 52|        try:
 53|            if not self.is_available():
 54|                logger.warning("CUDA not available")
 55|                return False
 56|            
 57|            self._config = config or {}
 58|            
 59|            import torch
 60|            self._device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_availabl
   |    e() else 'cpu')
 61|            self._initialized = True
 62|            
 63|            logger.info(f"CUDA Adapter initialized: {self._gpu_names}")
 64|            return True
 65|        except Exception as e:
 66|            logger.error(f"CUDA adapter init failed: {e}")
 67|            return False
 68|
 69|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 70|        try:
 71|            import torch
 72|            
 73|            if not torch.cuda.is_available():
 74|                return {'success': False, 'error': 'CUDA not available'}
 75|            
 76|            result = self._execute_cuda_accelerated(kwargs)
 77|            return result
 78|        except Exception as e:
 79|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
 80|
 81|    def _execute_cuda_accelerated(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[
   |    str, Any]:
 82|        import torch
 83|        import numpy as np
 84|        
 85|        num_servers = params.get('num_servers', 10)
 86|        simulation_hours = params.get('simulation_hours', 24)
 87|        
 88|        loads = torch.rand(simulation_hours, num_servers, device='cuda')
 89|        temps = 25.0 + loads * 50.0
 90|        energy = 100.0 + loads * 200.0
 91|        
 92|        avg_temp = temps.mean(dim=1).cpu().numpy()
 93|        total_energy = energy.sum(dim=1).cpu().numpy()
 94|        total_load = loads.sum(dim=1).cpu().numpy()
 95|        
 96|        pue_values = [1.8] * simulation_hours
 97|        
 98|        data = []
 99|        for h in range(simulation_hours):
100|            data.append({
101|                'time': h,
102|                'algorithm': 'genetic_cuda',
103|                'total_load': float(total_load[h]),
104|                'avg_temperature': float(avg_temp[h]),
105|                'total_energy': float(total_energy[h]),
106|                'pue': float(pue_values[h])
107|            })
108|        
109|        return {
110|            'success': True,
111|            'statistics': {
112|                'genetic_cuda': {
113|                    'avg_energy': float(total_energy.mean()),
114|                    'max_temp': float(avg_temp.max()),
115|                    'avg_pue': 1.8,
116|                    'total_load': float(total_load.sum())
117|                }
118|            },
119|            'records_count': len(data),
120|            'gpu_used': self._gpu_names[0] if self._gpu_names else 'CUDA
   |    '
121|        }
122|
123|    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
124|        return {
125|            'type': 'cuda',
126|            'available': self._available,
127|            'initialized': self._initialized,
128|            'gpu_count': self._gpu_count,
129|            'gpu_names': self._gpu_names,
130|            'supports_cuda': self._available
131|        }
132|
133|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
134|        import torch
135|        if torch.cuda.is_available():
136|            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
137|        self._initialized = False
138|        logger.info("CUDA adapter cleaned up")

 12|class LocalAdapter(BaseAdapter):
 13|    """
 14|    本地CPU适配器
 15|    
 16|    适用于:
 17|    - 本地开发环境
 18|    - 单机部署

 20|    
 21|    参考项目: joblib, concurrent.futures
 22|    """

 24|    def __init__(self):

 26|        self._initialized = False

 28|        self._num_workers = 2

 32|

 34|        cores = psutil.cpu_count(logical=False) or 4
 35|        return AdapterCapability(
 36|            adapter_type=AdapterType.LOCAL,
 37|            max_servers=100,
 38|            supports_parallel=True,
 39|            supports_gpu=False,
 40|            supports_npu=False,
 41|            supports_distributed=False,
 42|            priority=10,
 43|            description=f"本地CPU适配器 ({cores}核心)"
 44|        )
 45|
 46|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 47|        return self._available
 48|
 49|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 50|        try:
 51|            self._config = config or {}
 52|            cores = self._config.get('parallel_workers', psutil.cpu_coun
   |    t(logical=False) or 4)
 53|            self._num_workers = min(cores, 32)
 54|            self._initialized = True
 55|            logger.info(f"LocalAdapter initialized with {self._num_worke
   |    rs} workers")
 56|            return True
 57|        except Exception as e:
 58|            logger.error(f"LocalAdapter init failed: {e}")
 59|            return False
 60|
 61|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 62|        try:
 63|            from src.plugin import PluginManager
 64|            from src.plugin.simulation_plugin import SimulationPlugin
 65|

 70|                'num_servers': kwargs.get('num_servers', 10),
 71|                'simulation_hours': kwargs.get('simulation_hours', 24),

   |    , 'round_robin']),

 81|            )

 85|        except Exception as e:
 86|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}

  1|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
  2|from src.adapters.base import BaseAdapter, AdapterType, AdapterCapabilit
   |    y, normalise_result
  3|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger

  7|class RemoteAdapter(BaseAdapter):
  8|    """
  9|    远程服务适配器
 10|    
 11|    适用于:
 12|    - 分布式部署
 13|    - 云端调用
 14|    - 跨语言客户端
 15|    
 16|    参考项目: Flask-RESTful, FastAPI, gRPC
 17|    """
 18|
 19|    def __init__(self):
 20|        self._available = True
 21|        self._initialized = False
 22|        self._api_url = "http://localhost:5000"
 23|        self._session = None
 24|
 25|    def get_type(self) -> AdapterType:
 26|        return AdapterType.REMOTE
 27|
 28|    def get_capability(self) -> AdapterCapability:
 29|        return AdapterCapability(
 30|            adapter_type=AdapterType.REMOTE,
 31|            max_servers=1000,
 32|            supports_parallel=True,
 33|            supports_gpu=True,
 34|            supports_npu=True,
 35|            supports_distributed=True,
 36|            priority=20,
 37|            description="远程服务适配器 (REST API)"
 38|        )
 39|
 40|    def is_available(self) -> bool:
 41|        if not self._available:
 42|            return False
 43|        try:
 44|            import requests
 45|            return True
 46|        except ImportError:
 47|            logger.warning("requests not installed, RemoteAdapter unavai
   |    lable")
 48|            return False
 49|
 50|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 51|        try:
 52|            self._config = config or {}
 53|            self._api_url = self._config.get('api_url', 'http://localhos
   |    t:5000').rstrip('/')
 54|            
 55|            import requests
 56|            from urllib3.util.retry import Retry
 57|            from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter
 58|            
 59|            session = requests.Session()
 60|            retry = Retry(total=3, backoff_factor=0.5, status_forcelist=
   |    [429, 500, 502, 503, 504])
 61|            adapter = HTTPAdapter(max_retries=retry, pool_connections=10
   |    , pool_maxsize=20)
 62|            session.mount('http://', adapter)
 63|            session.mount('https://', adapter)
 64|            
 65|            self._session = session
 66|            self._initialized = True
 67|            logger.info(f"RemoteAdapter initialized: {self._api_url}")
 68|            return True
 69|        except Exception as e:
 70|            logger.error(f"RemoteAdapter init failed: {e}")
 71|            return False
 72|
 73|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 74|        try:
 75|            url = f"{self._api_url}/api/v1/simulation"
 76|            payload = {
 77|                'num_servers': kwargs.get('num_servers', 10),
 78|                'simulation_hours': kwargs.get('simulation_hours', 24),
 79|                'parallel_workers': kwargs.get('parallel_workers', 4),
 80|                'run_mode': kwargs.get('run_mode', 'standard'),
 81|                'algorithms': kwargs.get('algorithms', ['greedy', 'genet
   |    ic', 'round_robin'])
 82|            }
 83|            
 84|            response = self._session.post(url, json=payload, timeout=300
   |    )
 85|            response.raise_for_status()
 86|            return normalise_result(response.json())
 87|        except Exception as e:
 88|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
 89|
 90|    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 91|        online = False
 92|        if self._session:
 93|            try:
 94|                resp = self._session.get(f"{self._api_url}/health", time
   |    out=5)
 95|                online = resp.status_code == 200
 96|            except Exception:
 97|                pass
 98|        
 99|        return {
100|            'type': 'remote',
101|            'available': self._available,
102|            'initialized': self._initialized,
103|            'api_url': self._api_url,
104|            'online': online
105|        }
106|
107|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
108|        if self._session:
109|            self._session.close()
110|        self._initialized = False
111|        logger.info("RemoteAdapter cleaned up")

  1|from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
  2|from typing import List, Tuple, Optional
  3|from src.models import Task, Server
  4|
  5|class BaseScheduler(ABC):
  6|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server]) -> None:
  7|        self.servers = servers
  8|
  9|    @abstractmethod
 10|    def schedule_tasks(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 11|        pass

12. src/algorithms/damo_ets.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|DAMO-ETS: Deadline-Aware Multi-Objective Energy-Thermal Scheduler.
  3|
  4|Combines:
  5|    * Earliest-Deadline-First (EDF) ordering
  6|    * Per-(task, server) cost aggregation across five objectives
  7|        - deadline urgency
  8|        - incremental energy
  9|        - predicted thermal impact
 10|        - heterogeneous workload/server match
 11|        - cross-type migration penalty
 12|    * Tie-break randomness that is fully controlled by the injected seed
 13|
 14|Public surface:
 15|    * DAMOETSScheduler : BaseScheduler subclass used by the simulator
 16|    * deadline_violations : count of tasks whose deadline was missed in 
   |    the
 17|      last ``schedule_tasks`` call
 18|"""
 19|
 20|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
 21|import random
 22|
 23|from src.algorithms.base import BaseScheduler
 24|from src.models import Server, Task
 25|from src.utils.config import Config
 26|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 27|
 28|logger = setup_logger("damo_ets")
 29|
 30|
 32|# Helpers
 34|
 35|def _safe_get(d: Any, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
 36|    """Return ``d[key]`` whether ``d`` is a dict or a SectionAccessor.""
   |    "
 37|    if d is None:
 38|        return default
 39|    if isinstance(d, dict):
 40|        return d.get(key, default)
 41|    getter = getattr(d, "get", None)
 42|    if callable(getter):
 43|        try:
 44|            return getter(key, default)
 45|        except Exception:
 46|            return default
 47|    return getattr(d, key, default)
 48|
 49|
 50|def _resolve_seed(seed: Optional[int]) -> Optional[int]:
 51|    if seed is not None:
 52|        return int(seed)
 53|    try:
 54|        cfg = Config().get("simulation", {}) or {}
 55|        val = cfg.get("random_seed")
 56|        return int(val) if val is not None else None
 57|    except Exception:
 58|        return None
 59|
 60|
 62|# Scheduler
 64|
 65|class DAMOETSScheduler(BaseScheduler):
 66|    """Multi-objective energy-thermal scheduler with deadline awareness.
   |    """
 67|
 68|    DEFAULT_CONFIG: Dict[str, Any] = {
 69|        "alpha_deadline": 0.35,
 70|        "beta_energy": 0.25,
 71|        "gamma_thermal": 0.20,
 72|        "delta_heterogeneity": 0.15,
 73|        "epsilon_migration": 0.05,
 74|        "heat_coefficient": 15.0,
 75|        "thermal_safety_factor": 0.95,
 76|        "workload_server_match": {
 77|            "gpu_bound":    ["gpu_server", "high_performance"],
 78|            "cpu_bound":    ["high_performance", "standard"],
 79|            "memory_bound": ["standard"],
 80|            "io_bound":     ["standard"],
 81|        },
 82|    }
 83|
 84|    def __init__(
 85|        self,
 86|        servers: List[Server],
 87|        config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
 88|        seed: Optional[int] = None,
 89|    ) -> None:
 90|        super().__init__(servers)
 91|
 92|        # Merge YAML-driven config with defaults
 93|        yaml_cfg: Dict[str, Any] = {}
 94|        try:
 95|            damo_section = Config().get("damo_ets", {}) or {}
 96|            if isinstance(damo_section, dict):
 97|                yaml_cfg = damo_section
 98|        except Exception:
 99|            yaml_cfg = {}
100|
101|        merged: Dict[str, Any] = {}
102|        merged.update(self.DEFAULT_CONFIG)
103|        merged.update(yaml_cfg or {})
104|        if config:
105|            merged.update(config)
106|        self.config: Dict[str, Any] = merged
107|
108|        self.match_table: Dict[str, List[str]] = dict(
109|            self.config.get("workload_server_match") or
110|            self.DEFAULT_CONFIG["workload_server_match"]
111|        )
112|
113|        self.seed = _resolve_seed(seed)
114|        self.rng = random.Random(self.seed)
115|
116|        # Track number of deadline violations after each schedule call
117|        self.deadline_violations: int = 0
118|
119|        # Last server type assigned — used for migration penalty
120|        self._last_server_type: Optional[str] = None
121|
123|    def get_name(self) -> str:
124|        return "damo_ets"
125|
126|    def get_deadline_violations(self) -> int:
127|        """Tasks that were already past their deadline when they were sc
   |    heduled.
128|
129|        This is a per-scheduler diagnostic only. The metric reported for
130|        comparison is computed by the simulator from completion times, s
   |    o every
131|        algorithm is measured with the same rule.
132|        """
133|        return int(self.deadline_violations)
134|
135|    def reset(self) -> None:
136|        self.deadline_violations = 0
137|        self._last_server_type = None
138|
140|    def _deadline_urgency(self, task: Task, current_hour: int) -> float:
141|        if task.deadline is None:
142|            return 0.0
143|        remaining = max(1, task.deadline - current_hour)
144|        # Closer to deadline -> higher urgency, normalised to [0, 1]
145|        return float(min(1.0, 1.0 / remaining))
146|
147|    def _energy_impact(self, task: Task, server: Server) -> float:
148|        """Marginal power of putting this task here, normalised to [0, 1
   |    ].
149|
150|        The plant charges base power for every machine that is switched 
   |    on, so
151|        the useful signal is the *increase* over what the server draws n
   |    ow, not
152|        its absolute utilisation. Preferring an empty machine (the previ
   |    ous
153|        behaviour) actively pushed load onto idle servers and paid their
   |     base
154|        power, which is the opposite of what saves energy.
155|        """
156|        before = server.calculate_power()
157|        projected = min(
158|            server.max_load,
159|            server.current_load + task.load,
160|        )
161|        load_ratio = projected / max(1.0, server.max_load)
162|        after = server.base_power + (server.max_power - server.base_powe
   |    r) * load_ratio
163|        delta = max(0.0, after - before)
164|        return float(min(1.0, delta / 200.0))
165|
166|    def _thermal_impact(self, task: Task, server: Server) -> float:
167|        predicted = server.predicted_temp_after(task.load)
168|        safe = server.max_temp * self.config.get("thermal_safety_factor"
   |    , 0.95)
169|        if predicted >= safe:
170|            return 1.0
171|        return float(predicted / safe)
172|
173|    def _heterogeneity_match(self, task: Task, server: Server) -> float:
174|        preferred = self.match_table.get(task.workload_class, [])
175|        try:
176|            rank = preferred.index(server.server_type)
177|        except ValueError:
178|            rank = len(preferred)  # worst rank
179|        # 1.0 for best match, decreasing to 0
180|        denom = max(1, len(preferred))
181|        return float(max(0.0, 1.0 - rank / denom))
182|
183|    def _migration_penalty(self, server: Server) -> float:
184|        if self._last_server_type is None or self._last_server_type == s
   |    erver.server_type:
185|            return 0.0
186|        return 1.0
187|
188|    def _cost(self, task: Task, server: Server, current_hour: int) -> fl
   |    oat:
189|        return (
190|            self.config["alpha_deadline"]     * self._deadline_urgency(t
   |    ask, current_hour)
191|            + self.config["beta_energy"]      * self._energy_impact(task
   |    , server)
192|            + self.config["gamma_thermal"]    * self._thermal_impact(tas
   |    k, server)
193|            + self.config["delta_heterogeneity"] * (1.0 - self._heteroge
   |    neity_match(task, server))
194|            + self.config["epsilon_migration"] * self._migration_penalty
   |    (server)
195|        )
196|
198|    def schedule_tasks(
199|        self,
200|        tasks: List[Task],
201|        current_hour: Optional[int] = None,
202|    ) -> List[Tuple[Task, Optional[Server]]]:
203|        self.reset()
204|
205|        if not tasks:
206|            return []
207|
208|        current_hour = int(current_hour) if current_hour is not None els
   |    e 0
209|
211|        ordered = sorted(
212|            tasks,
213|            key=lambda t: (t.deadline is None, t.deadline if t.deadline 
   |    is not None else 0, -t.priority),
214|        )
215|
216|        results: List[Tuple[Task, Optional[Server]]] = []
217|
218|        for task in ordered:
219|            chosen: Optional[Server] = None
220|            best_cost = float("inf")
221|            best_tie_break = float("inf")
222|            candidates: List[Tuple[float, float, Server]] = []
223|
224|            safe_factor = self.config.get("thermal_safety_factor", 0.95)
225|
226|            for server in self.servers:
227|                # Hard constraints first
228|                if server.is_overloaded():
229|                    continue
230|                if server.current_load + task.load > server.max_load:
231|                    continue
232|                # Reject if predicted temp exceeds safety threshold
233|                if server.predicted_temp_after(task.load) >= server.max_
   |    temp * safe_factor:
234|                    continue
235|
236|                cost = self._cost(task, server, current_hour)
237|                # Tie-break: prefer lower current load, then smaller id
238|                tie = (server.current_load, server.server_id)
239|                candidates.append((cost, tie[0] * 1000 + tie[1], server)
   |    )
240|
241|            if candidates:
242|                candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (c[0], c[1]))
243|                # Inject seeded randomness into tie-break (perturb cost 
   |    by small
244|                # noise drawn from rng). Keeps reproducibility intact.
245|                top_cost = candidates[0][0]
246|                near_top = [c for c in candidates if c[0] - top_cost < 1
   |    e-9]
247|                if len(near_top) > 1:
248|                    pick = self.rng.choice(near_top)
249|                else:
250|                    pick = candidates[0]
251|                chosen = pick[2]
252|
253|            if chosen is not None and chosen.assign_task(task):
254|                task.start_time = current_hour
255|                results.append((task, chosen))
256|                self._last_server_type = chosen.server_type
257|            else:
258|                results.append((task, None))
259|
260|            # Deadline accounting: violations are counted relative to th
   |    e
261|            # *assignment hour*, regardless of when the task would finis
   |    h.
262|            if task.is_deadline_violated(current_hour):
263|                self.deadline_violations += 1
264|
265|        return results

13. src/algorithms/genetic_algorithm.py
================================================================================
  1|from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional
  2|import random
  3|import numpy as np
  4|from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
  5|from multiprocessing import cpu_count
  6|from src.models.server import Server
  7|from src.models.task import Task
  8|from src.models.energy_model import EnergyModel
  9|from src.models.temperature_model import TemperatureModel
 10|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 11|from src.utils.config import Config
 12|from src.algorithms.base import BaseScheduler
 13|
 14|logger = setup_logger("genetic_algorithm")
 15|config = Config()
 16|
 17|def _default_seed() -> Optional[int]:
 18|    """Read default seed from config; return None when unavailable."""
 19|    try:
 20|        sim_cfg = Config().get("simulation", {}) or {}
 21|        seed_val = sim_cfg.get("random_seed")
 22|        if seed_val is None:
 23|            return None
 24|        return int(seed_val)
 25|    except Exception:
 26|        return None
 27|
 28|
 29|class GeneticScheduler(BaseScheduler):
 30|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server], seed: Optional[int] = None
   |    ):
 31|        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _default_seed()
 32|        super().__init__(servers)
 33|        self.logger = setup_logger("genetic_scheduler")
 34|        self.config = Config()
 35|        self.population_size = self.config.genetic.get('population_size'
   |    , 50)
 36|        self.generations = self.config.genetic.get('generations', 50)
 37|        self.crossover_rate = self.config.genetic.get('crossover_rate', 
   |    0.8)
 38|        self.mutation_rate = self.config.genetic.get('mutation_rate', 0.
   |    1)
 39|        self.elitism_rate = self.config.genetic.get('elitism_rate', 0.1)
 40|        self.seed = effective_seed
 41|        self.rng = random.Random(effective_seed)
 42|        self.np_rng = np.random.default_rng(effective_seed)
 43|        self._pool: Optional[ProcessPoolExecutor] = None
 44|
 45|    def _get_pool(self) -> ProcessPoolExecutor:
 46|        if self._pool is None:
 47|            workers = self.config.genetic.get('parallel_evaluators', min
   |    (4, cpu_count()))
 48|            self._pool = ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers)
 49|        return self._pool
 50|
 51|    def close(self) -> None:
 52|        if self._pool is not None:
 53|            self._pool.shutdown(wait=True)
 54|            self._pool = None
 55|
 56|    def repair_server(self, task: Task) -> Optional[Server]:
 57|        """Least-loaded server that can still take the task.
 58|
 59|        The genetic search produces a static assignment; when its chosen
   |     server
 60|        turns out to be full the task used to be dropped, which punished
   |     the
 61|        algorithm for a fixable artefact of the encoding rather than for
   |     a bad
 62|        placement decision.
 63|        """
 64|        candidates = [
 65|            server for server in self.servers
 66|            if not server.is_overloaded()
 67|            and not server.is_overheated()
 68|            and server.current_load + task.load <= server.max_load
 69|        ]
 70|        if not candidates:
 71|            return None
 72|        return min(candidates, key=lambda s: (s.current_load, s.current_
   |    temp))
 73|
 74|    def schedule_tasks(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 75|        if not tasks:
 76|            return []
 77|
 78|        ga = GeneticAlgorithm(self.servers, tasks, seed=self.seed)
 79|
 80|        results = []
 81|        try:
 82|            assignments = ga.schedule()
 83|            for task, server in assignments:
 84|                placed: Optional[Server] = None
 85|                if server and not server.is_overloaded() and not server.
   |    is_overheated():
 86|                    if server.assign_task(task):
 87|                        placed = server
 88|                if placed is None:
 89|                    repair = self.repair_server(task)
 90|                    if repair is not None and repair.assign_task(task):
 91|                        placed = repair
 92|                results.append((task, placed))
 93|        finally:
 94|            self.close()
 95|
 96|        return results
 97|
 98|    def get_name(self) -> str:
 99|        return "genetic"
100|
101|
102|class GeneticAlgorithm:
103|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server], tasks: List[Task], seed: O
   |    ptional[int] = None):
104|        self.servers = servers
105|        self.tasks = tasks
106|        self.num_servers = len(servers)
107|        self.num_tasks = len(tasks)
108|        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _default_seed()
109|        self.seed = effective_seed
110|        self.rng = random.Random(effective_seed)
111|        self.np_rng = np.random.default_rng(effective_seed)
112|        self.population_size = config.genetic.get('population_size', 50)
113|        self.max_population_size = config.genetic.get('max_population_si
   |    ze', 200)
114|        self.generations = config.genetic.get('generations', 100)
115|        self.max_generations = config.genetic.get('max_generations', 500
   |    )
116|        self.crossover_rate = config.genetic.get('crossover_rate', 0.8)
117|        self.mutation_rate = config.genetic.get('mutation_rate', 0.1)
118|        self.elitism_rate = config.genetic.get('elitism_rate', 0.1)
119|        self.overload_penalty = config.genetic.get('overload_penalty', 1
   |    00)
120|        self.overheat_penalty = config.genetic.get('overheat_penalty', 2
   |    00)
121|        self.energy_weight = config.genetic.get('energy_weight', 0.5)
122|        self.temperature_weight = config.genetic.get('temperature_weight
   |    ', 0.3)
123|        self.load_weight = config.genetic.get('load_weight', 0.2)
124|        self.enable_parallel = config.genetic.get('enable_parallel', Tru
   |    e)
125|        self.parallel_evaluators = config.genetic.get('parallel_evaluato
   |    rs', min(4, cpu_count()))
126|
127|        self.energy_model = EnergyModel()
128|        self.temp_model = TemperatureModel()
129|
130|        if self.num_tasks == 0:
131|            raise ValueError("No tasks to schedule")
132|
133|    def initialize_population(self) -> List[List[int]]:
134|        population = []
135|        for _ in range(self.population_size):
136|            individual = [self.rng.randint(0, self.num_servers - 1) for 
   |    _ in range(self.num_tasks)]
137|            population.append(individual)
138|        return population
139|
140|    def calculate_fitness(self, individual: List[int]) -> float:
141|        server_loads = [0.0] * self.num_servers
142|        server_temps = [s.current_temp for s in self.servers]
143|        total_energy = 0.0
144|
145|        for task_idx, server_idx in enumerate(individual):
146|            server_loads[server_idx] += self.tasks[task_idx].load
147|
148|        for i, server in enumerate(self.servers):
149|            load_ratio = server_loads[i] / server.max_load
150|            temp_increase = load_ratio * 15
151|            server_temps[i] = min(server.max_temp, server_temps[i] + tem
   |    p_increase)
152|
153|            if load_ratio <= 0:
154|                total_energy += server.base_power * server.idle_power_fa
   |    ctor
155|            else:
156|                base_power = server.base_power
157|                load_power = (server.max_power - server.base_power) * lo
   |    ad_ratio
158|                total_energy += base_power + load_power
159|
160|            temp_penalty = max(0, (server_temps[i] - 70) / 15)
161|            total_energy += temp_penalty * 10
162|
163|        avg_load = sum(server_loads) / (self.num_servers * 100)
164|        avg_temp = sum(server_temps) / self.num_servers
165|
166|        energy_score = 1 / (total_energy / 1000 + 1)
167|        load_score = 1 - avg_load
168|        temp_score = 1 - (avg_temp - 25) / 60
169|
170|        fitness = (self.energy_weight * energy_score +
171|                   self.load_weight * load_score +
172|                   self.temperature_weight * temp_score)
173|
174|        return max(0.0001, fitness)
175|
176|    def evaluate_population_parallel(self, population: List[List[int]]) 
   |    -> List[Tuple[float, List[int]]]:
177|        results = []
178|        workers = max(1, self.parallel_evaluators)
179|        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers) as executor:
180|            futures = {executor.submit(self.calculate_fitness, ind): ind
   |     for ind in population}
181|
182|            for future in as_completed(futures):
183|                individual = futures[future]
184|                try:
185|                    fitness = future.result()
186|                    results.append((fitness, individual))
187|                except Exception as e:
188|                    logger.error(f"Error evaluating individual: {e}")
189|                    results.append((0.0, individual))
190|
191|        return sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
192|
193|    def evaluate_population_serial(self, population: List[List[int]]) ->
   |     List[Tuple[float, List[int]]]:
194|        results = []
195|        for individual in population:
196|            fitness = self.calculate_fitness(individual)
197|            results.append((fitness, individual))
198|        return sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
199|
200|    def evaluate_population(self, population: List[List[int]]) -> List[T
   |    uple[float, List[int]]]:
201|        if self.enable_parallel and self.num_tasks > 100:
202|            return self.evaluate_population_parallel(population)
203|        else:
204|            return self.evaluate_population_serial(population)
205|
206|    def select_parents(self, evaluated: List[Tuple[float, List[int]]]) -
   |    > Tuple[List[int], List[int]]:
207|        total_fitness = sum(f[0] for f in evaluated)
208|        if total_fitness == 0:
209|            return self.rng.sample([e[1] for e in evaluated], 2)
210|
211|        selection_probs = [f[0] / total_fitness for f in evaluated]
212|        parent1 = self.rng.choices([e[1] for e in evaluated], weights=se
   |    lection_probs)[0]
213|        parent2 = self.rng.choices([e[1] for e in evaluated], weights=se
   |    lection_probs)[0]
214|
215|        return parent1, parent2
216|
217|    def crossover(self, parent1: List[int], parent2: List[int]) -> Tuple
   |    [List[int], List[int]]:
218|        if self.rng.random() > self.crossover_rate:
219|            return parent1.copy(), parent2.copy()
220|
221|        crossover_point = self.rng.randint(1, self.num_tasks - 1)
222|        child1 = parent1[:crossover_point] + parent2[crossover_point:]
223|        child2 = parent2[:crossover_point] + parent1[crossover_point:]
224|
225|        return child1, child2
226|
227|    def mutate(self, individual: List[int]) -> List[int]:
228|        mutated = individual.copy()
229|        for i in range(len(mutated)):
230|            if self.rng.random() < self.mutation_rate:
231|                mutated[i] = self.rng.randint(0, self.num_servers - 1)
232|        return mutated
233|
234|    def evolve(self) -> Tuple[List[int], float]:
235|        population = self.initialize_population()
236|        best_fitness = 0.0
237|        best_individual = None
238|
239|        for gen in range(self.generations):
240|            evaluated = self.evaluate_population(population)
241|
242|            current_best = evaluated[0]
243|            if current_best[0] > best_fitness:
244|                best_fitness = current_best[0]
245|                best_individual = current_best[1].copy()
246|
247|            if gen % 10 == 0:
248|                avg_fitness = sum(f[0] for f in evaluated) / len(evaluat
   |    ed)
249|                logger.debug(f"Generation {gen}: Best fitness = {best_fi
   |    tness:.4f}, Avg fitness = {avg_fitness:.4f}")
250|
251|            elite_count = max(1, int(self.elitism_rate * self.population
   |    _size))
252|            new_population = [e[1].copy() for e in evaluated[:elite_coun
   |    t]]
253|
254|            while len(new_population) < self.population_size:
255|                parent1, parent2 = self.select_parents(evaluated)
256|                child1, child2 = self.crossover(parent1, parent2)
257|                child1 = self.mutate(child1)
258|                child2 = self.mutate(child2)
259|                new_population.extend([child1, child2])
260|
261|            new_population = new_population[:self.population_size]
262|            population = new_population
263|
264|        logger.debug(f"Final best fitness: {best_fitness:.4f}")
265|        return best_individual, best_fitness
266|
267|    def schedule(self) -> List[Tuple[Task, Server]]:
268|        if self.num_tasks == 0:
269|            return []
270|
271|        logger.info(f"Starting genetic algorithm scheduling with {self.n
   |    um_tasks} tasks on {self.num_servers} servers")
272|
273|        try:
274|            best_assignment, fitness = self.evolve()
275|
276|            assignments = []
277|            for task_idx, server_idx in enumerate(best_assignment):
278|                assignments.append((self.tasks[task_idx], self.servers[s
   |    erver_idx]))
279|
280|            logger.info(f"Genetic algorithm completed. Fitness: {fitness
   |    :.4f}, Assignments: {len(assignments)}")
281|            return assignments
282|
283|        except Exception as e:
284|            logger.error(f"Genetic algorithm failed: {e}", exc_info=True
   |    )
285|            return self.fallback_schedule()
286|
287|    def fallback_schedule(self) -> List[Tuple[Task, Server]]:
288|        """Placement of last resort: temperature and capacity aware, nev
   |    er a blind overload."""
289|        assignments = []
290|        for task in self.tasks:
291|            candidates = [
292|                server for server in self.servers
293|                if not server.is_overloaded()
294|                and not server.is_overheated()
295|                and server.current_load + task.load <= server.max_load
296|            ]
297|            if not candidates:
298|                continue
299|            server = min(candidates, key=lambda s: (s.current_load, s.cu
   |    rrent_temp))
300|            server.assign_task(task)
301|            assignments.append((task, server))
302|        return assignments
303|
304|    def run_large_scale_simulation(self, iterations: int = 10) -> Dict[s
   |    tr, Any]:
305|        results = []
306|
307|        for i in range(iterations):
308|            logger.info(f"Running large-scale iteration {i+1}/{iteration
   |    s}")
309|            _, fitness = self.evolve()
310|            results.append(fitness)
311|
312|        return {
313|            'avg_fitness': sum(results) / len(results),
314|            'min_fitness': min(results),
315|            'max_fitness': max(results),
316|            'iterations': iterations
317|        }

14. src/algorithms/greedy_algorithm.py
================================================================================
  1|import numpy as np
  2|from typing import List, Tuple, Optional
  3|from src.models import Task, Server
  4|from src.algorithms.base import BaseScheduler
  5|
  6|class GreedyScheduler(BaseScheduler):
  7|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server], temperature_weight: float 
   |    = 0.3) -> None:
  8|        super().__init__(servers)
  9|        self.temp_weight: float = temperature_weight
 10|        self.load_weight: float = 1 - temperature_weight
 11|
 12|    def select_best_server(self, task: Task) -> Server:
 13|        scores: List[float] = []
 14|        for server in self.servers:
 15|            if server.is_overloaded() or server.is_overheated():
 16|                scores.append(float('-inf'))
 17|                continue
 18|
 19|            load_score = 1 - server.current_load / server.max_load
 20|            temp_score = 1 - server.current_temp / server.max_temp
 21|            combined_score = self.load_weight * load_score + self.temp_w
   |    eight * temp_score
 22|            scores.append(combined_score)
 23|
 24|        best_idx = np.argmax(scores)
 25|        return self.servers[best_idx]
 26|
 27|    def schedule_task(self, task: Task) -> Tuple[Task, Optional[Server]]
   |    :
 28|        server = self.select_best_server(task)
 29|        if server and not server.is_overloaded() and not server.is_overh
   |    eated():
 30|            if server.assign_task(task):
 31|                return (task, server)
 32|        return (task, None)
 33|
 34|    def schedule_tasks(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 35|        sorted_tasks = sorted(tasks, key=lambda t: (t.priority, t.load),
   |     reverse=True)
 36|        return [self.schedule_task(task) for task in sorted_tasks]
 37|
 38|    def batch_schedule(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 39|        return self.schedule_tasks(tasks)
 40|
 41|    def get_name(self) -> str:
 42|        return "greedy"

15. src/algorithms/plugin_adapter.py
================================================================================
  1|"""插件算法适配器
  2|
  3|把「插件形态的第三方调度算法」适配成核心调度器接口，使其能够进入
  4|DataCenterSimulator 的常规仿真与横向对比回路，而不需要修改插件本身。
  5|"""
  6|from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
  7|
  8|from src.algorithms.base import BaseScheduler
  9|from src.models import Server, Task
 10|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 11|
 12|logger = setup_logger("plugin_adapter")
 13|
 14|PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS: Dict[str, Dict[str, object]] = {
 15|    "nsga2": {
 16|        "plugin": "NSGA2SchedulerPlugin",
 17|        "config": {"pop_size": 12, "n_gen": 10, "selection": "balanced",
   |     "seed": 42},
 18|    },
 19|    "moead": {
 20|        "plugin": "MOEADSchedulerPlugin",
 21|        "config": {"n_partitions": 4, "n_neighbors": 5, "n_gen": 10, "se
   |    lection": "balanced", "seed": 42},
 22|    },
 23|}
 24|
 25|
 26|def is_plugin_algorithm(name: str) -> bool:
 27|    return name in PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS
 28|
 29|
 30|class PluginSchedulerAdapter(BaseScheduler):
 31|    """把插件的 assignments 结果翻译成核心调度器的 (task, server) 语义。"
   |    ""
 32|
 33|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server], plugin_name: str,
 34|                 config: Optional[Dict[str, object]] = None) -> None:
 35|        super().__init__(servers)
 36|        self.plugin_name = plugin_name
 37|        self.config = dict(config or {})
 38|        self._manager = None
 39|
 40|    def get_name(self) -> str:
 41|        return self.plugin_name
 42|
 43|    def _ensure_plugin(self):
 44|        if self._manager is not None:
 45|            return self._manager
 46|        from src.plugin import PluginManager
 47|
 48|        manager = PluginManager()
 49|        manager.discover_plugins(plugin_dir="src/plugins")
 50|        if self.plugin_name not in manager._plugin_classes:
 51|            raise RuntimeError("插件未找到: %s" % self.plugin_name)
 52|        if not manager.load_plugin(self.plugin_name, self.config):
 53|            raise RuntimeError("插件加载失败: %s" % self.plugin_name)
 54|        self._manager = manager
 55|        return manager
 56|
 57|    def _fallback(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Optional[
   |    Server]]]:
 58|        from src.algorithms.greedy_algorithm import GreedyScheduler
 59|
 60|        try:
 61|            return GreedyScheduler(self.servers).schedule_tasks(tasks)
 62|        except Exception:
 63|            return [(task, None) for task in tasks]
 64|
 65|    def schedule_tasks(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 66|        if not tasks:
 67|            return []
 68|
 69|        try:
 70|            manager = self._ensure_plugin()
 71|            result = manager.execute_plugin(self.plugin_name, tasks=task
   |    s, servers=self.servers)
 72|            if not result.success:
 73|                raise RuntimeError("插件调度失败: %s [%s]" % (result.mes
   |    sage, result.error_code))
 74|
 75|            assignments = (result.data or {}).get("assignments", [])
 76|            by_id = {task.task_id: task for task in tasks}
 77|            placements: List[Tuple[Task, Optional[Server]]] = []
 78|            placed = set()
 79|
 80|            for item in assignments:
 81|                task = by_id.get(str(item.get("task_id")))
 82|                if task is None or task.task_id in placed:
 83|                    continue
 84|                index = int(item.get("server_id", -1))
 85|                server = self.servers[index] if 0 <= index < len(self.se
   |    rvers) else None
 86|                if server is not None and not server.is_overloaded() and
   |     not server.is_overheated():
 87|                    if server.assign_task(task):
 88|                        placements.append((task, server))
 89|                        placed.add(task.task_id)
 90|                        continue
 91|
 92|                placements.append((task, None))
 93|                placed.add(task.task_id)
 94|
 95|            pending = [task for task, server in placements if server is 
   |    None]
 96|            if pending:
 97|                placements = [(task, server) for task, server in placeme
   |    nts if server is not None]
 98|                for task in pending:
 99|                    candidates = [
100|                        server for server in self.servers
101|                        if not server.is_overloaded() and not server.is_
   |    overheated()
102|                    ]
103|                    target = min(candidates, key=lambda server: server.c
   |    urrent_load, default=None)
104|                    if target is not None and target.assign_task(task):
105|                        placements.append((task, target))
106|                    else:
107|                        placements.append((task, None))
108|
109|            return placements
110|        except Exception as exc:
111|            logger.warning("插件 %s 调度失败，回退贪心: %s", self.plugin_
   |    name, exc)
112|            return self._fallback(tasks)

16. src/algorithms/round_robin.py
================================================================================
  1|from typing import List, Tuple, Optional
  2|from src.models import Task, Server
  3|from src.algorithms.base import BaseScheduler
  4|
  5|class RoundRobinScheduler(BaseScheduler):
  6|    def __init__(self, servers: List[Server]) -> None:
  7|        super().__init__(servers)
  8|        self._next_idx: int = 0
  9|
 10|    def schedule_tasks(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Tuple[Task, Opti
   |    onal[Server]]]:
 11|        results = []
 12|        for task in tasks:
 13|            attempts = 0
 14|            while attempts < len(self.servers):
 15|                server = self.servers[self._next_idx % len(self.servers)
   |    ]
 16|                self._next_idx += 1
 17|                if not server.is_overloaded() and not server.is_overheat
   |    ed():
 18|                    if server.assign_task(task):
 19|                        results.append((task, server))
 20|                        break
 21|                attempts += 1
 22|            else:
 23|                results.append((task, None))
 24|        return results
 25|
 26|    def get_name(self) -> str:
 27|        return "round_robin"

 46|def _current_weights() -> Dict[str, float]:

 48|    weights: Dict[str, float] = {}

 57|def _config_snapshot() -> Dict[str, Any]:
 58|    config = Config()
 59|    simulation = config.get("simulation", {}) or {}
 60|    cooling = config.get("cooling", {}) or {}
 61|    energy = config.get("energy", {}) or {}
 62|    damo = config.get("damo_ets", {}) or {}
 63|    return {
 64|        "num_server_range": [
 65|            simulation.get("min_servers"),
 66|            simulation.get("max_servers"),
 67|        ],
 68|        "simulation_hour_range": [
 69|            simulation.get("min_simulation_hours"),
 70|            simulation.get("max_simulation_hours"),
 71|        ],
 72|        "sla_slack": simulation.get("sla_slack"),
 73|        "fan_speed_range": cooling.get("fan_speed_range"),
 74|        "fan_target_temp": cooling.get("fan_target_temp"),
 75|        "cooling_efficiency": cooling.get("cooling_efficiency"),
 76|        "idle_power_factor": energy.get("idle_power_factor"),
 77|        "part_load_penalty": energy.get("part_load_penalty"),
 78|        "temp_reference": energy.get("temp_reference"),
 79|        "damo_ets_weights": {key: damo.get(key) for key in WEIGHT_KEYS},
 80|        "server_types": config.get("server_types", []),

 84|def _suggest_run(text: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any] |
   |     None:
 85|    triggers = ("如果", "改成", "调到", "增加到", "减少到", "降到", "提
   |    高到", "换成", "试算", "跑一下", "试试", "台服务器")
 86|    if not any(word in text for word in triggers):
 87|        return None
 88|    snapshot = context.get("data_snapshot") or {}
 89|    defaults = {
 90|        "num_servers": snapshot.get("num_servers"),
 91|        "simulation_hours": snapshot.get("simulation_hours"),
 92|        "algorithms": snapshot.get("algorithms") or [],
 93|        "run_mode": "standard",
 94|        "site_id": snapshot.get("site"),
 95|        "school_name": snapshot.get("school_name"),
 96|    }
 97|    parsed = rule_parse(text, defaults=defaults)
 98|    params = dict(parsed.get("params") or {})
 99|
100|    try:
101|        from src.data.catalog import Catalog
102|        catalog = Catalog()
103|        if catalog.available():
104|            matches = [
105|                school for school in catalog.schools(limit=5000)
106|                if school.get("school_name") and school["school_name"] i
   |    n text
107|            ]
108|            if matches:
109|                school = max(matches, key=lambda item: len(item["school_
   |    name"]))
110|                params["school_name"] = school["school_name"]
111|                params["site_id"] = school.get("city") or params.get("si
   |    te_id")
112|    except Exception:
113|        pass
114|
115|    changed = {}
116|    for key in ("num_servers", "simulation_hours", "algorithms", "run_mo
   |    de", "site_id", "school_name"):
117|        if params.get(key) != defaults.get(key):
118|            changed[key] = params.get(key)
119|    if not changed:
120|        return None
121|    if not params.get("algorithms"):
122|        params["algorithms"] = list(defaults.get("algorithms") or [])
123|    params["visualization"] = True
124|    params["save_results"] = True
125|    if snapshot.get("site") and not params.get("site_id"):
126|        params["site_id"] = snapshot.get("site")
127|    label_parts = []
128|    if changed.get("school_name"):
129|        label_parts.append(str(changed["school_name"]))
130|    elif changed.get("site_id"):
131|        label_parts.append(str(changed["site_id"]))
132|    if "num_servers" in changed:
133|        label_parts.append("%s 台" % changed["num_servers"])
134|    if "simulation_hours" in changed:
135|        label_parts.append("%s 小时" % changed["simulation_hours"])
136|    if "algorithms" in changed:
137|        label_parts.append("%d 个算法" % len(changed["algorithms"]))
138|    label = "按 " + " / ".join(label_parts) + " 试算" if label_parts els
   |    e "按建议配置试算"
139|    current_algorithms = set(defaults.get("algorithms") or [])
140|    suggested_algorithms = set(params.get("algorithms") or [])
141|    already_matches_latest = (
142|        params.get("num_servers") == defaults.get("num_servers")
143|        and params.get("simulation_hours") == defaults.get("simulation_h
   |    ours")
144|        and params.get("site_id") == defaults.get("site_id")
145|        and (params.get("school_name") or None) == (defaults.get("school
   |    _name") or None)
146|        and suggested_algorithms == current_algorithms
147|    )
148|    reason = (
149|        "当前最新运行已经是这个配置，可以直接分析现有结果。"
150|        if already_matches_latest
151|        else "当前最新运行不是这个配置，需要按建议参数重新运行才能得到实
   |    测结果。"
152|    )
153|    return {
154|        "label": label,
155|        "params": params,
156|        "changed": changed,
157|        "notes": parsed.get("notes", []),
158|        "already_matches_latest": already_matches_latest,
159|        "reason": reason,
160|    }
