163|ALGORITHM_WORDS = {
164|    "greedy": ("贪心", "greedy"),
165|    "genetic": ("遗传", "genetic"),
166|    "round_robin": ("轮询", "round", "robin"),
167|    "damo_ets": ("damo", "多目标能效"),
168|    "nsga2": ("nsga", "nsga-ii", "nsga2"),
169|    "moead": ("moea", "moead", "moea-d"),

173|def _suggest_optimization(text: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[st
   |    r, Any] | None:
174|    normalized = str(text or "").strip()
175|    if not normalized:
176|        return None
177|    lowered = normalized.lower()
178|    range_match = re.search(r"(\d+)\s*(?:到|至|-|—|~)\s*(\d+)\s*台", nor
   |    malized)
179|    service_match = re.search(r"服务率[^0-9]{0,8}(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*%", n
   |    ormalized)
180|    temp_match = re.search(r"(?:温度|峰值温度|最高温度)[^0-9]{0,8}(?:不
   |    超过|不高于|低于|小于|≤|<=)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)", normalized)
181|    explicit = any(word in normalized for word in (
182|        "全局寻优", "方案寻优", "自动寻优", "配置寻优", "最优方案", "最
   |    佳方案",
183|        "最优配置", "方案最优", "最省电的方案", "最划算的方案", "性价比
   |    最高的方案",
184|    ))
185|    if not explicit and not (range_match and (service_match or temp_matc
   |    h)):
186|        return None
187|
188|    snapshot = context.get("data_snapshot") or {}
189|    default_servers = int(snapshot.get("num_servers") or 48)
190|    default_hours = int(snapshot.get("simulation_hours") or 24)
191|    server_min = default_servers
192|    server_max = max(default_servers, 80)
193|    if range_match:
194|        server_min = int(range_match.group(1))
195|        server_max = int(range_match.group(2))
196|    else:
197|        single = re.search(r"(?<!\d)(\d+)\s*台", normalized)
198|        if single:
199|            server_min = server_max = int(single.group(1))
200|    if server_min > server_max:
201|        server_min, server_max = server_max, server_min
202|
203|    hours_match = re.search(r"(\d+)\s*(?:个)?小时", normalized)
204|    hours = int(hours_match.group(1)) if hours_match else default_hours
205|    hours = max(1, min(72, hours))
206|
207|    floor = 0.98
208|    if service_match:
209|        value = float(service_match.group(1))
210|        floor = value / 100.0 if value > 1.0 else value
211|        floor = max(0.8, min(1.0, floor))
212|
213|    max_temp = None
214|    if temp_match:
215|        max_temp = max(20.0, min(100.0, float(temp_match.group(1))))
216|
217|    if "pue" in lowered:
218|        target = "pue"
219|    elif any(word in normalized for word in ("温度最低", "最凉", "散热最
   |    好", "温度优先")):
220|        target = "temp"
221|    elif any(word in normalized for word in ("最省电", "能耗最低", "能耗
   |    最小", "用电最低", "能耗优先")):
222|        target = "energy"
223|    else:
224|        target = "balanced"
225|
226|    algorithms = []
227|    for key, words in ALGORITHM_WORDS.items():
228|        if any(word in lowered for word in words):
229|            algorithms.append(key)
230|    if any(word in normalized for word in ("全部算法", "六种算法", "六个
   |    算法", "全算法")):
231|        algorithms = ["greedy", "genetic", "round_robin", "damo_ets", "n
   |    sga2", "moead"]
232|    if not algorithms:
233|        algorithms = ["greedy", "genetic", "round_robin", "damo_ets"]
234|
235|    site_id = snapshot.get("site") or "北京市"
236|    school_name = snapshot.get("school_name") or ""
237|    try:
238|        from src.data.catalog import Catalog
239|        catalog = Catalog()
240|        if catalog.available():
241|            matches = [
242|                school for school in catalog.schools(limit=5000)
243|                if school.get("school_name") and school["school_name"] i
   |    n normalized
244|            ]
245|            if matches:
246|                school = max(matches, key=lambda item: len(item["school_
   |    name"]))
247|                school_name = school["school_name"]
248|                site_id = school.get("city") or site_id
249|    except Exception:
250|        pass
251|
252|    params = {
253|        "server_min": server_min,
254|        "server_max": server_max,
255|        "simulation_hours": hours,
256|        "algorithms": algorithms,
257|        "target": target,
258|        "service_rate_floor": floor,
259|        "max_temp": max_temp,
260|        "site_id": site_id,
261|        "school_name": school_name,
262|        "include_weights": "不调权重" not in normalized and "不用权重" n
   |    ot in normalized,
263|    }
264|    target_text = {
265|        "pue": "PUE 最低",
266|        "energy": "单位能耗最低",
267|        "temp": "峰值温度最低",
268|        "balanced": "综合均衡",
269|    }[target]
270|    label = "在 %d–%d 台、%d 小时内寻找%s方案" % (server_min, server_max,
   |     hours, target_text)
271|    return {"params": params, "label": label}

279|        params.get("simulation_hours", 0),

282|        ("，峰值温度不超过 %.1f℃" % params["max_temp"]) if params.get("ma
   |    x_temp") is not None else "",
283|    )

285|CHAT_SYSTEM = """你是"校园数据中心智能调度系统"的 AI 助手（名字：小助）。
286|规则：
287|1. 只依据下面提供的"当前数据"回答，不得编造数字；数据里没有的项直接说"当前
   |    数据没有这一项"。
288|2. 用中文自然语言回答，**不要出现内部字段名**（如 max_temp、avg_pue、energy_
   |    per_served_load、service_rate），
289|   要说"峰值温度""平均 PUE""单位负载能耗""任务服务率"。
290|3. 先给一句结论，再列数据；列表最多 5 条，避免大段复述。
291|4. 当用户问"数据从哪来"时，依据当前数据里的 provenance 字段回答发布方与数
   |    据集名称
292|   （例如 ERA5 气温、阿里云集群轨迹、生态环境部碳因子），不要笼统说"目录数据"
   |    。
293|5. 算法名称一律用中文或规范写法：贪心算法、遗传算法、轮询算法、DAMO-ETS、NSGA
   |    -II、MOEA-D；
294|   不要写 greedy、genetic、round_robin、damo_ets 这类代码标识符。
295|6. 温度保留 1 位小数，PUE 保留 3 位小数，百分比保留 2 位小数。
296|7. 用户问怎么操作时，依据当前数据里的 ui_hints 告诉他点页面哪里；不要假装
   |    已经执行。
297|8. 严格区分两个指标：dropped_tasks 是"未被分配到服务器"的任务，deadline_vi
   |    olations 是"完成超时"的任务，
298|   不要把"未分配"说成"超时违约"，也不要把两者相加。
299|9. 当前数据里的 insights 是从最近一次仿真结果实时计算的。用户问"什么时候
   |    性价比最高""哪个时段最好"时，
300|   优先使用 safe_best_hours（已排除峰值温度达到 70℃ 或出现过热服务器的时
   |    段），给出具体小时、算法、
301|   单位服务负载能耗、温度和 PUE；如果 overall_best_hours 的第一名温度过高，
   |    要单独提醒，不要直接推荐。
302|   insights 里的 hour 是从 0 开始的时间索引，回答时换算成"第 hour+1 小时"
   |    。
303|10. 用户问"温度低于多少度怎么配置"时，使用 temperature_thresholds 或 temp
   |    erature_bands，给出该温区的小时数、
304|    最优算法、服务率、风扇转速和 PUE；同时说明这是当前配置下的观测结果，不是
   |    改变硬件后的反事实预测。
305|11. 用户问"数据更新了吗""这次运行是什么时候"时，使用 snapshot 里的 genera
   |    ted_at、site、num_servers、
306|    simulation_hours 和 algorithms 回答，不要沿用历史对话里的旧数字。
307|12. 用户的问题不限于预设示例。只要与当前仿真数据、调度、能耗、温度、PUE、服务
   |    率、配置调整有关，
308|    都要基于当前数据回答；不要要求用户只能问某几类问题。
309|13. 遇到当前数据没有实际运行过的配置调整（服务器数量、风扇曲线、冷却策略、算
   |    法权重等），按三层回答：
310|    (1) 当前数据观察到的关系和数值；(2) 调整后的方向、可能收益和风险；(3) 
   |    需要用哪些参数重新仿真确认。
311|    不得把推测写成实测结果；没有数据时直接说"当前数据没有这一项"。
312|    只能建议用户操作 context.capabilities.ui_limits 里列出的网页表单参数
   |    ；风扇曲线、冷却效率、权重不在表单里，
313|    应说明需要修改配置文件（config 目录下的 yaml 文件）后重新运行，不要说"
   |    在页面上直接调"。
314|14. context.config 给出了当前物理模型和参数边界；context.insights.relatio
   |    nships 只是相关性，不是因果结论。
315|15. 如果 context.suggested_run 存在，先看 already_matches_latest：
316|    - true：当前最新运行已经是这个配置，直接基于现有数据分析，不要再说需要
   |    重跑；
317|    - false：当前数据不是这个配置，明确说明差异（尤其是学校、台数、时长、算法）
   |    ，并告诉用户可以点消息下方的
318|      "按此配置试算"按钮验证。
319|16. 如果 context.executed_run.status 是 success，说明本轮已经实际运行完成
   |    ，必须直接使用最新 snapshot/insights
320|    回答，并提到"运行编号"；不要再说"需要重新运行"。
321|17. 如果 context.executed_run.status 是 running，说明任务已经启动但还没完
   |    成，不要给出未完成的数值，
322|    只说明进度和稍后查看。
323|18. 如果 context.executed_run.status 是 failed，说明失败原因，不要编造结果
   |    。
324|19. 如果 context.auto_run_budget.allowed 是 false，说明为什么没有自动执行
   |    ，并提示用户点"按此配置试算"
325|    或把参数调整到自动试算预算内。
326|20. 用户问"上次/最近几次/对比/变化"时，使用 context.recent_runs 和 contex
   |    t.latest_comparison；
327|    latest_comparison.left 是较早一次，right 是较新一次。只引用真实记录，不
   |    要编造历史。
328|21. 回答中使用"网页表单"，不要写"Web 表单"；配置文件统一写成"配置文件"或"
   |    配置文件（config 目录下的 yaml 文件）"。
329|22. 用户提到学校时，按该学校所在城市运行，并在回答中写出学校名称；只使用本
   |    站数据目录中的学校、城市、气温和仿真结果。
330|23. 你是本站专用助手，只操控本站的仿真、数据和运行历史，不访问互联网，不执行
   |    任意代码，不回答与本站无关的通用任务。
331|24. 只有在 snapshot.school_name 与用户提到的学校一致时，才能说该学校已经
   |    跑过；否则必须说明当前运行没有记录
332|    这个学校，建议按该校所在城市重新试算。
333|25. 面向用户时不要出现 run_id、runId 这类字段名，统一写"运行编号"；编号本身
   |    保持原样，不要翻译或改写。
334|当前数据：

338|def _data_provenance() -> list:
339|    try:
340|        from src.data.catalog import Catalog
341|        catalog = Catalog()
342|        if not catalog.available():
343|            return []
344|        return [
345|            {
346|                "publisher": row.get("publisher"),
347|                "name": row.get("name"),
348|                "url": row.get("url"),
349|            }
350|            for row in catalog.provenance()
351|        ]
352|    except Exception as exc:
353|        logger.warning("读取数据出处失败: %s" % exc)
354|        return []

357|def _auto_run_budget(params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
358|    manager = get_run_manager()
359|    active = manager.active_run()
360|    if active:
361|        return {
362|            "allowed": False,
363|            "max_servers": 100,
364|            "max_hours": 48,
365|            "reason": "当前已有运行任务（运行编号 %s）正在进行，请等它完成
   |    后再试。" % active.get("run_id"),
366|        }
367|    num_servers = int(params.get("num_servers") or 0)
368|    hours = int(params.get("simulation_hours") or 0)
369|    if num_servers > 100 or hours > 48:
370|        return {
371|            "allowed": False,
372|            "max_servers": 100,
373|            "max_hours": 48,
374|            "reason": "自动试算预算为最多 100 台、48 小时，当前请求是 %d 
   |    台、%d 小时。" % (
375|                num_servers, hours
376|            ),
377|        }
378|    return {
379|        "allowed": True,
380|        "max_servers": 100,
381|        "max_hours": 48,
382|        "reason": "",
383|    }

386|def _run_status_text(executed: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any]) 
   |    -> str:
387|    params = executed.get("params") or {}
388|    label = executed.get("label") or "建议配置"
389|    run_id = executed.get("run_id") or "unknown"
390|    status = executed.get("status")
391|    if status == "success":
392|        return "已按 %s 完成运行（运行编号：%s），页面数据已经同步。" % (labe
   |    l, run_id)
393|    if status == "failed":
394|        return "我尝试按 %s 运行（运行编号：%s），但失败了：%s。可以调整参数后
   |    重试。" % (
395|            label, run_id, executed.get("error") or "未知错误"
396|        )
397|    return "已按 %s 启动运行（运行编号：%s），当前仍在执行。运行完成后页面会
   |    自动刷新，你可以稍后再问我结果。" % (
398|        label, run_id
399|    )

402|def _compact_run(record: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
403|    return {
404|        "run_id": record.get("run_id"),
405|        "origin": record.get("origin"),
406|        "status": record.get("status"),
407|        "created_at": record.get("created_at"),
408|        "finished_at": record.get("finished_at"),
409|        "params": record.get("params") or {},
410|        "summary": record.get("summary") or {},
411|    }

414|def _chat_context() -> Dict[str, Any]:
415|    df = load_simulation_data()
416|    stats = _current_stats(df)
417|    meta = load_run_metadata() or {}
418|    insights = build_insights(df, meta)
419|    recent_runs = [
420|        _compact_run(item) for item in run_store.list_runs(10)
421|        if item.get("status") == "success"
422|    ][:5]
423|    latest_comparison = None
424|    if len(recent_runs) >= 2:
425|        raw_comparison = run_store.compare_runs(
426|            recent_runs[1]["run_id"], recent_runs[0]["run_id"]
427|        )
428|        if raw_comparison:
429|            latest_comparison = {
430|                "left": _compact_run(raw_comparison["left"]),
431|                "right": _compact_run(raw_comparison["right"]),
432|                "metrics": raw_comparison["metrics"],
433|                "rule": raw_comparison["rule"],

472|@ai_bp.route("/api/v1/ai/chat", methods=["POST"])
473|def ai_chat():
474|    data = request.json or {}
475|    raw_messages = data.get("messages") or []
476|    if not isinstance(raw_messages, list) or not raw_messages:
477|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "messages 不能为空"
   |    }), 400
478|
479|    messages = []
480|    for item in raw_messages[-10:]:
481|        if not isinstance(item, dict):
482|            continue
483|        role = item.get("role")
484|        content = str(item.get("content") or "").strip()
485|        if role in ("user", "assistant") and content:
486|            messages.append({"role": role, "content": content[:2000]})
487|    if not messages:
488|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "没有有效的对话内容
   |    "}), 400
489|
490|    context = _chat_context()
491|    latest_user = next((item["content"] for item in reversed(messages) i
   |    f item["role"] == "user"), "")
492|    optimization_job = None
493|    optimization_suggestion = _suggest_optimization(latest_user, context
   |    )
494|    if optimization_suggestion:
495|        try:
496|            optimizer = get_plan_optimizer()
497|            optimization_job = optimizer.active() or optimizer.start(opt
   |    imization_suggestion["params"])
498|            context["optimization"] = {
499|                "job_id": optimization_job.get("job_id"),
500|                "status": optimization_job.get("status"),
501|                "label": optimization_suggestion.get("label"),
502|                "params": optimization_job.get("params"),
503|            }
504|        except Exception as exc:
505|            logger.warning("启动全局寻优失败: %s" % exc)
506|            context["optimization_error"] = str(exc)
507|    suggested = None if optimization_job else _suggest_run(latest_user, 
   |    context)
508|    context["suggested_run"] = suggested
509|
510|    auto_run = bool(data.get("auto_run"))
511|    executed = None
512|    if auto_run and suggested:
513|        budget = _auto_run_budget(suggested["params"])
514|        context["auto_run_budget"] = budget
515|        if budget.get("allowed"):
516|            manager = get_run_manager()
517|            run_id = manager.submit(suggested["params"], origin="ai")
518|            record = manager.wait(run_id, timeout=120) or {}
519|            executed = {
520|                "run_id": run_id,
521|                "status": record.get("status") or "running",
522|                "params": suggested["params"],
523|                "label": suggested["label"],
524|                "summary": record.get("summary") or {},
525|                "error": record.get("error") or "",
526|            }
527|            if executed["status"] == "success":
528|                context = _chat_context()
529|                context["executed_run"] = executed
530|                context["suggested_run"] = None
531|            else:
532|                context["executed_run"] = executed
533|                context["suggested_run"] = suggested
534|
535|    service = get_ai_service()
536|    service.reload_key()
537|    system = CHAT_SYSTEM + json.dumps(context, ensure_ascii=False, defau
   |    lt=str)
538|
539|    if optimization_job:
540|        return jsonify({
541|            "success": True,
542|            "reply": _optimization_reply(optimization_job),
543|            "source": "optimizer",
544|            "model": get_ai_service().model,
545|            "key_source": get_ai_service().key_source,
546|            "snapshot": context.get("insights", {}).get("snapshot", {}),
547|            "suggested_run": None,
548|            "run": None,
549|            "auto_run": auto_run,
550|            "budget": None,
551|            "optimization": optimization_job,
552|        })
553|
554|    source = "deepseek"
555|    try:
556|        reply = humanize_algorithms(service.chat_messages(system, messag
   |    es))
557|    except Exception as exc:
558|        logger.warning("AI 对话回退本地规则: %s" % exc)
559|        reply = rule_chat(messages[-1]["content"], context)
560|        if executed:
561|            if executed["status"] == "success":
562|                reply = _run_status_text(executed, context) + "\n" + rep
   |    ly
563|            else:
564|                reply = _run_status_text(executed, context)
565|        source = "rule"
566|
567|    return jsonify({
568|        "success": True,
569|        "reply": reply,
570|        "source": source,
571|        "model": service.model,
572|        "key_source": service.key_source,
573|        "snapshot": context.get("insights", {}).get("snapshot", {}),
574|        "suggested_run": context.get("suggested_run"),
575|        "run": executed,
576|        "auto_run": auto_run,
577|        "budget": context.get("auto_run_budget"),
578|        "optimization": optimization_job,
579|        "optimization_error": context.get("optimization_error"),
580|    })

635|def _env_config_path() -> str:
636|    root = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), ".."
   |    , "..", "config")
637|    env = os.environ.get("DCS_ENV", "development")
638|    return os.path.abspath(os.path.join(root, "%s.yaml" % env))
639|
640|
641|def _apply_weights(weights: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
642|    path = _env_config_path()
643|    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
644|        text = handle.read()
645|    previous = dict(Config().get("damo_ets", {}) or {})
646|
647|    lines = ["  %s: %s" % (key, weights[key]) for key in WEIGHT_KEYS if 
   |    key in weights]
648|    if len(lines) != len(WEIGHT_KEYS):
649|        raise ValueError("权重参数不完整")
650|
651|    if "\ndamo_ets:" in text or text.startswith("damo_ets:"):
652|        parts = text.split("damo_ets:", 1)
653|        head = parts[0].rstrip("\n")
654|        rest = parts[1].split("\n")
655|        kept = []
656|        for line in rest:
657|            stripped = line.strip()
658|            if not stripped:
659|                continue
660|            if line.startswith(("  ", "\t")) and any(stripped.startswith
   |    (key + ":") for key in WEIGHT_KEYS):
661|                continue
662|            if not line.startswith(("  ", "\t")):
663|                kept.append("__BREAK__")
664|                kept.append(line)
665|                continue
666|            kept.append(line)
667|        body = []
668|        for line in kept:
669|            if line == "__BREAK__":
670|                body.extend(lines)
671|                break
672|            body.append(line)
673|        else:
674|            body.extend(lines)
675|        new_text = head + "\ndamo_ets:\n" + "\n".join(body)
676|        tail_lines = []
677|        if "__BREAK__" in kept:
678|            index = kept.index("__BREAK__")
679|            tail_lines = kept[index + 1:]
680|            new_text = head + "\ndamo_ets:\n" + "\n".join(body) + "\n" +
   |     "\n".join(tail_lines)
681|    else:
682|        new_text = text.rstrip("\n") + "\n\ndamo_ets:\n" + "\n".join(lin
   |    es) + "\n"
683|
684|    parsed = yaml.safe_load(new_text)
685|    if not isinstance(parsed, dict) or "damo_ets" not in parsed:
686|        raise ValueError("写入后的配置无法解析")
687|    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
688|        handle.write(new_text)
689|    Config._instance = None
690|    return {"path": path, "previous": previous, "applied": weights}
691|

694|def ai_tune():
695|    data = request.json or {}

   |    val=1000, default=100, name="服务器数量")

   |    x_val=720, default=24, name="仿真时长")
699|    except Exception as exc:
700|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "参数不合法: %s" % 
   |    exc}), 400

707|        return jsonify({"success": True, "result": sweep})
708|    except Exception as exc:
709|        logger.error("权重寻优失败: %s" % exc)

   |    c}), 500

713|@ai_bp.route("/api/v1/ai/apply-tuning", methods=["POST"])
714|def ai_apply_tuning():
715|    data = request.json or {}
716|    weights = data.get("weights") or {}
717|    cleaned: Dict[str, float] = {}
718|    try:
719|        for key in WEIGHT_KEYS:
720|            cleaned[key] = round(float(weights.get(key)), 4)
721|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
722|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "权重取值不合法"}),
   |     400
723|    total = sum(cleaned.values())
724|    if total <= 0 or abs(total - 1.0) > 0.02:
725|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "权重之和应接近 1.0
   |    ，当前为 %.3f" % total}), 400
726|    try:
727|        info = _apply_weights(cleaned)
728|        return jsonify({"success": True, "result": info})
729|    except Exception as exc:
730|        logger.error("写入权重失败: %s" % exc)
731|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "写入配置失败: %s" 
   |    % exc}), 500

770|def _prediction_series(records):
771|    fields = (
772|        "pue", "total_energy", "peak_temperature", "avg_temperature",
773|        "total_load", "served_load", "offered_load", "assigned", "droppe
   |    d",
774|        "ambient_temperature", "co2_kg",
775|    )
776|    grouped: Dict[str, list] = {}
777|    for row in records or []:
778|        algo = str(row.get("algorithm") or "unknown")
779|        item = {"time": int(row.get("time", 0) or 0)}
780|        for field in fields:
781|            value = row.get(field)
782|            try:
783|                item[field] = float(value)
784|            except (TypeError, ValueError):
785|                item[field] = None
786|        grouped.setdefault(algo, []).append(item)
787|    for series in grouped.values():
788|        series.sort(key=lambda item: item["time"])
789|    return grouped
790|
791|
792|def _prediction_explanation(stats, series, best):
793|    if best and best in stats:
794|        item = stats[best]
795|        avg_pue = float(item.get("avg_pue") or 0)
796|        max_temp = float(item.get("max_temp") or 0)
797|        service_rate = float(item.get("service_rate") or 0) * 100
798|        text = (
799|            "%s 的综合表现最好：平均 PUE %.2f，峰值温度 %.1f°C，服务率 %.1f
   |    %%。"
800|            % (best, avg_pue, max_temp, service_rate)
801|        )
802|        seq = series.get(best) or []
803|        if seq:
804|            peak = max(seq, key=lambda row: row.get("peak_temperature") 
   |    or 0)
805|            text += " 温度最高出现在第 %d 小时，建议在高温时段前提高制冷
   |    冗余，或把非紧急任务错峰安排。" % peak["time"]
806|        if service_rate >= 98:
807|            text += " 当前配置可以满足服务率门槛。"
808|        else:
809|            text += " 当前配置未达到 98% 服务率门槛，建议增加服务器或降低
   |    负载后复测。"
810|        return text
811|    return "当前配置下没有算法达到 98% 服务率门槛，建议增加服务器、降低负
   |    载或调整算法权重后重新预测。"
812|

814|@ai_bp.route("/api/v1/ai/predict", methods=["POST"])
815|def ai_predict():
816|    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
817|    try:
818|        horizon = validate_int(
819|            data.get("horizon_hours", data.get("simulation_hours")),
820|            min_val=6, max_val=48, default=24, name="预测时长",
821|        )
822|        params = validate_simulation_params({
823|            "num_servers": data.get("num_servers"),
824|            "simulation_hours": horizon,
825|            "parallel_workers": data.get("parallel_workers", 1),
826|            "run_mode": "standard",
827|            "server_type": data.get("server_type", "mixed"),
828|            "algorithms": data.get("algorithms") or ["greedy", "genetic"
   |    , "round_robin", "damo_ets", "nsga2", "moead"],
829|        })
830|    except Exception as exc:
831|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "参数不合法: %s" % 
   |    exc}), 400
832|
833|    site_id = str(data.get("site_id") or "").strip()
834|    school_name = str(data.get("school_name") or "").strip()
835|    if site_id:
836|        params["site_id"] = site_id
837|    if school_name:
838|        params["school_name"] = school_name
839|
840|    try:
841|        _, result = get_run_manager().run_prediction(params)
842|        if not result.success:
843|            return jsonify({"success": False, "message": result.message 
   |    or "预测失败"}), 500
844|        payload = result.data or {}
845|        stats = payload.get("statistics") or {}
846|        ranking = payload.get("ranking") or {}
847|        best = ranking.get("best")
848|        series = _prediction_series(payload.get("series"))
849|        assumptions = [
850|            "使用所选学校所在城市的 2025 年历史气象作为典型日情景",
851|            "任务流来自 2025 年阿里云 GPU 集群公开轨迹，按 0.3 缩放、1h 粒
   |    度",
852|            "预测时长 %d 小时，服务器 %d 台，服务器类型 %s" % (
853|                params["simulation_hours"], params["num_servers"], param
   |    s["server_type"],
854|            ),
855|            "结果为受控仿真，不是学校机房实时预测",
856|        ]
857|        return jsonify({
858|            "success": True,
859|            "scenario": {
860|                "site_id": params.get("site_id") or "北京市",
861|                "school_name": params.get("school_name") or "",
862|                "horizon_hours": params["simulation_hours"],
863|                "num_servers": params["num_servers"],
864|                "server_type": params["server_type"],
865|                "algorithms": params["algorithms"],
866|                "generated_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:
   |    %S"),
867|            },
868|            "series": series,
869|            "summary": stats,
870|            "ranking": ranking,
871|            "best_algorithm": best,
872|            "explanation": _prediction_explanation(stats, series, best),
873|            "assumptions": assumptions,
874|            "disclaimer": "这是基于历史气象与公开任务流的受控情景预测，不
   |    是学校机房实时预测。",
875|            "data_source": payload.get("data_source") or {},
876|        })
877|    except Exception as exc:
878|        logger.error("AI prediction failed: %s" % exc)
879|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "预测失败: %s" % ex
   |    c}), 500

 26|# Windows MIME 修正
 27|# Windows 注册表 HKLM\SOFTWARE\Classes\.js 常把 .js 注册为 text/plain,
 28|# 而本项目设置了 X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff, 浏览器会拒绝执行
 29|# Content-Type 为 text/plain 的脚本 -> 前端 JS 全部不执行 (指标卡片停在 
   |    --,
 30|# 数据分析图表空白, 插件列表卡在"正在加载")。
 31|# 这里显式注册 Web 资源 MIME, 不依赖操作系统注册表。
 33|mimetypes.add_type('text/javascript', '.js')
 34|mimetypes.add_type('text/css', '.css')
 35|mimetypes.add_type('text/html', '.html')
 36|mimetypes.add_type('application/json', '.json')
 37|mimetypes.add_type('application/manifest+json', '.webmanifest')
 38|mimetypes.add_type('image/svg+xml', '.svg')
 39|mimetypes.add_type('font/woff2', '.woff2')
 40|mimetypes.add_type('font/woff', '.woff')
 41|mimetypes.add_type('font/ttf', '.ttf')
 43|# 路径解析 (#3: 基于 __file__ 的绝对路径, 不依赖 CWD)
 45|_API_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))           # .../sr
   |    c/api/
 46|_SRC_DIR = os.path.dirname(_API_DIR)                            # .../sr
   |    c/
 47|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(_SRC_DIR)                        # .../Ca
   |    mpusDataCenterScheduler/
 48|FRONTEND_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'frontend')           # .../fr
   |    ontend
 49|

 51|def _load_session_secret():
 52|    env_secret = os.environ.get('DCS_SECRET_KEY')
 53|    if env_secret:
 54|        return env_secret
 55|    secret_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, 'data', '.session_secret')
 56|    try:
 57|        if os.path.isfile(secret_path):
 58|            with open(secret_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
 59|                value = handle.read().strip()
 60|                if value:
 61|                    return value
 62|        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(secret_path), exist_ok=True)
 63|        value = secrets.token_hex(32)
 64|        with open(secret_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
 65|            handle.write(value)
 66|        return value
 67|    except OSError:
 68|        return 'campus-datacenter-local-session-secret'
 69|
 70|app = Flask(__name__, static_folder=FRONTEND_DIR, template_folder=FRONTE
   |    ND_DIR)
 71|app.secret_key = _load_session_secret()
 72|app.config.update(
 73|    SESSION_COOKIE_NAME='dcs_session',
 74|    SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY=True,
 75|    SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE='Lax',
 76|    SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=False,
 77|)
 78|CORS(app)

 87|def algorithm_catalog():
 88|    """One source of truth for the algorithm list exposed to clients."""
 89|    catalog = [
 90|        {'name': 'greedy', 'description': '贪心调度算法', 'type': '实时
   |    调度', 'core': True},
 91|        {'name': 'genetic', 'description': '遗传优化算法', 'type': '全局
   |    优化', 'core': True},
 92|        {'name': 'round_robin', 'description': '轮询调度算法', 'type': '
   |    基准算法', 'core': True},
 93|        {'name': 'damo_ets', 'description': 'DAMO-ETS 多目标能效调度（能
   |    耗+温度+时延+异构匹配）',
 94|         'type': '多目标优化', 'core': True},
 95|    ]
 96|    for name, spec in PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS.items():
 97|        catalog.append({
 98|            'name': name,
 99|            'description': '{0} 插件算法'.format(spec.get('plugin')),
100|            'type': '插件算法',
101|            'plugin': spec.get('plugin'),
102|            'core': False,
103|        })
104|    return catalog

300|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins', methods=['GET'])
301|def list_plugins():
302|    try:
303|        plugins = []
304|        for plugin_name, plugin_class in plugin_manager._plugin_classes.
   |    items():
305|            plugin_instance = plugin_manager._plugin_instances.get(plugi
   |    n_name)
306|            try:
307|                manifest = plugin_instance.get_manifest() if plugin_inst
   |    ance else plugin_class().get_manifest()
308|                plugins.append({
309|                    'name': manifest.name,
310|                    'version': manifest.version,
311|                    'description': ",".join(manifest.supported_operation
   |    s),
312|                    'category': manifest.category,
313|                    'loaded': plugin_name in plugin_manager._plugin_inst
   |    ances
314|                })
315|            except Exception as e:
316|                logger.warning(f"Failed to get metadata for plugin {plug
   |    in_name}: {e}")
317|                plugins.append({
318|                    'name': plugin_name,
319|                    'version': 'unknown',
320|                    'description': '',
321|                    'category': 'algorithm',
322|                    'loaded': plugin_name in plugin_manager._plugin_inst
   |    ances
323|                })
324|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'plugins': plugins})
325|    except Exception as e:
326|        logger.error(f"Error listing plugins: {e}")
327|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Failed to list plu
   |    gins'}), 500

329|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins/<plugin_name>/load', methods=['POST'])
330|def load_plugin(plugin_name):
331|    try:
332|        plugin_name = validate_str(plugin_name, max_len=64, pattern=r'^[
   |    a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$',
333|                                   name='plugin_name')
334|        config = request.json or {}
335|        success = plugin_manager.load_plugin(plugin_name, config)
336|        if success:
337|            info = plugin_manager.get_plugin_info(plugin_name)
338|            metadata = info.to_dict() if info else {}
339|            return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Plugin loaded',
   |     'metadata': metadata})
340|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Failed to load plu
   |    gin'}), 500
341|    except ValueError as ve:
342|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
343|    except Exception as e:
344|        logger.error(f"Error loading plugin {plugin_name}: {e}")
345|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Internal server er
   |    ror'}), 500
346|
347|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins/<plugin_name>/unload', methods=['POST'])
348|def unload_plugin(plugin_name):
349|    try:
350|        plugin_name = validate_str(plugin_name, max_len=64, pattern=r'^[
   |    a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$',
351|                                   name='plugin_name')
352|        plugin_manager.unload_plugin(plugin_name)
353|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Plugin unloaded'})
354|    except ValueError as ve:
355|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
356|    except Exception as e:
357|        logger.error(f"Error unloading plugin {plugin_name}: {e}")
358|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Internal server er
   |    ror'}), 500

360|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins/<plugin_name>/execute', methods=['POST'])
361|def execute_plugin(plugin_name):
362|    try:
363|        plugin_name = validate_str(plugin_name, max_len=64, pattern=r'^[
   |    a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$',
364|                                   name='plugin_name')
365|        params = request.json or {}
366|        result = plugin_manager.execute_plugin(plugin_name, **params)
367|        if result is None:
368|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Failed to exec
   |    ute plugin'}), 500
369|        return jsonify(result)
370|    except ValueError as ve:
371|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
372|    except Exception as e:
373|        logger.error(f"Error executing plugin {plugin_name}: {e}")
374|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Internal server er
   |    ror'}), 500

376|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins/<plugin_name>/config', methods=['GET'])
377|def get_plugin_config(plugin_name):
378|    try:
379|        plugin_name = validate_str(plugin_name, max_len=64, pattern=r'^[
   |    a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$',
380|                                   name='plugin_name')
381|        plugin = plugin_manager._plugin_instances.get(plugin_name)
382|        if plugin:
383|            manifest = plugin.get_manifest()
384|            config = {
385|                'name': manifest.name,
386|                'version': manifest.version,
387|                'description': ",".join(manifest.supported_operations)
388|            }
389|            if hasattr(plugin, 'num_servers'):
390|                config['num_servers'] = plugin.num_servers
391|            if hasattr(plugin, 'simulation_hours'):
392|                config['simulation_hours'] = plugin.simulation_hours
393|            return jsonify({'success': True, 'config': config})
394|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Plugin not loaded'
   |    }), 404
395|    except ValueError as ve:
396|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
397|    except Exception as e:
398|        logger.error(f"Error getting plugin config {plugin_name}: {e}")
399|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Internal server er
   |    ror'}), 500
400|
401|@app.route('/api/v1/plugins/<plugin_name>/config', methods=['PUT'])
402|def update_plugin_config(plugin_name):
403|    try:
404|        plugin_name = validate_str(plugin_name, max_len=64, pattern=r'^[
   |    a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$',
405|                                   name='plugin_name')
406|        plugin = plugin_manager._plugin_instances.get(plugin_name)
407|        if not plugin:
408|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Plugin not loa
   |    ded'}), 404
409|
410|        config = request.json or {}
411|        if 'num_servers' in config:
412|            plugin.num_servers = validate_int(config['num_servers'], min
   |    _val=1, max_val=1000,
413|                                              default=plugin.num_servers
   |    , name='num_servers')
414|        if 'simulation_hours' in config:
415|            plugin.simulation_hours = validate_int(config['simulation_ho
   |    urs'], min_val=1, max_val=720,
416|                                                   default=plugin.simula
   |    tion_hours, name='simulation_hours')
417|
418|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Config updated'})
419|    except ValueError as ve:
420|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
421|    except Exception as e:
422|        logger.error(f"Error updating plugin config {plugin_name}: {e}")
423|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Internal server er
   |    ror'}), 500

425|@app.route('/api/v1/simulation', methods=['POST'])
426|def run_simulation():
427|    try:
428|        params = request.json or {}
429|        run_id, result = get_run_manager().run_sync(params, origin="web"
   |    )
430|        payload = result.to_dict()
431|        payload["run_id"] = run_id
432|        return jsonify(payload)
433|    except ValueError as ve:
434|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
435|    except Exception as e:
436|        logger.error(f"Error running simulation: {str(e)}")
437|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'Simulation failed'
   |    }), 500

439|def _current_data_source():
440|    meta = load_run_metadata() or {}
441|    return {
442|        'data_mode': meta.get('data_mode', 'synthetic'),
443|        'ambient_source': meta.get('ambient_source', 'synthetic-sine'),
444|        'scenario_source': meta.get('scenario_source'),
445|        'scenario_digest': meta.get('scenario_digest'),
446|        'sla_slack': meta.get('sla_slack'),
447|        'completion_mode': meta.get('completion_mode'),
448|        'emission_source': meta.get('emission_source'),
449|        'site_id': meta.get('site_id'),
450|        'school_name': meta.get('school_name'),
451|        'site_province': meta.get('site_province'),
452|        'emission_region': meta.get('emission_region'),
453|        'service_rate_floor': meta.get('service_rate_floor', SERVICE_RAT
   |    E_FLOOR),
454|        'ranking_rule': RANKING_RULE,
455|    }

463|@app.route('/api/v1/metrics', methods=['GET'])
464|def get_metrics():
465|    try:
466|        df = load_simulation_data()
467|        if df is None:
468|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'No simulation 
   |    data found'}), 404
469|
470|        result = summarize(df)
471|        ranking = rank_algorithms(result)
472|        ranks = {item['algorithm']: item['rank'] for item in ranking['el
   |    igible']}
473|        eligible = set(ranks)
474|
475|        for algo, metrics in result.items():
476|            metrics['eligible'] = algo in eligible
477|            metrics['rank'] = ranks.get(algo)
478|
479|        return jsonify({
480|            'success': True,
481|            'data': result,
482|            'ranking': ranking,
483|            'data_source': _current_data_source(),
484|            'controlled': _current_controlled(),
485|        })
486|    except Exception as e:
487|        logger.error(f"Error getting metrics: {str(e)}")
488|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500
489|

491|@app.route('/api/v1/cities', methods=['GET'])
492|def list_cities():
493|    try:
494|        from src.data.catalog import Catalog
495|        catalog = Catalog()
496|        if not catalog.available():
497|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '数据目录不可用
   |    '}), 503
498|        data = catalog.cities()
499|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'data': data, 'total': len(data
   |    )})
500|    except Exception as e:
501|        logger.error(f"Error listing cities: {str(e)}")
502|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500

505|@app.route('/api/v1/schools', methods=['GET'])
506|def list_schools():
507|    try:
508|        query = (request.args.get('q') or '').strip()
509|        city = (request.args.get('city') or '').strip() or None
510|        try:
511|            limit = int(request.args.get('limit', 50))
512|        except ValueError:
513|            limit = 50
514|        limit = max(1, min(limit, 200))
515|
516|        from src.data.catalog import Catalog
517|        catalog = Catalog()
518|        if not catalog.available():
519|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '数据目录不可用
   |    '}), 503
520|        return jsonify({
521|            'success': True,
522|            'data': catalog.schools(query or None, city=city, limit=limi
   |    t),
523|            'total': catalog.count_schools(query or None, city=city),
524|        })
525|    except Exception as e:
526|        logger.error(f"Error listing schools: {str(e)}")
527|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500

530|@app.route('/api/v1/data-source', methods=['GET'])
531|def get_data_source():
532|    try:
533|        payload = dict(_current_data_source())
534|        meta = load_run_metadata() or {}
535|        payload['first_hour_offered'] = meta.get('first_hour_offered')
536|        payload['first_hour_served'] = meta.get('first_hour_served')
537|        payload['controlled'] = meta.get('controlled')
538|        payload['generated_at'] = meta.get('generated_at')
539|        payload['catalog'] = None
540|
541|        try:
542|            from src.data.catalog import Catalog
543|            catalog = Catalog()
544|            if catalog.available():
545|                payload['catalog'] = {
546|                    'db_path': catalog.db_path,
547|                    'sources': catalog.provenance(),
548|                    'ambient_hours': len(catalog.ambient_series() or [])
   |    ,
549|                }
550|        except Exception as exc:
551|            logger.warning(f"Catalog unavailable: {exc}")
552|
553|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'data': payload})
554|    except Exception as e:
555|        logger.error(f"Error getting data source: {str(e)}")
556|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500
557|
558|@app.route('/api/v1/data', methods=['GET'])

562|        if df is None:

   |    data found'}), 404

567|            'data': df.to_dict('records')
568|        })
569|    except Exception as e:

571|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500

   |    on-api'})

 20|_CAPTCHA_CHARS = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'
 21|_login_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=12, window_sec=60.0)

 25|def _captcha_font(size=30):
 26|    font_dir = os.path.join(os.environ.get('WINDIR', r'C:\Windows'), 'Fo
   |    nts')
 27|    for name in ('arialbd.ttf', 'arial.ttf', 'segoeuib.ttf', 'segoeui.tt
   |    f', 'calibrib.ttf'):
 28|        path = os.path.join(font_dir, name)
 29|        if os.path.isfile(path):
 30|            try:
 31|                return ImageFont.truetype(path, size)
 32|            except OSError:
 33|                pass
 34|    try:
 35|        return ImageFont.truetype('DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf', size)
 36|    except OSError:
 37|        return ImageFont.load_default()

 39|
 40|def _render_captcha(code):
 41|    width, height = 132, 44
 42|    image = Image.new('RGB', (width, height), (238, 242, 247))
 43|    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
 44|
 45|    for _ in range(5):
 46|        draw.line(
 47|            [
 48|                (random.randint(0, width), random.randint(0, height)),
 49|                (random.randint(0, width), random.randint(0, height)),
 50|            ],
 51|            fill=(random.randint(155, 205), random.randint(165, 215), ra
   |    ndom.randint(175, 225)),
 52|            width=1,
 53|        )
 54|
 55|    for _ in range(90):
 56|        x = random.randint(0, width - 1)
 57|        y = random.randint(0, height - 1)
 58|        color = (random.randint(120, 190), random.randint(130, 195), ran
   |    dom.randint(145, 210))
 59|        draw.point((x, y), fill=color)
 60|
 61|    font = _captcha_font(29)
 62|    colors = [
 63|        (30, 64, 175),
 64|        (22, 104, 220),
 65|        (15, 118, 110),
 66|        (124, 45, 18),
 67|        (91, 33, 182),
 68|    ]
 69|    for index, char in enumerate(code):
 70|        x = 12 + index * 29 + random.randint(-2, 2)
 71|        y = random.randint(1, 8)
 72|        draw.text((x, y), char, font=font, fill=random.choice(colors))
 73|
 74|    image = image.filter(ImageFilter.SMOOTH)
 75|    buffer = io.BytesIO()
 76|    image.save(buffer, format='PNG', optimize=True)
 77|    return base64.b64encode(buffer.getvalue()).decode('ascii')

 80|def _consume_captcha(value):
 81|    expected = session.pop('captcha_code', None)
 82|    expires = session.pop('captcha_expires', 0)
 83|    if not expected or time.time() > float(expires or 0):
 84|        return False
 85|    return (value or '').strip().upper() == expected.upper()

 88|def _public_user(row):
 89|    data = {'id': row['id'], 'username': row['username']}
 90|    for key in ('created_at', 'last_login'):
 91|        try:
 92|            data[key] = row[key]
 93|        except (IndexError, KeyError):
 94|            pass
 95|    return data

 98|@auth_bp.route('/api/v1/auth/captcha', methods=['GET'])
 99|def get_captcha():
100|    code = ''.join(secrets.choice(_CAPTCHA_CHARS) for _ in range(4))
101|    session['captcha_code'] = code
102|    session['captcha_expires'] = time.time() + _CAPTCHA_TTL
103|    image = _render_captcha(code)
104|    return jsonify({
105|        'success': True,
106|        'image': 'data:image/png;base64,' + image,
107|        'expires_in': _CAPTCHA_TTL,
108|    })

111|@auth_bp.route('/api/v1/auth/login', methods=['POST'])
112|def login():
113|    client_ip = request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0'
114|    if not _login_limiter.is_allowed(client_ip):
115|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '尝试过于频繁，请稍
   |    后再试'}), 429
116|
117|    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
118|    username = (data.get('username') or '').strip()
119|    password = data.get('password') or ''
120|    captcha = (data.get('captcha') or '').strip()
121|
122|    if not username or not password:
123|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请输入账号和密码'}
   |    ), 400
124|    if not captcha:
125|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请输入图形验证码'}
   |    ), 400
126|    if not _consume_captcha(captcha):
127|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '验证码错误或已过期
   |    ，请刷新后重试'}), 400
128|
129|    user = get_user_store().verify_user(username, password)
130|    if user is None:
131|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '账号或密码错误'}),
   |     401
132|
133|    session.clear()
134|    session['user_id'] = user['id']
135|    session['username'] = user['username']
136|    session.permanent = False
137|    logger.info('User login: %s', user['username'])
138|    return jsonify({'success': True, 'user': user})
139|

141|@auth_bp.route('/api/v1/auth/register', methods=['POST'])
142|def register():
143|    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
144|    username = (data.get('username') or '').strip()
145|    password = data.get('password') or ''
146|    confirm_password = data.get('confirm_password') or ''
147|
148|    if not _USERNAME_RE.match(username):
149|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '用户名需为 3-32 位
   |    字母、数字、下划线或中文'}), 400
150|    if len(password) < 6 or len(password) > 64:
151|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '密码长度需为 6-64 
   |    位'}), 400
152|    if password != confirm_password:
153|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '两次输入的密码不一
   |    致'}), 400
154|
155|    try:
156|        user = get_user_store().create_user(username, password)
157|    except ValueError as exc:
158|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(exc)}), 409
159|
160|    logger.info('User registered: %s', user['username'])
161|    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': '注册成功，请登录', 'user
   |    ': _public_user(user)}), 201
162|

164|@auth_bp.route('/api/v1/auth/me', methods=['GET'])
165|def current_user():
166|    user_id = session.get('user_id')
167|    if not user_id:
168|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'authenticated': False, 'user':
   |     None})
169|
170|    user = get_user_store().get_by_id(user_id)
171|    if user is None:
172|        session.clear()
173|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'authenticated': False, 'user':
   |     None})
174|
175|    return jsonify({'success': True, 'authenticated': True, 'user': _pub
   |    lic_user(user)})
176|

178|@auth_bp.route('/api/v1/auth/change-password', methods=['POST'])
179|def change_password():
180|    user_id = session.get('user_id')
181|    if not user_id:
182|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请先登录'}), 401
183|
184|    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
185|    current_password = data.get('current_password') or ''
186|    new_password = data.get('new_password') or ''
187|    confirm_password = data.get('confirm_password') or ''
188|
189|    if not current_password:
190|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请输入当前密码'}),
   |     400
191|    if len(new_password) < 6 or len(new_password) > 64:
192|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '新密码长度需为 6-6
   |    4 位'}), 400
193|    if new_password != confirm_password:
194|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '两次输入的新密码不
   |    一致'}), 400
195|
196|    if not get_user_store().change_password(user_id, current_password, n
   |    ew_password):
197|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '当前密码不正确'}),
   |     400
198|
199|    logger.info('Password changed: %s', session.get('username'))
200|    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': '密码已修改'})

21. src/api/download_api.py

  1|"""
  2|应用下载API模块
  3|
  4|提供安全下载服务:
  5|- 下载令牌验证 (防刷/防盗链)
  6|- 文件完整性校验 (SHA256)
  7|- 下载统计上报
  8|- 版本信息查询
  9|- 自动更新检测与通知
 10|- 全平台支持: Android / iOS / Windows / macOS
 11|"""
 12|
 13|import os
 14|import hashlib
 15|import secrets
 16|import time
 17|import re
 18|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
 19|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 20|from datetime import datetime
 21|
 22|from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, send_file, Response, stre
   |    am_with_context
 23|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 24|from src.utils.download_stats import get_download_stats
 25|from src.utils.validation import (
 26|    validate_str, sanitize_filename, security_headers, RateLimiter, hash
   |    _sha256
 27|)
 28|
 29|logger = setup_logger("download_api")
 30|
 31|download_bp = Blueprint('download', __name__)
 32|
 34|# 路径解析 (基于 __file__ 的绝对路径, 不依赖 CWD)
 35|#
 36|#   _API_DIR        = .../PC端安装包/output/CampusDataCenterScheduler/sr
   |    c/api/
 37|#   _SRC_DIR        = .../PC端安装包/output/CampusDataCenterScheduler/sr
   |    c/
 38|#   _PROJECT_ROOT   = .../PC端安装包/output/CampusDataCenterScheduler/
 39|#   _OUTPUT_DIR     = .../PC端安装包/output/   (构建产物目录, 含 zip/exe
   |    )
 40|#   _RELEASES_DIR   = .../PC端安装包/releases/ (将来正式发布目录)
 42|_API_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 43|_SRC_DIR = os.path.dirname(_API_DIR)
 44|_PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(_SRC_DIR)
 45|_OUTPUT_DIR = os.path.dirname(_PROJECT_ROOT)         # .../PC端安装包/ou
   |    tput/
 46|_RELEASES_DIR = os.path.join(_OUTPUT_DIR, '..', 'releases')
 47|_RELEASES_DIR = os.path.abspath(_RELEASES_DIR)
 48|_DOWNLOAD_DIR = os.path.join(_PROJECT_ROOT, 'downloads')
 49|_PORTABLE_BASENAME = 'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_portable.zip'
 50|_PORTABLE_ZIP = os.path.join(_OUTPUT_DIR, _PORTABLE_BASENAME)
 51|
 52|
 53|def _portable_zip_path() -> Optional[str]:
 54|    for folder in _search_dirs():
 55|        candidate = os.path.join(folder, _PORTABLE_BASENAME)
 56|        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
 57|            return candidate
 58|    return None
 59|
 60|
 61|def _search_dirs() -> tuple:
 62|    """安装包可能出现的目录。
 63|
 64|    除构建产物目录外，也兼容两种常见摆放：
 65|      * 仓库内 releases/ 与 downloads/（便于随仓库分发占位包）
 66|      * 上一级的 02_启动器与安装包/（提交包里安装程序所在目录）
 67|    """
 68|    return (
 69|        _RELEASES_DIR,
 70|        os.path.join(_PROJECT_ROOT, 'releases'),
 71|        _DOWNLOAD_DIR,
 72|        _OUTPUT_DIR,
 73|        os.path.join(_OUTPUT_DIR, '02_启动器与安装包'),
 74|        os.path.join(_OUTPUT_DIR, 'downloads'),
 75|    )
 76|
 77|
 78|def _default_app_type(platform: str) -> str:
 79|    return 'desktop' if platform in ('windows', 'windows_arm', 'macos') 
   |    else 'mobile'
 80|
 82|# 配置
 84|TOKEN_EXPIRY_SEC = 600
 85|TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r'^[a-f0-9]{64}$')
 86|_active_tokens: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
 87|_rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=30, window_sec=60.0)
 88|
 90|# 文件解析: platform/filename → 实际文件路径
 92|FILE_BASENAMES = {
 93|    'mobile_android':  'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_android.apk',
 94|    'mobile_ios':      'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_ios.ipa',
 95|    'windows_x64':     'CampusDataCenterScheduler_Setup_v1.0.0.exe',
 96|    'windows_arm64':   'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_portable.zip',
 97|    'macos':           'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_macos.dmg',
 98|}
 99|
100|CONTENT_TYPES = {
101|    '.exe': 'application/x-msdownload',
102|    '.apk': 'application/vnd.android.package-archive',
103|    '.ipa': 'application/octet-stream',
104|    '.dmg': 'application/x-apple-diskimage',
105|    '.zip': 'application/zip',
106|}
107|
108|
109|def _app_key_for(platform: str, filename: str) -> Optional[str]:
110|    """根据 platform + filename 解析出 app_key (mobile_android / windows
   |    _x64 ...)"""
111|    fn = filename.lower()
112|    if platform == 'android' and (fn.endswith('.apk') or fn.endswith('.z
   |    ip')):
113|        return 'mobile_android'
114|    if platform == 'ios' and (fn.endswith('.ipa') or fn.endswith('.app')
   |     or fn.endswith('.zip')):
115|        return 'mobile_ios'
116|    if platform == 'macos' and (fn.endswith('.dmg') or fn.endswith('.zip
   |    ')):
117|        return 'macos'
118|    if platform == 'windows':
119|        if 'arm' in fn:
120|            return 'windows_arm64'
121|        return 'windows_x64'
122|    if platform == 'windows_arm':
123|        return 'windows_arm64'
124|    return None
125|
126|
127|def _resolve_file_path(platform: str, filename: str) -> Optional[str]:
128|    """
129|    将 platform/filename 解析为绝对文件路径.
130|
131|    查找顺序:
132|      1. releases/<basename>      (生产构建产物)
133|      2. output/<basename>        (CI 直接产物)
134|      3. Windows x64 退化到 portable zip (开发期替代)
135|    """
136|    app_key = _app_key_for(platform, filename)
137|    if not app_key:
138|        return None
139|    basename = FILE_BASENAMES.get(app_key)
140|    if not basename:
141|        return None
142|
143|    for d in _search_dirs():
144|        candidate = os.path.join(d, basename)
145|        if os.path.isfile(candidate):
146|            return candidate
147|
148|    # 开发期/安装版退化: Windows x64 没有真 .exe 时, 用 portable zip 替
   |    代
149|    if app_key == 'windows_x64':
150|        portable = _portable_zip_path()
151|        if portable:
152|            return portable
153|
154|    return None
155|
156|
157|APP_KEY_PLATFORM = {
158|    'mobile_android': 'android',
159|    'mobile_ios': 'ios',
160|    'windows_x64': 'windows',
161|    'windows_arm64': 'windows_arm',
162|    'macos': 'macos',
163|}
164|
165|
166|def _file_sha256(path: str) -> str:
167|    digest = hashlib.sha256()
168|    with open(path, 'rb') as handle:
169|        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
170|            digest.update(chunk)
171|    return digest.hexdigest()
172|
173|
174|def _file_size_mb(path: str) -> float:
175|    return round(os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024), 2)
176|
177|
178|def _resolved_app_file(app_key: str) -> Optional[str]:
179|    basename = FILE_BASENAMES.get(app_key)
180|    if not basename:
181|        return None
182|    platform = APP_KEY_PLATFORM.get(app_key, '')
183|    return _resolve_file_path(platform, basename)
184|
185|
186|def _app_metadata(app_key: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
187|    item = dict(APP_VERSIONS.get(app_key, {}))
188|    path = _resolved_app_file(app_key)
189|    if path:
190|        item['sha256'] = _file_sha256(path)
191|        item['size_mb'] = _file_size_mb(path)
192|    else:
193|        # 找不到实际文件时不要回落到写死的旧数值, 直接不返回这两个字段
194|        item.pop('sha256', None)
195|        item.pop('size_mb', None)
196|    return item
197|
198|
200|# Token 管理
202|def _generate_token(platform: str, app_type: str, filename: str,
203|                    file_path: Optional[str]) -> str:
204|    token = secrets.token_hex(32)
205|    _active_tokens[token] = {
206|        'platform': platform,
207|        'app_type': app_type,
208|        'filename': filename,
209|        'file_path': file_path,           # #4: 绑定具体文件路径, 防 tok
   |    en 滥用
210|        'app_key': _app_key_for(platform, filename),
211|        'created_at': time.time(),
212|        'expires_at': time.time() + TOKEN_EXPIRY_SEC,
213|        'used': False,
214|        'ip': None,
215|    }
216|    return token
217|
218|
219|def _cleanup_expired_tokens() -> None:
220|    now = time.time()
221|    expired = [t for t, d in _active_tokens.items() if d['expires_at'] <
   |     now]
222|    for t in expired:
223|        del _active_tokens[t]
224|
225|
227|# 路由: 下载令牌验证
229|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/verify', methods=['POST'])
230|def verify_download():
231|    """
232|    下载安全验证接口
233|
234|    Request JSON:
235|      { platform, app_type, filename }
236|
237|    Response:
238|      { success, token, expires_in, message, file_available, file_size }
239|    """
240|    try:
241|        data = request.json or {}
242|        client_ip = request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0'
243|
244|        if not _rate_limiter.is_allowed(client_ip):
245|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请求过于频繁'}
   |    ), 429
246|
247|        platform = validate_str(
248|            data.get('platform', ''), max_len=32,
249|            pattern=r'^(android|ios|windows|windows_arm|macos)$',
250|            default='', name='platform'
251|        )
252|        app_type_raw = (data.get('app_type') or '').strip()
253|        if app_type_raw:
254|            app_type = validate_str(
255|                app_type_raw, max_len=32,
256|                pattern=r'^(mobile|desktop|windows)$',
257|                default='', name='app_type'
258|            )
259|        else:
260|            # 前端偶尔只传 platform, 这里按平台推断, 不再当作错误
261|            app_type = _default_app_type(platform)
262|        filename = sanitize_filename(data.get('filename', ''), max_len=1
   |    28)
263|
264|        if not platform or not filename:
265|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '参数缺失'}), 4
   |    00
266|
267|        referrer = request.headers.get('Referer', '')
268|        if referrer and 'datacenter' not in referrer.lower():
269|            logger.warning(f"可疑的Referrer: {referrer}")
270|
271|        # #4: 在 verify 时解析出真实文件路径, 绑定到 token
272|        file_path = _resolve_file_path(platform, filename)
273|        file_available = file_path is not None
274|        file_size = os.path.getsize(file_path) if file_available else 0
275|
276|        _cleanup_expired_tokens()
277|        token = _generate_token(platform, app_type, filename, file_path)
278|        _active_tokens[token]['ip'] = client_ip
279|
280|        logger.info(
281|            f"Download token issued: {platform}/{filename} "
282|            f"file_available={file_available} size={file_size}"
283|        )
284|
285|        return jsonify({
286|            'success': True,
287|            'token': token,
288|            'expires_in': TOKEN_EXPIRY_SEC,
289|            'file_available': file_available,
290|            'file_size': file_size,
291|            'message': '验证通过' if file_available else '验证通过, 但安
   |    装包暂未就绪'
292|        })
293|    except ValueError as e:
294|        logger.warning(f"Download verify 参数不合法: {e}")
295|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': f'参数不合法: {e}'}
   |    ), 400
296|    except Exception as e:
297|        logger.error(f"Download verify error: {e}")
298|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '服务器错误'}), 500
299|
300|
302|# 路由: 文件下载 (核心修复 #1)
304|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/file/<token>', methods=['GET'])
305|def download_file(token: str):
306|    """
307|    通过 token 下载文件 (前端 download.js 第 257 行调用)
308|
309|    安全约束:
310|      - token 必须是 64 位 hex
311|      - token 必须存在且未过期
312|      - token 必须绑定到一个真实存在的文件 (#4)
313|      - token 单次使用, 用后失效 (防重放)
314|    """
315|    try:
316|        # 1. 格式校验
317|        if not TOKEN_RE.match(token or ''):
318|            logger.warning(f"非法 token 格式: {token[:16] if token else 
   |    'None'}...")
319|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '非法 token'}),
   |     400
320|
321|        # 2. 存在性 + 过期
322|        _cleanup_expired_tokens()
323|        record = _active_tokens.get(token)
324|        if not record:
325|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'token 无效或已
   |    过期'}), 404
326|
327|        # 3. 重放保护 (无论文件是否存在, 已用过的 token 都不能再用)
328|        if record.get('used'):
329|            logger.warning(f"token 重放: {token[:16]}... (已用过)")
330|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': 'token 已使用'}
   |    ), 410
331|
332|        # 4. 文件存在性 (#4: 路径由 verify 时绑定, 不接受客户端传入路径)
333|        file_path = record.get('file_path')
334|        if not file_path or not os.path.isfile(file_path):
335|            # 文件缺失时, 不消耗 token (允许重试到文件就绪)
336|            logger.warning(f"文件不存在: {file_path} (token={token[:16]}
   |    ...)")
337|            return jsonify({
338|                'success': False,
339|                'message': '安装包暂未就绪, 请稍后再试',
340|                'platform': record.get('platform'),
341|                'filename': record.get('filename'),
342|            }), 404
343|
344|        # 5. 标记已用 (单次有效, 防下载链接外泄后被滥用) - 只在文件存在
   |    时消耗
345|        record['used'] = True
346|
347|        # 6. 推断 Content-Type
348|        ext = os.path.splitext(file_path)[1].lower()
349|        content_type = CONTENT_TYPES.get(ext, 'application/octet-stream'
   |    )
350|        download_name = record['filename'] or os.path.basename(file_path
   |    )
351|
352|        logger.info(
353|            f"文件下载: {download_name} ({os.path.getsize(file_path)} by
   |    tes) "
354|            f"token={token[:16]}..."
355|        )
356|
357|        # 7. 流式发送 (大文件分块, 避免 OOM)
358|        return _send_file_stream(file_path, download_name, content_type)
359|
360|    except Exception as e:
361|        logger.error(f"File download error: {e}")
362|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '服务器错误'}), 500
363|
364|
365|def _send_file_stream(file_path: str, download_name: str, content_type: 
   |    str):
366|    """分块流式发送文件, 支持 Range (大文件断点续传)"""
367|    file_size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
368|    range_header = request.headers.get('Range', None)
369|
370|    # 解析 Range
371|    start = 0
372|    end = file_size - 1
373|    status_code = 200
374|    headers = {
375|        'Content-Type': content_type,
376|        'Content-Disposition': f'attachment; filename="{download_name}"'
   |    ,
377|        'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes',
378|        'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
379|    }
380|
381|    if range_header:
382|        m = re.match(r'bytes=(\d+)-(\d*)', range_header)
383|        if m:
384|            start = int(m.group(1))
385|            end = int(m.group(2)) if m.group(2) else file_size - 1
386|            end = min(end, file_size - 1)
387|            length = end - start + 1
388|            status_code = 206
389|            headers['Content-Range'] = f'bytes {start}-{end}/{file_size}
   |    '
390|            headers['Content-Length'] = str(length)
391|
392|    def generate():
393|        chunk_size = 64 * 1024  # 64KB
394|        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
395|            f.seek(start)
396|            remaining = end - start + 1
397|            while remaining > 0:
398|                chunk = f.read(min(chunk_size, remaining))
399|                if not chunk:
400|                    break
401|                remaining -= len(chunk)
402|                yield chunk
403|
404|    headers['Content-Length'] = headers.get('Content-Length', str(file_s
   |    ize))
405|    return Response(
406|        stream_with_context(generate()),
407|        status=status_code,
408|        headers=headers,
409|    )
410|
411|
413|# 路由: 静态路径下载 (兼容 APP_VERSIONS 里的 download_url)
415|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/file/<platform>/<arch>', methods=['
   |    GET'])
416|def download_file_legacy(platform: str, arch: str):
417|    """
418|    兼容 APP_VERSIONS 中的静态 download_url, 例如:
419|      /api/v1/download/file/windows/x64
420|      /api/v1/download/file/macos/dmg
421|
422|    与 token 路由不同, 此类直接走 IP 限流 + 简单 referrer 校验。
423|    """
424|    try:
425|        client_ip = request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0'
426|        if not _rate_limiter.is_allowed(client_ip):
427|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '请求过于频繁'}
   |    ), 429
428|
429|        # 映射: (platform, arch) → 内部 platform 标识
430|        if platform == 'windows' and arch == 'x64':
431|            real_platform, real_arch = 'windows', 'x64'
432|        elif platform == 'windows' and arch == 'arm64':
433|            real_platform, real_arch = 'windows_arm', 'arm64'
434|        elif platform == 'macos' and arch == 'dmg':
435|            real_platform, real_arch = 'macos', 'dmg'
436|        elif platform == 'android' and arch == 'apk':
437|            real_platform, real_arch = 'android', 'apk'
438|        elif platform == 'ios' and arch in ('ipa', 'app'):
439|            real_platform, real_arch = 'ios', 'ipa'
440|        else:
441|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '不支持的平台/
   |    架构'}), 400
442|
443|        app_key = {
444|            'android': 'mobile_android',
445|            'ios': 'mobile_ios',
446|            'macos': 'macos',
447|            'windows': 'windows_x64',
448|            'windows_arm': 'windows_arm64',
449|        }.get(real_platform)
450|        basename = FILE_BASENAMES.get(app_key) if app_key else None
451|        if not basename:
452|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '无此安装包'}),
   |     404
453|
454|        file_path = None
455|        for d in _search_dirs():
456|            candidate = os.path.join(d, basename)
457|            if os.path.isfile(candidate):
458|                file_path = candidate
459|                break
460|        if not file_path and app_key == 'windows_x64':
461|            file_path = _portable_zip_path()
462|
463|        if not file_path:
464|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '安装包暂未就绪
   |    '}), 404
465|
466|        ext = os.path.splitext(file_path)[1].lower()
467|        content_type = CONTENT_TYPES.get(ext, 'application/octet-stream'
   |    )
468|        return _send_file_stream(file_path, basename, content_type)
469|
470|    except Exception as e:
471|        logger.error(f"Legacy download error: {e}")
472|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '服务器错误'}), 500
473|

566|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/checksum', methods=['POST'])
567|def verify_checksum():
568|    """
569|    SHA256完整性校验接口
570|    
571|    客户端下载完成后将本地SHA256发送至服务端比对。
572|    """
573|    try:
574|        data = request.json or {}
575|        filename = data.get('filename', '')
576|        client_sha256 = data.get('sha256', '')
577|
578|        if not filename or not client_sha256:
579|            return jsonify({'valid': False, 'message': '校验参数缺失'}),
   |     400
580|
581|        key_map = {
582|            'CampusDataCenterScheduler_Setup_v1.0.0.exe': 'windows_x64',
583|            'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_portable.zip': 'windows_ar
   |    m64',
584|            'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_android.apk': 'mobile_andr
   |    oid',
585|            'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_ios.ipa': 'mobile_ios',
586|            'CampusDataCenterScheduler_v1.0.0_macos.dmg': 'macos',
587|        }
588|
589|        app_key = key_map.get(filename, '')
590|        app_info = APP_VERSIONS.get(app_key, None) if app_key else None
591|        
592|        if app_info:
593|            expected = app_info.get('sha256') or ''
594|            if not expected:
595|                path = _resolved_app_file(app_key)
596|                if path:
597|                    expected = _file_sha256(path)
598|            valid = bool(expected) and client_sha256.lower() == expected
   |    .lower()
599|            logger.info(f"SHA256 check for {filename}: {'MATCH' if valid
   |     else 'MISMATCH'}")
600|            return jsonify({'valid': valid, 'message': '校验通过' if val
   |    id else '文件校验失败'})
601|        
602|        return jsonify({'valid': True, 'message': '无校验基准'})
603|
604|    except Exception as e:
605|        logger.error(f"Checksum error: {e}")
606|        return jsonify({'valid': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500

624|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/check-update', methods=['POST'])
625|def check_update():
626|    """
627|    客户端版本更新检测接口
628|    
629|    客户端发送当前版本号和服务端比对，
630|    返回是否需要更新及最新版本信息。
631|    
632|    Request JSON:
633|    {
634|        "platform": "android" | "ios" | "windows" | "windows_arm" | "mac
   |    os",
635|        "current_version": "v3.1.0",
636|        "current_build": "3100"
637|    }
638|    """
639|    try:
640|        data = request.json or {}
641|        platform = validate_str(data.get('platform', ''), max_len=32,
642|                                pattern=r'^(android|ios|windows|windows_
   |    arm|macos)$',
643|                                default='', name='platform')
644|        current_version = validate_str(data.get('current_version', ''), 
   |    max_len=16,
645|                                       pattern=r'^v?\d+\.\d+\.\d+$',
646|                                       default='', name='current_version
   |    ')
647|        current_build = validate_str(data.get('current_build', ''), max_
   |    len=10,
648|                                     pattern=r'^\d+$',
649|                                     default='0', name='current_build')
650|
651|        platform_key_map = {
652|            'android': 'mobile_android',
653|            'ios': 'mobile_ios',
654|            'windows': 'windows_x64',
655|            'windows_arm': 'windows_arm64',
656|            'macos': 'macos'
657|        }
658|
659|        app_key = platform_key_map.get(platform)
660|        if not app_key:
661|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '不支持的平台'}
   |    ), 400
662|
663|        latest_info = APP_VERSIONS.get(app_key, {})
664|        if not latest_info:
665|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '版本信息不可用
   |    '}), 404
666|
667|        latest_build_num = int(latest_info.get('build', '0'))
668|        current_build_num = int(current_build) if current_build else 0
669|        update_available = latest_build_num > current_build_num
670|
671|        return jsonify({
672|            'success': True,
673|            'update_available': update_available,
674|            'current_version': current_version or 'unknown',
675|            'latest_version': latest_info['version'],
676|            'latest_build': latest_info['build'],
677|            'size_mb': latest_info['size_mb'],
678|            'release_date': latest_info['release_date'],
679|            'release_notes': latest_info['release_notes'],
680|            'download_url': latest_info.get('download_url'),
681|            'app_store_url': latest_info.get('app_store_url'),
682|            'min_os': latest_info['min_os'],
683|            'message': '发现新版本！' if update_available else '当前已是
   |    最新版本'
684|        })
685|    except ValueError as ve:
686|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(ve)}), 400
687|    except Exception as e:
688|        logger.error(f"Update check error: {e}")
689|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '服务器错误'}), 500

701|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/stats', methods=['POST'])
702|def record_stats():
703|    """上报下载统计"""
704|    try:
705|        data = request.json or {}
706|        platform = data.get('platform', 'unknown')
707|        app_type = data.get('app_type', 'unknown')
708|        filename = data.get('filename', '')
709|        size_mb = float(data.get('size_mb', 0))
710|        user_agent = data.get('user_agent', '')[:200]
711|        referrer = data.get('referrer', '')[:200]
712|        client_ip = request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0'
713|
714|        stats = get_download_stats()
715|        record = stats.record_download(
716|            platform=platform, app_type=app_type,
717|            filename=filename, size_mb=size_mb,
718|            user_agent=user_agent, ip_address=client_ip
719|        )
720|
721|        summary = stats.get_summary()
722|        return jsonify({
723|            'success': True,
724|            'record': record,
725|            'total': summary['total']
726|        })
727|    except Exception as e:
728|        logger.error(f"Download stats recording error: {e}")
729|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500

731|@download_bp.route('/api/v1/download/stats/reset', methods=['POST'])
732|def reset_stats():
733|    """重置下载统计（管理接口）"""
734|    try:
735|        valid_key = os.environ.get('DCS_ADMIN_KEY')
736|        if not valid_key:
737|            logger.warning("DCS_ADMIN_KEY 未配置, 统计重置接口未启用")
738|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '管理接口未启用
   |    （未配置 DCS_ADMIN_KEY）'}), 403
739|        api_key = request.headers.get('X-API-Key', '')
740|        if not api_key or api_key != valid_key:
741|            logger.warning(f"Unauthorized stats reset attempt from {requ
   |    est.remote_addr}")
742|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '未授权'}), 403
743|
744|        stats = get_download_stats()
745|        stats.reset_stats()
746|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': '统计已重置'})
747|    except Exception as e:
748|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500

================================================================================
22. src/api/grpc_adapter.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|gRPC/WebSocket 适配层
  3|
  4|提供跨语言接口支持:
  5|- gRPC: 高性能RPC（Go/Java/C#/Python客户端）
  6|- WebSocket: 实时数据推送（Web浏览器、实时监控）
  7|
  8|参考项目: gRPC (Google), Socket.IO, Flask-SocketIO
  9|"""
 10|
 11|import json
 12|import time
 13|from typing import Dict, Any, Generator, Optional
 14|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 15|from src.utils.config import Config
 16|
 17|logger = setup_logger("grpc_adapter")
 18|config = Config()
 19|
 20|class GrpcServerAdapter:
 21|    """
 22|    gRPC服务端适配器
 23|    
 24|    使系统可通过gRPC协议被跨语言客户端调用。
 25|    Proto定义: proto/scheduler.proto
 26|    
 27|    参考项目: gRPC, grpcio
 28|    """
 29|
 30|    def __init__(self, host: str = '0.0.0.0', port: int = 50051):
 31|        self.host = host
 32|        self.port = port
 33|        self._server = None
 34|
 35|    def start(self) -> bool:
 36|        try:
 37|            import grpc
 38|            from concurrent import futures
 39|            import proto.scheduler_pb2 as pb2
 40|            import proto.scheduler_pb2_grpc as pb2_grpc
 41|            
 42|            self._server = grpc.server(futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_wo
   |    rkers=10))
 43|            pb2_grpc.add_DataCenterSchedulerServicer_to_server(
 44|                _GrpcServiceImpl(), self._server
 45|            )
 46|            self._server.add_insecure_port(f'{self.host}:{self.port}')
 47|            self._server.start()
 48|            logger.info(f"gRPC server started on {self.host}:{self.port}
   |    ")
 49|            return True
 50|        except ImportError:
 51|            logger.warning("grpcio not installed, gRPC server unavailabl
   |    e")
 52|            return False
 53|        except Exception as e:
 54|            logger.error(f"gRPC server start failed: {e}")
 55|            return False
 56|
 57|    def stop(self) -> None:
 58|        if self._server:
 59|            self._server.stop(0)
 60|            logger.info("gRPC server stopped")
 61|
 62|    def wait_for_termination(self) -> None:
 63|        if self._server:
 64|            self._server.wait_for_termination()
 65|
 66|
 67|class _GrpcServiceImpl:
 68|    """gRPC服务实现（自动生成client后替换为实际实现）"""
 69|
 70|    def RunSimulation(self, request, context):
 71|        from src.plugin import PluginManager
 72|        from src.plugin.simulation_plugin import SimulationPlugin
 73|        
 74|        pm = PluginManager()
 75|        pm.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin)
 76|        
 77|        plugin_config = {
 78|            'num_servers': request.num_servers,
 79|            'simulation_hours': request.simulation_hours,
 80|            'parallel_workers': request.parallel_workers,
 81|            'run_mode': request.run_mode,
 82|            'server_type': request.server_type
 83|        }
 84|        
 85|        pm.load_plugin('SimulationPlugin', plugin_config)
 86|        result = pm.execute_plugin('SimulationPlugin',
 87|            algorithms=list(request.algorithms),
 88|            visualization=request.visualization,
 89|            save_results=request.save_results
 90|        )
 91|        pm.unload_plugin('SimulationPlugin')
 92|        
 93|        return result
 94|
 95|    def RunSimulationStream(self, request, context):
 96|        total_hours = request.simulation_hours
 97|        for hour in range(total_hours):
 98|            result = {
 99|                'time': hour,
100|                'algorithm': request.algorithms[0] if request.algorithms
   |     else 'greedy',
101|                'total_load': 100.0,
102|                'avg_temperature': 25.0 + 10.0 * (hour % 12) / 12,
103|                'total_energy': 500.0,
104|                'pue': 1.75
105|            }
106|            yield result
107|            time.sleep(0.1)
108|
109|    def GetMetrics(self, request, context):
110|        from src.utils import load_simulation_data
111|        df = load_simulation_data()
112|        if df is None:
113|            return {'success': False, 'data': {}}
114|        return {'success': True, 'data': df.to_dict('records')}
115|
116|    def HealthCheck(self, request, context):
117|        return {
118|            'status': 'healthy',
119|            'service': 'datacenter-scheduler',
120|            'timestamp': str(time.time())
121|        }
122|
123|    def ListAlgorithms(self, request, context):
124|        return {
125|            'algorithms': [
126|                {'name': 'greedy', 'description': '贪心调度算法', 'type'
   |    : '实时调度'},
127|                {'name': 'genetic', 'description': '遗传优化算法', 'type
   |    ': '全局优化'},
128|                {'name': 'round_robin', 'description': '轮询调度算法', '
   |    type': '基准算法'}
129|            ]
130|        }
131|
132|
133|class WebSocketAdapter:
134|    """
135|    WebSocket适配器
136|    
137|    提供实时数据推送能力，适用于:
138|    - Web可视化面板实时更新
139|    - 移动端App数据推送
140|    - 第三方监控系统集成
141|    
142|    参考项目: Socket.IO, Flask-SocketIO, WebSockets (RFC 6455)
143|    """
144|
145|    def __init__(self):
146|        self._clients = set()
147|        self._running = False
148|
149|    def register(self, ws_server) -> None:
150|        """注册到WebSocket服务器"""
151|        self._running = True
152|        logger.info("WebSocket adapter registered")
153|
154|    async def broadcast_metric_update(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Non
   |    e:
155|        """广播指标更新到所有客户端"""
156|        pass
157|
158|    async def broadcast_simulation_progress(self, progress: float, messa
   |    ge: str) -> None:
159|        """广播仿真进度"""
160|        pass
161|
162|    async def handle_client_message(self, client_id: str, message: Dict[
   |    str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
163|        """处理客户端消息"""
164|        action = message.get('action', '')
165|        
166|        handlers = {
167|            'run_simulation': self._handle_run_simulation,
168|            'get_metrics': self._handle_get_metrics,
169|            'ping': self._handle_ping
170|        }
171|        
172|        handler = handlers.get(action)
173|        if handler:
174|            return await handler(message)
175|        return {'error': f'Unknown action: {action}'}
176|
177|    async def _handle_run_simulation(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> D
   |    ict[str, Any]:
178|        from src.sdk import DataCenterSimulatorSDK
179|        sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode='local')
180|        result = sdk.run_simulation(**message.get('params', {}))
181|        return result
182|
183|    async def _handle_get_metrics(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict
   |    [str, Any]:
184|        from src.sdk import DataCenterSimulatorSDK
185|        sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode='local')
186|        result = sdk.get_metrics()
187|        return result
188|
189|    async def _handle_ping(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, A
   |    ny]:
190|        return {'action': 'pong', 'timestamp': time.time()}
