  1|import json
  2|import os
  3|from datetime import datetime
  4|
  5|from flask import Blueprint, jsonify

  8|from src.services import run_store
  9|from src.simulation.metrics import rank_algorithms
 10|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger

 12|map_bp = Blueprint('map', __name__)
 13|logger = setup_logger('map_api')
 14|
 15|_API_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 16|_SRC_DIR = os.path.dirname(_API_DIR)
 17|_PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(_SRC_DIR)
 18|_FRONTEND_DIR = os.path.join(_PROJECT_ROOT, 'frontend')
 19|_COORDS_PATH = os.path.join(_FRONTEND_DIR, 'static', 'maps', 'city_coord
   |    s.json')

 21|
 22|def _load_coords():
 23|    with open(_COORDS_PATH, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as handle:
 24|        return json.load(handle)
 25|
 26|
 27|@map_bp.route('/api/v1/map/data', methods=['GET'])
 28|def map_data():
 29|    try:
 30|        catalog = Catalog()
 31|        if not catalog.available():
 32|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '数据目录不可用
   |    '}), 503
 33|
 34|        coords = _load_coords()
 35|        cities = catalog.cities()
 36|        climate = {row['city']: row.get('avg_temp') for row in catalog.c
   |    ity_climate_summary()}
 37|        emission_cache = {}
 38|
 39|        city_payload = []
 40|        province_acc = {}
 41|        total_schools = 0
 42|
 43|        for row in cities:
 44|            city = row.get('city') or ''
 45|            province = row.get('province') or ''
 46|            school_count = int(row.get('school_count') or 0)
 47|            total_schools += school_count
 48|            region = region_for_province(province)
 49|            if region not in emission_cache:
 50|                emission_cache[region] = catalog.emission_factor(region)
 51|            carbon = emission_cache.get(region)
 52|            avg_temp = climate.get(city)
 53|
 54|            acc = province_acc.setdefault(province, {
 55|                'name': province,
 56|                'region': region,
 57|                'school_count': 0,
 58|                'temp_sum': 0.0,
 59|                'temp_weight': 0,
 60|                'carbon_factor': carbon,
 61|            })
 62|            acc['school_count'] += school_count
 63|            if avg_temp is not None:
 64|                acc['temp_sum'] += float(avg_temp) * school_count
 65|                acc['temp_weight'] += school_count
 66|
 67|            coord = coords.get(city)
 68|            if not coord:
 69|                continue
 70|            city_payload.append({
 71|                'city': city,
 72|                'province': province,
 73|                'region': region,
 74|                'latitude': coord.get('latitude'),
 75|                'longitude': coord.get('longitude'),
 76|                'school_count': school_count,
 77|                'avg_temp': round(float(avg_temp), 2) if avg_temp is not
   |     None else None,
 78|                'carbon_factor': carbon,
 79|            })
 80|
 81|        provinces = []
 82|        for acc in province_acc.values():
 83|            avg_temp = None
 84|            if acc['temp_weight']:
 85|                avg_temp = round(acc['temp_sum'] / acc['temp_weight'], 2
   |    )
 86|            provinces.append({
 87|                'name': acc['name'],
 88|                'region': acc['region'],
 89|                'school_count': acc['school_count'],
 90|                'avg_temp': avg_temp,
 91|                'carbon_factor': acc['carbon_factor'],
 92|            })
 93|
 94|        city_payload.sort(key=lambda item: (-item['school_count'], item[
   |    'city']))
 95|        provinces.sort(key=lambda item: (-item['school_count'], item['na
   |    me']))
 96|        return jsonify({
 97|            'success': True,
 98|            'year': 2025,
 99|            'totals': {
100|                'schools': total_schools,
101|                'cities': len(city_payload),
102|                'provinces': len(provinces),
103|            },
104|            'provinces': provinces,
105|            'cities': city_payload,
106|            'sources': catalog.provenance(),
107|            'generated_at': datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
   |    ,
108|        })
109|    except Exception as exc:
110|        logger.error('Map data error: %s', exc)
111|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '地图数据加载失败'}
   |    ), 500
112|
113|def _monthly_climate(catalog, city):
114|    daily = catalog.city_daily(city) or []
115|    buckets = {}
116|    for row in daily:
117|        date_text = str(row.get('date') or '')
118|        try:
119|            month = int(date_text[5:7])
120|        except (TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
121|            continue
122|        item = buckets.setdefault(month, {'tmean': [], 'tmax': [], 'tmin
   |    ': []})
123|        for key in ('tmean', 'tmax', 'tmin'):
124|            value = row.get(key + '_c')
125|            if value is not None:
126|                item[key].append(float(value))
127|
128|    months = []
129|    for month in range(1, 13):
130|        item = buckets.get(month)
131|        if not item or not item['tmean']:
132|            months.append({'month': month, 'tmean': None, 'tmax': None, 
   |    'tmin': None})
133|            continue
134|        months.append({
135|            'month': month,
136|            'tmean': round(sum(item['tmean']) / len(item['tmean']), 2),
137|            'tmax': round(max(item['tmax']), 2) if item['tmax'] else Non
   |    e,
138|            'tmin': round(min(item['tmin']), 2) if item['tmin'] else Non
   |    e,
139|        })
140|    return months
141|
142|
143|def _latest_city_run(city):
144|    for run in run_store.list_runs(100):
145|        params = run.get('params') or {}
146|        if run.get('status') != 'success' or params.get('site_id') != ci
   |    ty:
147|            continue
148|        summary = run.get('summary') or {}
149|        if not summary:
150|            continue
151|        ranking = rank_algorithms(summary)
152|        best = ranking.get('best')
153|        if not best:
154|            continue
155|        metrics = summary.get(best) or {}
156|        return {
157|            'run_id': run.get('run_id'),
158|            'created_at': run.get('created_at'),
159|            'num_servers': params.get('num_servers'),
160|            'simulation_hours': params.get('simulation_hours'),
161|            'algorithm': best,
162|            'metrics': {
163|                'avg_pue': metrics.get('avg_pue'),
164|                'max_temp': metrics.get('max_temp'),
165|                'service_rate': metrics.get('service_rate'),
166|                'energy_per_served_load': metrics.get('energy_per_served
   |    _load'),
167|            },
168|        }
169|    return None
170|

172|@map_bp.route('/api/v1/map/city/<path:city>', methods=['GET'])
173|def map_city(city):
174|    try:
175|        catalog = Catalog()

177|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '数据目录不可用
   |    '}), 503
178|
179|        city_name = str(city or '').strip()[:64]
180|        rows = [row for row in catalog.cities() if row.get('city') == ci
   |    ty_name]
181|        if not rows:
182|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '城市不存在'}),
   |     404
183|
184|        row = rows[0]
185|        province = row.get('province') or catalog.city_province(city_nam
   |    e) or ''
186|        region = region_for_province(province)
187|        school_count = int(row.get('school_count') or 0)
188|        climate = {item['city']: item.get('avg_temp') for item in catalo
   |    g.city_climate_summary()}
189|        avg_temp = climate.get(city_name)
190|        carbon = catalog.emission_factor(region)
191|        latest = _latest_city_run(city_name)
192|
193|        if latest:
194|            metrics = latest.get('metrics') or {}
195|            recommendation = {
196|                'servers': latest.get('num_servers') or 48,
197|                'algorithm': latest.get('algorithm'),
198|                'hours': latest.get('simulation_hours') or 24,
199|                'pue': metrics.get('avg_pue'),
200|                'max_temp': metrics.get('max_temp'),
201|                'service_rate': metrics.get('service_rate'),
202|                'source': 'latest_run',
203|                'run_id': latest.get('run_id'),
204|            }
205|        else:
206|            recommendation = {
207|                'servers': 48,
208|                'algorithm': 'damo_ets',
209|                'hours': 24,
210|                'pue': None,
211|                'max_temp': None,
212|                'service_rate': None,
213|                'source': 'baseline',
214|                'run_id': None,
215|            }
216|
217|        return jsonify({
218|            'success': True,
219|            'city': city_name,
220|            'province': province,
221|            'region': region,
222|            'school_count': school_count,
223|            'avg_temp': round(float(avg_temp), 2) if avg_temp is not Non
   |    e else None,
224|            'carbon_factor': carbon,
225|            'months': _monthly_climate(catalog, city_name),
226|            'latest_run': latest,
227|            'recommendation': recommendation,
228|        })
229|    except Exception as exc:
230|        logger.error('Map city error: %s', exc)
231|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '城市详情加载失败'}
   |    ), 500

  1|"""Run registry, async simulation jobs and live events."""
  2|

  4|from queue import Empty

  6|import pandas as pd
  7|from flask import Blueprint, Response, jsonify, request, stream_with_con
   |    text

  9|from src.services import run_store
 10|from src.services.run_manager import get_run_manager
 11|
 12|run_bp = Blueprint("runs", __name__)
 13|
 14|
 15|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs", methods=["GET"])
 16|def list_runs():
 17|    get_run_manager()
 18|    try:
 19|        limit = int(request.args.get("limit", 20))
 20|    except ValueError:
 21|        limit = 20
 22|    return jsonify({"success": True, "runs": run_store.list_runs(limit)}
   |    )
 23|
 24|
 25|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs", methods=["POST"])
 26|def create_run():
 27|    body = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
 28|    origin = "ai" if body.get("origin") == "ai" else "web"
 29|    try:
 30|        run_id = get_run_manager().submit(body, origin=origin)
 31|    except ValueError as exc:
 32|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": str(exc)}), 400
 33|    except Exception as exc:
 34|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "创建运行失败: %s" 
   |    % exc}), 500
 35|    return jsonify({"success": True, "run_id": run_id, "status": "queued
   |    "})
 36|
 37|
 38|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs/latest", methods=["GET"])
 39|def latest_run():
 40|    get_run_manager()
 41|    record = run_store.latest_run()
 42|    if not record:
 43|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "暂无运行记录"}), 4
   |    04
 44|    return jsonify({"success": True, "run": record})
 45|
 46|
 47|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs/compare", methods=["GET"])
 48|def compare_runs():
 49|    left = (request.args.get("left") or "").strip()
 50|    right = (request.args.get("right") or "").strip()
 51|    if not left or not right:
 52|        runs = run_store.list_runs(2)
 53|        if len(runs) < 2:
 54|            return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "至少需要两次运
   |    行才能对比"}), 404
 55|        right, left = runs[0]["run_id"], runs[1]["run_id"]
 56|    comparison = run_store.compare_runs(left, right)
 57|    if not comparison:
 58|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "运行记录不存在"}),
   |     404
 59|    return jsonify({"success": True, "comparison": comparison})
 60|
 61|
 62|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs/<run_id>", methods=["GET"])
 63|def get_run(run_id: str):
 64|    record = run_store.get_run(run_id)
 65|    if not record:
 66|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "运行记录不存在"}),
   |     404
 67|    return jsonify({"success": True, "run": record})
 68|
 69|
 70|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs/<run_id>/summary", methods=["GET"])
 71|def get_run_summary(run_id: str):
 72|    record = run_store.get_run(run_id)
 73|    if not record:
 74|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "运行记录不存在"}),
   |     404
 75|    return jsonify({
 76|        "success": True,
 77|        "run_id": run_id,
 78|        "status": record.get("status"),
 79|        "summary": record.get("summary") or {},
 80|        "params": record.get("params") or {},
 81|    })
 82|
 83|
 84|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/runs/<run_id>/series", methods=["GET"])
 85|def get_run_series(run_id: str):
 86|    record = run_store.get_run(run_id)
 87|    if not record:
 88|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "运行记录不存在"}),
   |     404
 89|    df = run_store.load_results(run_id)
 90|    if df is None:
 91|        return jsonify({"success": False, "message": "结果文件不存在"}),
   |     404
 92|    algorithm = (request.args.get("algorithm") or "").strip()
 93|    if algorithm:
 94|        df = df[df["algorithm"] == algorithm]
 95|    df = df.where(pd.notnull(df), None)
 96|    return jsonify({
 97|        "success": True,
 98|        "run_id": run_id,
 99|        "data": df.to_dict("records"),
100|    })
101|
102|
103|@run_bp.route("/api/v1/events", methods=["GET"])
104|def run_events():
105|    manager = get_run_manager()
106|    queue = manager.events.subscribe()
107|
108|    def stream():
109|        try:
110|            yield "event: ready\ndata: {}\n\n"
111|            while True:
112|                try:
113|                    event = queue.get(timeout=15)
114|                except Empty:
115|                    yield ": ping\n\n"
116|                    continue
117|                yield "data: " + json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False, d
   |    efault=str) + "\n\n"
118|        finally:
119|            manager.events.unsubscribe(queue)
120|
121|    return Response(
122|        stream_with_context(stream()),
123|        mimetype="text/event-stream",
124|        headers={
125|            "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
126|            "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
127|            "Connection": "keep-alive",
128|        },
129|    )

 12|@speech_bp.route('/api/v1/speech/session', methods=['POST'])
 13|def create_session():
 14|    """创建语音识别会话"""
 15|    try:
 16|        data = request.json or {}
 17|        language = validate_str(
 18|            data.get('language', 'zh-CN'), max_len=10,
 19|            pattern=r'^[a-z]{2,3}(-[A-Z]{2,3})?$',
 20|            default='zh-CN', name='language'
 21|        )
 22|        svc = get_speech_service()
 23|        session = svc.create_session(language)
 24|
 25|        lang_info = SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES.get(language, SUPPORTED_LANGUAGE
   |    S['zh-CN'])
 26|
 27|        return jsonify({
 28|            'success': True,
 29|            'session_id': session.session_id,
 30|            'language': language,
 31|            'language_name': lang_info['name'],
 32|            'offline_available': lang_info['offline_available'],
 33|            'web_api_code': lang_info['web_api_code']
 34|        })
 35|    except Exception as e:
 36|        logger.error(f"Speech session creation error: {e}")
 37|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500

 47|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500
 48|
 49|@speech_bp.route('/api/v1/speech/result', methods=['POST'])
 50|def submit_result():
 51|    """提交识别结果"""
 52|    try:
 53|        data = request.json or {}
 54|        session_id = validate_str(data.get('session_id', ''), max_len=64
   |    , name='session_id')
 55|        text = validate_str(data.get('text', ''), max_len=5000, name='te
   |    xt')
 56|        confidence = float(data.get('confidence', 0.0))
 57|        is_final = bool(data.get('is_final', False))
 58|        engine_used = data.get('engine_used', 'web_api')
 59|        language = data.get('language', 'zh-CN')
 60|
 61|        svc = get_speech_service()
 62|        result = svc.add_result(session_id, text, confidence,
 63|                                is_final=is_final, engine_used=engine_us
   |    ed)
 64|
 65|        return jsonify({
 66|            'success': True,
 67|            'result': {
 68|                'text': result.text if result else text,
 69|                'confidence': confidence,
 70|                'is_final': is_final
 71|            }
 72|        })
 73|    except Exception as e:
 74|        logger.error(f"Speech result submission error: {e}")
 75|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500
 76|
 77|@speech_bp.route('/api/v1/speech/session/<session_id>', methods=['GET'])
 78|def get_session(session_id):
 79|    """获取会话状态和全部文字"""
 80|    try:
 81|        svc = get_speech_service()
 82|        session = svc.get_session(session_id)
 83|        if not session:
 84|            return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': '会话不存在'}),
   |     404
 85|
 86|        all_text = svc.get_all_text(session_id, final_only=False)
 87|
 88|        return jsonify({
 89|            'success': True,
 90|            'session_id': session_id,
 91|            'language': session.language,
 92|            'state': session.state.value,
 93|            'text': all_text,
 94|            'result_count': len(session.results)
 95|        })
 96|    except Exception as e:
 97|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500
 98|
 99|@speech_bp.route('/api/v1/speech/session/<session_id>', methods=['DELETE
   |    '])
100|def cleanup_session(session_id):
101|    """清理会话"""
102|    try:
103|        svc = get_speech_service()
104|        svc.cleanup_session(session_id)
105|        return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': '会话已清理'})
106|    except Exception as e:
107|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500
108|
109|@speech_bp.route('/api/v1/speech/health', methods=['GET'])
110|def speech_health():
111|    """语音服务健康检查"""
112|    try:
113|        svc = get_speech_service()
114|        return jsonify({
115|            'success': True,
116|            'supported_languages': len(SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES),
117|            'engine': svc._engine.value if hasattr(svc._engine, 'value')
   |     else str(svc._engine),
118|            'active_sessions': len(svc._sessions)
119|        })
120|    except Exception as e:
121|        return jsonify({'success': False, 'message': str(e)}), 500

 25|def run_simulation(args):
 26|    """运行仿真命令（支持环境自动检测）"""
 27|    adapter = _select_adapter(args)
 28|    
 29|    if not adapter.initialize():
 30|        logger.error("适配器初始化失败")
 31|        return
 32|
 33|    logger.info(f"使用适配器: {adapter.get_type().value}")
 34|    logger.info(f"  能力: {adapter.get_capability().description}")
 35|    
 36|    algorithms = args.algorithms.split(',') if args.algorithms else ['gr
   |    eedy', 'genetic', 'round_robin']
 37|    
 38|    params = {
 39|        'num_servers': args.servers,
 40|        'simulation_hours': args.hours,
 41|        'parallel_workers': args.workers,
 42|        'run_mode': args.mode,
 43|        'algorithms': algorithms,
 44|        'visualization': not args.no_plot,
 45|        'save_results': not args.no_save
 46|    }
 47|    
 48|    logger.info(f"参数: servers={args.servers}, hours={args.hours}, work
   |    ers={args.workers}, mode={args.mode}")
 49|    logger.info(f"算法: {algorithms}")
 50|    
 51|    result = adapter.execute(**params)
 52|    
 53|    _display_result(result)
 54|
 55|def _select_adapter(args) -> 'BaseAdapter':
 56|    """根据参数和环境选择适配器"""
 57|    adapter_map = {
 58|        'auto': AutoAdapter,
 59|        'local': LocalAdapter,
 60|        'remote': RemoteAdapter,
 61|    }
 62|    if CudaAdapter is not None:
 63|        adapter_map['cuda'] = CudaAdapter
 64|    if AscendAdapter is not None:
 65|        adapter_map['ascend'] = AscendAdapter
 66|    
 67|    adapter_type = args.adapter or 'auto'
 68|    adapter_cls = adapter_map.get(adapter_type, AutoAdapter)
 69|    adapter = adapter_cls()
 70|    
 71|    if adapter_type == 'auto':
 72|        logger.info("正在检测环境...")
 73|        status = adapter.get_status()
 74|        logger.info(f"可用适配器: {len(status.get('available_adapters', 
   |    []))} 个")
 75|        for a in status.get('available_adapters', []):
 76|            logger.info(f"  - {a['name']}: {'可用' if a['available'] els
   |    e '不可用'}")
 77|    
 78|    return adapter

 80|def _display_payload(result: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 81|    """Accept either a PluginResult or a plain dict and expose a dict.""
   |    "
 82|    if hasattr(result, 'success') and hasattr(result, 'data'):
 83|        payload = dict(result.data) if isinstance(result.data, dict) els
   |    e {}
 84|        payload['success'] = bool(result.success)
 85|        if not payload.get('success'):
 86|            payload['error'] = result.message or result.error_code or '
   |    未知错误'
 87|        return payload
 88|    if isinstance(result, dict):
 89|        return result
 90|    return {'success': False, 'error': '返回类型无法识别'}
 91|
 92|
 93|def _display_result(result: Any) -> None:
 94|    """显示仿真结果"""
 95|    result = _display_payload(result)
 96|    if result.get('success'):
 97|        logger.info("=" * 60)
 98|        logger.info("仿真结果统计")
 99|        logger.info("=" * 60)
100|        
101|        for algo, metrics in result.get('statistics', {}).items():
102|            logger.info(f"【{algo.upper()}算法】")
103|            logger.info(f"  平均能耗: {metrics.get('avg_energy', 0):.2f}
   |     W")
104|            logger.info(f"  最高温度: {metrics.get('max_temp', 0):.2f} °
   |    C")
105|            logger.info(f"  平均PUE: {metrics.get('avg_pue', 0):.2f}")
106|            logger.info(f"  总处理负载: {metrics.get('total_load', 0):.0
   |    f}")
107|        
108|        logger.info(f"总记录数: {result.get('records_count', 0)}")
109|        
110|        if 'csv_path' in result:
111|            logger.info(f"数据文件: {result['csv_path']}")
112|        if 'excel_path' in result:
113|            logger.info(f"Excel文件: {result['excel_path']}")
114|        if 'plots' in result:
115|            logger.info("图表文件:")
116|            for name, path in result['plots'].items():
117|                logger.info(f"  - {path}")
118|        if 'adapter_type' in result:
119|            logger.info(f"适配器: {result['adapter_type']}")
120|    else:
121|        logger.error(f"仿真失败: {result.get('error', 'Unknown error')}"
   |    )

192|

194|        cuda = CudaAdapter()
195|        if cuda.is_available():

202|

204|        logger.warning("没有可用的适配器进行测试")
205|        return
206|
207|    for name, adapter in adapters_to_test:
208|        adapter.initialize({
209|            'num_servers': args.bench_servers,
210|            'simulation_hours': args.bench_hours,
211|            'parallel_workers': 2
212|        })
213|        logger.info(f"测试 {name}...")
214|        import time

217|            num_servers=args.bench_servers,
218|            simulation_hours=args.bench_hours,
219|            algorithms=['greedy'],
220|            visualization=False,
221|            save_results=False
222|        )

224|        logger.info(f"  耗时: {elapsed:.2f}s")
225|        bench = _display_payload(result)
226|        logger.info(f"  结果: {'成功' if bench.get('success') else '失败
   |    '}")

229|def train_rl_agent(args):
230|    """训练 RL 调度器（GitHub Skills 集成 — Stable Baselines3 PPO）"""
231|    from src.plugins._deps import has_gymnasium, has_sb3, get_dependency
   |    _status
232|
233|    logger.info("=" * 60)
234|    logger.info("RL 调度器训练 (GitHub Skills 集成)")
235|    logger.info("=" * 60)
236|    logger.info(f"依赖探测: gymnasium={has_gymnasium()}, stable-baseline
   |    s3={has_sb3()}")
237|    if not has_gymnasium() or not has_sb3():
238|        logger.error("依赖缺失，请先执行: pip install gymnasium stable-ba
   |    selines3")
239|        return
240|
241|    from src.plugin import PluginManager
242|
243|    pm = PluginManager()
244|    discovered = pm.discover_plugins(plugin_dir="src/plugins")
245|    logger.info(f"自动发现插件: {discovered} 个")
246|    for name in pm._plugin_classes.keys():
247|        logger.info(f"  - {name}")
248|
249|    if "RLSchedulerPlugin" not in pm._plugin_classes:
250|        logger.error("未发现 RLSchedulerPlugin，请检查 src/plugins/rl_ppo
   |    _scheduler.py")
251|        return
252|
253|    config = {
254|        "num_servers": args.servers,
255|        "simulation_hours": args.hours,
256|        "model_path": args.save_path,
257|    }
258|    if not pm.load_plugin("RLSchedulerPlugin", config=config):
259|        logger.error("RLSchedulerPlugin 加载失败")
260|        return
261|
262|    logger.info(
263|        f"开始训练 PPO agent: servers={args.servers}, hours={args.hours}
   |    , "
264|        f"timesteps={args.timesteps}, save={args.save_path}"
265|    )
266|    result = pm.execute_plugin(
267|        "RLSchedulerPlugin",
268|        algorithms=["rl_ppo"],
269|        train=True,
270|        total_timesteps=args.timesteps,
271|        model_path=args.save_path,
272|        learning_rate=args.lr,
273|        seed=args.seed,
274|    )
275|
276|    if result.success:
277|        logger.info("✓ 训练完成")
278|        logger.info(f"  模型路径: {result.data.get('model_path')}")
279|        logger.info(f"  训练耗时: {result.data.get('training_time_s')} s
   |    ")
280|        logger.info(f"  训练步数: {result.data.get('total_timesteps')}")
281|        logger.info(f"  推理: pm.execute_plugin('RLSchedulerPlugin', tra
   |    in=False, model_path='{args.save_path}')")
282|    else:
283|        logger.error(f"训练失败: {result.message} [{result.error_code}]"
   |    )
284|        if result.error_detail:
285|            logger.error(f"  详情: {result.error_detail}")

303|def create_parser():
304|    """创建命令行解析器"""
305|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
306|        description='校园数据中心智能散热与算力调度系统 - CLI工具 v1.0',
307|        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter
308|    )
309|
310|    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest='command', help='可用命令')
311|
312|    run_parser = subparsers.add_parser('run', help='运行仿真')
313|    run_parser.add_argument('--servers', type=int, default=10, help='服
   |    务器数量 (1-1000)')
314|    run_parser.add_argument('--hours', type=int, default=24, help='仿真
   |    时长/小时 (1-720)')
315|    run_parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=4, help='并行
   |    工作器数')
316|    run_parser.add_argument('--mode', type=str, default='standard',
317|                           choices=['standard', 'distributed', 'large_sc
   |    ale'],
318|                           help='运行模式')
319|    run_parser.add_argument('--algorithms', type=str, default='greedy,ge
   |    netic,round_robin',
320|                           help='算法列表(逗号分隔)')
321|    run_parser.add_argument('--adapter', type=str, default='auto',
322|                           choices=['auto', 'local', 'remote', 'cuda', '
   |    ascend'],
323|                           help='适配器类型')
324|    run_parser.add_argument('--no-plot', action='store_true', help='跳过
   |    绘图')
325|    run_parser.add_argument('--no-save', action='store_true', help='跳过
   |    保存')
326|    run_parser.set_defaults(func=run_simulation)
327|
328|    api_parser = subparsers.add_parser('api', help='启动API服务')
329|    api_parser.add_argument('--host', type=str, default='0.0.0.0', help=
   |    '绑定地址')
330|    api_parser.add_argument('--port', type=int, default=5000, help='端口
   |    号')
331|    api_parser.add_argument('--debug', action='store_true', help='调试模
   |    式')
332|    api_parser.set_defaults(func=run_api_server)
333|
334|    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser('list', help='列出可用算法')
335|    list_parser.set_defaults(func=list_algorithms)
336|
337|    env_parser = subparsers.add_parser('env', help='环境检测')
338|    env_parser.set_defaults(func=detect_environment)
339|
340|    bench_parser = subparsers.add_parser('benchmark', help='适配器基准测
   |    试')
341|    bench_parser.add_argument('--bench-servers', type=int, default=10, h
   |    elp='测试服务器数')
342|    bench_parser.add_argument('--bench-hours', type=int, default=4, help
   |    ='测试仿真时长')
343|    bench_parser.add_argument('--remote-url', type=str, help='远程API地
   |    址')
344|    bench_parser.set_defaults(func=benchmark_adapters)
345|
346|    # 检查 GitHub Skills 可选依赖
347|    deps_parser = subparsers.add_parser('deps', help='检查 GitHub Skills
   |     可选依赖')
348|    deps_parser.set_defaults(func=check_deps)
349|
350|    return parser
351|
352|def cli():
353|    """CLI入口函数"""
354|    parser = create_parser()
355|    args = parser.parse_args()
356|
357|    if args.command is None:
358|        parser.print_help()
359|        logger.info("可用命令:")
360|        logger.info("  run       运行仿真（支持自动环境检测）")
361|        logger.info("  api       启动API服务")
362|        logger.info("  list      列出可用算法")
363|        logger.info("  env       检测运行环境")
364|        logger.info("  benchmark 适配器基准测试")
365|        logger.info("  deps      检查可选依赖")
366|        return
367|
368|    try:
369|        args.func(args)
370|    except Exception as e:
371|        logger.error(f"命令执行失败: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
372|        return 1
373|    return 0
374|
375|if __name__ == '__main__':
376|    import sys
377|    sys.exit(cli())

================================================================================
29. src/data/catalog.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Read-only access to the provenance-tagged data catalog.
  2|
  3|The catalog is a plain SQLite file (``data/catalog.db``) built by the sc
   |    ripts in
  4|``tools/etl``. Every row carries a ``source_id`` pointing at
  5|``meta_data_source``, so any number that ends up in a report can be trac
   |    ed back
  6|to a publisher, a URL and a retrieval timestamp.
  7|
  8|Nothing here reaches out to the network: the simulation must run offline
   |    , so the
  9|catalog is a frozen snapshot taken while preparing the demo.
 10|"""
 11|
 12|import os
 13|import sqlite3
 14|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
 15|
 16|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 17|
 18|logger = setup_logger("catalog")
 19|

 53|class Catalog:
 54|    """Thin, read-only wrapper around the catalog database."""
 55|
 56|    def __init__(self, db_path: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
 57|        raw = db_path or DEFAULT_DB
 58|        self.db_path = raw if os.path.isabs(raw) else os.path.join(PROJE
   |    CT_ROOT, raw)
 59|        self._conn: Optional[sqlite3.Connection] = None
 60|
 62|    def connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
 63|        if self._conn is None:
 64|            conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
 65|            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
 66|            self._conn = conn
 67|        return self._conn
 68|
 69|    def close(self) -> None:
 70|        if self._conn is not None:
 71|            self._conn.close()
 72|            self._conn = None
 73|
 74|    def __enter__(self) -> "Catalog":
 75|        self.connect()
 76|        return self
 77|
 78|    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
 79|        self.close()
 80|
 81|    def available(self) -> bool:
 82|        if not os.path.exists(self.db_path):
 83|            return False
 84|        try:
 85|            conn = self.connect()
 86|            names = {
 87|                row["name"]
 88|                for row in conn.execute(
 89|                    "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
 90|                )
 91|            }
 92|        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
 93|            logger.warning("读取数据目录失败: %s", exc)
 94|            return False
 95|        return "dim_ambient_hourly" in names
 96|
 97|    def table_names(self) -> List[str]:
 98|        conn = self.connect()
 99|        return [
100|            row["name"]
101|            for row in conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHER
   |    E type='table'")
102|        ]
103|
104|    def _rows(self, sql: str, params: Tuple[Any, ...] = ()) -> List[sqli
   |    te3.Row]:
105|        try:
106|            return list(self.connect().execute(sql, params))
107|        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
108|            logger.warning("查询失败 (%s): %s", sql.split()[1] if len(sq
   |    l.split()) > 1 else "?", exc)
109|            return []
110|
112|    def provenance(self, source_id: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[s
   |    tr, Any]]:
113|        if source_id:
114|            rows = self._rows(
115|                "SELECT * FROM meta_data_source WHERE source_id = ?", (s
   |    ource_id,)
116|            )
117|        else:
118|            rows = self._rows("SELECT * FROM meta_data_source ORDER BY s
   |    ource_id")
119|        return [dict(row) for row in rows]
120|
121|    def versions(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
122|        return [dict(row) for row in self._rows(
123|            "SELECT * FROM meta_dataset_version ORDER BY created_at DESC
   |    "
124|        )]
125|
126|    def ambient_series(self, site_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[
   |    List[float]]:
127|        """Hourly outdoor temperature for a site.
128|
129|        Uses the stored hourly series when one exists. Otherwise falls b
   |    ack to
130|        the city's daily max/min/mean and expands it with a standard diu
   |    rnal
131|        curve (peak at 15:00, trough at 03:00), which is how a school ma
   |    ps to
132|        its local climate without storing 8760 rows for 345 cities.
133|        """
134|        if site_id:
135|            rows = self._rows(
136|                "SELECT temp_c FROM dim_ambient_hourly WHERE site_id = ?
   |     ORDER BY ts",
137|                (site_id,),
138|            )
139|        else:
140|            rows = self._rows("SELECT temp_c FROM dim_ambient_hourly ORD
   |    ER BY ts")
141|        values = [float(row["temp_c"]) for row in rows if row["temp_c"] 
   |    is not None]
142|        if values:
143|            return values
144|        if site_id:
145|            daily = self.city_daily(site_id)
146|            if daily:
147|                return self._expand_daily(daily)
148|        return None
149|
150|    @staticmethod
151|    def _expand_daily(daily: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[float]:
152|        import math
153|        series: List[float] = []
154|        for row in daily:
155|            tmax = float(row["tmax_c"])
156|            tmin = float(row["tmin_c"])
157|            tmean = float(row["tmean_c"])
158|            amplitude = max(0.0, (tmax - tmin) / 2.0)
159|            for hour in range(24):
160|                series.append(
161|                    tmean + amplitude * math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * (hour -
   |     9) / 24.0)
162|                )
163|        return series
164|
165|    def city_daily(self, city: str) -> Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
166|        rows = self._rows(
167|            "SELECT city, date, tmax_c, tmin_c, tmean_c FROM dim_city_da
   |    ily "
168|            "WHERE city = ? ORDER BY date",
169|            (city,),
170|        )
171|        return [dict(row) for row in rows] or None
172|
173|    def cities(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
174|        rows = self._rows(
175|            "SELECT d1.city, "
176|            "  (SELECT d2.province FROM dim_school d2 WHERE d2.city = d1
   |    .city "
177|            "   GROUP BY d2.province ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 1) AS 
   |    province, "
178|            "  COUNT(*) AS school_count "
179|            "FROM dim_school d1 GROUP BY d1.city ORDER BY province, d1.c
   |    ity"
180|        )
181|        return [dict(row) for row in rows]
182|
183|    def city_climate_summary(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
184|        rows = self._rows(
185|            "SELECT city, AVG(tmean_c) AS avg_temp FROM dim_city_daily G
   |    ROUP BY city"
186|        )
187|        return [dict(row) for row in rows]
188|
189|    def _school_filter(self, query: Optional[str], city: Optional[str]):
190|        clauses = []
191|        params: List[Any] = []
192|        if city:
193|            clauses.append("city = ?")
194|            params.append(city)
195|        if query:
196|            clauses.append("school_name LIKE ?")
197|            params.append("%" + query + "%")
198|        where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
199|        return where, tuple(params)
200|
201|    def count_schools(self, query: Optional[str] = None, city: Optional[
   |    str] = None) -> int:
202|        where, params = self._school_filter(query, city)
203|        rows = self._rows("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM dim_school" + where
   |    , params)
204|        return int(rows[0]["n"]) if rows else 0
205|
206|    def schools(self, query: Optional[str] = None, city: Optional[str] =
   |     None,
207|                limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
208|        where, params = self._school_filter(query, city)
209|        rows = self._rows(
210|            "SELECT school_name, province, city, level, department FROM 
   |    dim_school"
211|            + where + " ORDER BY school_name LIMIT ?",
212|            params + (int(limit),),
213|        )
214|        return [dict(row) for row in rows]
215|
216|    def task_stream(
217|        self, trace_id: Optional[str] = None, hours: Optional[int] = Non
   |    e
218|    ) -> Optional[List[List[Tuple[int, int, str, int, int]]]]:
219|        """Rebuild the pre-built task stream as per-hour spec lists.
220|
221|        Rows are expected to carry the hour bucket, resource request, pr
   |    iority,
222|        workload class, duration and deadline offset recorded during imp
   |    ort.
223|        """
224|        clauses: List[str] = []
225|        params: List[Any] = []
226|        if trace_id:
227|            clauses.append("trace_id = ?")
228|            params.append(trace_id)
229|        where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
230|        rows = self._rows(
231|            "SELECT seq, hour, load_units, priority, workload_class, dur
   |    ation_h,"
232|            " deadline_offset_h FROM fact_task_arrivals" + where + " ORD
   |    ER BY seq",
233|            tuple(params),
234|        )
235|        if not rows:
236|            return None
237|
238|        buckets: Dict[int, List[Tuple[int, int, str, int, int]]] = {}
239|        for row in rows:
240|            hour = int(row["hour"])
241|            if hours is not None and hour >= hours:
242|                continue
243|            buckets.setdefault(hour, []).append((
244|                int(row["load_units"]),
245|                int(row["priority"]),
246|                str(row["workload_class"]),
247|                int(row["duration_h"]),
248|                int(row["deadline_offset_h"]),
249|            ))
250|
251|        if not buckets:
252|            return None
253|        top = max(buckets)
254|        return [buckets.get(hour, []) for hour in range(top + 1)]
255|
256|    def pue_benchmarks(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
257|        return [dict(row) for row in self._rows(
258|            "SELECT * FROM dim_pue_benchmark ORDER BY pue_limit"
259|        )]
260|
261|    def city_province(self, city: str) -> Optional[str]:
262|        rows = self._rows(
263|            "SELECT province FROM dim_school WHERE city = ? "
264|            "GROUP BY province ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 1",
265|            (city,),
266|        )
267|        return rows[0]["province"] if rows else None
268|
269|    def emission_factor(
270|        self, region: Optional[str] = None, year: Optional[int] = None
271|    ) -> Optional[float]:
272|        if region:
273|            sql = "SELECT kg_co2_per_kwh FROM dim_emission_factor WHERE 
   |    region = ?"
274|            params: List[Any] = [region]
275|            if year is not None:
276|                sql += " AND year = ?"
277|                params.append(int(year))
278|            rows = self._rows(sql, tuple(params))
279|            for row in rows:
280|                if row["kg_co2_per_kwh"] is not None:
281|                    return float(row["kg_co2_per_kwh"])
282|
283|        rows = self._rows(
284|            "SELECT kg_co2_per_kwh FROM dim_emission_factor "
285|            "WHERE region = '全国（电力平均）' LIMIT 1"
286|        )
287|        for row in rows:
288|            if row["kg_co2_per_kwh"] is not None:
289|                return float(row["kg_co2_per_kwh"])
290|        return None
291|
292|    def summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
293|        """Compact description used by the API and the dashboard."""
294|        info: Dict[str, Any] = {
295|            "db_path": self.db_path,
296|            "available": self.available(),
297|        }
298|        if not info["available"]:
299|            return info
300|        info["tables"] = self.table_names()
301|        info["sources"] = self.provenance()
302|        ambient = self.ambient_series()
303|        info["ambient_hours"] = len(ambient) if ambient else 0
304|        return info

31. src/models/energy_model.py
================================================================================
  1|import numpy as np
  2|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
  3|from src.models.server import Server
  4|
  5|class EnergyModel:
  6|    """IT + cooling power model.
  7|
  8|    Cooling is derived from the heat the IT load actually produces, scal
   |    ed by
  9|    the outdoor temperature. A part-load term keeps the chillers from lo
   |    oking
 10|    perfectly efficient when the room is nearly empty, which is what mak
   |    es PUE
 11|    depend on the scheduling decision instead of only on the weather.
 12|    """
 13|
 14|    def __init__(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:
 15|        cfg = config
 16|        if cfg is None:
 17|            try:
 18|                from src.utils.config import Config
 19|                cfg = Config().get("energy", {}) or {}
 20|            except Exception:
 21|                cfg = {}
 22|
 23|        self.cop_base: float = float(cfg.get("cop_base", 2.5))
 24|        self.cop_delta_coefficient: float = float(cfg.get("cop_delta_coe
   |    fficient", 0.1))
 25|        self.heat_to_power_ratio: float = float(cfg.get("heat_to_power_r
   |    atio", 0.8))
 26|        self.pue_base: float = float(cfg.get("pue_base", 1.8))
 27|        self.target_temp: float = float(cfg.get("target_temp", 22))
 28|        self.part_load_penalty: float = float(cfg.get("part_load_penalty
   |    ", 0.5))
 29|        self.temp_penalty_coefficient: float = float(cfg.get("temp_penal
   |    ty_coefficient", 1.0))
 30|        self.temp_reference: float = float(cfg.get("temp_reference", 35.
   |    0))
 31|        self.cooling_efficiency_base: float = 0.5
 32|
 34|    def server_power(self, server: Server) -> float:
 35|        return server.calculate_power()
 36|
 37|    def calculate_server_energy(self, server: Server) -> float:
 38|        return server.calculate_power()
 39|
 40|    def total_it_power(self, servers: List[Server]) -> float:
 41|        return float(sum(s.calculate_power() for s in servers))
 42|
 44|    def get_cop(self, ambient_temp: float, target_temp: Optional[float] 
   |    = None) -> float:
 45|        target = self.target_temp if target_temp is None else float(targ
   |    et_temp)
 46|        delta_temp = max(0.0, target - ambient_temp)
 47|        return self.cop_base + delta_temp * self.cop_delta_coefficient
 48|
 49|    def rated_heat(self, servers: List[Server]) -> float:
 50|        if not servers:
 51|            return 0.0
 52|        return float(
 53|            sum(max(0.0, s.max_power - s.base_power) for s in servers) *
   |     self.heat_to_power_ratio
 54|        )
 55|
 56|    def heat_load_ratio(self, servers: List[Server]) -> float:
 57|        rated = self.rated_heat(servers)
 58|        if rated <= 0:
 59|            return 1.0
 60|        heat = self.total_it_power(servers) * self.heat_to_power_ratio
 61|        return float(min(1.0, max(0.0, heat / rated)))
 62|
 63|    def temperature_lift_factor(self, peak_temp: Optional[float]) -> flo
   |    at:
 64|        """Chillers work harder when the hot aisle runs hotter."""
 65|        if peak_temp is None:
 66|            return 1.0
 67|        reference = max(1.0, self.temp_reference)
 68|        lift = max(0.0, (float(peak_temp) - reference) / reference)
 69|        return float(1.0 + self.temp_penalty_coefficient * lift)
 70|
 71|    def calculate_cooling_energy(
 72|        self,
 73|        servers: List[Server],
 74|        ambient_temp: float,
 75|        target_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 76|        peak_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 77|    ) -> float:
 78|        total_heat = self.total_it_power(servers) * self.heat_to_power_r
   |    atio
 79|        cop = max(1e-6, self.get_cop(ambient_temp, target_temp))
 80|        ideal = total_heat / cop
 81|        ratio = self.heat_load_ratio(servers)
 82|        partial = 1.0 + self.part_load_penalty * (1.0 - ratio)
 83|        return float(ideal * partial * self.temperature_lift_factor(peak
   |    _temp))
 84|
 85|    def calculate_total_energy(
 86|        self,
 87|        servers: List[Server],
 88|        ambient_temp: float,
 89|        target_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 90|        peak_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 91|    ) -> float:
 92|        return self.total_it_power(servers) + self.calculate_cooling_ene
   |    rgy(
 93|            servers, ambient_temp, target_temp, peak_temp
 94|        )
 95|
 96|    def get_pue(
 97|        self,
 98|        servers: List[Server],
 99|        ambient_temp: float,
100|        target_temp: Optional[float] = None,
101|        peak_temp: Optional[float] = None,
102|    ) -> Optional[float]:
103|        """Return the PUE, or None when there is no IT load to divide by
   |    .
104|
105|        Returning None keeps "the room only draws standby power" visible
   |     in the
106|        output instead of masking it as a plausible-looking 1.8.
107|        """
108|        it_power = self.total_it_power(servers)
109|        if it_power <= 0:
110|            return None
111|        total = it_power + self.calculate_cooling_energy(
112|            servers, ambient_temp, target_temp, peak_temp
113|        )
114|        return float(total / it_power)
115|
116|    def get_dcie(self, servers: List[Server], ambient_temp: float) -> Op
   |    tional[float]:
117|        pue = self.get_pue(servers, ambient_temp)
118|        if pue is None:
119|            return None
120|        return float(1.0 / pue)

32. src/models/server.py
================================================================================
  1|from typing import List, Optional
  2|from src.models.task import Task
  3|
  4|HEAT_TO_POWER_RATIO = 0.8
  5|DEFAULT_DISSIPATION = 8.0
  6|DEFAULT_THERMAL_INERTIA = 0.25
  7|
  8|
  9|class Server:
 10|    """A single machine.
 11|
 12|    Thermal behaviour is a first-order lag towards an equilibrium that d
   |    epends
 13|    on the generated heat, the outdoor temperature and how hard the fans
   |     run:
 14|
 15|        equilibrium = ambient + heat / (dissipation * cooling_efficiency
   |    )
 16|
 17|    With the nominal coefficients this keeps a fully loaded standard ser
   |    ver
 18|    around 45 C at 25 C ambient, which is a realistic band; the previous
 19|    linear model pinned every loaded server at ``max_temp`` within hours
   |    .
 20|    """
 21|
 22|    def __init__(
 23|        self,
 24|        server_id: int,
 25|        max_load: float = 100,
 26|        max_temp: float = 85,
 27|        base_power: float = 100,
 28|        max_power: float = 300,
 29|        initial_temp: float = 25,
 30|        server_type: str = "standard",
 31|        idle_power_factor: float = 0.6,
 32|        dissipation_coefficient: float = DEFAULT_DISSIPATION,
 33|        thermal_inertia: float = DEFAULT_THERMAL_INERTIA,
 34|    ) -> None:
 35|        self.server_id: int = server_id
 36|        self.max_load: float = max_load
 37|        self.max_temp: float = max_temp
 38|        self.base_power: float = base_power
 39|        self.max_power: float = max_power
 40|        self.idle_power_factor: float = float(idle_power_factor)
 41|        self.dissipation_coefficient: float = float(dissipation_coeffici
   |    ent)
 42|        self.thermal_inertia: float = float(thermal_inertia)
 43|        self.current_temp: float = initial_temp
 44|        self.current_load: float = 0
 45|        self.server_type: str = server_type
 46|        self.last_ambient: float = initial_temp
 47|        self.last_fan_speed: float = 0.5
 48|        self.tasks: List[Task] = []
 49|
 51|    def assign_task(self, task: Task) -> bool:
 52|        if self.current_load + task.load <= self.max_load:
 53|            self.current_load += task.load
 54|            self.tasks.append(task)
 55|            return True
 56|        return False
 57|
 58|    def remove_task(self, task: Task) -> bool:
 59|        if task in self.tasks:
 60|            self.tasks.remove(task)
 61|            self.current_load -= task.load
 62|            return True
 63|        return False
 64|
 65|    def is_overloaded(self) -> bool:
 66|        return self.current_load >= self.max_load * 0.95
 67|
 68|    def is_overheated(self) -> bool:
 69|        return self.current_temp >= self.max_temp * 0.95
 70|
 71|    def utilization(self) -> float:
 72|        return self.current_load / self.max_load
 73|
 75|    def calculate_power(self) -> float:
 76|        """Instantaneous draw.
 77|
 78|        An idle server still draws a fraction of its base power, which i
   |    s what
 79|        makes consolidation (fewer servers kept busy) actually save ener
   |    gy.
 80|        """
 81|        if self.current_load <= 0:
 82|            return self.base_power * self.idle_power_factor
 83|        load_factor = self.current_load / self.max_load
 84|        return self.base_power + (self.max_power - self.base_power) * lo
   |    ad_factor
 85|
 86|    def heat_generation(self, load: Optional[float] = None) -> float:
 87|        current = self.current_load if load is None else load
 88|        load_factor = current / self.max_load
 89|        return float(load_factor * (self.max_power - self.base_power) * 
   |    HEAT_TO_POWER_RATIO)
 90|
 92|    def equilibrium_temp(self, ambient_temp: float, cooling_efficiency: 
   |    float) -> float:
 93|        efficiency = max(0.05, float(cooling_efficiency))
 94|        return ambient_temp + self.heat_generation() / (
 95|            self.dissipation_coefficient * efficiency
 96|        )
 97|
 98|    def update_temperature(self, ambient_temp: float, cooling_efficiency
   |    : float = 0.5) -> None:
 99|        self.last_ambient = ambient_temp
100|        self.last_fan_speed = cooling_efficiency
101|        target = self.equilibrium_temp(ambient_temp, cooling_efficiency)
102|        self.current_temp = min(
103|            self.max_temp,
104|            max(min(ambient_temp, target), self.current_temp + self.ther
   |    mal_inertia * (target - self.current_temp)),
105|        )
106|
107|    def predicted_temp_after(self, additional_load: float) -> float:
108|        """Conservative upper bound on the temperature after adding a ta
   |    sk.
109|
110|        Uses half the nominal dissipation so the estimate errs on the sa
   |    fe side.
111|        """
112|        heat = self.heat_generation(self.current_load + additional_load)
113|        degraded = self.dissipation_coefficient * 0.5
114|        target = self.last_ambient + heat / degraded
115|        return float(min(self.max_temp, max(self.current_temp, target)))
116|
117|    def __repr__(self) -> str:
118|        return (
119|            f"Server(id={self.server_id}, type={self.server_type}, "
120|            f"load={self.current_load:.1f}, temp={self.current_temp:.1f}
   |    )"
121|        )

================================================================================
33. src/models/task.py
================================================================================
  1|import uuid
  2|from typing import Optional
  3|
  4|class Task:
  5|    def __init__(
  6|        self,
  7|        load: int = 10,
  8|        priority: int = 1,
  9|        deadline: Optional[int] = None,
 10|        arrival_time: int = 0,
 11|        workload_class: str = "cpu_bound",
 12|        duration: int = 0,
 13|    ) -> None:
 14|        self.task_id: str = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
 15|        self.load: int = load
 16|        self.priority: int = priority
 17|        self.deadline: Optional[int] = deadline
 18|        self.arrival_time: int = arrival_time
 19|        self.workload_class: str = workload_class
 20|        self.duration: int = int(duration)
 21|        self.remaining_hours: float = float(duration)
 22|        self.created_time: int = 0
 23|        self.start_time: Optional[int] = None
 24|        self.completed_time: Optional[int] = None
 25|
 26|    def is_completed(self) -> bool:
 27|        return self.completed_time is not None
 28|
 29|    def get_waiting_time(self, current_time: int) -> int:
 30|        if self.start_time is None:
 31|            return current_time - self.created_time
 32|        return self.start_time - self.created_time
 33|
 34|    def is_deadline_violated(self, current_time: int) -> bool:
 35|        """Whether the deadline had already passed at ``current_time``.
 36|
 37|        This is an assignment-time check. The authoritative SLA metric i
   |    s
 38|        computed after completion via ``missed_deadline``.
 39|        """
 40|        if self.deadline is None:
 41|            return False
 42|        return current_time > self.deadline
 43|
 44|    def missed_deadline(self, last_hour: int) -> bool:
 45|        """Whether the task failed to meet its deadline within the horiz
   |    on.
 46|
 47|        A task counts as a miss when it finished after its deadline, or 
   |    when it
 48|        never finished although the deadline has already elapsed.
 49|        """
 50|        if self.deadline is None:
 51|            return False
 52|        if self.completed_time is not None:
 53|            return self.completed_time > self.deadline
 54|        return self.deadline <= last_hour
 55|
 56|    def service_hour(self, amount: float = 1.0) -> float:
 57|        """Consume one hour of execution service and return what is left
   |    ."""
 58|        self.remaining_hours = max(0.0, self.remaining_hours - amount)
 59|        return self.remaining_hours
 60|
 61|    def is_service_complete(self) -> bool:
 62|        return self.remaining_hours <= 0.0
 63|
 64|    @property
 65|    def slack_hours(self) -> Optional[int]:
 66|        if self.deadline is None:
 67|            return None
 68|        return self.deadline - self.arrival_time
 69|
 70|    def __repr__(self) -> str:
 71|        return (
 72|            f"Task({self.task_id}, load={self.load}, priority={self.prio
   |    rity}, "
 73|            f"class={self.workload_class}, deadline={self.deadline}, "
 74|            f"duration={self.duration})"
 75|        )

34. src/models/temperature_model.py
================================================================================
  1|import numpy as np
  2|from typing import List, Optional
  3|from src.models.server import Server
  4|
  5|class TemperatureModel:
  6|    """Room-level bookkeeping and the fan speed control law."""
  7|
  8|    def __init__(
  9|        self,
 10|        room_volume: float = 100,
 11|        air_flow_rate: float = 10,
 12|        initial_temp: float = 25,
 13|        fan_target_temp: float = 35,
 14|    ) -> None:
 15|        self.room_volume: float = room_volume
 16|        self.air_flow_rate: float = air_flow_rate
 17|        self.current_temp: float = initial_temp
 18|        self.target_temp: float = fan_target_temp
 19|
 20|    def update_room_temperature(self, servers: List[Server], ambient_tem
   |    p: float, cooling_power: float) -> float:
 21|        total_heat = sum(s.calculate_power() * 0.8 for s in servers)
 22|        heat_removed = cooling_power * 0.9
 23|        heat_change = total_heat - heat_removed
 24|        temp_change = heat_change / (1000 * 1.2 * self.room_volume)
 25|        self.current_temp = max(
 26|            ambient_temp - 5,
 27|            min(ambient_temp + 20, self.current_temp + temp_change)
 28|        )
 29|        return self.current_temp
 30|
 31|    def get_cooling_demand(self) -> float:
 32|        delta = self.current_temp - self.target_temp
 33|        if delta <= 0:
 34|            return 0.0
 35|        return delta * 100
 36|
 37|    def adjust_fan_speed(
 38|        self,
 39|        target_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 40|        reference_temp: Optional[float] = None,
 41|    ) -> float:
 42|        """Fan speed in [0.2, 1.0] driven by the hottest machine.
 43|
 44|        ``reference_temp`` lets the caller feed in the peak server tempe
   |    rature;
 45|        without it the room temperature is used.
 46|        """
 47|        if target_temp is not None:
 48|            self.target_temp = float(target_temp)
 49|
 50|        current = self.current_temp if reference_temp is None else float
   |    (reference_temp)
 51|        delta = current - self.target_temp
 52|
 53|        if delta < -2:
 54|            return 0.2
 55|        elif delta < 0:
 56|            return 0.4
 57|        elif delta < 2:
 58|            return 0.6
 59|        elif delta < 5:
 60|            return 0.8
 61|        else:
 62|            return 1.0
