36. src/plugin/base.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|插件抽象基类 v1.0 — 标准化接口规范
  3|
  4|所有插件必须继承此基类并实现其抽象方法。
  5|
  6|核心生命周期:
  7|    __init__ → initialize(config) → [pre_execute → execute → post_execut
   |    e]×N → cleanup
  8|
  9|增强功能 (新增):
 10|    - PluginResult 统一返回类型
 11|    - 执行前后钩子 (pre_execute / post_execute)
 12|    - 错误处理钩子 (on_error)
 13|    - 事件回调注册系统
 14|    - 配置验证 (validate_config)
 15|    - 运行状态查询 (get_status)
 16|    - 插件清单 (get_manifest) 供第三方发现
 17|
 18|用法示例:
 19|
 20|    class MyPlugin(PluginBase):
 21|        def get_manifest(self):
 22|            return PluginManifest(
 23|                name="MyPlugin",
 24|                version="1.0.0",
 25|                supported_operations=["process", "analyze"],
 26|                input_schema={"data": "list"},
 27|                output_schema={"result": "dict"}
 28|            )
 29|
 30|        def initialize(self, config):
 31|            self.threshold = config.get("threshold", 0.5)
 32|            return True
 33|
 34|        def execute(self, **kwargs):
 35|            data = kwargs.get("data", [])
 36|            processed = [x for x in data if x > self.threshold]
 37|            return PluginResult.ok(data=processed, message="处理完成")
 38|"""
 39|
 40|from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
 41|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
 42|import time
 43|from datetime import datetime
 44|
 45|from src.plugin.protocol import (
 46|    PluginResult, PluginInfo, PluginManifest, PluginStatus,
 47|    PluginPriority, PluginEvent, PluginCallback, PluginConfig,
 48|    PluginContext
 49|)
 50|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 51|
 52|
 53|class PluginBase(ABC):
 54|    """
 55|    插件抽象基类
 56|
 57|    接口契约:
 58|        必须实现: get_manifest, initialize, execute, cleanup
 59|        可选覆盖: pre_execute, post_execute, on_error, validate_config
 60|
 61|    所有插件的 execute() 必须返回 PluginResult 实例。
 62|    """
 63|
 64|    def __init__(self):
 65|        self.logger = setup_logger(self.__class__.__name__)
 66|        self._status = PluginStatus.REGISTERED
 67|        self._context: PluginContext = {}
 68|        self._config: PluginConfig = {}
 69|        self._callbacks: Dict[str, List[PluginCallback]] = {}
 70|        self._execution_count: int = 0
 71|        self._total_execution_time_ms: float = 0.0
 72|        self._last_error: Optional[str] = None
 73|        self._loaded_at: Optional[str] = None
 74|
 76|    # 必须实现的抽象方法
 78|
 79|    @abstractmethod
 80|    def get_manifest(self) -> PluginManifest:
 81|        """
 82|        返回插件清单 — 描述插件的能力、接口和兼容性
 83|
 84|        第三方应用通过此清单:
 85|        - 发现可调用的操作
 86|        - 了解输入参数格式
 87|        - 确认版本兼容性
 88|
 89|        Returns:
 90|            PluginManifest: 包含名称/版本/操作列表/参数schema的完整清单
 91|        """
 92|        pass
 93|
 94|    @abstractmethod
 95|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[PluginConfig] = None) -> bool:
 96|        """
 97|        初始化插件 — 分配资源、验证配置、建立连接
 98|
 99|        调用时机: 插件加载时由 PluginManager 调用
100|        调用频率: 每次 load_plugin 调用一次
101|
102|        Args:
103|            config: 配置字典，包含插件运行所需参数
104|                    传入 None 表示使用默认配置
105|
106|        Returns:
107|            bool: True 表示初始化成功，False 表示失败
108|
109|        最佳实践:
110|        - 在此方法中进行资源分配（文件、网络、数据库连接）
111|        - 验证 config 中的关键参数
112|        - 失败时记录详细日志
113|        """
114|        pass
115|
116|    @abstractmethod
117|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> PluginResult:
118|        """
119|        执行插件核心业务逻辑
120|
121|        调用时机: 插件加载后，由 PluginManager.execute_plugin() 调用
122|        调用频率: 可多次调用
123|
124|        Args:
125|            **kwargs: 可变关键字参数，由调用方传入
126|
127|        Returns:
128|            PluginResult: 统一返回格式，包含:
129|                - success: 执行是否成功
130|                - data: 业务产出数据
131|                - message: 状态描述
132|                - error_code: 错误码
133|
134|        注意:
135|        - **绝对不要**抛出未捕获异常
136|        - 所有错误通过 PluginResult.fail() 返回
137|        - 构造函数中已分配 logger，使用 self.logger 记录日志
138|        """
139|        pass
140|
141|    @abstractmethod
142|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
143|        """
144|        清理插件资源 — 释放所有分配的资源
145|
146|        调用时机: 插件卸载时由 PluginManager 调用
147|        调用频率: 一次
148|
149|        要求:
150|        - 幂等性: 多次调用不会出错
151|        - 防御性: 即使资源未初始化也不会崩溃
152|        - 彻底性: 释放所有数据库连接/文件句柄/线程池等
153|        """
154|        pass
155|
157|    # 可选覆盖的钩子方法 (Hook Methods)
159|
160|    def validate_config(self, config: PluginConfig) -> bool:
161|        """
162|        验证配置参数是否符合插件要求
163|
164|        可覆盖此方法实现自定义验证逻辑:
165|        - 检查必填参数是否存在
166|        - 验证参数取值范围
167|        - 确认参数类型正确
168|
169|        Args:
170|            config: 待验证的配置字典
171|
172|        Returns:
173|            True 表示配置有效，False 表示配置无效
174|
175|        示例:
176|            def validate_config(self, config):
177|                if 'num_servers' not in config:
178|                    self.logger.error("缺少必填参数: num_servers")
179|                    return False
180|                if config['num_servers'] < 1 or config['num_servers'] > 
   |    1000:
181|                    self.logger.error("num_servers 必须在 [1, 1000] 范围
   |    内")
182|                    return False
183|                return True
184|        """
185|        return True
186|
187|    def pre_execute(self, **kwargs) -> Optional[PluginResult]:
188|        """
189|        执行前钩子 — 在主逻辑执行前调用
190|
191|        可用于:
192|        - 参数预处理和验证
193|        - 设置执行上下文
194|        - 记录执行开始日志
195|
196|        Args:
197|            **kwargs: 传入 execute() 的相同参数
198|
199|        Returns:
200|            None 表示继续执行
201|            PluginResult 表示短路跳过后续执行（如参数验证失败）
202|        """
203|        return None
204|
205|    def post_execute(self, result: PluginResult, **kwargs) -> PluginResu
   |    lt:
206|        """
207|        执行后钩子 — 在主逻辑执行后调用
208|
209|        可用于:
210|        - 结果后处理
211|        - 记录执行完成日志
212|        - 更新统计指标
213|
214|        Args:
215|            result: execute() 返回的原始结果
216|            **kwargs: 传入 execute() 的相同参数
217|
218|        Returns:
219|            PluginResult: 处理后的结果（可修改原结果或返回新结果）
220|        """
221|        return result
222|
223|    def on_error(self, error: Exception, operation: str) -> PluginResult
   |    :
224|        """
225|        错误处理钩子 — 当操作发生异常时调用
226|
227|        Args:
228|            error: 发生的异常对象
229|            operation: 发生异常的操作名称 (如 'initialize', 'execute', '
   |    cleanup')
230|
231|        Returns:
232|            PluginResult: 封装错误信息的失败结果
233|
234|        默认实现:
235|        - 记录详细错误日志
236|        - 返回包含错误信息的 PluginResult.fail()
237|        """
238|        self._last_error = str(error)
239|        self.logger.error(f"[{operation}] 异常: {error}", exc_info=True)
240|        return PluginResult.fail(
241|            message=f"{operation} 执行异常",
242|            error_code=f"{operation.upper()}_ERROR",
243|            error_detail=str(error)
244|        )
245|
247|    # 事件回调系统
249|
250|    def on(self, event_type: str, callback: PluginCallback) -> None:
251|        """
252|        注册事件回调
253|
254|        Args:
255|            event_type: 事件类型 (如 'STATE_CHANGED', 'METRICS_UPDATED')
256|            callback: 回调函数，接收 PluginEvent 参数
257|        """
258|        if event_type not in self._callbacks:
259|            self._callbacks[event_type] = []
260|        self._callbacks[event_type].append(callback)
261|
262|    def off(self, event_type: str, callback: PluginCallback) -> None:
263|        """
264|        注销事件回调
265|
266|        Args:
267|            event_type: 事件类型
268|            callback: 要注销的回调函数
269|        """
270|        if event_type in self._callbacks:
271|            self._callbacks[event_type] = [
272|                cb for cb in self._callbacks[event_type] if cb != callba
   |    ck
273|            ]
274|
275|    def emit(self, event_type: str, data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = Non
   |    e,
276|             result: Optional[PluginResult] = None) -> None:
277|        """
278|        触发事件 — 通知所有注册的回调函数
279|
280|        Args:
281|            event_type: 事件类型
282|            data: 随事件传递的附加数据
283|            result: 关联的 PluginResult（如果有）
284|        """
285|        event = PluginEvent(
286|            event_type=event_type,
287|            plugin_name=self.get_manifest().name,
288|            data=data,
289|            result=result
290|        )
291|        callbacks = self._callbacks.get(event_type, [])
292|        for cb in callbacks:
293|            try:
294|                cb(event)
295|            except Exception as e:
296|                self.logger.warning(f"事件回调异常 [{event_type}]: {e}")
297|
299|    # 状态查询方法
301|
302|    def get_status(self) -> PluginStatus:
303|        """获取插件当前运行状态"""
304|        return self._status
305|
306|    def get_info(self) -> PluginInfo:
307|        """
308|        获取插件完整注册信息
309|
310|        Returns:
311|            PluginInfo: 包含名称/版本/状态/统计等完整信息的结构体
312|        """
313|        manifest = self.get_manifest()
314|        return PluginInfo(
315|            name=manifest.name,
316|            version=manifest.version,
317|            description=manifest.supported_operations[0] if manifest.sup
   |    ported_operations else "",
318|            status=self._status,
319|            last_loaded_at=self._loaded_at,
320|            last_executed_at=datetime.now().isoformat() if self._executi
   |    on_count > 0 else None,
321|            execution_count=self._execution_count
322|        )
323|
324|    def get_config(self) -> PluginConfig:
325|        """获取当前生效的配置（只读）"""
326|        return dict(self._config)
327|
328|    def get_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
329|        """
330|        获取插件运行指标
331|
332|        Returns:
333|            dict: 包含 total_executions, avg_execution_time_ms, last_err
   |    or 等指标
334|        """
335|        avg_time = (self._total_execution_time_ms / self._execution_coun
   |    t
336|                    if self._execution_count > 0 else 0)
337|        return {
338|            "plugin_name": self.get_manifest().name,
339|            "status": self._status.name,
340|            "total_executions": self._execution_count,
341|            "avg_execution_time_ms": round(avg_time, 2),
342|            "total_execution_time_ms": round(self._total_execution_time_
   |    ms, 2),
343|            "last_error": self._last_error
344|        }
345|
347|    # 内部辅助方法
349|
350|    def _set_status(self, status: PluginStatus) -> None:
351|        """更新状态并发送状态变更事件"""
352|        old_status = self._status
353|        self._status = status
354|        self.emit("STATE_CHANGED", {
355|            "old_status": old_status.name,
356|            "new_status": status.name
357|        })
358|
359|    def _execute_wrapper(self, **kwargs) -> PluginResult:
360|        """
361|        执行包装器 — 统一的执行流程
362|
363|        流程:
364|            1. 检查状态 (必须 READY)
365|            2. 调用 pre_execute 钩子
366|            3. 如果 pre_execute 返回非 None，直接返回
367|            4. 调用 execute 并计时
368|            5. 调用 post_execute 钩子
369|            6. 返回最终结果
370|        """
371|        if self._status != PluginStatus.READY:
372|            return PluginResult.fail(
373|                message=f"插件未就绪 (当前状态: {self._status.name})",
374|                error_code="INVALID_STATE"
375|            )
376|
377|        short_circuit = self.pre_execute(**kwargs)
378|        if short_circuit is not None:
379|            return short_circuit
380|
381|        self._set_status(PluginStatus.RUNNING)
382|        self.emit("PLUGIN_STARTED", {"kwargs_keys": list(kwargs.keys())}
   |    )
383|
384|        start = time.perf_counter()
385|        result = PluginResult.fail("未执行")
386|        try:
387|            result = self.execute(**kwargs)
388|            if not isinstance(result, PluginResult):
389|                result = PluginResult.ok(data=result, message="执行完成"
   |    )
390|        except Exception as e:
391|            result = self.on_error(e, "execute")
392|        finally:
393|            elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000
394|            result.execution_time_ms = round(elapsed, 2)
395|            self._execution_count += 1
396|            self._total_execution_time_ms += elapsed
397|            self._set_status(PluginStatus.READY)
398|
399|        result = self.post_execute(result, **kwargs)
400|        self.emit("PLUGIN_COMPLETED", result=result)
401|        return result

================================================================================
37. src/plugin/manager.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|插件管理器 v1.0 — 标准化统一调用机制
  3|
  4|核心职责:
  5|    - 插件注册/发现/加载/卸载 (生命周期管理)
  6|    - 统一执行入口 (基于 _execute_wrapper 的标准流程)
  7|    - 依赖解析与拓扑排序加载
  8|    - 事件总线 (插件间通信)
  9|    - 并发执行控制 (线程安全)
 10|    - 状态监控与指标收集
 11|    - 热重载支持
 12|
 13|线程安全:
 14|    使用 threading.RLock 保护所有共享状态。
 15|
 16|第三方应用调用示例:
 17|
 18|    pm = PluginManager()
 19|
 20|    # 1. 注册插件
 21|    pm.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin, priority=PluginPriority.HIGH)
 22|
 23|    # 2. 加载插件
 24|    pm.load_plugin("SimulationPlugin", config={"num_servers": 20})
 25|
 26|    # 3. 执行插件 (统一入口)
 27|    result = pm.execute_plugin("SimulationPlugin", algorithms=["greedy",
   |     "genetic"])
 28|
 29|    # 4. 检查结果
 30|    if result.success:
 31|        print(f"完成: {result.message}")
 32|        print(f"数据: {result.data}")
 33|    else:
 34|        print(f"失败 [{result.error_code}]: {result.message}")
 35|
 36|    # 5. 卸载
 37|    pm.unload_plugin("SimulationPlugin")
 38|"""
 39|
 40|import importlib
 41|import os
 42|import threading
 43|import time
 44|from typing import Dict, List, Type, Optional, Set
 45|from datetime import datetime
 46|
 47|from src.plugin.base import PluginBase
 48|from src.plugin.protocol import (
 49|    PluginResult, PluginInfo, PluginManifest, PluginStatus,
 50|    PluginPriority, PluginEvent, PluginCallback, PluginConfig
 51|)
 52|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 53|
 54|# Fail-closed list: modules that stay on disk but are not discovered.
 55|# rl_ppo_scheduler needs gymnasium + stable-baselines3 + a trained model
   |    .
 56|DISABLED_PLUGIN_MODULES = {"rl_ppo_scheduler"}
 57|
 58|
 59|class PluginManager:
 60|    """
 61|    插件管理器
 62|
 63|    状态管理:
 64|        每个插件通过 _plugin_instances 和 _plugin_infos 追踪全生命周期。
 65|
 66|    事件系统:
 67|        通过 subscribe / unsubscribe / _emit_global 实现全局事件总线，
 68|        插件间可通过事件解耦通信。
 69|    """
 70|
 71|    def __init__(self):
 72|        self._logger = setup_logger("plugin_manager")
 73|        self._lock = threading.RLock()
 74|        self._plugin_classes: Dict[str, Type[PluginBase]] = {}
 75|        self._plugin_instances: Dict[str, PluginBase] = {}
 76|        self._plugin_infos: Dict[str, PluginInfo] = {}
 77|        self._plugin_priorities: Dict[str, PluginPriority] = {}
 78|        self._global_callbacks: Dict[str, List[PluginCallback]] = {}
 79|        self._metrics: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
 80|        self._started_at: str = datetime.now().isoformat()
 81|
 83|    # 插件注册
 85|
 86|    def register_plugin(self, plugin_class: Type[PluginBase],
 87|                        priority: PluginPriority = PluginPriority.NORMAL
   |    ,
 88|                        dependencies: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 89|        """
 90|        注册一个插件类到管理器
 91|
 92|        Args:
 93|            plugin_class: 继承自 PluginBase 的插件类
 94|            priority: 执行优先级 (默认 NORMAL)
 95|            dependencies: 依赖的其他插件名称列表
 96|
 97|        Returns:
 98|            True 注册成功
 99|
100|        Raises:
101|            TypeError: 如果 plugin_class 不是 PluginBase 的子类
102|        """
103|        if not issubclass(plugin_class, PluginBase):
104|            raise TypeError(f"{plugin_class.__name__} 必须继承 PluginBas
   |    e")
105|
106|        manifest = plugin_class().get_manifest()
107|        name = manifest.name
108|
109|        with self._lock:
110|            self._plugin_classes[name] = plugin_class
111|            self._plugin_priorities[name] = priority
112|
113|            info = PluginInfo(
114|                name=name,
115|                version=manifest.version,
116|                description=",".join(manifest.supported_operations),
117|                status=PluginStatus.REGISTERED,
118|                priority=priority,
119|                dependencies=dependencies or [],
120|                config_schema=manifest.input_schema,
121|                tags=manifest.supported_operations,
122|                registered_at=datetime.now().isoformat()
123|            )
124|            self._plugin_infos[name] = info
125|
126|        self._logger.info(f"插件已注册: {name} v{manifest.version} (优先
   |    级: {priority.name})")
127|        self._emit_global("PLUGIN_REGISTERED", {"name": name, "version":
   |     manifest.version})
128|        return True
129|
131|    # 插件加载
133|
134|    def load_plugin(self, name: str, config: Optional[PluginConfig] = No
   |    ne) -> bool:
135|        """
136|        加载并初始化指定插件
137|
138|        流程:
139|            1. 检查是否已注册
140|            2. 解析并加载依赖插件
141|            3. 验证配置参数
142|            4. 实例化插件类
143|            5. 调用 initialize() 初始化
144|
145|        Args:
146|            name: 插件名称
147|            config: 配置参数字典
148|
149|        Returns:
150|            True 加载成功
151|        """
152|        with self._lock:
153|            if name not in self._plugin_classes:
154|                self._logger.error(f"加载失败: 插件 '{name}' 未注册")
155|                return False
156|
157|            if name in self._plugin_instances:
158|                self._logger.warning(f"插件 '{name}' 已加载，跳过重复加载
   |    ")
159|                return True
160|
161|            info = self._plugin_infos[name]
162|
163|            # 解析依赖
164|            for dep_name in info.dependencies:
165|                if dep_name not in self._plugin_instances:
166|                    self._logger.info(f"加载依赖插件: {dep_name} → {name
   |    }")
167|                    if not self.load_plugin(dep_name):
168|                        self._logger.error(f"依赖插件加载失败: {dep_name
   |    }")
169|                        return False
170|
171|            # 实例化
172|            try:
173|                plugin_class = self._plugin_classes[name]
174|                plugin = plugin_class()
175|                info.status = PluginStatus.INITIALIZING
176|                self._plugin_infos[name] = info
177|            except Exception as e:
178|                return self._handle_load_error(name, e, "实例化", info)
179|
180|            # 配置验证
181|            config = config or {}
182|            if not plugin.validate_config(config):
183|                self._logger.error(f"加载失败: 插件 '{name}' 配置验证不
   |    通过")
184|                info.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
185|                self._plugin_infos[name] = info
186|                return False
187|            plugin._config = config
188|
189|            # 初始化
190|            try:
191|                if not plugin.initialize(config):
192|                    info.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
193|                    self._plugin_infos[name] = info
194|                    self._logger.error(f"初始化失败: 插件 '{name}'")
195|                    return False
196|            except Exception as e:
197|                return self._handle_load_error(name, e, "初始化", info)
198|
199|            plugin._status = PluginStatus.READY
200|            plugin._loaded_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
201|            self._plugin_instances[name] = plugin
202|            info.status = PluginStatus.READY
203|            info.last_loaded_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
204|            self._plugin_infos[name] = info
205|
206|            self._logger.info(f"插件已加载: {name} (状态: READY)")
207|            self._emit_global("PLUGIN_INITIALIZED", {"name": name})
208|            return True
209|
210|    def _handle_load_error(self, name: str, error: Exception,
211|                           stage: str, info: PluginInfo) -> bool:
212|        info.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
213|        self._plugin_infos[name] = info
214|        self._logger.error(f"{stage}失败 [{name}]: {error}", exc_info=Tr
   |    ue)
215|        self._emit_global("PLUGIN_ERROR", {"name": name, "stage": stage,
   |     "error": str(error)})
216|        return False
217|
219|    # 插件执行 (统一入口)
221|
222|    def execute_plugin(self, name: str, **kwargs) -> Optional[PluginResu
   |    lt]:
223|        """
224|        执行插件 — 统一的第三方调用入口
225|
226|        使用 _execute_wrapper 确保一致的执行流程:
227|            pre_execute → execute → post_execute → 计时 → 指标收集
228|
229|        Args:
230|            name: 插件名称
231|            **kwargs: 传递给 execute() 的参数
232|
233|        Returns:
234|            PluginResult: 标准化执行结果
235|
236|        用法:
237|            result = pm.execute_plugin("SimulationPlugin", algorithms=["
   |    greedy"])
238|            if result.success:
239|                print(result.data['statistics'])
240|        """
241|        with self._lock:
242|            if name not in self._plugin_instances:
243|                self._logger.error(f"执行失败: 插件 '{name}' 未加载")
244|                return PluginResult.fail(
245|                    message=f"插件 '{name}' 未加载",
246|                    error_code="PLUGIN_NOT_LOADED"
247|                )
248|
249|            plugin = self._plugin_instances[name]
250|
251|        result = plugin._execute_wrapper(**kwargs)
252|
253|        plugin_info = self._plugin_infos.get(name)
254|        if plugin_info:
255|            plugin_info.execution_count = plugin._execution_count
256|            plugin_info.last_executed_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
257|
258|        self._logger.info(
259|            f"插件执行完成: {name} "
260|            f"({'成功' if result.success else '失败'}) "
261|            f"[{result.execution_time_ms:.0f}ms]"
262|        )
263|        self._emit_global("METRICS_UPDATED", {"name": name, "metrics": p
   |    lugin.get_metrics()})
264|        return result
265|
267|    # 插件卸载
269|
270|    def unload_plugin(self, name: str) -> bool:
271|        """
272|        卸载指定插件并释放资源
273|
274|        Args:
275|            name: 插件名称
276|
277|        Returns:
278|            True 卸载成功
279|        """
280|        with self._lock:
281|            if name not in self._plugin_instances:
282|                self._logger.warning(f"卸载跳过: 插件 '{name}' 未加载")
283|                return False
284|
285|            plugin = self._plugin_instances[name]
286|            try:
287|                plugin._set_status(PluginStatus.STOPPING)
288|                plugin.cleanup()
289|                plugin._set_status(PluginStatus.STOPPED)
290|            except Exception as e:
291|                self._logger.error(f"清理异常 [{name}]: {e}")
292|
293|            del self._plugin_instances[name]
294|            if name in self._plugin_infos:
295|                self._plugin_infos[name].status = PluginStatus.STOPPED
296|
297|            self._logger.info(f"插件已卸载: {name}")
298|            self._emit_global("PLUGIN_STOPPED", {"name": name})
299|            return True
300|
302|    # 插件查询
304|
305|    def get_plugin(self, name: str) -> Optional[PluginBase]:
306|        """获取已加载的插件实例"""
307|        with self._lock:
308|            return self._plugin_instances.get(name)
309|
310|    def get_plugin_info(self, name: str) -> Optional[PluginInfo]:
311|        """获取插件注册信息"""
312|        with self._lock:
313|            return self._plugin_infos.get(name)
314|
315|    def list_plugins(self) -> List[Dict]:
316|        """列出所有已注册插件的元信息"""
317|        with self._lock:
318|            result = []
319|            for name, info in self._plugin_infos.items():
320|                d = info.to_dict()
321|                d["loaded"] = name in self._plugin_instances
322|                d["priority"] = self._plugin_priorities.get(name, Plugin
   |    Priority.NORMAL).name
323|                result.append(d)
324|            return result
325|
326|    def list_loaded_plugins(self) -> List[str]:
327|        """列出所有已加载插件的名称"""
328|        with self._lock:
329|            return list(self._plugin_instances.keys())
330|
331|    def get_plugin_metrics(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
332|        """获取插件运行指标"""
333|        plugin = self.get_plugin(name)
334|        if plugin:
335|            return plugin.get_metrics()
336|        return None
337|
338|    def get_all_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
339|        """获取所有插件的运行指标"""
340|        with self._lock:
341|            return {name: p.get_metrics() for name, p in self._plugin_in
   |    stances.items()}
342|
344|    # 插件配置管理
346|
347|    def get_plugin_config(self, name: str) -> Optional[PluginConfig]:
348|        """获取插件当前配置"""
349|        plugin = self.get_plugin(name)
350|        return plugin.get_config() if plugin else None
351|
352|    def update_plugin_config(self, name: str, config_update: PluginConfi
   |    g) -> bool:
353|        """
354|        更新插件配置 (需重新加载生效)
355|
356|        Args:
357|            name: 插件名称
358|            config_update: 要更新的配置 (合并到现有配置)
359|
360|        Returns:
361|            True 更新成功
362|        """
363|        with self._lock:
364|            if name not in self._plugin_infos:
365|                return False
366|            plugin = self._plugin_instances.get(name)
367|            if plugin:
368|                merged = dict(plugin._config)
369|                merged.update(config_update)
370|                plugin._config = merged
371|                if not plugin.validate_config(merged):
372|                    self._logger.warning(f"配置更新后验证失败: {name}")
373|                    return False
374|            self._logger.info(f"配置已更新: {name}")
375|            return True
376|
378|    # 热重载
380|
381|    def reload_plugin(self, name: str) -> bool:
382|        """
383|        热重载插件 (卸载后重新加载，保留配置)
384|
385|        Args:
386|            name: 插件名称
387|
388|        Returns:
389|            True 重载成功
390|        """
391|        with self._lock:
392|            config = self.get_plugin_config(name)
393|            self.unload_plugin(name)
394|            time.sleep(0.1)
395|            return self.load_plugin(name, config)
396|
398|    # 批量操作
400|
401|    def load_all(self, configs: Optional[Dict[str, PluginConfig]] = None
   |    ) -> Dict[str, bool]:
402|        """
403|        加载所有已注册的插件
404|
405|        Args:
406|            configs: 各插件的配置字典 {name: config}
407|
408|        Returns:
409|            {name: success} 加载结果映射
410|        """
411|        results = {}
412|        with self._lock:
413|            names = list(self._plugin_classes.keys())
414|        for name in names:
415|            cfg = (configs or {}).get(name)
416|            results[name] = self.load_plugin(name, cfg)
417|        return results
418|
419|    def unload_all(self) -> None:
420|        """卸载所有已加载插件"""
421|        with self._lock:
422|            names = list(self._plugin_instances.keys())
423|        for name in reversed(names):
424|            self.unload_plugin(name)
425|
426|    def execute_all(self, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, PluginResult]:
427|        """
428|        按优先级顺序执行所有已加载插件
429|
430|        Args:
431|            **kwargs: 传递给各插件的公共参数
432|
433|        Returns:
434|            {name: PluginResult} 执行结果映射
435|        """
436|        results = {}
437|        with self._lock:
438|            items = [(name, self._plugin_priorities.get(name, PluginPrio
   |    rity.NORMAL).value)
439|                     for name in self._plugin_instances.keys()]
440|        items.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
441|
442|        for name, _ in items:
443|            results[name] = self.execute_plugin(name, **kwargs)
444|        return results
445|
447|    # 事件总线
449|
450|    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: PluginCallback) -> No
   |    ne:
451|        """
452|        订阅全局事件
453|
454|        Args:
455|            event_type: 事件类型
456|            callback: 回调函数 (PluginEvent) → None
457|
458|        用法:
459|            def on_completed(event):
460|                print(f"{event.plugin_name} 执行完成: {event.result.mess
   |    age}")
461|
462|            pm.subscribe("PLUGIN_COMPLETED", on_completed)
463|        """
464|        with self._lock:
465|            if event_type not in self._global_callbacks:
466|                self._global_callbacks[event_type] = []
467|            self._global_callbacks[event_type].append(callback)
468|
469|    def unsubscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: PluginCallback) -> 
   |    None:
470|        """取消事件订阅"""
471|        with self._lock:
472|            if event_type in self._global_callbacks:
473|                self._global_callbacks[event_type] = [
474|                    cb for cb in self._global_callbacks[event_type] if c
   |    b != callback
475|                ]
476|
477|    def _emit_global(self, event_type: str, data: Optional[Dict] = None)
   |     -> None:
478|        """触发全局事件"""
479|        event = PluginEvent(
480|            event_type=event_type,
481|            plugin_name="PluginManager",
482|            data=data
483|        )
484|        callbacks = self._global_callbacks.get(event_type, [])
485|        for cb in callbacks:
486|            try:
487|                cb(event)
488|            except Exception as e:
489|                self._logger.warning(f"全局事件回调异常 [{event_type}]: 
   |    {e}")
490|
492|    # 自动发现
494|
495|    def discover_plugins(self, plugin_dir: str = "plugins") -> int:
496|        """
497|        自动发现并注册指定目录中的插件模块
498|
499|        Args:
500|            plugin_dir: 插件目录名（相对于项目根目录），支持两种形式:
501|                - 文件路径: "plugins" / "src/plugins" / "./src/plugins"
502|                - Python 包: "src.plugins"
503|                内部统一转成文件系统路径与 Python 模块前缀。
504|
505|        Returns:
506|            int: 成功注册的插件数量
507|        """
508|        # 归一化: 去掉 ./ 前缀与尾部斜杠
509|        norm = plugin_dir.replace("\\", "/").strip("/")
510|        if norm.startswith("./"):
511|            norm = norm[2:]
512|
513|        # 推断: 含 '.' 且首段不是盘符 → 当作 Python 包; 否则文件路径
514|        is_python_package = (
515|            "." in norm
516|            and not (len(norm) >= 2 and norm[1] == ":")
517|        )
518|        if is_python_package:
519|            module_prefix = norm
520|            file_subpath = norm.replace(".", os.sep)
521|        else:
522|            file_subpath = norm
523|            module_prefix = norm.replace("/", ".").replace(os.sep, ".")
524|
525|        plugin_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".."
   |    , file_subpath)
526|        if not os.path.exists(plugin_path):
527|            self._logger.warning(f"插件目录不存在: {plugin_path}")
528|            return 0
529|
530|        count = 0
531|        disabled = set(getattr(self, "disabled_plugins", DISABLED_PLUGIN
   |    _MODULES))
532|        for filename in sorted(os.listdir(plugin_path)):
533|            if not filename.endswith(".py") or filename.startswith("_"):
534|                continue
535|            module_name = filename[:-3]
536|            if module_name in disabled:
537|                self._logger.info(f"插件已禁用，跳过: {module_name}")
538|                continue
539|            try:
540|                module = importlib.import_module(f"{module_prefix}.{modu
   |    le_name}")
541|                for attr_name in dir(module):
542|                    obj = getattr(module, attr_name)
543|                    if (isinstance(obj, type) and issubclass(obj, Plugin
   |    Base)
544|                            and obj != PluginBase
545|                            and obj.__module__ == module.__name__):
546|                        self.register_plugin(obj)
547|                        count += 1
548|            except Exception as e:
549|                self._logger.error(f"模块加载失败 {module_name}: {e}")
550|
551|        self._logger.info(f"插件发现完成: {count} 个插件已注册 (目录: {p
   |    lugin_path})")
552|        return count
553|
555|    # 依赖解析
557|
558|    def resolve_dependencies(self, name: str) -> List[str]:
559|        """
560|        解析插件的完整依赖链 (拓扑排序)
561|
562|        Args:
563|            name: 插件名称
564|
565|        Returns:
566|            List[str]: 按依赖顺序排列的插件名称列表（被依赖的在前）
567|        """
568|        resolved: List[str] = []
569|        visited: Set[str] = set()
570|        visiting: Set[str] = set()
571|
572|        def dfs(current: str):
573|            if current in visited:
574|                return
575|            if current in visiting:
576|                raise RuntimeError(f"检测到循环依赖: {current}")
577|            visiting.add(current)
578|            info = self._plugin_infos.get(current)
579|            if info:
580|                for dep in info.dependencies:
581|                    dfs(dep)
582|            visiting.discard(current)
583|            visited.add(current)
584|            if current != name:
585|                resolved.append(current)
586|
587|        try:
588|            dfs(name)
589|        except RuntimeError as e:
590|            self._logger.error(str(e))
591|            return []
592|
593|        resolved.append(name)
594|        return resolved
595|
597|    # 状态查询
599|
600|    def get_system_status(self) -> Dict:
601|        """
602|        获取整个插件系统的运行状态
603|
604|        Returns:
605|            dict: 包含已注册/已加载/错误插件数量及整体健康状态
606|        """
607|        with self._lock:
608|            total = len(self._plugin_infos)
609|            loaded = len(self._plugin_instances)
610|            errors = sum(1 for info in self._plugin_infos.values()
611|                         if info.status == PluginStatus.ERROR)
612|            healthy = (errors == 0)
613|
614|            return {
615|                "total_registered": total,
616|                "total_loaded": loaded,
617|                "status": "healthy" if healthy else "degraded",
618|                "error_count": errors,
619|                "started_at": self._started_at,
620|                "uptime_seconds": round(
621|                    (datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(self._start
   |    ed_at)).total_seconds(), 1
622|                ),
623|                "plugins": self.list_plugins()
624|            }

================================================================================
38. src/plugin/protocol.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|插件系统标准化接口协议 v1.0
  3|Plugin System Standardized Protocol & Data Types
  4|
  5|本模块定义插件系统的核心数据类型和接口规范，确保:
  6|- 第三方应用通过统一的数据结构调用插件功能
  7|- 所有插件返回一致格式的结果
  8|- 插件状态可被外部系统查询和监控
  9|- 支持异步事件通知机制
 10|
 11|设计原则:
 12|- 不可变数据类 (dataclass + frozen)
 13|- 类型安全 (所有字段有明确类型注解)
 14|- 可序列化 (支持 JSON 序列化)
 15|- 向前兼容 (扩展字段使用 Optional)
 16|"""
 17|
 18|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 19|from enum import Enum, auto
 20|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Callable, Union
 21|from datetime import datetime
 22|import json
 23|
 24|
 25|class PluginStatus(Enum):
 26|    """
 27|    插件生命周期状态机
 28|
 29|    状态流转:
 30|        UNREGISTERED → REGISTERED → INITIALIZING → READY
 31|            READY → RUNNING → COMPLETED → READY
 32|            READY → PAUSED → READY
 33|            READY → STOPPING → STOPPED
 34|            任意状态 → ERROR
 35|
 36|    UNREGISTERED: 插件未被管理器识别
 37|    REGISTERED:   插件类已注册到管理器，尚未实例化
 38|    INITIALIZING: 插件正在执行 initialize() 方法
 39|    READY:        插件已初始化完成，等待执行
 40|    RUNNING:      插件正在执行 execute() 方法
 41|    COMPLETED:    插件一次执行完成（自动回到 READY）
 42|    PAUSED:       插件被暂停（管理器可暂停长时间运行插件）
 43|    STOPPING:     插件正在执行 cleanup() 方法
 44|    STOPPED:      插件已停止但未卸载
 45|    ERROR:        插件遇到不可恢复的错误
 46|    """
 47|    UNREGISTERED = auto()
 48|    REGISTERED = auto()
 49|    INITIALIZING = auto()
 50|    READY = auto()
 51|    RUNNING = auto()
 52|    COMPLETED = auto()
 53|    PAUSED = auto()
 54|    STOPPING = auto()
 55|    STOPPED = auto()
 56|    ERROR = auto()
 57|
 58|
 59|class PluginPriority(Enum):
 60|    """
 61|    插件执行优先级
 62|
 63|    管理器在并发调度时根据优先级决定执行顺序:
 64|    - CRITICAL: 系统关键插件，必须优先执行
 65|    - HIGH: 重要业务插件
 66|    - NORMAL: 常规插件（默认）
 67|    - LOW: 后台任务插件
 68|    - IDLE: 仅在系统空闲时执行
 69|    """
 70|    CRITICAL = 0
 71|    HIGH = 1
 72|    NORMAL = 2
 73|    LOW = 3
 74|    IDLE = 4
 75|
 76|
 77|@dataclass
 78|class PluginResult:
 79|    """
 80|    插件执行结果 — 统一返回类型
 81|
 82|    所有插件的 execute() 方法必须返回此类型的实例。
 83|
 84|    字段说明:
 85|        success: 执行是否成功（布尔值，必填）
 86|        data: 插件产出的业务数据（任意可序列化对象）
 87|        message: 人类可读的状态描述
 88|        error_code: 错误码（成功时为空字符串）
 89|        error_detail: 详细错误信息（含堆栈跟踪，仅生产环境脱敏）
 90|        execution_time_ms: 执行耗时（毫秒）
 91|        warnings: 非致命的警告信息列表
 92|        metrics: 插件自定义的性能指标
 93|        timestamp: 结果生成时间戳
 94|    """
 95|    success: bool
 96|    data: Optional[Any] = None
 97|    message: str = ""
 98|    error_code: str = ""
 99|    error_detail: str = ""
100|    execution_time_ms: float = 0.0
101|    warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
102|    metrics: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
103|    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isofor
   |    mat())
104|
105|    @classmethod
106|    def ok(cls, data: Any = None, message: str = "执行成功",
107|           execution_time_ms: float = 0.0, **kwargs) -> "PluginResult":
108|        """
109|        工厂方法：快速创建一个成功结果
110|
111|        用法:
112|            return PluginResult.ok(data={"temperature": 25.0})
113|            return PluginResult.ok(message="仿真完成", data=df)
114|        """
115|        return cls(
116|            success=True,
117|            data=data,
118|            message=message,
119|            execution_time_ms=execution_time_ms,
120|            **kwargs
121|        )
122|
123|    @classmethod
124|    def fail(cls, message: str = "执行失败", error_code: str = "UNKNOWN_
   |    ERROR",
125|             error_detail: str = "", **kwargs) -> "PluginResult":
126|        """
127|        工厂方法：快速创建一个失败结果
128|
129|        用法:
130|            return PluginResult.fail("参数缺失", error_code="MISSING_PAR
   |    AM")
131|            return PluginResult.fail("连接超时", "TIMEOUT", detail)
132|        """
133|        return cls(
134|            success=False,
135|            message=message,
136|            error_code=error_code,
137|            error_detail=error_detail,
138|            **kwargs
139|        )
140|
141|    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
142|        """转换为可 JSON 序列化的字典"""
143|        return {
144|            "success": self.success,
145|            "data": self.data,
146|            "message": self.message,
147|            "error_code": self.error_code,
148|            "error_detail": self.error_detail,
149|            "execution_time_ms": self.execution_time_ms,
150|            "warnings": self.warnings,
151|            "metrics": self.metrics,
152|            "timestamp": self.timestamp
153|        }
154|
155|    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
156|        """转换为 JSON 字符串"""
157|        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=ind
   |    ent, default=str)
158|
159|    def has_warnings(self) -> bool:
160|        """是否有警告信息"""
161|        return len(self.warnings) > 0
162|
163|
164|@dataclass
165|class PluginInfo:
166|    """
167|    插件注册信息 — 完整描述一个已注册插件
168|
169|    字段说明:
170|        name: 插件唯一标识名称
171|        version: 语义化版本号 (如 "2.1.0")
172|        description: 功能描述
173|        author: 作者/组织名称
174|        status: 当前状态
175|        priority: 执行优先级
176|        dependencies: 依赖的其他插件名称列表
177|        config_schema: 配置参数的 JSON Schema
178|        tags: 分类标签列表
179|        registered_at: 注册时间戳
180|        last_loaded_at: 最近一次加载时间
181|        last_executed_at: 最近一次执行时间
182|        execution_count: 累计执行次数
183|    """
184|    name: str
185|    version: str = "0.0.0"
186|    description: str = ""
187|    author: str = ""
188|    status: PluginStatus = PluginStatus.REGISTERED
189|    priority: PluginPriority = PluginPriority.NORMAL
190|    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
191|    config_schema: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
192|    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
193|    registered_at: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().is
   |    oformat())
194|    last_loaded_at: Optional[str] = None
195|    last_executed_at: Optional[str] = None
196|    execution_count: int = 0
197|
198|    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
199|        return {
200|            "name": self.name,
201|            "version": self.version,
202|            "description": self.description,
203|            "author": self.author,
204|            "status": self.status.name,
205|            "priority": self.priority.name,
206|            "dependencies": self.dependencies,
207|            "config_schema": self.config_schema,
208|            "tags": self.tags,
209|            "registered_at": self.registered_at,
210|            "last_loaded_at": self.last_loaded_at,
211|            "last_executed_at": self.last_executed_at,
212|            "execution_count": self.execution_count
213|        }
214|
215|
216|@dataclass
217|class PluginManifest:
218|    """
219|    插件清单 — 描述插件的能力和接口
220|
221|    第三方应用通过读取此清单了解插件的:
222|    - 输入参数规格
223|    - 输出格式
224|    - 支持的操作
225|    - 版本兼容性
226|    """
227|    name: str
228|    version: str
229|    supported_operations: List[str]
230|    input_schema: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
231|    output_schema: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
232|    min_system_version: str = "1.0.0"
233|    max_instances: int = 1
234|    category: str = "algorithm"
235|
236|
237|@dataclass
238|class PluginEvent:
239|    """
240|    插件事件 — 用于插件间通信和外部系统通知
241|
242|    事件类型包括:
243|        PLUGIN_REGISTERED: 插件已注册
244|        PLUGIN_INITIALIZED: 插件初始化完成
245|        PLUGIN_STARTED: 插件开始执行
246|        PLUGIN_COMPLETED: 插件执行完成
247|        PLUGIN_ERROR: 插件发生错误
248|        PLUGIN_STOPPED: 插件已停止
249|        STATE_CHANGED: 任意状态变更
250|        METRICS_UPDATED: 性能指标更新
251|    """
252|    event_type: str
253|    plugin_name: str
254|    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isofor
   |    mat())
255|    data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
256|    result: Optional[PluginResult] = None
257|
258|    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
259|        d = {
260|            "event_type": self.event_type,
261|            "plugin_name": self.plugin_name,
262|            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
263|            "data": self.data
264|        }
265|        if self.result:
266|            d["result"] = self.result.to_dict()
267|        return d
268|
269|
270|PluginCallback = Callable[[PluginEvent], None]
271|"""插件事件回调函数类型签名"""
272|
273|PluginConfig = Dict[str, Any]
274|"""插件配置参数类型"""
275|
276|PluginContext = Dict[str, Any]
277|"""插件运行上下文类型"""

39. src/plugin/simulation_plugin.py - 仿真插件执行器
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|仿真插件 v1.0 — 数据中心智能调度仿真
  3|
  4|继承自 PluginBase v1.0 标准接口，实现:
  5|    - PluginManifest 完整清单
  6|    - validate_config 配置验证
  7|    - pre_execute 参数预处理
  8|    - execute 返回 PluginResult
  9|    - post_execute 指标收集
 10|
 11|支持模式:
 12|    - standard: 标准单机仿真
 13|    - distributed: 分布式并行仿真
 14|    - large_scale: 大规模压力测试
 15|"""
 16|
 17|from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
 18|import os
 19|import pandas as pd
 20|
 21|from src.plugin.base import PluginBase
 22|from src.plugin.protocol import PluginResult, PluginManifest
 23|from src.algorithms import PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS
 24|from src.simulation import DataCenterSimulator
 25|from src.simulation.distributed_simulator import DistributedSimulator
 26|from src.visualization import (
 27|    plot_energy_comparison, plot_temperature_trend,
 28|    plot_load_distribution, plot_pue_comparison, plot_cooling_power,
 29|    plot_service_rate
 30|)
 31|from src.simulation.metrics import summarize
 32|from src.utils import save_simulation_data, export_results_to_excel, sav
   |    e_run_metadata
 33|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 34|
 35|
 36|class SimulationPlugin(PluginBase):
 37|
 38|    def get_manifest(self) -> PluginManifest:
 39|        return PluginManifest(
 40|            name="SimulationPlugin",
 41|            version="2.0.0",
 42|            supported_operations=["run_comparison"],
 43|            input_schema={
 44|                "num_servers": {"type": "int", "min": 1, "max": 1000, "d
   |    efault": 10},
 45|                "simulation_hours": {"type": "int", "min": 1, "max": 720
   |    , "default": 24},
 46|                "algorithms": {"type": "list", "items": "str", "default"
   |    : ["greedy","genetic","round_robin"]},
 47|                "parallel_workers": {"type": "int", "min": 1, "max": 32,
   |     "default": 4},
 48|                "run_mode": {"type": "str", "enum": ["standard","distrib
   |    uted","large_scale"], "default": "standard"},
 49|                "server_type": {"type": "str", "enum": ["mixed","standar
   |    d","high_performance","gpu_server"], "default": "mixed"},
 50|                "visualization": {"type": "bool", "default": True},
 51|                "save_results": {"type": "bool", "default": True}
 52|            },
 53|            output_schema={
 54|                "success": "bool",
 55|                "data": {"statistics": "dict", "records_count": "int", "
   |    algorithms": "list"},
 56|                "message": "str",
 57|                "execution_time_ms": "float"
 58|            },
 59|            min_system_version="2.0.0",
 60|            category="executor"
 61|        )
 62|
 63|    def validate_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
 64|        num_servers = config.get("num_servers", 10)
 65|        if not isinstance(num_servers, int) or num_servers < 1 or num_se
   |    rvers > 1000:
 66|            self.logger.error(f"num_servers 必须在 [1, 1000] 范围内，当前
   |    值: {num_servers}")
 67|            return False
 68|        sim_hours = config.get("simulation_hours", 24)
 69|        if not isinstance(sim_hours, int) or sim_hours < 1 or sim_hours 
   |    > 720:
 70|            self.logger.error(f"simulation_hours 必须在 [1, 720] 范围内，
   |    当前值: {sim_hours}")
 71|            return False
 72|        workers = config.get("parallel_workers", 4)
 73|        if workers < 1 or workers > 32:
 74|            self.logger.error(f"parallel_workers 必须在 [1, 32] 范围内，
   |    当前值: {workers}")
 75|            return False
 76|        return True
 77|
 78|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 79|        try:
 80|            cfg = config or {}
 81|            self.num_servers = cfg.get("num_servers", 10)
 82|            self.simulation_hours = cfg.get("simulation_hours", 24)
 83|            self.parallel_workers = cfg.get("parallel_workers", 4)
 84|            self.run_mode = cfg.get("run_mode", "standard")
 85|            self.server_type = cfg.get("server_type", "mixed")
 86|            self.site_id = cfg.get("site_id") or None
 87|            self.results_dir = cfg.get("results_dir", "data")
 88|            self.reports_dir = cfg.get("reports_dir", "reports")
 89|            self.simulator = None
 90|            self.distributed_simulator = None
 91|
 92|            os.makedirs(self.results_dir, exist_ok=True)
 93|            os.makedirs(self.reports_dir, exist_ok=True)
 94|
 95|            if self.run_mode in ("distributed", "large_scale"):
 96|                self.distributed_simulator = DistributedSimulator(
 97|                    num_servers=self.num_servers,
 98|                    parallel_workers=self.parallel_workers
 99|                )
100|            else:
101|                self.simulator = DataCenterSimulator(
102|                    num_servers=self.num_servers,
103|                    simulation_hours=self.simulation_hours,
104|                    data_source_override=(
105|                        {"site_id": self.site_id} if self.site_id else N
   |    one
106|                    ),
107|                )
108|            return True
109|        except Exception as e:
110|            self.logger.error(f"SimulationPlugin 初始化失败: {e}")
111|            return False
112|
113|    def pre_execute(self, **kwargs) -> Optional[PluginResult]:
114|        algorithms = kwargs.get("algorithms", ["greedy", "genetic", "rou
   |    nd_robin"])
115|        valid_algos = {"greedy", "genetic", "round_robin", "damo_ets"}.u
   |    nion(PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS.keys())
116|        for algo in algorithms:
117|            if algo not in valid_algos:
118|                return PluginResult.fail(
119|                    message=f"不支持的算法: '{algo}'，有效值: {sorted(val
   |    id_algos)}",
120|                    error_code="INVALID_ALGORITHM"
121|                )
122|        return None
123|
124|    def execute(self, **kwargs) -> PluginResult:
125|        algorithms = kwargs.get("algorithms", ["greedy", "genetic", "rou
   |    nd_robin"])
126|        run_visualization = kwargs.get("visualization", True)
127|        save_results = kwargs.get("save_results", True)
128|        include_series = kwargs.get("include_series", False)
129|
130|        try:
131|            if self.run_mode in ("distributed", "large_scale"):
132|                df = self._run_distributed_comparison(algorithms)
133|            else:
134|                df = self.simulator.run_comparison(algorithms=algorithms
   |    )
135|
136|            stats = self._calculate_statistics(df)
137|            warnings: List[str] = []
138|            skipped = getattr(self.simulator, "skipped_algorithms", []) 
   |    if self.simulator else []
139|            if skipped:
140|                warnings.append("以下算法执行失败已跳过: " + ", ".join(s
   |    kipped))
141|
142|            if save_results:
143|                csv_path = save_simulation_data(df)
144|                excel_path = export_results_to_excel(df)
145|
146|            if run_visualization:
147|                plots = self._generate_plots(df)
148|        except Exception as e:
149|            return PluginResult.fail(
150|                message=f"仿真执行异常: {e}",
151|                error_code="SIMULATION_ERROR",
152|                error_detail=str(e)
153|            )
154|
155|        payload = {
156|            "statistics": stats,
157|            "records_count": len(df),
158|            "algorithms": algorithms,
159|            "run_mode": self.run_mode,
160|            "server_count": self.num_servers,
161|            "simulation_hours": self.simulation_hours
162|        }
163|        if include_series:
164|            payload["series"] = df.to_dict("records")
165|        if save_results:
166|            payload["csv_path"] = csv_path
167|            payload["excel_path"] = excel_path
168|        if run_visualization:
169|            payload["plots"] = plots
170|
171|        if self.simulator is not None:
172|            payload["first_hour_offered"] = self.simulator.control_signa
   |    ture(df)
173|            payload["first_hour_served"] = self.simulator.first_hour_loa
   |    ds(df)
174|            payload["controlled"] = self.simulator.is_controlled(df)
175|            payload["ranking"] = self.simulator.get_ranking(df)
176|            payload["data_source"] = self.simulator.run_metadata
177|            if save_results:
178|                save_run_metadata({
179|                    **self.simulator.run_metadata,
180|                    "algorithms": algorithms,
181|                    "num_servers": self.num_servers,
182|                    "simulation_hours": self.simulation_hours,
183|                    "controlled": payload["controlled"],
184|                })
185|
186|        return PluginResult.ok(
187|            data=payload,
188|            message=f"仿真完成 ({self.run_mode} 模式)",
189|            warnings=warnings,
190|            metrics={
191|                "server_count": self.num_servers,
192|                "simulation_hours": self.simulation_hours,
193|                "algorithm_count": len(algorithms),
194|                "data_points": len(df)
195|            }
196|        )
197|
198|    def post_execute(self, result: PluginResult, **kwargs) -> PluginResu
   |    lt:
199|        if result.success:
200|            self.logger.info(
201|                f"仿真完成: {result.data.get('records_count', 0)} 条记录
   |    , "
202|                f"{len(result.data.get('algorithms', []))} 个算法"
203|            )
204|        return result
205|
206|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
207|        self.simulator = None
208|        self.distributed_simulator = None
209|
210|    def _run_distributed_comparison(self, algorithms: List[str]) -> pd.D
   |    ataFrame:
211|        if not self.distributed_simulator:
212|            raise RuntimeError("分布式仿真器未初始化")
213|        results = self.distributed_simulator.run_parallel_comparison(
214|            algorithms=algorithms, hours=self.simulation_hours
215|        )
216|        all_data = []
217|        for algo, algo_results in results.items():
218|            for hr in algo_results:
219|                all_data.append({
220|                    "time": hr["hour"], "algorithm": hr["algorithm"],
221|                    "total_load": hr["total_load"], "avg_temperature": h
   |    r["avg_temperature"],
222|                    "total_energy": hr["total_energy"], "pue": hr["pue"]
223|                })
224|        return pd.DataFrame(all_data)
225|
226|    def _calculate_statistics(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
227|        """Same metric definitions as the standalone simulator, one code
   |     path."""
228|        return summarize(df)
229|
230|    def _generate_plots(self, df) -> Dict[str, str]:
231|        """Best-effort plotting: a missing column skips one chart, not t
   |    he run."""
232|        os.makedirs(self.reports_dir, exist_ok=True)
233|        targets = [
234|            ("energy", "energy_comparison.png", "total_energy", plot_ene
   |    rgy_comparison),
235|            ("temperature", "temperature_trend.png", "avg_temperature", 
   |    plot_temperature_trend),
236|            ("load", "load_distribution.png", "total_load", plot_load_di
   |    stribution),
237|            ("pue", "pue_comparison.png", "pue", plot_pue_comparison),
238|            ("cooling", "cooling_power.png", "cooling_power", plot_cooli
   |    ng_power),
239|            ("service", "service_rate.png", "assigned", plot_service_rat
   |    e),
240|        ]
241|
242|        plots: Dict[str, str] = {}
243|        for key, filename, column, painter in targets:
244|            if column not in df.columns:
245|                self.logger.warning(f"结果缺少 {column} 列, 已跳过 {file
   |    name}")
246|                continue
247|            path = os.path.join(self.reports_dir, filename)
248|            try:
249|                painter(df, path)
250|                plots[key] = path
251|            except Exception as exc:
252|                self.logger.warning(f"生成 {filename} 失败: {exc}")
253|        return plots
254|
255|    def run_single_simulation(self, algorithm: str = "greedy") -> Dict[s
   |    tr, Any]:
256|        if not self.simulator and not self.distributed_simulator:
257|            return {"success": False, "error": "插件未初始化"}
258|        try:
259|            if self.distributed_simulator:
260|                results = self.distributed_simulator.run_distributed_sim
   |    ulation(
261|                    hours=self.simulation_hours, algorithm=algorithm
262|                )
263|                df = pd.DataFrame([{
264|                    "time": r["hour"], "algorithm": r["algorithm"],
265|                    "total_load": r["total_load"], "avg_temperature": r[
   |    "avg_temperature"],
266|                    "total_energy": r["total_energy"], "pue": r["pue"]
267|                } for r in results])
268|            else:
269|                df = self.simulator.run_simulation(algorithm=algorithm)
270|            return {"success": True, "statistics": self._calculate_stati
   |    stics(df), "records_count": len(df)}
271|        except Exception as e:
272|            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}
273|
274|    def run_large_scale_test(self, server_counts: List[int]) -> Dict[str
   |    , Any]:
275|        if not self.distributed_simulator:
276|            return {"success": False, "error": "分布式仿真器未初始化"}
277|        try:
278|            results = self.distributed_simulator.run_large_scale_test(
279|                server_counts=server_counts, hours=self.simulation_hours
280|            )
281|            return {"success": True, "results": results, "test_hours": s
   |    elf.simulation_hours}
282|        except Exception as e:
283|            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

41. src/plugins/_deps.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|GitHub Skills 可选依赖探测 (Lazy Detection)
  3|
  4|对 Gymnasium / Stable Baselines3 / pymoo 三个可选依赖做软探测，
  5|未安装时返回 False 而不抛异常，供插件层做降级处理。
  6|
  7|设计原则:
  8|    - 所有探测函数使用 try/except ImportError，绝不向上抛
  9|    - 提供 ``get_dependency_status()`` 统一汇总，方便 CLI / API 透出
 10|    - 提供 ``install_hint`` 字段，便于生成安装指引
 11|
 12|对应测试: ``tests/plugins/test_pymoo_plugin.py::TestDependencyMissingFal
   |    lback``
 13|"""
 14|
 15|from __future__ import annotations
 16|
 17|from typing import Any, Dict
 18|
 19|
 20|def has_gymnasium() -> bool:
 21|    """检查 ``gymnasium`` (RL 环境标准 API) 是否安装。"""
 22|    try:
 23|        import gymnasium  # noqa: F401
 24|        return True
 25|    except ImportError:
 26|        return False
 27|
 28|
 29|def has_sb3() -> bool:
 30|    """检查 ``stable_baselines3`` (DLR-RM RL 算法库) 是否安装。"""
 31|    try:
 32|        import stable_baselines3  # noqa: F401
 33|        return True
 34|    except ImportError:
 35|        return False
 36|
 37|
 38|def has_pymoo() -> bool:
 39|    """检查 ``pymoo`` (多目标优化库) 是否安装。"""
 40|    try:
 41|        import pymoo  # noqa: F401
 42|        return True
 43|    except ImportError:
 44|        return False
 45|
 46|
 47|def _probe_version(import_name: str, mod: Any = None) -> str:
 48|    """安全获取已安装库的版本号。"""
 49|    try:
 50|        m = mod if mod is not None else __import__(import_name)
 51|        return getattr(m, "__version__", "unknown")
 52|    except Exception:
 53|        return "unknown"
 54|
 55|
 56|def get_dependency_status() -> Dict[str, Any]:
 57|    """
 58|    返回三个可选依赖的统一状态。
 59|
 60|    Returns:
 61|        dict: ``{
 62|            "gymnasium": {"available": bool, "version": str, "install_hi
   |    nt": str},
 63|            "stable_baselines3": {...},
 64|            "pymoo": {...},
 65|        }``
 66|    """
 67|    status: Dict[str, Any] = {}
 68|
 69|    # gymnasium
 70|    g_avail = has_gymnasium()
 71|    g_ver = _probe_version("gymnasium") if g_avail else None
 72|    status["gymnasium"] = {
 73|        "available": g_avail,
 74|        "version": g_ver,
 75|        "install_hint": "pip install gymnasium>=1.0",
 76|    }
 77|
 78|    # stable_baselines3
 79|    sb3_avail = has_sb3()
 80|    sb3_ver = _probe_version("stable_baselines3") if sb3_avail else None
 81|    status["stable_baselines3"] = {
 82|        "available": sb3_avail,
 83|        "version": sb3_ver,
 84|        "install_hint": "pip install stable-baselines3>=2.0",
 85|    }
 86|
 87|    # pymoo
 88|    p_avail = has_pymoo()
 89|    p_ver = None
 90|    if p_avail:
 91|        try:
 92|            from pymoo import __version__ as _p_ver
 93|            p_ver = _p_ver
 94|        except Exception:
 95|            p_ver = "unknown"
 96|    status["pymoo"] = {
 97|        "available": p_avail,
 98|        "version": p_ver,
 99|        "install_hint": "pip install pymoo>=0.6",
100|    }
101|
102|    return status
