42. src/plugins/datacenter_gym_env.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|Gymnasium Env 适配器 — DataCenterSimulator → gymnasium.Env
  3|
  4|把 ``DataCenterSimulator`` 包装为符合 ``gymnasium.Env`` 接口规范的环境，
  5|供 Stable Baselines3 PPO 训练使用。
  6|
  7|State (observation) 编码 (归一化到 [0, 1]):
  8|    - 服务器特征 (num_servers * 3): [load_ratio, temp_ratio, type_norm] 
   |    × N
  9|    - 当前任务特征 (4):           [load_norm, priority_norm, deadline_no
   |    rm, wc_oh(1)]
 10|
 11|Action space:
 12|    - Discrete(num_servers)  — 选择把当前任务分配到哪台服务器
 13|
 14|Reward:
 15|    reward = -Δenergy - 0.5*Δtemp - 10*deadline_violation + 0.1*balance
 16|
 17|依赖:
 18|    pip install gymnasium
 19|
 20|来源: https://github.com/Farama-Foundation/Gymnasium (MIT)
 21|"""
 22|
 23|from __future__ import annotations
 24|
 25|import math
 26|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
 27|
 28|from src.plugins._deps import has_gymnasium
 29|
 30|if has_gymnasium():
 31|    import gymnasium as gym
 32|    from gymnasium import spaces
 33|    import numpy as np
 34|else:
 35|    gym = None  # type: ignore
 36|    spaces = None  # type: ignore
 37|    np = None  # type: ignore
 38|
 39|from src.simulation import DataCenterSimulator
 40|from src.models import Task
 41|
 42|
 43|class DataCenterGymEnv:
 44|    """
 45|    DataCenter Gymnasium Environment
 46|
 47|    与 ``gymnasium.Env`` 兼容（非继承，因为无 gym 时需要软降级）。
 48|
 49|    用法::
 50|
 51|        env = DataCenterGymEnv(num_servers=10, simulation_hours=24, seed
   |    =42)
 52|        obs, info = env.reset()
 53|        for _ in range(1000):
 54|            action = env.action_space.sample()
 55|            obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)
 56|            if terminated or truncated:
 57|                break
 58|
 59|    注意:
 60|        - 如果 gymnasium 未安装，构造时直接抛 ``ImportError``，调用方需提
   |    前探测
 61|        - 不继承 ``gym.Env`` 是为了保持模块在无依赖时也能 ``import``
 62|    """
 63|
 64|    metadata = {"render_modes": []}
 65|
 66|    # 服务器类型 one-hot 索引映射（与 Server.server_type 字符串对应）
 67|    SERVER_TYPES = ["standard", "high_performance", "gpu_server"]
 68|    WORKLOAD_CLASSES = ["cpu_bound", "memory_bound", "io_bound"]
 69|
 70|    def __init__(
 71|        self,
 72|        num_servers: int = 10,
 73|        simulation_hours: int = 24,
 74|        seed: Optional[int] = None,
 75|        config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
 76|    ) -> None:
 77|        if not has_gymnasium():
 78|            raise ImportError(
 79|                "gymnasium 未安装。请运行: pip install gymnasium>=1.0"
 80|            )
 81|        if num_servers < 1:
 82|            raise ValueError(f"num_servers 必须 >= 1, 当前={num_servers}
   |    ")
 83|
 84|        self.num_servers: int = num_servers
 85|        self.simulation_hours: int = simulation_hours
 86|        self._seed_value: Optional[int] = seed
 87|        self._config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = config
 88|        self._current_time: int = 0
 89|        self._current_task: Optional[Task] = None
 90|        self._step_count: int = 0
 91|        self._max_steps: int = simulation_hours * num_servers  # heurist
   |    ic horizon
 92|
 93|        # observation = servers (num_servers * 3) + task (4)
 94|        self.observation_dim: int = num_servers * 3 + 4
 95|        self.observation_space = spaces.Box(
 96|            low=0.0,
 97|            high=1.0,
 98|            shape=(self.observation_dim,),
 99|            dtype=np.float32,
100|        )
101|        self.action_space = spaces.Discrete(num_servers)
102|
103|        # simulator / servers 状态在 reset() 时初始化
104|        self.simulator: Optional[DataCenterSimulator] = None
105|        self._prev_total_power: float = 0.0
106|        self._prev_max_temp: float = 25.0
107|
109|    # Gymnasium API
111|
112|    def reset(
113|        self,
114|        seed: Optional[int] = None,
115|        options: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
116|    ) -> Tuple["np.ndarray", Dict[str, Any]]:
117|        if seed is not None:
118|            self._seed_value = seed
119|        self.simulator = DataCenterSimulator(
120|            num_servers=self.num_servers,
121|            simulation_hours=self.simulation_hours,
122|            seed=self._seed_value,
123|            config=self._config,
124|        )
125|        self._current_time = 0
126|        self._step_count = 0
127|        self._current_task = self._generate_task(self._current_time)
128|
129|        self._prev_total_power = sum(s.calculate_power() for s in self.s
   |    imulator.servers)
130|        self._prev_max_temp = max(s.current_temp for s in self.simulator
   |    .servers)
131|        return self._get_obs(), self._get_info()
132|
133|    def step(self, action: int) -> Tuple["np.ndarray", float, bool, bool
   |    , Dict[str, Any]]:
134|        assert self.simulator is not None, "请先调用 reset()"
135|        if not self.action_space.contains(int(action)):
136|            raise ValueError(f"action 必须在 [0, {self.num_servers})")
137|
138|        server = self.simulator.servers[int(action)]
139|        task = self._current_task
140|
141|        # 分配任务（如果超载，则视为失败放置 — 仍记入状态）
142|        assigned = False
143|        deadline_violation = 0
144|        if task is not None:
145|            deadline_violation = 1 if task.is_deadline_violated(self._cu
   |    rrent_time) else 0
146|            assigned = server.assign_task(task)
147|
148|        # 更新温度
149|        for s in self.simulator.servers:
150|            s.update_temperature(ambient_temp=25.0, cooling_efficiency=0
   |    .5)
151|
152|        # 统计差量
153|        total_power = sum(s.calculate_power() for s in self.simulator.se
   |    rvers)
154|        max_temp = max(s.current_temp for s in self.simulator.servers)
155|        delta_energy = (total_power - self._prev_total_power) / 1000.0  
   |    # 归一化
156|        delta_temp = (max_temp - self._prev_max_temp) / 100.0
157|
158|        # 负载均衡奖励（标准差越小越好）
159|        loads = [s.current_load / max(s.max_load, 1.0) for s in self.sim
   |    ulator.servers]
160|        load_std = float(np.std(loads)) if len(loads) > 0 else 0.0
161|        balance_bonus = 0.1 * (1.0 - min(load_std, 1.0))
162|
163|        reward = -max(delta_energy, 0.0) - 0.5 * max(delta_temp, 0.0) - 
   |    10.0 * deadline_violation + balance_bonus
164|        # 关键决策：未分配成功的任务要重罚
165|        if not assigned:
166|            reward -= 5.0
167|
168|        self._prev_total_power = total_power
169|        self._prev_max_temp = max_temp
170|        self._step_count += 1
171|        self._current_time += 1
172|
173|        # 推进到下一任务 / 终止
174|        terminated = False
175|        truncated = False
176|        if self._step_count >= self._max_steps or self._current_time >= 
   |    self.simulation_hours:
177|            truncated = True
178|        else:
179|            self._current_task = self._generate_task(self._current_time)
180|            if self._current_task is None:
181|                terminated = True
182|
183|        return self._get_obs(), float(reward), terminated, truncated, se
   |    lf._get_info()
184|
185|    def render(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - 不支持渲染
186|        return None
187|
188|    def close(self) -> None:
189|        self.simulator = None
190|
192|    # 内部辅助
194|
195|    def _generate_task(self, current_time: int) -> Optional[Task]:
196|        """生成一个当前时刻的任务。简单随机生成（与仿真器 TaskGenerator 行
   |    为近似）。"""
197|        if self.simulator is None or self.simulator.task_generator is No
   |    ne:
198|            return None
199|        try:
200|            tasks = self.simulator.task_generator.generate_tasks(current
   |    _time)
201|        except Exception:
202|            return None
203|        if not tasks:
204|            return None
205|        return tasks[0]
206|
207|    def _get_obs(self) -> "np.ndarray":
208|        if self.simulator is None:
209|            return np.zeros(self.observation_dim, dtype=np.float32)
210|        servers = self.simulator.servers
211|        srv_feats: List[float] = []
212|        for s in servers:
213|            srv_feats.append(min(s.current_load / max(s.max_load, 1.0), 
   |    1.0))
214|            srv_feats.append(min(s.current_temp / max(s.max_temp, 1.0), 
   |    1.0))
215|            srv_feats.append(self._type_norm(s.server_type))
216|        task_feats: List[float] = [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
217|        if self._current_task is not None:
218|            t = self._current_task
219|            task_feats[0] = min(t.load / 50.0, 1.0)
220|            task_feats[1] = min(t.priority / 3.0, 1.0) if t.priority els
   |    e 0.0
221|            task_feats[2] = (
222|                1.0 if t.deadline is None else min(t.deadline / 100.0, 1
   |    .0)
223|            )
224|            task_feats[3] = 1.0 if t.workload_class == "cpu_bound" else 
   |    0.0
225|        obs = np.array(srv_feats + task_feats, dtype=np.float32)
226|        return obs
227|
228|    def _get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
229|        if self.simulator is None:
230|            return {}
231|        servers = self.simulator.servers
232|        return {
233|            "current_time": self._current_time,
234|            "step_count": self._step_count,
235|            "total_load": float(sum(s.current_load for s in servers)),
236|            "max_temp": float(max(s.current_temp for s in servers)),
237|            "total_power": float(sum(s.calculate_power() for s in server
   |    s)),
238|            "current_task": (
239|                None if self._current_task is None else self._current_ta
   |    sk.task_id
240|            ),
241|        }
242|
243|    def _type_norm(self, server_type: str) -> float:
244|        if server_type in self.SERVER_TYPES:
245|            return float(self.SERVER_TYPES.index(server_type)) / max(len
   |    (self.SERVER_TYPES) - 1, 1)
246|        return 0.0
247|
248|    @property
249|    def unwrapped(self):
250|        return self
251|
252|    @property
253|    def np_random(self) -> "np.random.Generator":
254|        return np.random.default_rng(self._seed_value)
255|
256|
257|__all__ = ["DataCenterGymEnv"]

================================================================================
43. src/plugins/moead_scheduler.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|MOEADSchedulerPlugin — pymoo MOEAD 分解多目标调度器 (GitHub Skills 集成)
  3|
  4|与 NSGA2 同构（同样的 Problem 定义），但用基于参考向量分解的 MOEAD 算法。
  5|
  6|差异:
  7|    - 算法: ``MOEAD(ref_dirs=get_reference_directions("das-dennis", 3, n
   |    _partitions),
  8|                    n_neighbors, prob_neighbor_mating)``
  9|    - 解的选择：在 ref_dirs 均匀覆盖下挑与偏好向量夹角最小的解
 10|    - 解码阶段：与 NSGA2 相同（整数编码 → server_id 列表）
 11|
 12|依赖:
 13|    pip install pymoo
 14|
 15|来源: https://github.com/anyoptimization/pymoo (Apache 2.0)
 16|"""
 17|
 18|from __future__ import annotations
 19|
 20|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
 21|
 22|from src.plugin.base import PluginBase
 23|from src.plugin.protocol import PluginManifest, PluginResult
 24|from src.plugins._deps import has_pymoo
 25|
 26|
 27|OVERLOAD_PENALTY = 50.0
 28|
 29|
 30|class MOEADSchedulerPlugin(PluginBase):
 31|    """pymoo MOEAD 多目标调度器（基于参考向量分解）"""
 32|
 34|    # 清单 / 配置
 36|
 37|    def get_manifest(self) -> PluginManifest:
 38|        return PluginManifest(
 39|            name="MOEADSchedulerPlugin",
 40|            version="1.0.0",
 41|            supported_operations=["schedule"],
 42|            input_schema={
 43|                "n_partitions": {"type": "int", "default": 12, "min": 2}
   |    ,
 44|                "n_neighbors": {"type": "int", "default": 15, "min": 2},
 45|                "prob_neighbor_mating": {"type": "float", "default": 0.7
   |    , "min": 0.0, "max": 1.0},
 46|                "n_gen": {"type": "int", "default": 200, "min": 1},
 47|                "selection": {
 48|                    "type": "str",
 49|                    "enum": ["energy_priority", "balanced", "temp_priori
   |    ty"],
 50|                    "default": "balanced",
 51|                },
 52|                "seed": {"type": "int", "default": 42},
 53|            },
 54|            output_schema={
 55|                "assignments": "list",
 56|                "pareto_size": "int",
 57|                "generation": "int",
 58|                "objectives": "dict",
 59|            },
 60|            min_system_version="1.0.0",
 61|            category="algorithm",
 62|        )
 63|
 64|    def validate_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
 65|        n_partitions = config.get("n_partitions", 12)
 66|        if not isinstance(n_partitions, int) or n_partitions < 2:
 67|            self.logger.error(f"n_partitions 必须 >= 2, 当前={n_partitio
   |    ns}")
 68|            return False
 69|        n_neighbors = config.get("n_neighbors", 15)
 70|        if not isinstance(n_neighbors, int) or n_neighbors < 2:
 71|            self.logger.error(f"n_neighbors 必须 >= 2, 当前={n_neighbors
   |    }")
 72|            return False
 73|        n_gen = config.get("n_gen", 200)
 74|        if not isinstance(n_gen, int) or n_gen < 1:
 75|            self.logger.error(f"n_gen 必须 >= 1, 当前={n_gen}")
 76|            return False
 77|        return True
 78|
 80|    # 生命周期
 82|
 83|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 84|        cfg = dict(config or {})
 85|        if not self.validate_config(cfg):
 86|            return False
 87|        self._deps_ok = has_pymoo()
 88|        self._n_partitions: int = int(cfg.get("n_partitions", 12))
 89|        self._n_neighbors: int = int(cfg.get("n_neighbors", 15))
 90|        self._prob_neighbor_mating: float = float(cfg.get("prob_neighbor
   |    _mating", 0.7))
 91|        self._n_gen: int = int(cfg.get("n_gen", 200))
 92|        self._selection: str = str(cfg.get("selection", "balanced"))
 93|        self._seed: Optional[int] = cfg.get("seed", 42)
 94|        return True
 95|
 96|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
 97|        return None
 98|
100|    # 执行
102|
103|    def execute(self, **kwargs: Any) -> PluginResult:
104|        if not has_pymoo():
105|            return PluginResult.fail(
106|                message="pymoo 未安装。请执行: pip install pymoo>=0.6",
107|                error_code="DEPENDENCY_MISSING",
108|            )
109|        tasks: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("tasks")
110|        servers: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("servers")
111|        if not tasks or servers is None:
112|            return PluginResult.fail(
113|                message="MOEAD 调度需要 tasks 与 servers 参数",
114|                error_code="MISSING_PARAM",
115|            )
116|        try:
117|            return self._solve(tasks, servers)
118|        except Exception as e:
119|            self.logger.error(f"MOEAD 调度失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
120|            return PluginResult.fail(
121|                message=f"MOEAD 调度失败: {e}",
122|                error_code="MOEAD_ERROR",
123|                error_detail=str(e),
124|            )
125|
127|    # 内部
129|
130|    def _solve(self, tasks: List[Any], servers: List[Any]) -> PluginResu
   |    lt:
131|        from pymoo.core.problem import ElementwiseProblem
132|        from pymoo.algorithms.moo.moead import MOEAD
133|        from pymoo.operators.crossover.sbx import SBX
134|        from pymoo.operators.mutation.pm import PM
135|        from pymoo.operators.repair.rounding import RoundingRepair
136|        from pymoo.operators.sampling.rnd import IntegerRandomSampling
137|        from pymoo.optimize import minimize
138|        from pymoo.termination import get_termination
139|        from pymoo.util.ref_dirs import get_reference_directions
140|
141|        num_tasks = len(tasks)
142|        num_servers = len(servers)
143|        if num_tasks == 0 or num_servers == 0:
144|            return PluginResult.fail(
145|                message="tasks 与 servers 均不能为空",
146|                error_code="EMPTY_INPUT",
147|            )
148|
149|        task_loads = [float(t.load) for t in tasks]
150|        task_deadlines = [
151|            t.deadline if t.deadline is not None else 10**9 for t in tas
   |    ks
152|        ]
153|        max_loads = [float(s.max_load) for s in servers]
154|        base_powers = [float(s.base_power) for s in servers]
155|        max_powers = [float(s.max_power) for s in servers]
156|        init_loads = [float(s.current_load) for s in servers]
157|        max_loads_safe = [max(ml, 1.0) for ml in max_loads]
158|
159|        class SchedulingProblem(ElementwiseProblem):
160|            def __init__(self_inner):
161|                super().__init__(
162|                    n_var=num_tasks,
163|                    n_obj=3,
164|                    n_ieq_constr=0,
165|                    xl=0,
166|                    xu=num_servers - 1,
167|                )
168|
169|            def _evaluate(self_inner, x, out, *args, **kwargs):
170|                assign = [int(round(float(v))) for v in x]
171|                assign = [max(0, min(num_servers - 1, a)) for a in assig
   |    n]
172|                srv_load = list(init_loads)
173|                for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
174|                    srv_load[s_idx] += task_loads[i]
175|                total_energy = 0.0
176|                for j in range(num_servers):
177|                    lf = srv_load[j] / max_loads_safe[j]
178|                    total_energy += base_powers[j] + (max_powers[j] - ba
   |    se_powers[j]) * lf
179|                lfs = [srv_load[j] / max_loads_safe[j] for j in range(nu
   |    m_servers)]
180|                max_temp_proxy = 25.0 + 60.0 * max(lfs) if lfs else 25.0
181|                violations = 0
182|                for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
183|                    if task_deadlines[i] < 10 and (
184|                        srv_load[s_idx] / max_loads_safe[s_idx] > 0.9
185|                    ):
186|                        violations += 1
187|                overload = sum(
188|                    max(0.0, srv_load[j] - max_loads[j]) for j in range(
   |    num_servers)
189|                )
190|                out["F"] = [float(total_energy + overload * OVERLOAD_PEN
   |    ALTY),
191|                            float(max_temp_proxy), float(violations)]
192|
193|        # 参考方向 (das-dennis)
194|        ref_dirs = get_reference_directions("das-dennis", 3, n_partition
   |    s=self._n_partitions)
195|        algorithm = MOEAD(
196|            ref_dirs=ref_dirs,
197|            n_neighbors=self._n_neighbors,
198|            prob_neighbor_mating=self._prob_neighbor_mating,
199|            crossover=SBX(prob=0.9, eta=3.0, repair=RoundingRepair()),
200|            mutation=PM(prob=1.0 / max(num_tasks, 1), eta=3.0, repair=Ro
   |    undingRepair()),
201|        )
202|        res = minimize(
203|            SchedulingProblem(),
204|            algorithm,
205|            termination=get_termination("n_gen", self._n_gen),
206|            seed=self._seed,
207|            verbose=False,
208|        )
209|
210|        if res.X is None or (hasattr(res.X, "shape") and res.X.shape[0] 
   |    == 0):
211|            return PluginResult.fail(
212|                message="MOEAD 未找到可行解",
213|                error_code="NO_SOLUTION",
214|            )
215|
216|        F = res.F
217|        X = res.X
218|        if len(F.shape) == 1:
219|            F = F.reshape(1, -1)
220|            X = X.reshape(1, -1)
221|        idx = self._select_by_preference(F, ref_dirs)
222|        best_x = X[idx]
223|        assign = [int(round(float(v))) for v in best_x]
224|        assign = [max(0, min(num_servers - 1, a)) for a in assign]
225|
226|        assignments: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
227|        for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
228|            assignments.append(
229|                {
230|                    "task_id": getattr(tasks[i], "task_id", str(i)),
231|                    "server_id": s_idx,
232|                    "load": task_loads[i],
233|                }
234|            )
235|        return PluginResult.ok(
236|            data={
237|                "assignments": assignments,
238|                "pareto_size": int(F.shape[0]),
239|                "generation": self._n_gen,
240|                "objectives": {
241|                    "energy": float(F[idx, 0]),
242|                    "max_temp_proxy": float(F[idx, 1]),
243|                    "deadline_violations": float(F[idx, 2]),
244|                },
245|                "selection": self._selection,
246|            },
247|            message=f"MOEAD 调度完成: {len(assignments)} 个任务, pareto=
   |    {F.shape[0]}",
248|        )
249|
250|    def _select_by_preference(self, F: Any, ref_dirs: Any) -> int:
251|        """
252|        在 ref_dirs 均匀覆盖下，挑 weighted-sum 最小的解。
253|        （若 ref_dirs 与 F 维度不一致，则退化为加权和最小）
254|        """
255|        import numpy as np
256|        selection_weights = {
257|            "energy_priority": (1.0, 0.3, 5.0),
258|            "balanced": (1.0, 1.0, 10.0),
259|            "temp_priority": (0.3, 1.0, 5.0),
260|        }
261|        w = selection_weights.get(self._selection, (1.0, 1.0, 10.0))
262|        Fs = np.asarray(F, dtype=float)
263|        mins = Fs.min(axis=0)
264|        maxs = Fs.max(axis=0)
265|        rng = np.where((maxs - mins) > 0, (maxs - mins), 1.0)
266|        norm = (Fs - mins) / rng
267|        scores = norm @ np.array(w, dtype=float)
268|        return int(np.argmin(scores))
269|
270|
271|__all__ = ["MOEADSchedulerPlugin"]

================================================================================
44. src/plugins/nsga2_scheduler.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|NSGA2SchedulerPlugin — pymoo NSGA2 多目标调度器 (GitHub Skills 集成)
  3|
  4|把调度问题建模为 pymoo 的 ``ElementwiseProblem``:
  5|
  6|    决策变量 x ∈ [0, num_servers-1]^num_tasks  (整数编码)
  7|    目标函数:
  8|        f1 = 总能耗
  9|        f2 = 最高温度代理
 10|        f3 = deadline 违约计数
 11|    不等式约束:
 12|        g1 = max(0, sum(max(0, srv_load - srv_max)))
 13|
 14|算法: ``NSGA2(pop_size, sampling=IntegerRandomSampling,
 15|              crossover=SBX(repair=RoundingRepair), mutation=PM(repair=R
   |    oundingRepair))``
 16|
 17|从 Pareto 前沿根据 ``selection`` 偏好（energy_priority / balanced / temp_
   |    priority）
 18|选出一个折中解，输出 ``[{task_id, server_id}, ...]``。
 19|
 20|依赖:
 21|    pip install pymoo
 22|
 23|来源: https://github.com/anyoptimization/pymoo (Apache 2.0)
 24|"""
 25|
 26|from __future__ import annotations
 27|
 28|import copy
 29|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
 30|
 31|from src.plugin.base import PluginBase
 32|from src.plugin.protocol import PluginManifest, PluginResult
 33|from src.plugins._deps import has_pymoo
 34|
 35|
 36|class NSGA2SchedulerPlugin(PluginBase):
 37|    """pymoo NSGA2 多目标调度器"""
 38|
 39|    SELECTION_MODES = {"energy_priority", "balanced", "temp_priority"}
 40|    SELECTION_WEIGHTS = {
 41|        "energy_priority": (1.0, 0.3, 5.0),
 42|        "balanced": (1.0, 1.0, 10.0),
 43|        "temp_priority": (0.3, 1.0, 5.0),
 44|    }
 45|
 47|    # 清单 / 配置
 49|
 50|    def get_manifest(self) -> PluginManifest:
 51|        return PluginManifest(
 52|            name="NSGA2SchedulerPlugin",
 53|            version="1.0.0",
 54|            supported_operations=["schedule"],
 55|            input_schema={
 56|                "pop_size": {"type": "int", "default": 100, "min": 4},
 57|                "n_gen": {"type": "int", "default": 200, "min": 1},
 58|                "selection": {
 59|                    "type": "str",
 60|                    "enum": sorted(self.SELECTION_MODES),
 61|                    "default": "balanced",
 62|                },
 63|                "seed": {"type": "int", "default": 42},
 64|            },
 65|            output_schema={
 66|                "assignments": "list",
 67|                "pareto_size": "int",
 68|                "generation": "int",
 69|                "objectives": "dict",
 70|            },
 71|            min_system_version="1.0.0",
 72|            category="algorithm",
 73|        )
 74|
 75|    def validate_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
 76|        selection = config.get("selection", "balanced")
 77|        if selection not in self.SELECTION_MODES:
 78|            self.logger.error(
 79|                f"selection 必须在 {self.SELECTION_MODES} 中, 当前={sele
   |    ction}"
 80|            )
 81|            return False
 82|        pop_size = config.get("pop_size", 100)
 83|        if not isinstance(pop_size, int) or pop_size < 4:
 84|            self.logger.error(f"pop_size 必须 >= 4, 当前={pop_size}")
 85|            return False
 86|        n_gen = config.get("n_gen", 200)
 87|        if not isinstance(n_gen, int) or n_gen < 1:
 88|            self.logger.error(f"n_gen 必须 >= 1, 当前={n_gen}")
 89|            return False
 90|        return True
 91|
 93|    # 生命周期
 95|
 96|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 97|        cfg = dict(config or {})
 98|        if not self.validate_config(cfg):
 99|            return False
100|
101|        self._deps_ok = has_pymoo()
102|        self._pop_size: int = int(cfg.get("pop_size", 100))
103|        self._n_gen: int = int(cfg.get("n_gen", 200))
104|        self._selection: str = str(cfg.get("selection", "balanced"))
105|        self._seed: Optional[int] = cfg.get("seed", 42)
106|        return True
107|
108|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
109|        return None
110|
112|    # 执行
114|
115|    def execute(self, **kwargs: Any) -> PluginResult:
116|        if not has_pymoo():
117|            return PluginResult.fail(
118|                message="pymoo 未安装。请执行: pip install pymoo>=0.6",
119|                error_code="DEPENDENCY_MISSING",
120|            )
121|        tasks: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("tasks")
122|        servers: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("servers")
123|        if not tasks or servers is None:
124|            return PluginResult.fail(
125|                message="NSGA2 调度需要 tasks 与 servers 参数",
126|                error_code="MISSING_PARAM",
127|            )
128|        try:
129|            return self._solve(tasks, servers)
130|        except Exception as e:
131|            self.logger.error(f"NSGA2 调度失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
132|            return PluginResult.fail(
133|                message=f"NSGA2 调度失败: {e}",
134|                error_code="NSGA2_ERROR",
135|                error_detail=str(e),
136|            )
137|
139|    # 内部
141|
142|    def _solve(self, tasks: List[Any], servers: List[Any]) -> PluginResu
   |    lt:
143|        from pymoo.core.problem import ElementwiseProblem
144|        from pymoo.algorithms.moo.nsga2 import NSGA2
145|        from pymoo.operators.crossover.sbx import SBX
146|        from pymoo.operators.mutation.pm import PM
147|        from pymoo.operators.repair.rounding import RoundingRepair
148|        from pymoo.operators.sampling.rnd import IntegerRandomSampling
149|        from pymoo.optimize import minimize
150|        from pymoo.termination import get_termination
151|
152|        num_tasks = len(tasks)
153|        num_servers = len(servers)
154|        if num_tasks == 0 or num_servers == 0:
155|            return PluginResult.fail(
156|                message="tasks 与 servers 均不能为空",
157|                error_code="EMPTY_INPUT",
158|            )
159|
160|        # 任务/服务器静态特征（提前计算一次）
161|        task_loads = [float(t.load) for t in tasks]
162|        task_deadlines = [
163|            t.deadline if t.deadline is not None else 10**9 for t in tas
   |    ks
164|        ]
165|        max_loads = [float(s.max_load) for s in servers]
166|        base_powers = [float(s.base_power) for s in servers]
167|        max_powers = [float(s.max_power) for s in servers]
168|        init_loads = [float(s.current_load) for s in servers]
169|        max_loads_safe = [max(ml, 1.0) for ml in max_loads]
170|
171|        # 保存 servers 副本用于目标评估（避免污染输入对象）
172|        class SchedulingProblem(ElementwiseProblem):
173|            def __init__(self_inner):
174|                super().__init__(
175|                    n_var=num_tasks,
176|                    n_obj=3,
177|                    n_ieq_constr=1,
178|                    xl=0,
179|                    xu=num_servers - 1,
180|                )
181|
182|            def _evaluate(self_inner, x, out, *args, **kwargs):
183|                assign = [int(round(float(v))) for v in x]
184|                assign = [max(0, min(num_servers - 1, a)) for a in assig
   |    n]
185|                srv_load = list(init_loads)
186|                for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
187|                    srv_load[s_idx] += task_loads[i]
188|                # 目标 1: 总能耗
189|                total_energy = 0.0
190|                for j in range(num_servers):
191|                    lf = srv_load[j] / max_loads_safe[j]
192|                    total_energy += base_powers[j] + (max_powers[j] - ba
   |    se_powers[j]) * lf
193|                # 目标 2: 最高温度代理
194|                lfs = [srv_load[j] / max_loads_safe[j] for j in range(nu
   |    m_servers)]
195|                max_temp_proxy = 25.0 + 60.0 * max(lfs) if lfs else 25.0
196|                # 目标 3: deadline 违约（紧 deadline + 高负载判定）
197|                violations = 0
198|                for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
199|                    if task_deadlines[i] < 10 and (
200|                        srv_load[s_idx] / max_loads_safe[s_idx] > 0.9
201|                    ):
202|                        violations += 1
203|                # 约束: 容量超载
204|                overload = sum(
205|                    max(0.0, srv_load[j] - max_loads[j]) for j in range(
   |    num_servers)
206|                )
207|                out["F"] = [float(total_energy), float(max_temp_proxy), 
   |    float(violations)]
208|                out["G"] = [float(overload)]
209|
210|        algorithm = NSGA2(
211|            pop_size=self._pop_size,
212|            sampling=IntegerRandomSampling(),
213|            crossover=SBX(prob=0.9, eta=3.0, repair=RoundingRepair()),
214|            mutation=PM(prob=1.0 / max(num_tasks, 1), eta=3.0, repair=Ro
   |    undingRepair()),
215|        )
216|        res = minimize(
217|            SchedulingProblem(),
218|            algorithm,
219|            termination=get_termination("n_gen", self._n_gen),
220|            seed=self._seed,
221|            verbose=False,
222|        )
223|
224|        if res.X is None or (hasattr(res.X, "shape") and res.X.shape[0] 
   |    == 0):
225|            return PluginResult.fail(
226|                message="NSGA2 未找到可行解",
227|                error_code="NO_SOLUTION",
228|            )
229|
230|        # res.F shape = (N, 3); res.X shape = (N, num_tasks) or (num_tas
   |    ks,) if N==1
231|        F = res.F
232|        X = res.X
233|        if len(F.shape) == 1:
234|            F = F.reshape(1, -1)
235|            X = X.reshape(1, -1)
236|        idx = self._select_by_preference(F)
237|        best_x = X[idx]
238|        assign = [int(round(float(v))) for v in best_x]
239|        assign = [max(0, min(num_servers - 1, a)) for a in assign]
240|
241|        assignments: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
242|        for i, s_idx in enumerate(assign):
243|            assignments.append(
244|                {
245|                    "task_id": getattr(tasks[i], "task_id", str(i)),
246|                    "server_id": s_idx,
247|                    "load": task_loads[i],
248|                }
249|            )
250|        return PluginResult.ok(
251|            data={
252|                "assignments": assignments,
253|                "pareto_size": int(F.shape[0]),
254|                "generation": self._n_gen,
255|                "objectives": {
256|                    "energy": float(F[idx, 0]),
257|                    "max_temp_proxy": float(F[idx, 1]),
258|                    "deadline_violations": float(F[idx, 2]),
259|                },
260|                "selection": self._selection,
261|            },
262|            message=f"NSGA2 调度完成: {len(assignments)} 个任务, pareto=
   |    {F.shape[0]}",
263|        )
264|
265|    def _select_by_preference(self, F: Any) -> int:
266|        """根据 selection 偏好从 Pareto 前沿选解。"""
267|        w = self.SELECTION_WEIGHTS.get(self._selection, (1.0, 1.0, 10.0)
   |    )
268|        # 归一化每列 → 加权和 → 最小索引
269|        import numpy as np
270|        Fs = np.asarray(F, dtype=float)
271|        mins = Fs.min(axis=0)
272|        maxs = Fs.max(axis=0)
273|        rng = np.where((maxs - mins) > 0, (maxs - mins), 1.0)
274|        norm = (Fs - mins) / rng
275|        scores = norm @ np.array(w, dtype=float)
276|        return int(np.argmin(scores))
277|
278|
279|__all__ = ["NSGA2SchedulerPlugin"]

================================================================================
45. src/plugins/rl_ppo_scheduler.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|RLSchedulerPlugin — Stable Baselines3 PPO 强化学习调度器 (GitHub Skills 
   |    集成)
  3|
  4|继承 ``PluginBase`` v1.0，实现:
  5|
  6|    * ``train``     — 用 PPO 在 DataCenterGymEnv 上训练，保存模型到 ``mod
   |    el_path``
  7|    * ``predict``   — 加载已训练模型，对一批 ``tasks`` 选出 server_id
  8|    * ``schedule``  — predict 的别名，方便直接 ``algorithms=["rl_ppo"]`` 
   |    调用
  9|    * ``save``/``load`` — 显式的 SB3 模型持久化入口
 10|
 11|依赖:
 12|    pip install gymnasium stable-baselines3
 13|
 14|依赖缺失时会返回 ``PluginResult.fail(error_code="DEPENDENCY_MISSING")`` 
   |    而不崩溃。
 15|
 16|来源: https://github.com/DLR-RM/stable-baselines3 (MIT)
 17|"""
 18|
 19|from __future__ import annotations
 20|
 21|import os
 22|import time
 23|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
 24|
 25|from src.plugin.base import PluginBase
 26|from src.plugin.protocol import PluginManifest, PluginResult
 27|from src.plugins._deps import has_gymnasium, has_sb3
 28|
 29|
 30|class RLSchedulerPlugin(PluginBase):
 31|    """Stable Baselines3 PPO 调度器插件"""
 32|
 34|    # 清单 / 配置
 36|
 37|    def get_manifest(self) -> PluginManifest:
 38|        return PluginManifest(
 39|            name="RLSchedulerPlugin",
 40|            version="1.0.0",
 41|            supported_operations=["train", "predict", "schedule", "save"
   |    , "load"],
 42|            input_schema={
 43|                "algorithms": {"type": "list", "default": ["rl_ppo"]},
 44|                "train": {"type": "bool", "default": False},
 45|                "total_timesteps": {"type": "int", "default": 10000, "mi
   |    n": 1},
 46|                "model_path": {"type": "str", "default": "data/policies/
   |    ppo_datacenter.zip"},
 47|                "learning_rate": {"type": "float", "default": 3e-4},
 48|                "n_steps": {"type": "int", "default": 128},
 49|                "batch_size": {"type": "int", "default": 32},
 50|                "num_servers": {"type": "int", "default": 10, "min": 1},
 51|                "simulation_hours": {"type": "int", "default": 24, "min"
   |    : 1},
 52|                "seed": {"type": "int", "default": 42},
 53|            },
 54|            output_schema={
 55|                "model_path": "str",
 56|                "assignments": "list",
 57|                "training_time_s": "float",
 58|            },
 59|            min_system_version="1.0.0",
 60|            category="algorithm",
 61|        )
 62|
 63|    def validate_config(self, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
 64|        num_servers = config.get("num_servers", 10)
 65|        if not isinstance(num_servers, int) or num_servers < 1:
 66|            self.logger.error(f"num_servers 必须 >= 1, 当前={num_servers
   |    }")
 67|            return False
 68|        sim_hours = config.get("simulation_hours", 24)
 69|        if not isinstance(sim_hours, int) or sim_hours < 1:
 70|            self.logger.error(f"simulation_hours 必须 >= 1, 当前={sim_ho
   |    urs}")
 71|            return False
 72|        return True
 73|
 75|    # 生命周期
 77|
 78|    def initialize(self, config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> boo
   |    l:
 79|        cfg = dict(config or {})
 80|        if not self.validate_config(cfg):
 81|            return False
 82|
 83|        # 依赖检查（若缺失则初始化失败 — 符合测试契约）
 84|        if not (has_gymnasium() and has_sb3()):
 85|            self.logger.warning(
 86|                "stable-baselines3 / gymnasium 未安装，RLSchedulerPlugin 
   |    初始化失败。"
 87|                "请执行: pip install stable-baselines3 gymnasium"
 88|            )
 89|            self._deps_ok = False
 90|            return False
 91|
 92|        self._deps_ok = True
 93|        self._num_servers: int = int(cfg.get("num_servers", 10))
 94|        self._simulation_hours: int = int(cfg.get("simulation_hours", 24
   |    ))
 95|        self._seed: int = int(cfg.get("seed", 42))
 96|        self._learning_rate: float = float(cfg.get("learning_rate", 3e-4
   |    ))
 97|        self._n_steps: int = int(cfg.get("n_steps", 128))
 98|        self._batch_size: int = int(cfg.get("batch_size", 32))
 99|        self._default_model_path: str = str(
100|            cfg.get("model_path", "data/policies/ppo_datacenter.zip")
101|        )
102|
103|        self._env = None
104|        self._model = None
105|        return True
106|
107|    def cleanup(self) -> None:
108|        self._env = None
109|        self._model = None
110|
112|    # 执行
114|
115|    def execute(self, **kwargs: Any) -> PluginResult:
116|        # 依赖缺失 → 直接降级返回
117|        if not (has_gymnasium() and has_sb3()):
118|            return PluginResult.fail(
119|                message=(
120|                    "stable-baselines3 / gymnasium 未安装。请先执行: "
121|                    "pip install stable-baselines3 gymnasium"
122|                ),
123|                error_code="DEPENDENCY_MISSING",
124|            )
125|
126|        algorithms: List[str] = list(kwargs.get("algorithms") or ["rl_pp
   |    o"])
127|        is_train: bool = bool(kwargs.get("train", False))
128|        total_timesteps: int = int(kwargs.get("total_timesteps", 10000))
129|        model_path: Optional[str] = kwargs.get("model_path") or self._de
   |    fault_model_path
130|        learning_rate: float = float(kwargs.get("learning_rate", self._l
   |    earning_rate))
131|        tasks: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("tasks")
132|        servers: Optional[List[Any]] = kwargs.get("servers")
133|
134|        if is_train:
135|            return self._train_model(
136|                total_timesteps=total_timesteps,
137|                model_path=model_path,
138|                learning_rate=learning_rate,
139|            )
140|        return self._predict_and_schedule(
141|            tasks=tasks, servers=servers, model_path=model_path
142|        )
143|
145|    # 内部：训练 / 推理
147|
148|    def _train_model(
149|        self, total_timesteps: int, model_path: str, learning_rate: floa
   |    t
150|    ) -> PluginResult:
151|        try:
152|            from stable_baselines3 import PPO
153|            from src.plugins.datacenter_gym_env import DataCenterGymEnv
154|
155|            self._env = DataCenterGymEnv(
156|                num_servers=self._num_servers,
157|                simulation_hours=self._simulation_hours,
158|                seed=self._seed,
159|            )
160|            self._model = PPO(
161|                "MlpPolicy",
162|                self._env,
163|                learning_rate=learning_rate,
164|                n_steps=self._n_steps,
165|                batch_size=self._batch_size,
166|                verbose=0,
167|                seed=self._seed,
168|            )
169|            start = time.perf_counter()
170|            self._model.learn(total_timesteps=total_timesteps)
171|            elapsed = round(time.perf_counter() - start, 3)
172|
173|            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(model_path) or ".", exist_ok=Tru
   |    e)
174|            self._model.save(model_path)
175|
176|            return PluginResult.ok(
177|                data={
178|                    "model_path": model_path,
179|                    "training_time_s": elapsed,
180|                    "total_timesteps": total_timesteps,
181|                },
182|                message=f"PPO 训练完成: {total_timesteps} steps, {elapse
   |    d}s",
183|            )
184|        except Exception as e:
185|            self.logger.error(f"训练失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
186|            return PluginResult.fail(
187|                message=f"训练失败: {e}",
188|                error_code="TRAIN_ERROR",
189|                error_detail=str(e),
190|            )
191|
192|    def _predict_and_schedule(
193|        self,
194|        tasks: Optional[List[Any]],
195|        servers: Optional[List[Any]],
196|        model_path: Optional[str],
197|    ) -> PluginResult:
198|        if not tasks or servers is None:
199|            return PluginResult.fail(
200|                message="推理模式需要 tasks 与 servers 参数",
201|                error_code="MISSING_PARAM",
202|            )
203|        if not model_path or not os.path.exists(model_path):
204|            return PluginResult.fail(
205|                message=f"模型文件不存在: {model_path}",
206|                error_code="MODEL_NOT_FOUND",
207|            )
208|        try:
209|            from stable_baselines3 import PPO
210|            from src.plugins.datacenter_gym_env import DataCenterGymEnv
211|
212|            model = PPO.load(model_path)
213|            env = DataCenterGymEnv(
214|                num_servers=self._num_servers,
215|                simulation_hours=self._simulation_hours,
216|                seed=self._seed,
217|            )
218|            assignments: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
219|            obs, _info = env.reset()
220|            for task in tasks:
221|                # 取 env 内部 _current_task = task，然后用 model.predict
222|                env._current_task = task  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
223|                obs = env._get_obs()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
224|                action, _state = model.predict(obs, deterministic=True)
225|                server_id = int(action)
226|                if 0 <= server_id < len(servers):
227|                    server = servers[server_id]
228|                    if server.assign_task(task):
229|                        assignments.append(
230|                            {
231|                                "task_id": task.task_id,
232|                                "server_id": server_id,
233|                                "load": task.load,
234|                            }
235|                        )
236|                        continue
237|                # 默认分配：负载最低的服务器
238|                fallback = min(
239|                    range(len(servers)),
240|                    key=lambda i: servers[i].current_load / max(servers[
   |    i].max_load, 1.0),
241|                )
242|                if servers[fallback].assign_task(task):
243|                    assignments.append(
244|                        {
245|                            "task_id": task.task_id,
246|                            "server_id": fallback,
247|                            "load": task.load,
248|                            "fallback": True,
249|                        }
250|                    )
251|                else:
252|                    assignments.append(
253|                        {"task_id": task.task_id, "server_id": None, "re
   |    jected": True}
254|                    )
255|            return PluginResult.ok(
256|                data={
257|                    "assignments": assignments,
258|                    "model_path": model_path,
259|                    "total_tasks": len(assignments),
260|                },
261|                message=f"PPO 推理完成: {len(assignments)} 个任务",
262|            )
263|        except Exception as e:
264|            self.logger.error(f"推理失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
265|            return PluginResult.fail(
266|                message=f"推理失败: {e}",
267|                error_code="PREDICT_ERROR",
268|                error_detail=str(e),
269|            )
270|
271|
272|__all__ = ["RLSchedulerPlugin"]

  1|"""
  2|校园数据中心智能调度系统 - SDK客户端 v1.0
  3|
  4|提供简洁的API接口，支持多环境自适应对接。
  5|
  6|支持的接入方式:
  7|1. 本地模式    - 直接调用本地插件（自动检测依赖可用性）
  8|2. 远程模式    - 通过REST API调用远程服务（含连接池+重试）
  9|3. 混合模式    - 本地优先，远程兜底（自动故障转移）
 10|
 11|环境自适应:
 12|- CPU核心数检测 → 自动调整并行度
 13|- GPU/昇腾NPU检测 → 硬件加速策略
 14|- 操作系统识别 → 平台特定优化
 15|- Python版本检测 → 兼容性适配
 16|
 17|示例用法:
 18|    # 自动检测环境
 19|    sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(auto_detect=True)
 20|    
 21|    # 本地模式
 22|    sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode='local')
 23|    result = sdk.run_simulation(num_servers=10, algorithms=['greedy', 'g
   |    enetic'])
 24|    
 25|    # 远程模式（含重试）
 26|    sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode='remote', api_url="http://localhos
   |    t:5000")
 27|    result = sdk.run_simulation(num_servers=10)
 28|    
 29|    # 混合模式（自动故障转移）
 30|    sdk = DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode='hybrid', api_url="http://localhos
   |    t:5000")
 31|"""
 32|
 33|import requests
 34|import platform
 35|import psutil
 36|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Union, Callable
 37|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 38|from urllib3.util.retry import Retry
 39|from requests.adapters import HTTPAdapter
 40|from src.plugin import PluginManager
 41|from src.plugin.simulation_plugin import SimulationPlugin
 42|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 43|import os

 47|@dataclass
 48|class EnvironmentInfo:
 49|    """环境信息数据结构"""
 50|    os_name: str = ""
 51|    os_version: str = ""
 52|    python_version: str = ""
 53|    cpu_cores: int = 1
 54|    cpu_logical: int = 1
 55|    total_memory_gb: float = 0
 56|    available_memory_gb: float = 0
 57|    has_gpu: bool = False
 58|    gpu_count: int = 0
 59|    has_ascend_npu: bool = False
 60|    is_docker: bool = False
 61|    is_kubernetes: bool = False
 62|    platform_arch: str = ""
 63|
 64|class EnvironmentDetector:
 65|    """环境探测器 - 自动检测运行时环境"""
 66|
 67|    @staticmethod
 68|    def detect() -> EnvironmentInfo:
 69|        info = EnvironmentInfo()
 70|        
 71|        info.os_name = platform.system()
 72|        info.os_version = platform.version()
 73|        info.python_version = platform.python_version()
 74|        info.platform_arch = platform.machine()
 75|        
 76|        try:
 77|            info.cpu_cores = psutil.cpu_count(logical=False) or 1
 78|            info.cpu_logical = psutil.cpu_count(logical=True) or 1
 79|        except Exception:
 80|            info.cpu_cores = 1
 81|            info.cpu_logical = 1
 82|        
 83|        try:
 84|            mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
 85|            info.total_memory_gb = mem.total / (1024 ** 3)
 86|            info.available_memory_gb = mem.available / (1024 ** 3)
 87|        except Exception:
 88|            info.total_memory_gb = 0
 89|            info.available_memory_gb = 0
 90|        
 91|        info.has_gpu = EnvironmentDetector._detect_gpu()
 92|        info.gpu_count = EnvironmentDetector._count_gpu()
 93|        info.has_ascend_npu = EnvironmentDetector._detect_ascend()
 94|        info.is_docker = EnvironmentDetector._detect_docker()
 95|        info.is_kubernetes = EnvironmentDetector._detect_kubernetes()
 96|        
 97|        return info
 98|
 99|    @staticmethod
100|    def _detect_gpu() -> bool:
101|        try:
102|            import torch
103|            return torch.cuda.is_available()
104|        except ImportError:
105|            return False
106|
107|    @staticmethod
108|    def _count_gpu() -> int:
109|        try:
110|            import torch
111|            return torch.cuda.device_count() if torch.cuda.is_available(
   |    ) else 0
112|        except ImportError:
113|            return 0
114|
115|    @staticmethod
116|    def _detect_ascend() -> bool:
117|        try:
118|            import torch_npu
119|            return torch_npu.npu.is_available()
120|        except ImportError:
121|            pass
122|        if os.path.exists('/usr/local/Ascend'):
123|            return True
124|        return False
125|
126|    @staticmethod
127|    def _detect_docker() -> bool:
128|        return os.path.exists('/.dockerenv') or 'docker' in open('/proc/
   |    1/cgroup', 'r').read() if os.path.exists('/proc/1/cgroup') else Fals
   |    e
129|
130|    @staticmethod
131|    def _detect_kubernetes() -> bool:
132|        return bool(os.environ.get('KUBERNETES_SERVICE_HOST'))
133|
134|class RetryableSession:
135|    """可重试的HTTP会话"""
136|
137|    def __init__(self, max_retries: int = 3, backoff_factor: float = 0.5
   |    ,
138|                 status_forcelist: List[int] = None):
139|        self.session = requests.Session()
140|        
141|        if status_forcelist is None:
142|            status_forcelist = [429, 500, 502, 503, 504]
143|        
144|        retry_strategy = Retry(
145|            total=max_retries,
146|            backoff_factor=backoff_factor,
147|            status_forcelist=status_forcelist,
148|            allowed_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT"]
149|        )
150|        
151|        adapter = HTTPAdapter(
152|            max_retries=retry_strategy,
153|            pool_connections=10,
154|            pool_maxsize=20
155|        )
156|        
157|        self.session.mount("http://", adapter)
158|        self.session.mount("https://", adapter)
159|        self.session.headers.update({
160|            'User-Agent': f'DataCenterSchedulerSDK/2.0 ({platform.system
   |    ()}; Python/{platform.python_version()})'
161|        })
162|
163|    def post(self, url: str, json_data: Dict = None, timeout: int = 120)
   |     -> requests.Response:
164|        return self.session.post(url, json=json_data, timeout=timeout)
165|
166|    def get(self, url: str, timeout: int = 30) -> requests.Response:
167|        return self.session.get(url, timeout=timeout)
168|
169|    def close(self):
170|        self.session.close()
171|
172|class DataCenterSimulatorSDK:
173|    """
174|    数据中心仿真SDK v1.0
175|    
176|    支持三种运行模式:
177|    - local: 纯本地调用
178|    - remote: 纯远程API调用
179|    - hybrid: 本地优先，远程兜底
180|    """
181|
182|    def __init__(
183|        self,
184|        mode: str = 'auto',
185|        api_url: Optional[str] = None,
186|        max_retries: int = 3,
187|        timeout: int = 300,
188|        auto_detect: bool = True
189|    ):
190|        """
191|        初始化SDK
192|        
193|        Args:
194|            mode: 运行模式 ('auto', 'local', 'remote', 'hybrid')
195|            api_url: 远程API地址（remote/hybrid模式需要）
196|            max_retries: 远程请求最大重试次数
197|            timeout: 请求超时秒数
198|            auto_detect: 是否自动检测环境
199|        """
200|        self.max_retries = max_retries
201|        self.timeout = timeout
202|        self.plugin_manager = None
203|        self.session: Optional[RetryableSession] = None
204|        
205|        if auto_detect:
206|            self.env_info = EnvironmentDetector.detect()
207|            logger.info(f"Environment detected: {self.env_info}")
208|        else:
209|            self.env_info = EnvironmentInfo()
210|        
211|        self._resolve_mode(mode, api_url)
212|        
213|        if self._local_mode or self._is_hybrid():
214|            self.plugin_manager = PluginManager()
215|            self.plugin_manager.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin)
216|            logger.info("SDK initialized in LOCAL/HYBRID mode with plugi
   |    n manager")
217|        
218|        if self._remote_mode or self._is_hybrid():
219|            if not api_url:
220|                api_url = "http://localhost:5000"
221|            self.api_url = api_url.rstrip('/')
222|            self.session = RetryableSession(max_retries=max_retries)
223|            logger.info(f"SDK initialized with remote endpoint: {self.ap
   |    i_url}")
224|
225|    def _resolve_mode(self, mode: str, api_url: Optional[str]) -> None:
226|        if mode == 'auto':
227|            if api_url:
228|                self._mode = 'hybrid'
229|            else:
230|                self._mode = 'local'
231|        else:
232|            self._mode = mode
233|        
234|        self._local_mode = self._mode == 'local'
235|        self._remote_mode = self._mode == 'remote'
236|
237|    def _is_hybrid(self) -> bool:
238|        return self._mode == 'hybrid'
239|
240|    def _auto_parallel_workers(self) -> int:
241|        cores = self.env_info.cpu_cores
242|        if cores <= 2:
243|            return 1
244|        elif cores <= 4:
245|            return 2
246|        elif cores <= 8:
247|            return 4
248|        else:
249|            return min(cores // 2, 32)
250|
251|    def get_environment_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
252|        """获取环境检测信息"""
253|        return {
254|            'os': self.env_info.os_name,
255|            'os_version': self.env_info.os_version,
256|            'python_version': self.env_info.python_version,
257|            'cpu_cores': self.env_info.cpu_cores,
258|            'cpu_logical': self.env_info.cpu_logical,
259|            'memory_gb': round(self.env_info.total_memory_gb, 2),
260|            'has_gpu': self.env_info.has_gpu,
261|            'gpu_count': self.env_info.gpu_count,
262|            'has_ascend_npu': self.env_info.has_ascend_npu,
263|            'is_docker': self.env_info.is_docker,
264|            'is_kubernetes': self.env_info.is_kubernetes,
265|            'platform_arch': self.env_info.platform_arch,
266|            'recommended_workers': self._auto_parallel_workers()
267|        }
268|
269|    def run_simulation(
270|        self,
271|        num_servers: int = 10,
272|        simulation_hours: int = 24,
273|        algorithms: List[str] = None,
274|        parallel_workers: Optional[int] = None,
275|        run_mode: str = 'standard',
276|        visualization: bool = True,
277|        save_results: bool = True
278|    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
279|        """
280|        运行仿真
281|        
282|        Args:
283|            num_servers: 服务器数量 (1-1000)
284|            simulation_hours: 仿真时长（小时）(1-720)
285|            algorithms: 算法列表
286|            parallel_workers: 并行工作器数（None=自动检测）
287|            run_mode: 运行模式 ('standard', 'distributed', 'large_scale'
   |    )
288|            visualization: 是否生成可视化图表
289|            save_results: 是否保存结果
290|        
291|        Returns:
292|            仿真结果字典
293|        """
294|        if algorithms is None:
295|            algorithms = ['greedy', 'genetic', 'round_robin']
296|        
297|        if parallel_workers is None:
298|            parallel_workers = self._auto_parallel_workers()
299|        
300|        config = {
301|            'num_servers': num_servers,
302|            'simulation_hours': simulation_hours,
303|            'parallel_workers': parallel_workers,
304|            'run_mode': run_mode,
305|        }
306|
307|        if self._is_hybrid():
308|            return self._run_hybrid(config, algorithms, visualization, s
   |    ave_results)
309|        elif self._local_mode:
310|            return self._run_local(config, algorithms, visualization, sa
   |    ve_results)
311|        else:
312|            return self._run_remote(config, algorithms)
313|
314|    def _run_hybrid(
315|        self, config: Dict[str, Any], algorithms: List[str],
316|        visualization: bool, save_results: bool
317|    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
318|        try:
319|            return self._run_local(config, algorithms, visualization, sa
   |    ve_results)
320|        except Exception as e:
321|            logger.warning(f"Local execution failed ({e}), falling back 
   |    to remote")
322|            if self.session:
323|                return self._run_remote(config, algorithms)
324|            return {'success': False, 'error': f'Local failed and remote
   |     not available: {e}'}
325|
326|    def _run_local(
327|        self, config: Dict[str, Any], algorithms: List[str],
328|        visualization: bool, save_results: bool
329|    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
330|        try:
331|            self.plugin_manager.load_plugin('SimulationPlugin', config)
332|            result = self.plugin_manager.execute_plugin(
333|                'SimulationPlugin',
334|                algorithms=algorithms,
335|                visualization=visualization,
336|                save_results=save_results
337|            )
338|            self.plugin_manager.unload_plugin('SimulationPlugin')
339|            return result
340|        except Exception as e:
341|            logger.error(f"Local simulation error: {str(e)}")
342|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
343|
344|    def _run_remote(
345|        self, config: Dict[str, Any], algorithms: List[str]
346|    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
347|        try:
348|            url = f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/simulation"
349|            payload = {
350|                'num_servers': config.get('num_servers', 10),
351|                'simulation_hours': config.get('simulation_hours', 24),
352|                'parallel_workers': config.get('parallel_workers', 4),
353|                'run_mode': config.get('run_mode', 'standard'),
354|                'algorithms': algorithms
355|            }
356|            
357|            response = self.session.post(url, json_data=payload, timeout
   |    =self.timeout)
358|            response.raise_for_status()
359|            return response.json()
360|        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
361|            logger.error(f"Remote API error: {str(e)}")
362|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
363|
364|    def get_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
365|        if self._is_hybrid():
366|            return self._try_local_then_remote('get_metrics')
367|        return self._get_metrics_local() if self._local_mode else self._
   |    get_metrics_remote()
368|
369|    def _get_metrics_local(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
370|        from src.utils import load_simulation_data
371|        import pandas as pd
372|        
373|        df = load_simulation_data()
374|        if df is None:
375|            return {'success': False, 'message': 'No data found'}
376|        
377|        result = {}
378|        for algo in df['algorithm'].unique():
379|            algo_df = df[df['algorithm'] == algo]
380|            result[algo] = {
381|                'avg_energy': float(algo_df['total_energy'].mean()),
382|                'max_temp': float(algo_df['avg_temperature'].max()),
383|                'avg_pue': float(algo_df['pue'].mean()),
384|                'total_load': float(algo_df['total_load'].sum())
385|            }
386|        return {'success': True, 'data': result}
387|
388|    def _get_metrics_remote(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
389|        try:
390|            response = self.session.get(f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/metrics"
   |    )
391|            response.raise_for_status()
392|            return response.json()
393|        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
394|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
395|
396|    def get_raw_data(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
397|        if self._local_mode or self._is_hybrid():
398|            return self._get_raw_data_local()
399|        return self._get_raw_data_remote()
400|
401|    def _get_raw_data_local(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
402|        from src.utils import load_simulation_data
403|        df = load_simulation_data()
404|        if df is None:
405|            return {'success': False, 'message': 'No data found'}
406|        return {'success': True, 'data': df.to_dict('records')}
407|
408|    def _get_raw_data_remote(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
409|        try:
410|            response = self.session.get(f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/data")
411|            response.raise_for_status()
412|            return response.json()
413|        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
414|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
415|
416|    def _try_local_then_remote(self, method: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
417|        try:
418|            local_result = getattr(self, f'_{method}_local')()
419|            if local_result.get('success'):
420|                return local_result
421|        except Exception:
422|            pass
423|        try:
424|            return getattr(self, f'_{method}_remote')()
425|        except Exception as e:
426|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
427|
428|    def list_algorithms(self) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
429|        return [
430|            {'name': 'greedy', 'description': '贪心调度算法', 'type': '
   |    实时调度'},
431|            {'name': 'genetic', 'description': '遗传优化算法', 'type': '
   |    全局优化'},

   |    ': '基准算法'},

   |    耗+温度+deadline）',

436|             'source': 'github.com/anyoptimization/pymoo'},

   |    ',
438|             'type': '多目标优化', 'plugin': 'MOEADSchedulerPlugin',
439|             'source': 'github.com/anyoptimization/pymoo'},

442|    def list_plugins(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
443|        if self._local_mode or self._is_hybrid():
444|            plugins = []
445|            for name, plugin_class in self.plugin_manager._plugin_classe
   |    s.items():
446|                is_loaded = name in self.plugin_manager._plugin_instance
   |    s
447|                manifest = plugin_class().get_manifest()
448|                plugins.append({
449|                    'name': name, 'version': manifest.version,
450|                    'description': ",".join(manifest.supported_operation
   |    s), 'loaded': is_loaded
451|                })
452|            return {'success': True, 'plugins': plugins}
453|        try:
454|            response = self.session.get(f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/plugins"
   |    )
455|            response.raise_for_status()
456|            return response.json()
457|        except Exception as e:
458|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
459|
460|    def load_plugin(self, plugin_name: str, config: Optional[Dict[str, A
   |    ny]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
461|        if self._local_mode or self._is_hybrid():
462|            success = self.plugin_manager.load_plugin(plugin_name, confi
   |    g or {})
463|            if success:
464|                info = self.plugin_manager.get_plugin_info(plugin_name)
465|                return {'success': True, 'message': 'Plugin loaded', 'me
   |    tadata': info.to_dict() if info else {}}
466|            return {'success': False, 'message': 'Failed to load plugin'
   |    }
467|        try:
468|            response = self.session.post(f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/plugins
   |    /{plugin_name}/load", json_data=config or {})
469|            response.raise_for_status()
470|            return response.json()
471|        except Exception as e:
472|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
473|
474|    def unload_plugin(self, plugin_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
475|        if self._local_mode or self._is_hybrid():
476|            self.plugin_manager.unload_plugin(plugin_name)
477|            return {'success': True, 'message': 'Plugin unloaded'}
478|        try:
479|            response = self.session.post(f"{self.api_url}/api/v1/plugins
   |    /{plugin_name}/unload")
480|            response.raise_for_status()
481|            return response.json()
482|        except Exception as e:
483|            return {'success': False, 'error': str(e)}
484|
485|    def health_check(self) -> bool:
486|        if self._local_mode:
487|            return True
488|        if self._is_hybrid():
489|            local_ok = self.plugin_manager is not None
490|            remote_ok = False
491|            if self.session:
492|                try:
493|                    response = self.session.get(f"{self.api_url}/health"
   |    , timeout=5)
494|                    remote_ok = response.status_code == 200
495|                except Exception:
496|                    pass
497|            return local_ok or remote_ok
498|        try:
499|            response = self.session.get(f"{self.api_url}/health", timeou
   |    t=5)
500|            return response.status_code == 200
501|        except Exception:
502|            return False
503|
504|    def get_system_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
505|        env = self.get_environment_info()
506|        loaded = len(self.plugin_manager._plugin_instances) if self.plug
   |    in_manager else 0
507|        registered = len(self.plugin_manager._plugin_classes) if self.pl
   |    ugin_manager else 0
508|        
509|        return {
510|            'success': True,
511|            'status': {
512|                'mode': self._mode,
513|                'loaded_plugins': loaded,
514|                'registered_plugins': registered,
515|                'status': 'healthy' if self.health_check() else 'degrade
   |    d'
516|            },
517|            'environment': env
518|        }
519|
520|    def close(self):
521|        if self.plugin_manager:
522|            for name in list(self.plugin_manager._plugin_instances.keys(
   |    )):
523|                self.plugin_manager.unload_plugin(name)
524|        if self.session:
525|            self.session.close()
526|        logger.info("SDK closed")
527|
528|    def __repr__(self) -> str:
529|        return f"DataCenterSimulatorSDK(mode={self._mode}, api_url={self
   |    .api_url if hasattr(self, 'api_url') else None})"
530|
531|    def __enter__(self):
532|        return self
533|
534|    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
535|        self.close()
536|        return False

 67|DISPLAY_NAMES = {
 68|    "damo_ets": "DAMO-ETS",
 69|    "round_robin": "轮询算法",
 70|    "greedy": "贪心算法",
 71|    "genetic": "遗传算法",

 76|_NORMALIZED_NAMES = {
 77|    re.sub(r"[_\-\s]", "", key): value for key, value in DISPLAY_NAMES.i
   |    tems()
 78|}

 80|_ALGO_PATTERN = re.compile(
 81|    r"\b(damo[_\-\s]?ets|round[_\-\s]?robin|nsga[_\-\s]?(?:2|ii)|"
 82|    r"moea[_\-\s]?d|greedy|genetic)\b",

 87|def humanize_algorithms(text: str) -> str:
 88|    """Replace code identifiers such as ``round_robin`` with display nam
   |    es."""
 89|    if not isinstance(text, str):
 90|        return text
 91|
 92|    def replace(match: "re.Match[str]") -> str:
 93|        key = re.sub(r"[_\-\s]", "", match.group(1).lower())
 94|        return _NORMALIZED_NAMES.get(key, match.group(0))
 95|
 96|    return _ALGO_PATTERN.sub(replace, text)
 97|
 98|
 99|WEIGHT_LABELS = {
100|    "alpha_deadline": "α 截止权重",
101|    "beta_energy": "β 能耗权重",
102|    "gamma_thermal": "γ 温度权重",
103|    "delta_heterogeneity": "δ 异构权重",
104|    "epsilon_migration": "ε 迁移权重",
105|}
106|
107|
108|def localize(text: Any) -> Any:
109|    if not isinstance(text, str):
110|        return text
111|    result = text
112|    for key, label in list(DISPLAY_NAMES.items()) + list(WEIGHT_LABELS.i
   |    tems()):
113|        result = re.sub(r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_])" + re.escape(key) + r"(?![A-
   |    Za-z0-9_])", label, result)
114|    return result
115|
116|
117|def localize_result(result: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
118|    if result.get("summary"):
119|        result["summary"] = localize(result["summary"])
120|    for field in ("risks", "actions", "reasons"):
121|        if result.get(field):
122|            result[field] = [localize(item) for item in result[field]]
123|    for field in ("caution", "notice"):
124|        if result.get(field):
125|            result[field] = localize(result[field])
126|    return result

129|def load_credentials() -> Tuple[str, str, str]:
130|    key = os.environ.get(KEY_ENV, "").strip()
131|    model = os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_MODEL", "").strip()
132|    if key:
133|        return key, "env", model
134|    try:
135|        with open(KEY_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
136|            lines = [line.strip() for line in handle if line.strip()]
137|        if lines:
138|            return lines[0], "file", (lines[1] if len(lines) > 1 else ""
   |    )
139|    except OSError:
140|        pass
141|    return "", "none", ""

144|def _num(value: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
145|    try:
146|        return float(value)
147|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
148|        return default
149|
150|
151|def rule_analyze(stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], weights: Optional[Dic
   |    t[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
152|    if not stats:
153|        return {
154|            "summary": "暂无可分析的仿真数据，请先运行一次仿真。",
155|            "best_algorithm": "",
156|            "risks": [],
157|            "tuning": {},
158|            "actions": [],
159|            "source": "rule",
160|        }
161|
162|    by_energy = sorted(stats.items(), key=lambda kv: _num(kv[1].get("avg
   |    _energy")))
163|    best_energy, best_row = by_energy[0]
164|    hottest = max(stats, key=lambda k: _num(stats[k].get("avg_temp", sta
   |    ts[k].get("max_temp"))))
165|    weakest = min(stats, key=lambda k: _num(stats[k].get("service_rate")
   |    , 1.0))
166|    baseline = _num(stats.get("round_robin", {}).get("avg_energy")) or _
   |    num(by_energy[-1][1].get("avg_energy"))
167|    saving = 0.0
168|    if baseline:
169|        saving = (baseline - _num(best_row.get("avg_energy"))) / baselin
   |    e * 100.0
170|
171|    max_temp = max(_num(row.get("max_temp")) for row in stats.values())
172|    avg_pue = sum(_num(row.get("avg_pue")) for row in stats.values()) / 
   |    len(stats)
173|    weakest_rate = _num(stats[weakest].get("service_rate"), 1.0)
174|
175|    summary = (
176|        "%s 在当前配置下平均能耗最低（%.0f W，较轮询基线低 %.1f%%）；"
177|        "集群最高温度 %.1f °C，平均 PUE %.2f。"
178|        % (best_energy, _num(best_row.get("avg_energy")), saving, max_te
   |    mp, avg_pue)
179|    )
180|    if weakest_rate >= 0.999:
181|        summary += "四个算法的任务服务率均达到 100%%，指标可直接横向比较。
   |    "
182|
183|    risks: List[str] = []
184|    actions: List[str] = []
185|    tuning: Dict[str, Any] = {}
186|
187|    if weakest_rate < 0.98:
188|        dropped = int(_num(stats[weakest].get("dropped_tasks")))
189|        risks.append(
190|            "%s 有 %d 个任务未成功分配（服务率 %.1f%%），其能耗与温度偏低主
   |    要是因为接的任务更少，"
191|            "与其他算法不在同一口径上。" % (weakest, dropped, weakest_rat
   |    e * 100)
192|        )
193|        actions.append("对外对比能耗前先公布服务率，或为 %s 增加可用服务
   |    器数量后重跑。" % weakest)
194|
195|    if max_temp > 70:
196|        risks.append("最高温度达到 %.1f °C，已接近 85 °C 的强制散热阈值。"
   |     % max_temp)
197|        gamma = _num((weights or {}).get("gamma_thermal"), 0.20)
198|        tuning["gamma_thermal"] = round(min(0.40, gamma + 0.05), 2)
199|        actions.append("提高 DAMO-ETS 的温度权重 gamma_thermal，让调度优
   |    先避开高温机架。")
200|
201|    if avg_pue > 1.4:
202|        risks.append("平均 PUE 为 %.2f，高于 1.4 的常规水平，冷却开销偏大。
   |    " % avg_pue)
203|        actions.append("检查冷却目标温度与风扇曲线，或降低单机负载密度。")
204|
205|    deadline = max(int(_num(row.get("deadline_violations"))) for row in 
   |    stats.values())
206|    if deadline > 0:
207|        risks.append("存在 %d 次 deadline 违约，说明当前分配策略对时延敏
   |    感任务保护不足。" % deadline)
208|        alpha = _num((weights or {}).get("alpha_deadline"), 0.35)
209|        tuning["alpha_deadline"] = round(min(0.60, alpha + 0.05), 2)
210|
211|    if not risks:
212|        risks.append("本次仿真未发现明显风险：服务率、温度与 PUE 均在合理
   |    区间。")
213|
214|    if not tuning:
215|        tuning = {
216|            "alpha_deadline": _num((weights or {}).get("alpha_deadline")
   |    , 0.35),
217|            "gamma_thermal": _num((weights or {}).get("gamma_thermal"), 
   |    0.20),
218|        }
219|        actions.append("当前权重已较均衡，可在自动调参实验中进一步搜索更
   |    优组合。")
220|
221|    actions.append("把本轮结果与上一轮对比，确认调参带来的变化方向是否一
   |    致。")
222|
223|    return {
224|        "summary": summary,
225|        "best_algorithm": best_energy,
226|        "risks": risks[:4],
227|        "tuning": tuning,
228|        "actions": actions[:4],
229|        "source": "rule",
230|    }

232|
233|def _to_int(token: str) -> int:
234|    token = token.strip()
235|    if token.isdigit():
236|        return int(token)
237|    return CN_NUMBERS.get(token, 0)
238|
239|
240|def rule_parse(text: str, defaults: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> 
   |    Dict[str, Any]:
241|    defaults = defaults or {}
242|    params: Dict[str, Any] = {}
243|    notes: List[str] = []
244|    number = r"(\d+|[一二两三四五六七八九十]+)"
245|    servers = re.search(number + r"\s*(?:台|个|节点|服务器)", text)
246|    hours = re.search(number + r"\s*(?:个?小时|h\b)", text)
247|    days = re.search(number + r"\s*(?:天|日)", text)
248|    if servers:
249|        params["num_servers"] = _to_int(servers.group(1))
250|    elif defaults.get("num_servers"):
251|        params["num_servers"] = int(defaults["num_servers"])
252|    if hours:
253|        params["simulation_hours"] = _to_int(hours.group(1))
254|    elif days:
255|        params["simulation_hours"] = _to_int(days.group(1)) * 24
256|        notes.append("按 1 天 = 24 小时换算得到仿真时长。")
257|    elif defaults.get("simulation_hours"):
258|        params["simulation_hours"] = int(defaults["simulation_hours"])
259|
260|    picked = []
261|    lowered = text.lower()
262|    for alias, algo in ALGO_ALIASES.items():
263|        if alias in lowered and algo not in picked:
264|            picked.append(algo)
265|    if picked:
266|        params["algorithms"] = picked
267|    elif defaults.get("algorithms"):
268|        params["algorithms"] = list(defaults["algorithms"])
269|        notes.append("未识别到具体算法，沿用当前运行的算法列表。")
270|    else:
271|        params["algorithms"] = list(ALL_ALGORITHMS)
272|        notes.append("未识别到具体算法，默认对比全部算法。")
273|
274|    if any(word in text for word in ("大规模", "压测", "1000", "500")):
275|        params["run_mode"] = "large_scale"
276|    elif "分布式" in text:
277|        params["run_mode"] = "distributed"
278|    elif defaults.get("run_mode"):
279|        params["run_mode"] = defaults["run_mode"]
280|
281|    if any(word in text for word in ("压住温度", "降温", "散热", "过热")
   |    ):
282|        if "damo_ets" not in params["algorithms"]:
283|            params["algorithms"].append("damo_ets")
284|        notes.append("检测到温度控制意图，已加入 DAMO-ETS 并在建议中提高
   |    温度权重。")
285|    if "省电" in text or "能耗" in text or "节能" in text:
286|        notes.append("检测到节能意图，建议用能耗列做主指标。")
287|
288|    if not params.get("num_servers"):
289|        if defaults.get("num_servers"):
290|            params["num_servers"] = int(defaults["num_servers"])
291|        else:
292|            params["num_servers"] = 10
293|            notes.append("未识别到服务器数量，将使用默认值 10。")
294|
295|    if params.get("num_servers", 0) > 1000:
296|        params["num_servers"] = 1000
297|        notes.append("服务器数量超过系统上限，已按 1000 台处理。")
298|    if params.get("simulation_hours", 0) > 720:
299|        original = params["simulation_hours"]
300|        params["simulation_hours"] = 720
301|        notes.append("仿真时长 %d 小时超过系统上限 720 小时，已按上限处理
   |    。" % original)
302|
303|    notes = [localize(item) for item in notes]
304|    return {"params": params, "notes": notes, "source": "rule"}
305|
306|
307|def rule_explain_tuning(sweep: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
308|    rows = sweep.get("rows", [])
309|    best = sweep.get("best", {})
310|    baseline = sweep.get("baseline", {})
311|    if not rows or not best:
312|        return {"summary": "暂无可解释的寻优结果。", "reasons": [], "caut
   |    ion": ""}
313|
314|    best_metrics = best["metrics"]
315|    base_metrics = baseline["metrics"]
316|    energy_delta = best_metrics["avg_energy"] - base_metrics["avg_energy
   |    "]
317|    temp_delta = best_metrics["max_temp"] - base_metrics["max_temp"]
318|    energy_pct = energy_delta / base_metrics["avg_energy"] * 100 if base
   |    _metrics["avg_energy"] else 0.0
319|
320|    summary = (
321|        "%d 组方案中「%s」综合最优：平均能耗 %.0f W（较默认 %+.1f%%），最高温
   |    度 %.2f °C（较默认 %+.2f °C），"
322|        "服务率 %.1f%%。规模为 %d 台 / %d 小时，固定随机种子 %d。"
323|        % (sweep.get("candidates", len(rows)), best["name"], best_metric
   |    s["avg_energy"], energy_pct,
324|           best_metrics["max_temp"], temp_delta, best_metrics["service_r
   |    ate"] * 100,
325|           sweep["scale"]["num_servers"], sweep["scale"]["simulation_hou
   |    rs"], sweep["scale"]["seed"])
326|    )
327|
328|    reasons: List[str] = []
329|    base_weights = baseline.get("weights", {})
330|    changes = sorted(
331|        ((key, float(best.get("weights", {}).get(key, 0)) - float(base_w
   |    eights.get(key, 0))) for key in WEIGHT_KEYS),
332|        key=lambda item: abs(item[1]),
333|        reverse=True,
334|    )
335|    if changes:
336|        key, delta = changes[0]
337|        direction = "提高" if delta > 0 else "降低"
338|        reasons.append(
339|            "该方案把 %s 从 %.2f %s到 %.2f，是相对默认权重最明显的调整；对
   |    应最高温度变化 %+.2f °C、能耗变化 %+.1f%%。"
340|            % (key, float(base_weights.get(key, 0)), direction, float(be
   |    st.get("weights", {}).get(key, 0)), temp_delta, energy_pct)
341|        )
342|
343|    thermal_rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda row: float(row.get("weights",
   |     {}).get("gamma_thermal", 0)), reverse=True)
344|    hottest_weight = thermal_rows[0] if thermal_rows else None
345|    if hottest_weight and hottest_weight["name"] != best["name"]:
346|        reasons.append(
347|            "对照发现「%s」把 gamma_thermal 加到 %.2f，但最高温度 %.2f °C 
   |    并不比最优方案 %.2f °C 更低，"
348|            "说明单纯加大温度权重会让调度过度保守、任务积压，并不能换取更
   |    低的温度。"
349|            % (hottest_weight["name"], float(hottest_weight.get("weights
   |    ", {}).get("gamma_thermal", 0)),
350|               hottest_weight["metrics"]["max_temp"], best_metrics["max_
   |    temp"])
351|        )
352|
353|    spread = max(row["metrics"]["avg_energy"] for row in rows) - min(row
   |    ["metrics"]["avg_energy"] for row in rows)
354|    reasons.append(
355|        "全部候选方案的能耗极差为 %.0f W，说明在当前规模下权重对能耗的影
   |    响幅度有限，主要收益来自温度侧。"
356|        % spread
357|    )
358|
359|    caution = (
360|        "本结论基于 %d 台 / %d 小时 / seed=%d 的单场景搜索，换规模或换种
   |    子后最优组合可能变化，"
361|        "正式结论建议至少在两种规模上复跑确认。"
362|        % (sweep["scale"]["num_servers"], sweep["scale"]["simulation_hou
   |    rs"], sweep["scale"]["seed"])
363|    )
364|    return {"summary": summary, "reasons": reasons[:3], "caution": cauti
   |    on, "source": "rule"}
365|
366|
367|TUNE_SYSTEM = """你是数据中心能效调度专家。用户给出 DAMO-ETS 权重寻优实验
   |    的结果表，
368|请输出 JSON：{"summary": "一句总结", "reasons": ["机理分析，最多3条"], "ca
   |    ution": "适用边界"}。
369|要求：只能引用给定数据，不要编造数值；要指出最优方案与默认权重的差异及其代
   |    价。
370|算法名称用中文或规范写法（贪心算法、遗传算法、轮询算法、DAMO-ETS、NSGA-II、MOE
   |    A-D），
371|权重参数用中文名（α 截止权重、β 能耗权重、γ 温度权重、δ 异构权重、ε 迁移权重）
   |    。"""
