374|class AIService:
375|    def __init__(self) -> None:
376|        self.api_key, self.key_source, model_override = load_credentials
   |    ()
377|        self.base_url = os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL", DEFAULT_BASE
   |    _URL).rstrip("/")
378|        self.model = model_override or os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_MODEL", 
   |    "").strip() or DEFAULT_MODEL
379|        self.timeout = float(os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_TIMEOUT", "90"))
380|        self.max_tokens = int(os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_MAX_TOKENS", "409
   |    6"))
381|        effort = os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_REASONING_EFFORT", "none").str
   |    ip().lower()
382|        self.reasoning_effort = "" if effort in ("", "auto", "default") 
   |    else effort
383|        self._cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
384|        self.last_error = ""
385|        self.last_ok_at = ""
386|
387|    @property
388|    def available(self) -> bool:
389|        return bool(self.api_key)
390|
391|    def reload_key(self) -> None:
392|        self.api_key, self.key_source, model_override = load_credentials
   |    ()
393|        self.model = model_override or os.environ.get("DEEPSEEK_MODEL", 
   |    "").strip() or DEFAULT_MODEL
394|
395|    def status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
396|        reason = ""
397|        if not self.available:
398|            reason = "未配置 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY，当前使用本地专家规则"
399|        elif self.last_error:
400|            reason = "大模型最近一次调用失败，已回退本地规则"
401|        return {
402|            "available": self.available,
403|            "model": self.model,
404|            "key_source": self.key_source,
405|            "last_error": self.last_error,
406|            "last_ok_at": self.last_ok_at,
407|            "reason": reason,
408|        }
409|
410|    def _post(self, payload: Dict[str, Any], use_env_proxy: bool) -> Dic
   |    t[str, Any]:
411|        session = requests.Session()
412|        if not use_env_proxy:
413|            session.trust_env = False
414|        response = session.post(
415|            self.base_url + "/chat/completions",
416|            headers={
417|                "Authorization": "Bearer " + self.api_key,
418|                "Content-Type": "application/json",
419|            },
420|            json=payload,
421|            timeout=self.timeout,
422|        )
423|        if response.status_code == 400 and "reasoning_effort" in respons
   |    e.text:
424|            logger.warning("当前接口不支持 reasoning_effort, 已自动去掉
   |    该参数重试")
425|            self.reasoning_effort = ""
426|            retry_payload = dict(payload)
427|            retry_payload.pop("reasoning_effort", None)
428|            return self._post(retry_payload, use_env_proxy)
429|        response.raise_for_status()
430|        return response.json()
431|
432|    @staticmethod
433|    def short_error(exc: Exception) -> str:
434|        text = str(exc)
435|        lowered = text.lower()
436|        if "token 预算" in text:
437|            return text
438|        if "proxy" in lowered:
439|            return "环境代理不可用，直连也失败，请检查网络或代理设置"
440|        if "timed out" in lowered or "timeout" in lowered:
441|            return "连接 DeepSeek 超时，请检查网络"
442|        if "401" in text or "unauthorized" in lowered or "invalid api ke
   |    y" in lowered:
443|            return "API Key 无效或已过期"
444|        if "402" in text or "insufficient balance" in lowered:
445|            return "DeepSeek 账户余额不足"
446|        if "403" in text:
447|            return "API Key 无权访问该模型"
448|        return text[:120]
449|
450|    def chat(self, system: str, user: str, json_mode: bool = False) -> s
   |    tr:
451|        return self.chat_messages(system, [{"role": "user", "content": u
   |    ser}], json_mode)
452|
453|    def chat_messages(self, system: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]],
454|                      json_mode: bool = False) -> str:
455|        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
456|            "model": self.model,
457|            "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}] + list(m
   |    essages),
458|            "temperature": 0.3,
459|            "max_tokens": self.max_tokens,
460|            "stream": False,
461|        }
462|        if json_mode:
463|            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}
464|        if self.reasoning_effort:
465|            payload["reasoning_effort"] = self.reasoning_effort
466|
467|        try:
468|            data = self._post(payload, True)
469|        except (requests.exceptions.ConnectionError, requests.exceptions
   |    .Timeout) as exc:
470|            logger.warning("经环境代理访问 DeepSeek 失败, 尝试直连: %s" 
   |    % exc)
471|            data = self._post(payload, False)
472|
473|        choice = data["choices"][0]
474|        content = choice["message"].get("content") or ""
475|        if not content.strip():
476|            if choice.get("finish_reason") == "length":
477|                raise RuntimeError(
478|                    "模型思考占满了 token 预算, 未能给出结论; 可设置 DEE
   |    PSEEK_REASONING_EFFORT=none 关闭思考, 或提高 DEEPSEEK_MAX_TOKENS (当
   |    前 %d)" % self.max_tokens
479|                )
480|            raise RuntimeError("模型返回了空内容")
481|        return content
482|
483|    @staticmethod
484|    def _extract_json(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
485|        text = text.strip()
486|        start = text.find("{")
487|        end = text.rfind("}")
488|        if start >= 0 and end > start:
489|            text = text[start:end + 1]
490|        return json.loads(text)
491|
492|    def analyze(self, stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], weights: Optiona
   |    l[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
493|        fallback = rule_analyze(stats, weights)
494|        if not self.available:
495|            return fallback
496|
497|        cache_key = json.dumps(stats, sort_keys=True)
498|        cached = self._cache.get(cache_key)
499|        if cached:
500|            return cached
501|
502|        payload = {
503|            "当前指标": stats,
504|            "DAMO-ETS 当前权重": weights or {},
505|            "对照基线": "round_robin（轮询）",
506|        }
507|        try:
508|            content = self.chat(ANALYZE_SYSTEM, json.dumps(payload, ensu
   |    re_ascii=False), json_mode=True)
509|            parsed = self._extract_json(content)
510|            result = {
511|                "summary": str(parsed.get("summary") or fallback["summar
   |    y"]),
512|                "best_algorithm": str(parsed.get("best_algorithm") or fa
   |    llback["best_algorithm"]),
513|                "risks": list(parsed.get("risks") or fallback["risks"])[
   |    :4],
514|                "tuning": dict(parsed.get("tuning") or fallback["tuning"
   |    ]),
515|                "actions": list(parsed.get("actions") or fallback["actio
   |    ns"])[:4],
516|                "source": "llm",
517|                "model": self.model,
518|            }
519|            self._cache[cache_key] = result
520|            self.last_error = ""
521|            self.last_ok_at = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
522|            return localize_result(result)
523|        except Exception as exc:
524|            logger.error("DeepSeek 诊断失败，回退本地规则: %s" % exc)
525|            self.last_error = self.short_error(exc)
526|            fallback["source"] = "rule"
527|            fallback["notice"] = "大模型调用失败，已回退本地专家规则"
528|            return localize_result(fallback)
529|
530|    def explain_tuning(self, sweep: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
531|        fallback = rule_explain_tuning(sweep)
532|        if not self.available:
533|            return localize_result(fallback)
534|        try:
535|            payload = {
536|                "择优规则": sweep.get("rule"),
537|                "实验规模": sweep.get("scale"),
538|                "结果表": [
539|                    {"方案": row["name"], "权重": row["weights"], "指标"
   |    : row["metrics"]}
540|                    for row in sweep.get("rows", [])
541|                ],
542|                "最优方案": sweep.get("best", {}).get("name"),
543|                "默认方案": sweep.get("baseline", {}).get("name"),
544|            }
545|            content = self.chat(TUNE_SYSTEM, json.dumps(payload, ensure_
   |    ascii=False), json_mode=True)
546|            parsed = self._extract_json(content)
547|            return localize_result({
548|                "summary": str(parsed.get("summary") or fallback["summar
   |    y"]),
549|                "reasons": list(parsed.get("reasons") or fallback["reaso
   |    ns"])[:3],
550|                "caution": str(parsed.get("caution") or fallback["cautio
   |    n"]),
551|                "source": "llm",
552|                "model": self.model,
553|            })
554|        except Exception as exc:
555|            logger.error("DeepSeek 寻优解释失败，回退本地规则: %s" % exc)
556|            self.last_error = self.short_error(exc)
557|            fallback["notice"] = "大模型调用失败，已回退本地规则解释"
558|            return localize_result(fallback)
559|
560|    def parse_intent(self, text: str, defaults: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]
   |     = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
561|        fallback = rule_parse(text, defaults)
562|        if not self.available:
563|            return fallback
564|        try:
565|            content = self.chat(PARSE_SYSTEM, text, json_mode=True)
566|            parsed = self._extract_json(content)
567|            params = dict(fallback["params"])
568|            for key in ("num_servers", "simulation_hours", "run_mode"):
569|                if parsed.get(key):
570|                    params[key] = parsed[key]
571|            if parsed.get("algorithms"):
572|                valid = [a for a in parsed["algorithms"] if a in ALL_ALG
   |    ORITHMS]
573|                if valid:
574|                    params["algorithms"] = valid
575|            notes = [localize(item) for item in fallback["notes"]]
576|            return {"params": params, "notes": notes, "source": "llm"}
577|        except Exception as exc:
578|            logger.error("DeepSeek 参数解析失败，回退本地规则: %s" % exc)
579|            self.last_error = self.short_error(exc)
580|            fallback["notice"] = "大模型调用失败，已回退本地规则解析"
581|            return fallback

586|
587|def _fmt(value: Any, digits: int = 3) -> str:
588|    try:
589|        return f"{float(value):.{digits}f}"
590|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
591|        return "--"
592|
593|
594|def _pct_value(value: Any) -> str:
595|    try:
596|        return f"{float(value) * 100:.2f}"
597|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
598|        return "--"

601|def _snapshot_reply(snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
602|    names = "、".join(DISPLAY_NAMES.get(item, item) for item in snapshot.
   |    get("algorithms") or [])
603|    return (
604|        "当前回答基于最新一次运行：%s，%s 台服务器 × %s 小时，参与算法 %s。
   |    数据生成时间 %s。"
605|        "你重新点「开始运行」后，我会自动读取新结果。"
606|        % (
607|            snapshot.get("site") or "默认站点",
608|            snapshot.get("num_servers") or "未知",
609|            snapshot.get("simulation_hours") or "未知",
610|            names or "未知",
611|            snapshot.get("generated_at") or "未知",
612|        )

616|def _best_hours_reply(insights: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[str]:
617|    safe_items = insights.get("safe_best_hours") or []
618|    overall_items = insights.get("overall_best_hours") or []
619|    items = safe_items or overall_items
620|    if not items:
621|        return None
622|    top = items[0]
623|    lines = [
624|        "按单位服务负载能耗，当前配置下性价比最高的时段是第 %d 小时：%s，%s
   |     W/负载，峰值温度 %s℃，平均 PUE %s，服务率 %s%%。"
625|        % (
626|            int(top.get("hour") or 0) + 1,
627|            DISPLAY_NAMES.get(top.get("algorithm"), top.get("algorithm")
   |     or "未知算法"),
628|            _fmt(top.get("energy_per_load")),
629|            _fmt(top.get("peak_temp"), 1),
630|            _fmt(top.get("pue")),
631|            _pct_value(top.get("service_rate")),
632|        )
633|    ]
634|    for item in items[1:3]:
635|        lines.append(
636|            "- 第 %d 小时：%s，%s W/负载，峰值温度 %s℃"
637|            % (
638|                int(item.get("hour") or 0) + 1,
639|                DISPLAY_NAMES.get(item.get("algorithm"), item.get("algor
   |    ithm") or "未知算法"),
640|                _fmt(item.get("energy_per_load")),
641|                _fmt(item.get("peak_temp"), 1),
642|            )
643|        )
644|    if safe_items:
645|        lines.append("以上时段已排除峰值温度达到 70℃ 或出现过热服务器的
   |    时段。")
646|    if safe_items and overall_items:
647|        raw = overall_items[0]
648|        raw_key = (raw.get("hour"), raw.get("algorithm"))
649|        safe_key = (top.get("hour"), top.get("algorithm"))
650|        if raw_key != safe_key:
651|            lines.append(
652|                "纯能耗最低的是第 %d 小时（%s，%s W/负载），但峰值温度 %s℃，
   |    不建议作为常规配置。"
653|                % (
654|                    int(raw.get("hour") or 0) + 1,
655|                    DISPLAY_NAMES.get(raw.get("algorithm"), raw.get("alg
   |    orithm") or "未知算法"),
656|                    _fmt(raw.get("energy_per_load")),
657|                    _fmt(raw.get("peak_temp"), 1),
658|                )
659|            )
660|    lines.append("这是当前一次仿真的观测结果；换城市、服务器数量或时长后需
   |    要重新运行。")
661|    return "\n".join(lines)

664|def _temperature_reply(insights: Dict[str, Any], threshold: Optional[flo
   |    at] = None,
665|                       above: bool = False) -> Optional[str]:
666|    if threshold is not None:
667|        key = "temperature_thresholds_above" if above else "temperature_
   |    thresholds"
668|        items = insights.get(key) or []
669|        if not items:
670|            return None
671|        record = min(items, key=lambda item: abs(float(item.get("thresho
   |    ld_c") or 0) - threshold))
672|        level = int(record.get("threshold_c") or 0)
673|        best = record.get("best_algorithm")
674|        data = (record.get("by_algorithm") or {}).get(best) if best else
   |     None
675|        if not best or not data:
676|            return "当前数据在这个温区没有足够样本，暂时给不出可靠建议。"
677|        hours = record.get("hours_above" if above else "hours_below") or
   |     0
678|        direction = "高于或等于" if above else "低于"
679|        text = (
680|            "当峰值温度%s %d℃ 时，当前数据覆盖 %d 个运行小时；表现最好的是
   |     %s："
681|            "单位服务负载能耗 %s W/负载，平均 PUE %s，服务率 %s%%，平均风扇
   |    转速 %s。"
682|            % (
683|                direction, level, hours,
684|                DISPLAY_NAMES.get(best, best),
685|                _fmt(data.get("avg_energy_per_load")),
686|                _fmt(data.get("avg_pue")),
687|                _pct_value(data.get("avg_service_rate")),
688|                _fmt(data.get("avg_fan_speed"), 2),
689|            )
690|        )
691|        if not record.get("best_reliable"):
692|            text += " 该温区服务率偏低，结论只作参考。"
693|        text += (
694|            "\n建议：这个温区优先用 %s；低温时可适当整合负载、减少空转服务
   |    器，"
695|            "高温时保留散热余量。当前数据没有验证改变风扇曲线后的效果，调
   |    整前建议重新仿真。"
696|            % DISPLAY_NAMES.get(best, best)
697|        )
698|        return text
699|
700|    bands = insights.get("temperature_bands") or []
701|    lines = ["按当前数据，不同峰值温度区间的配置建议："]
702|    for band in bands:
703|        best = band.get("best_algorithm")
704|        data = (band.get("by_algorithm") or {}).get(best) if best else N
   |    one
705|        if not best or not data:
706|            continue
707|        line = (
708|            "- %s（%d 小时）：优先 %s，单位服务负载能耗 %s W/负载，PUE %s，服
   |    务率 %s%%，风扇转速 %s。"
709|            % (
710|                band.get("label") or "未知温区",
711|                int(band.get("hours") or 0),
712|                DISPLAY_NAMES.get(best, best),
713|                _fmt(data.get("avg_energy_per_load")),
714|                _fmt(data.get("avg_pue")),
715|                _pct_value(data.get("avg_service_rate")),
716|                _fmt(data.get("avg_fan_speed"), 2),
717|            )
718|        )
719|        if not band.get("best_reliable"):
720|            line += " 服务率偏低，仅作参考。"
721|        lines.append(line)
722|    if len(lines) == 1:
723|        return None
724|    lines.append("低温区间可适当整合负载、减少空转服务器；高温区间要保留散
   |    热余量。以上是观测建议，改变配置前建议重新仿真验证。")
725|    return "\n".join(lines)

728|def _config_advice_reply(text: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
729|    insights = context.get("insights") or {}
730|    snapshot = insights.get("snapshot") or context.get("data_snapshot") 
   |    or {}
731|    config = context.get("config") or {}
732|    relationships = insights.get("relationships") or {}
733|    best = insights.get("overall_best")
734|    lines = ["当前数据没有实际运行过这个配置，我只能给出基于现有观测的方
   |    向性判断。"]
735|    if snapshot:
736|        lines.append(
737|            "当前快照：%s，%s 台服务器 × %s 小时。"
738|            % (snapshot.get("site") or "默认站点", snapshot.get("num_ser
   |    vers") or "未知",
739|               snapshot.get("simulation_hours") or "未知")
740|        )
741|    if best:
742|        lines.append("当前表现最好的算法是 %s。" % DISPLAY_NAMES.get(best
   |    , best))
743|    pairs = []
744|    if relationships.get("load_vs_peak_temp") is not None:
745|        pairs.append("负载与峰值温度相关系数 %s" % _fmt(relationships["l
   |    oad_vs_peak_temp"], 3))
746|    if relationships.get("ambient_vs_pue") is not None:
747|        pairs.append("环境温度与 PUE 相关系数 %s" % _fmt(relationships["
   |    ambient_vs_pue"], 3))
748|    if relationships.get("fan_vs_cooling") is not None:
749|        pairs.append("风扇转速与冷却功率相关系数 %s" % _fmt(relationship
   |    s["fan_vs_cooling"], 3))
750|    if pairs:
751|        lines.append("当前观测：" + "，".join(pairs) + "。这些只是相关性，不
   |    是因果结论。")
752|
753|    if any(word in text for word in ("服务器", "台", "节点", "冗余", "关
   |    掉")):
754|        lines.append("调整服务器数量会同时影响整合程度、空闲功耗和服务率：
   |    减少服务器可能省电，但当前服务率本就不高，继续减少大概率增加未分配任务
   |    ；增加服务器能缓解容量瓶颈，但空闲功耗会抬高单位服务负载能耗。")
755|    elif any(word in text for word in ("风扇", "散热", "冷却")):
756|        lines.append("降低风扇转速或冷却功率会减少冷却能耗，但散热余量变
   |    小，峰值温度和过热小时数可能上升；高温时段的风险最大。")
757|    elif any(word in text for word in ("算法", "nsga", "moea", "damo", "
   |    贪心", "遗传", "轮询")):
758|        lines.append("更换算法会改变任务分配和热点分布，方向取决于当前瓶
   |    颈：容量不足时先看服务率，温度接近上限时先看峰值温度和过热小时数。")
759|
760|    server_range = config.get("num_server_range") or []
761|    fan_range = config.get("fan_speed_range") or []
762|    if len(server_range) == 2 and server_range[0] is not None and server
   |    _range[1] is not None:
763|        lines.append(
764|            "当前允许的服务器数量范围是 %s–%s 台%s。"
765|            % (
766|                server_range[0], server_range[1],
767|                "，风扇转速范围是 %s–%s" % (fan_range[0], fan_range[1]) i
   |    f len(fan_range) == 2 else "",
768|            )
769|        )

771|        "要得到具体数值，需要按新参数重新运行仿真。网页表单可直接改服务器

   |    or {}

   |    mp") or 0),

  4|再输出 Pareto 候选、推荐方案和取舍说明。所有候选都在内存中完成，不覆盖
  5|当前页面数据；只有用户点击「应用并运行」时才正式写入运行记录。

 72|def _now() -> str:
 73|    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
 74|
 75|
 76|def _round(value: Any, digits: int = 4) -> Optional[float]:
 77|    try:
 78|        number = float(value)
 79|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
 80|        return None
 81|    if not math.isfinite(number):
 82|        return None
 83|    return round(number, digits)
 84|
 85|
 86|def _server_counts(low: int, high: int) -> List[int]:
 87|    if high <= low:
 88|        return [low]
 89|    middle = int(round((low + high) / 2.0))
 90|    values = [low, middle, high]
 91|    result: List[int] = []
 92|    for value in values:
 93|        if value not in result:
 94|            result.append(value)
 95|    return result

 98|def _evaluate(spec: Dict[str, Any], base: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, A
   |    ny]:
 99|    simulator = DataCenterSimulator(
100|        num_servers=spec["num_servers"],
101|        simulation_hours=base["simulation_hours"],
102|        seed=base["seed"],
103|        data_source_override=({"site_id": base["site_id"]} if base.get("
   |    site_id") else None),
104|    )

106|        simulator.damo_ets_scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(
107|            simulator.servers, config=dict(spec["weights"]), seed=base["
   |    seed"]
108|        )
109|    frame = simulator.run_simulation(spec["algorithm"])
110|    stats = summarize(frame).get(spec["algorithm"]) or {}
111|    metrics = {
112|        "avg_pue": _round(stats.get("avg_pue"), 4),
113|        "avg_energy": _round(stats.get("avg_energy"), 1),
114|        "max_temp": _round(stats.get("max_temp"), 2),
115|        "avg_temp": _round(stats.get("avg_temp"), 2),
116|        "service_rate": _round(stats.get("service_rate"), 4),
117|        "energy_per_served_load": _round(stats.get("energy_per_served_lo
   |    ad"), 4),
118|        "dropped_tasks": int(stats.get("dropped_tasks") or 0),
119|        "deadline_violations": int(stats.get("deadline_violations") or 0
   |    ),

121|    }
122|    return {
123|        "num_servers": spec["num_servers"],
124|        "algorithm": spec["algorithm"],
125|        "algorithm_label": ALGORITHM_LABELS.get(spec["algorithm"], spec[
   |    "algorithm"]),
126|        "weights": dict(spec["weights"]) if spec.get("weights") else Non
   |    e,

   |    t_source"),

   |    rio_source"),

137|
138|def _metric(row: Dict[str, Any], key: str) -> float:
139|    value = (row.get("metrics") or {}).get(key)
140|    try:
141|        number = float(value)
142|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
143|        return float("inf")
144|    return number if math.isfinite(number) else float("inf")
145|
146|
147|def _normalized(value: float, low: float, high: float) -> float:
148|    if not math.isfinite(value):
149|        return 1.0
150|    if high <= low:
151|        return 0.0
152|    return min(1.0, max(0.0, (value - low) / (high - low)))
153|
154|
155|def _score(row: Dict[str, Any], pool: List[Dict[str, Any]], target: str)
   |     -> float:
156|    pue = _metric(row, "avg_pue")
157|    energy = _metric(row, "energy_per_served_load")
158|    temp = _metric(row, "max_temp")
159|    if target == "pue":
160|        return 0.0 if not math.isfinite(pue) else round(pue, 6)
161|    if target == "energy":
162|        return 0.0 if not math.isfinite(energy) else round(energy, 6)
163|    if target == "temp":
164|        return 0.0 if not math.isfinite(temp) else round(temp, 6)
165|    pue_values = [_metric(item, "avg_pue") for item in pool]
166|    energy_values = [_metric(item, "energy_per_served_load") for item in
   |     pool]
167|    temp_values = [_metric(item, "max_temp") for item in pool]
168|    pue_score = _normalized(pue, min(pue_values), max(pue_values))
169|    energy_score = _normalized(energy, min(energy_values), max(energy_va
   |    lues))
170|    temp_score = _normalized(temp, min(temp_values), max(temp_values))
171|    return round(0.45 * pue_score + 0.35 * energy_score + 0.20 * temp_sc
   |    ore, 6)
172|
173|
174|def _dominates(left: Dict[str, Any], right: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
175|    keys = ("avg_pue", "energy_per_served_load", "max_temp")
176|    left_values = [_metric(left, key) for key in keys]
177|    right_values = [_metric(right, key) for key in keys]
178|    if any(not math.isfinite(value) for value in left_values):
179|        return False
180|    no_worse = all(a <= b for a, b in zip(left_values, right_values))
181|    strictly_better = any(a < b for a, b in zip(left_values, right_value
   |    s))
182|    return no_worse and strictly_better
183|
184|
185|def _mark_pareto(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
186|    count = 0
187|    for row in rows:
188|        row["pareto"] = not any(
189|            other is not row and _dominates(other, row)
190|            for other in rows
191|        )
192|        if row["pareto"]:
193|            count += 1
194|    return count
195|
196|
197|def _pick_top(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], limit: int = MAX_PLANS) -> Lis
   |    t[Dict[str, Any]]:
198|    ordered = sorted(rows, key=lambda item: item.get("score", float("inf
   |    ")))
199|    picked: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
200|    used_algorithms = set()
201|    for row in ordered:
202|        if row["algorithm"] in used_algorithms:
203|            continue
204|        picked.append(row)
205|        used_algorithms.add(row["algorithm"])
206|        if len(picked) >= limit:
207|            break
208|    used_counts = {row["num_servers"] for row in picked}
209|    if len(picked) < limit:
210|        for row in ordered:
211|            if row in picked or row["num_servers"] in used_counts:
212|                continue
213|            picked.append(row)
214|            used_counts.add(row["num_servers"])
215|            if len(picked) >= limit:
216|                break
217|    if len(picked) < limit:
218|        for row in ordered:
219|            if row in picked:
220|                continue
221|            picked.append(row)
222|            if len(picked) >= limit:
223|                break
224|    for index, row in enumerate(picked, start=1):
225|        row["rank"] = index
226|    return picked
227|
228|
229|def _plan_text(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
230|    metrics = row.get("metrics") or {}
231|    parts = [
232|        "%d 台 + %s" % (row["num_servers"], row.get("algorithm_label") o
   |    r row["algorithm"]),
233|    ]
234|    if row.get("weight_label"):
235|        parts[0] += "（%s）" % row["weight_label"]
236|    if metrics.get("avg_pue") is not None:
237|        parts.append("平均 PUE %.3f" % metrics["avg_pue"])
238|    if metrics.get("energy_per_served_load") is not None:
239|        parts.append("单位能耗 %.2f" % metrics["energy_per_served_load"]
   |    )
240|    if metrics.get("max_temp") is not None:
241|        parts.append("峰值温度 %.1f℃" % metrics["max_temp"])
242|    if metrics.get("service_rate") is not None:
243|        parts.append("服务率 %.1f%%" % (metrics["service_rate"] * 100.0)
   |    )
244|    return "，".join(parts)

247|def _explain(best: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], baseline: Optional[Dict[str
   |    , Any]],
248|             top: List[Dict[str, Any]], params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[
   |    str, Any]:
249|    target_label = TARGET_LABELS.get(params["target"], params["target"])
250|    if not best:
251|        return {
252|            "summary": "本轮没有找到满足约束的方案，建议放宽服务率或温度
   |    限制后重试。",
253|            "reasons": [],
254|            "caution": "所有候选方案都未通过约束筛选。",
255|        }
256|    reasons = [
257|        "目标：%s，服务率门槛 %.1f%%。" % (target_label, params["service_ra
   |    te_floor"] * 100.0),
258|        "最优方案：" + _plan_text(best) + "。",
259|    ]
260|    if baseline and baseline is not best:
261|        best_metrics = best.get("metrics") or {}
262|        base_metrics = baseline.get("metrics") or {}
263|        changes = []
264|        if best_metrics.get("avg_pue") is not None and base_metrics.get(
   |    "avg_pue") is not None:
265|            delta = best_metrics["avg_pue"] - base_metrics["avg_pue"]
266|            changes.append("PUE %s %.3f" % ("降低" if delta <= 0 else "
   |    升高", abs(delta)))
267|        if best_metrics.get("energy_per_served_load") is not None and ba
   |    se_metrics.get("energy_per_served_load") is not None:
268|            delta = best_metrics["energy_per_served_load"] - base_metric
   |    s["energy_per_served_load"]
269|            changes.append("单位能耗 %s %.4f" % ("降低" if delta <= 0 el
   |    se "升高", abs(delta)))
270|        if best_metrics.get("max_temp") is not None and base_metrics.get
   |    ("max_temp") is not None:
271|            delta = best_metrics["max_temp"] - base_metrics["max_temp"]
272|            changes.append("峰值温度 %s %.1f℃" % ("降低" if delta <= 0 e
   |    lse "升高", abs(delta)))
273|        if changes:
274|            reasons.append("相比基准「%s」，%s。" % (
275|                "%d 台 + %s" % (baseline["num_servers"], baseline.get("a
   |    lgorithm_label") or baseline["algorithm"]),
276|                "，".join(changes),
277|            ))
278|    if len(top) > 1:
279|        reasons.append("备选：" + "；".join(_plan_text(row) for row in top
   |    [1:]) + "。")
280|    caution = ""
281|    if params.get("max_temp") is not None:
282|        caution = "峰值温度约束为 %.1f℃；如需更低温，可继续收紧温度上限后
   |    重跑。" % params["max_temp"]
283|    return {"summary": "推荐「%s」。" % _plan_text(best), "reasons": reason
   |    s, "caution": caution}
284|

286|class PlanOptimizer:
287|    def __init__(self) -> None:
288|        self._jobs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
289|        self._lock = threading.Lock()
290|        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_p
   |    refix="dcs-optimize")
291|
292|    def start(self, raw: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
293|        params = self._validate(raw)
294|        job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
295|        job = {
296|            "job_id": job_id,
297|            "status": "queued",
298|            "progress": 0,
299|            "done": 0,
300|            "total": 0,
301|            "message": "已提交，等待当前仿真任务结束",
302|            "params": params,
303|            "result": None,
304|            "error": "",
305|            "created_at": _now(),
306|            "finished_at": None,
307|        }
308|        with self._lock:
309|            self._jobs[job_id] = job
310|        self._executor.submit(self._run, job_id)
311|        return self.snapshot(job_id)
312|
313|    def active(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
314|        with self._lock:
315|            for job in self._jobs.values():
316|                if job.get("status") in ("queued", "running"):
317|                    return dict(job)
318|        return None
319|    def snapshot(self, job_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
320|        with self._lock:
321|            job = self._jobs.get(job_id)
322|            return dict(job) if job else None
323|
324|    def apply(self, job_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
325|        job = self.snapshot(job_id)
326|        if not job:
327|            raise ValueError("寻优任务不存在")
328|        if job.get("status") != "success" or not job.get("result"):
329|            raise ValueError("寻优尚未完成")
330|        result = job["result"]
331|        best = result.get("best")
332|        if not best:
333|            raise ValueError("没有可应用的推荐方案")
334|        params = job["params"]
335|        applied_weights = None
336|        if best.get("weights"):
337|            from src.api.ai_api import _apply_weights
338|            applied_weights = _apply_weights(best["weights"])
339|        algorithms = list(params["algorithms"])
340|        if best["algorithm"] not in algorithms:
341|            algorithms.append(best["algorithm"])
342|        run_params = {
343|            "num_servers": best["num_servers"],
344|            "simulation_hours": params["simulation_hours"],
345|            "algorithms": algorithms,
346|            "run_mode": "standard",
347|            "server_type": "mixed",
348|            "parallel_workers": 4,
349|            "site_id": params.get("site_id") or "",
350|            "school_name": params.get("school_name") or "",
351|            "visualization": True,
352|            "save_results": True,
353|        }
354|        manager = get_run_manager()
355|        run_id = manager.submit(run_params, origin="optimizer")
356|        return {
357|            "run_id": run_id,
358|            "params": run_params,
359|            "plan": best,
360|            "applied_weights": applied_weights,
361|        }

363|    def _validate(self, raw: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
364|        low = validate_int(raw.get("server_min"), min_val=4, max_val=MAX
   |    _SERVERS, default=48, name="最小服务器数")
365|        high = validate_int(raw.get("server_max"), min_val=4, max_val=MA
   |    X_SERVERS, default=max(80, low), name="最大服务器数")
366|        if high < low:
367|            raise ValueError("最大服务器数不能小于最小服务器数")
368|        hours = validate_int(raw.get("simulation_hours"), min_val=1, max
   |    _val=MAX_HOURS, default=24, name="仿真时长")
369|        target = validate_str(raw.get("target"), max_len=16, pattern=r"^
   |    (pue|energy|temp|balanced)$", default="balanced", name="优化目标")
370|        floor = validate_float(raw.get("service_rate_floor"), min_val=0.
   |    8, max_val=1.0, default=0.98, name="服务率门槛")
371|        max_temp = raw.get("max_temp")
372|        if max_temp in ("", None):
373|            max_temp = None
374|        else:
375|            max_temp = validate_float(max_temp, min_val=20.0, max_val=10
   |    0.0, name="温度上限")
376|        algorithms = validate_algorithms(raw.get("algorithms") or DEFAUL
   |    T_ALGORITHMS)
377|        site_id = str(raw.get("site_id") or "").strip()[:64]
378|        school_name = str(raw.get("school_name") or "").strip()[:128]
379|        return {
380|            "server_min": low,
381|            "server_max": high,
382|            "simulation_hours": hours,
383|            "target": target,
384|            "service_rate_floor": round(floor, 4),
385|            "max_temp": max_temp,
386|            "algorithms": algorithms,

388|            "seed": int(raw.get("seed") or 42),
389|            "site_id": site_id,
390|            "school_name": school_name,

393|    def _update(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
394|        with self._lock:
395|            job = self._jobs.get(job_id)
396|            if job:
397|                job.update(fields)
398|
399|    def _run(self, job_id: str) -> None:
400|        job = self.snapshot(job_id)
401|        if not job:
402|            return
403|        params = job["params"]
404|        manager = get_run_manager()
405|        started = time.perf_counter()
406|        try:
407|            with manager.simulation_lock():
408|                self._update(job_id, status="running", message="开始评估
   |    候选方案", progress=2)
409|                counts = _server_counts(params["server_min"], params["se
   |    rver_max"])
410|                middle = counts[len(counts) // 2]
411|                stage_one: List[Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Dict[str, Any]]] =
   |     []
412|                for algorithm in params["algorithms"]:
413|                    if algorithm == "damo_ets" and params["include_weigh
   |    ts"]:
414|                        for profile_key, profile in WEIGHT_PROFILES.item
   |    s():
415|                            stage_one.append((
416|                                {
417|                                    "num_servers": middle,
418|                                    "algorithm": algorithm,
419|                                    "weights": profile["weights"],
420|                                    "weight_label": profile["label"],
421|                                },
422|                                params,
423|                            ))
424|                    stage_one.append((
425|                        {"num_servers": middle, "algorithm": algorithm},
426|                        params,
427|                    ))
428|                total = len(stage_one) + max(0, len(counts) - 1) * 2
429|                self._update(job_id, total=total)
430|                rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
431|                failed: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
432|                for index, (spec, base) in enumerate(stage_one, start=1)
   |    :
433|                    self._run_candidate(job_id, spec, base, rows, failed
   |    , index, total)
434|                qualified = [row for row in rows if self._qualified(row,
   |     params)]
435|                pool = qualified or rows
436|                for row in rows:
437|                    row["score"] = _score(row, pool, params["target"])
438|                ranked = sorted([row for row in rows if row.get("score")
   |     is not None], key=lambda item: item["score"])
439|                leaders: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
440|                seen = set()
441|                for row in ranked:
442|                    key = (row["algorithm"], tuple(sorted((row.get("weig
   |    hts") or {}).items())))
443|                    if key in seen:
444|                        continue
445|                    seen.add(key)
446|                    leaders.append(row)
447|                    if len(leaders) >= 2:
448|                        break
449|                for count in counts:
450|                    if count == middle:
451|                        continue
452|                    for leader in leaders:
453|                        spec = {
454|                            "num_servers": count,
455|                            "algorithm": leader["algorithm"],
456|                            "weights": leader.get("weights"),
457|                            "weight_label": leader.get("weight_label"),
458|                        }
459|                        self._run_candidate(job_id, spec, params, rows, 
   |    failed, len(rows) + 1, total)
460|                qualified = [row for row in rows if self._qualified(row,
   |     params)]
461|                pool = qualified or rows
462|                for row in rows:
463|                    row["score"] = _score(row, pool, params["target"])
464|                    row["qualified"] = self._qualified(row, params)
465|                pareto_rows = qualified or rows
466|                pareto_count = _mark_pareto(pareto_rows)
467|                top = _pick_top(qualified or rows, MAX_PLANS)
468|                baseline = self._baseline(rows, middle)
469|                best = top[0] if top else None
470|                result = {
471|                    "rule": self._rule(params),
472|                    "target": params["target"],
473|                    "target_label": TARGET_LABELS.get(params["target"], 
   |    params["target"]),
474|                    "service_rate_floor": params["service_rate_floor"],
475|                    "max_temp": params["max_temp"],
476|                    "scale": {"server_min": params["server_min"], "serve
   |    r_max": params["server_max"], "simulation_hours": params["simulation
   |    _hours"]},
477|                    "site_id": params.get("site_id"),
478|                    "school_name": params.get("school_name"),
479|                    "candidates": len(rows) + len(failed),
480|                    "evaluated": len(rows),
481|                    "failed": failed,
482|                    "elapsed_sec": round(time.perf_counter() - started, 
   |    2),
483|                    "pareto_count": pareto_count,
484|                    "baseline": baseline,
485|                    "best": best,
486|                    "top": top,
487|                }
488|                result["explanation"] = _explain(best, baseline, top, pa
   |    rams)
489|                self._update(job_id, status="success", progress=100, don
   |    e=len(rows) + len(failed), message="寻优完成", result=result, finish
   |    ed_at=_now())
490|        except Exception as exc:
491|            logger.error("全局寻优失败: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
492|            self._update(job_id, status="failed", progress=100, message=
   |    "寻优失败", error=str(exc), finished_at=_now())
493|
494|    def _run_candidate(self, job_id: str, spec: Dict[str, Any], base: Di
   |    ct[str, Any],
495|                       rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], failed: List[Dict[str
   |    , Any]],
496|                       index: int, total: int) -> None:
497|        started = time.perf_counter()
498|        try:
499|            row = _evaluate(spec, base)
500|            rows.append(row)
501|            logger.info("寻优候选: %s 台 / %s -> PUE %s, 温度 %s, 服务率
   |     %s", spec["num_servers"], spec["algorithm"], row["metrics"].get("av
   |    g_pue"), row["metrics"].get("max_temp"), row["metrics"].get("service
   |    _rate"))
502|        except Exception as exc:
503|            failed.append({
504|                "num_servers": spec["num_servers"],
505|                "algorithm": spec["algorithm"],
506|                "weight_label": spec.get("weight_label"),
507|                "error": str(exc),
508|            })
509|            logger.warning("寻优候选失败: %s 台 / %s: %s", spec["num_ser
   |    vers"], spec["algorithm"], exc)
510|        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
511|        done = len(rows) + len(failed)
512|        progress = int(min(95, max(3, done / max(total, 1) * 95)))
513|        self._update(job_id, progress=progress, done=done, total=max(tot
   |    al, done), message="已完成 %d/%d 组候选（最近一组 %.1f 秒）" % (done, 
   |    max(total, done), elapsed))
514|
515|    def _qualified(self, row: Dict[str, Any], params: Dict[str, Any]) ->
   |     bool:
516|        rate = (row.get("metrics") or {}).get("service_rate")
517|        if rate is not None and float(rate) < params["service_rate_floor
   |    "]:
518|            return False
519|        limit = params.get("max_temp")
520|        temp = (row.get("metrics") or {}).get("max_temp")
521|        if limit is not None and temp is not None and float(temp) > floa
   |    t(limit):
522|            return False
523|        return True
524|
525|    def _baseline(self, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], middle: int) -> Opti
   |    onal[Dict[str, Any]]:
526|        greedy = [row for row in rows if row["algorithm"] == "greedy" an
   |    d row["num_servers"] == middle]
527|        if greedy:
528|            return greedy[0]
529|        same_count = [row for row in rows if row["num_servers"] == middl
   |    e]
530|        return same_count[0] if same_count else (rows[0] if rows else No
   |    ne)
531|
532|    def _rule(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
533|        limit = params.get("max_temp")
534|        temp_text = "、峰值温度不高于 %.1f℃" % limit if limit is not None
   |     else ""
535|        return "先筛除服务率低于 %.1f%%%s的方案，再按「%s」排序，并输出 Pare
   |    to 候选" % (
536|            params["service_rate_floor"] * 100.0, temp_text, TARGET_LABE
   |    LS.get(params["target"], params["target"])
537|        )
538|
539|
540|_optimizer: Optional[PlanOptimizer] = None
541|_optimizer_lock = threading.Lock()
542|
543|
544|def get_plan_optimizer() -> PlanOptimizer:
545|    global _optimizer
546|    if _optimizer is None:
547|        with _optimizer_lock:
548|            if _optimizer is None:
549|                _optimizer = PlanOptimizer()
550|    return _optimizer

  1|"""Serialized simulation runner with a small event bus for live UI updat
   |    es."""
  2|
  3|import os
  4|import threading
  5|import time
  6|from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
  7|from datetime import datetime
  8|from queue import Empty, Full, Queue
  9|from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple
 10|
 11|from src.plugin import PluginManager
 12|from src.plugin.protocol import PluginResult
 13|from src.plugin.simulation_plugin import SimulationPlugin
 14|from src.services import run_store
 15|from src.simulation.metrics import summarize
 16|from src.utils import load_run_metadata, load_simulation_data, save_run_
   |    metadata
 17|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 18|from src.utils.validation import validate_simulation_params

 20|logger = setup_logger("run_manager")
 21|
 22|
 23|def _now() -> str:
 24|    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
 25|
 26|
 27|class EventBus:
 28|    def __init__(self) -> None:
 29|        self._subscribers = set()
 30|        self._lock = threading.Lock()
 31|
 32|    def subscribe(self) -> Queue:
 33|        queue: Queue = Queue(maxsize=200)
 34|        with self._lock:
 35|            self._subscribers.add(queue)
 36|        return queue
 37|
 38|    def unsubscribe(self, queue: Queue) -> None:
 39|        with self._lock:
 40|            self._subscribers.discard(queue)
 41|
 42|    def publish(self, event: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 43|        with self._lock:
 44|            subscribers = list(self._subscribers)
 45|        for queue in subscribers:
 46|            try:
 47|                queue.put_nowait(event)
 48|            except Full:
 49|                pass
 50|
 51|
 52|class RunManager:
 53|    def __init__(self) -> None:
 54|        self.events = EventBus()
 55|        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_p
   |    refix="dcs-run")
 56|        self._run_lock = threading.Lock()
 57|        try:
 58|            run_store.import_legacy_run()
 59|        except Exception as exc:
 60|            logger.warning("导入历史运行失败: %s" % exc)
 61|        try:
 62|            for record in run_store.list_runs(50):
 63|                if record.get("status") in ("queued", "running"):
 64|                    run_store.update_run(
 65|                        record["run_id"],
 66|                        status="failed",
 67|                        progress=100,
 68|                        finished_at=_now(),
 69|                        error="服务重启，任务已中断",
 70|                    )
 71|                    logger.warning("标记中断运行: %s", record["run_id"])
 72|        except Exception as exc:
 73|            logger.warning("清理中断运行失败: %s" % exc)
 74|
 75|    def submit(self, params: Dict[str, Any], origin: str = "web") -> str
   |    :
 76|        validated = self._validate(params)
 77|        run_id = run_store.create_run(validated, origin=origin)
 78|        self._publish(run_id, "run_created", 0, "queued", validated)
 79|        self._executor.submit(self._execute, run_id, validated, origin)
 80|        return run_id
 81|
 82|    def run_sync(self, params: Dict[str, Any], origin: str = "web") -> T
   |    uple[str, PluginResult]:
 83|        validated = self._validate(params)
 84|        run_id = run_store.create_run(validated, origin=origin)
 85|        self._publish(run_id, "run_created", 0, "queued", validated)
 86|        result = self._execute(run_id, validated, origin)
 87|        return run_id, result
 88|
 89|    def run_prediction(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Dict[str, 
   |    Any], PluginResult]:
 90|        """Run a forecast scenario without overwriting the main simulati
   |    on results."""
 91|        validated = self._validate(params)
 92|        config = {
 93|            "num_servers": validated["num_servers"],
 94|            "simulation_hours": validated["simulation_hours"],
 95|            "parallel_workers": validated["parallel_workers"],
 96|            "run_mode": validated["run_mode"],
 97|            "server_type": validated["server_type"],
 98|        }
 99|        if validated.get("site_id"):
100|            config["site_id"] = validated["site_id"]
101|
102|        with self._run_lock:
103|            manager = PluginManager()
104|            manager.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin)
105|            if not manager.load_plugin("SimulationPlugin", config):
106|                raise RuntimeError("预测仿真加载失败")
107|            try:
108|                result = manager.execute_plugin(
109|                    "SimulationPlugin",
110|                    algorithms=validated["algorithms"],
111|                    save_results=False,
112|                    visualization=False,
113|                    include_series=True,
114|                )
115|            finally:
116|                manager.unload_plugin("SimulationPlugin")
117|        return validated, result
118|
119|    def simulation_lock(self):
120|        return self._run_lock
121|
122|    def wait(self, run_id: str, timeout: float = 120.0) -> Optional[Dict
   |    [str, Any]]:
123|        deadline = time.time() + timeout
124|        while time.time() < deadline:
125|            record = run_store.get_run(run_id)
126|            if record and record.get("status") in ("success", "failed"):
127|                return record
128|            time.sleep(0.5)
129|        return run_store.get_run(run_id)
130|
131|    def active_run(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
132|        for record in run_store.list_runs(20):
133|            if record.get("status") in ("queued", "running"):
134|                return record
135|        return None
136|
137|    def _validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
138|        raw = dict(params or {})
139|        validated = validate_simulation_params(raw)
140|        site_id = raw.get("site_id")
141|        if site_id is not None:
142|            site_id = str(site_id).strip()[:64]
143|            if site_id:
144|                validated["site_id"] = site_id
145|        school_name = raw.get("school_name")
146|        if school_name is not None:
147|            school_name = str(school_name).strip()[:128]
148|            if school_name:
149|                validated["school_name"] = school_name
150|        return validated
151|
152|    def _execute(self, run_id: str, params: Dict[str, Any], origin: str)
   |     -> PluginResult:
153|        with self._run_lock:
154|            run_store.update_run(run_id, status="running", progress=5, s
   |    tarted_at=_now())
155|            self._publish(run_id, "run_progress", 5, "running", params)
156|            manager = PluginManager()
157|            try:
158|                manager.register_plugin(SimulationPlugin)
159|                manager.discover_plugins(plugin_dir="src/plugins")
160|                config = {
161|                    "num_servers": params["num_servers"],
162|                    "simulation_hours": params["simulation_hours"],
163|                    "parallel_workers": params["parallel_workers"],
164|                    "run_mode": params["run_mode"],
165|                    "server_type": params["server_type"],
166|                }
167|                if params.get("site_id"):
168|                    config["site_id"] = params["site_id"]
169|                if not manager.load_plugin("SimulationPlugin", config):
170|                    raise RuntimeError("SimulationPlugin 加载失败")
171|
172|                run_store.update_run(run_id, progress=20)
173|                self._publish(run_id, "run_progress", 20, "running", par
   |    ams)
174|                result = manager.execute_plugin(
175|                    "SimulationPlugin", algorithms=params["algorithms"]
176|                )
177|                manager.unload_plugin("SimulationPlugin")
178|                if not result.success:
179|                    raise RuntimeError(result.message or "仿真执行失败")
180|
181|                run_store.update_run(run_id, progress=80)
182|                self._publish(run_id, "run_progress", 80, "running", par
   |    ams)
183|                df = load_simulation_data()
184|                if df is None or df.empty:
185|                    raise RuntimeError("仿真结果文件为空")
186|                metadata = load_run_metadata() or {}
187|                metadata.update({
188|                    "run_id": run_id,
189|                    "origin": origin,
190|                    "generated_at": _now(),
191|                    "num_servers": params["num_servers"],
192|                    "simulation_hours": params["simulation_hours"],
193|                    "algorithms": params["algorithms"],
194|                })
195|                if params.get("site_id") and not metadata.get("site_id")
   |    :
196|                    metadata["site_id"] = params["site_id"]
197|                if params.get("school_name"):
198|                    metadata["school_name"] = params["school_name"]
199|                save_run_metadata(metadata)
200|                summary = summarize(df)
201|                run_store.save_results(run_id, df, metadata, summary)
202|                run_store.update_run(
203|                    run_id,
204|                    status="success",
205|                    progress=100,
206|                    finished_at=_now(),
207|                )
208|                self._publish(
209|                    run_id, "run_completed", 100, "success", params,
210|                    summary=summary, origin=origin,
211|                )
212|                return result
213|            except Exception as exc:
214|                logger.error("运行 %s 失败: %s" % (run_id, exc))
215|                try:
216|                    manager.unload_plugin("SimulationPlugin")
217|                except Exception:
218|                    pass
219|                run_store.update_run(
220|                    run_id,
221|                    status="failed",
222|                    progress=100,
223|                    finished_at=_now(),
224|                    error=str(exc),
225|                )
226|                self._publish(
227|                    run_id, "run_failed", 100, "failed", params,
228|                    error=str(exc), origin=origin,
229|                )
230|                return PluginResult.fail(
231|                    message=str(exc), error_code="RUN_FAILED", error_det
   |    ail=str(exc)
232|                )
233|
234|    def _publish(self, run_id: str, event_type: str, progress: int,
235|                 status: str, params: Dict[str, Any], **extra: Any) -> N
   |    one:
236|        event = {
237|            "type": event_type,
238|            "run_id": run_id,
239|            "progress": progress,
240|            "status": status,
241|            "params": params,
242|            "timestamp": _now(),
243|        }
244|        event.update(extra)
245|        self.events.publish(event)
246|
247|
248|_manager: Optional[RunManager] = None
249|_manager_lock = threading.Lock()
250|
251|
252|def get_run_manager() -> RunManager:
253|    global _manager
254|    if _manager is None:
255|        with _manager_lock:
256|            if _manager is None:
257|                _manager = RunManager()
258|    return _manager
259|

52. src/services/run_store.py

  1|"""Persistent run registry shared by the web UI, the assistant and repor
   |    ts."""
  2|
  3|import json
  4|import os
  5|import sqlite3
  6|import threading
  7|import uuid
  8|from datetime import datetime
  9|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
 10|
 11|import pandas as pd
 12|
 13|ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..
   |    "))
 14|DATA_DIR = os.path.join(ROOT, "data")
 15|RUNS_DIR = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "runs")
 16|DB_PATH = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "runs.db")
 17|LEGACY_RESULTS = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "simulation_results.csv")
 18|LEGACY_METADATA = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "run_metadata.json")
 19|
 20|_LOCK = threading.RLock()
 21|
 22|
 23|def _now() -> str:
 24|    return datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
 25|
 26|
 27|def _connect() -> sqlite3.Connection:
 28|    os.makedirs(DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
 29|    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=30)
 30|    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
 31|    return conn
 32|
 33|
 34|def _init() -> None:
 35|    with _LOCK, _connect() as conn:
 36|        conn.execute(
 37|            """
 38|            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runs (
 39|                run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
 40|                origin TEXT NOT NULL,
 41|                status TEXT NOT NULL,
 42|                progress INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 43|                params_json TEXT,
 44|                site_id TEXT,
 45|                num_servers INTEGER,
 46|                simulation_hours INTEGER,
 47|                algorithms_json TEXT,
 48|                created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
 49|                started_at TEXT,
 50|                finished_at TEXT,
 51|                results_path TEXT,
 52|                metadata_path TEXT,
 53|                summary_path TEXT,
 54|                summary_json TEXT,
 55|                error TEXT
 56|            )
 57|            """
 58|        )
 59|        conn.commit()
 60|
 61|
 62|_init()
 63|
 64|
 65|def _dump(value: Any) -> str:
 66|    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
 67|
 68|
 69|def _loads(value: Optional[str], default: Any) -> Any:
 70|    if not value:
 71|        return default
 72|    try:
 73|        return json.loads(value)
 74|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
 75|        return default
 76|
 77|
 78|def _row_to_run(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 79|    item = dict(row)
 80|    item["params"] = _loads(item.pop("params_json", None), {})
 81|    item["algorithms"] = _loads(item.pop("algorithms_json", None), [])
 82|    item["summary"] = _loads(item.pop("summary_json", None), {})
 83|    return item
 84|
 85|
 86|def create_run(params: Dict[str, Any], origin: str = "web", run_id: Opti
   |    onal[str] = None) -> str:
 87|    run_id = run_id or (datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S") + "-" +
   |     uuid.uuid4().hex[:6])
 88|    os.makedirs(run_dir(run_id), exist_ok=True)
 89|    with _LOCK, _connect() as conn:
 90|        conn.execute(
 91|            """
 92|            INSERT INTO runs (
 93|                run_id, origin, status, progress, params_json, site_id,
 94|                num_servers, simulation_hours, algorithms_json, created_
   |    at
 95|            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 96|            """,
 97|            (
 98|                run_id, origin, "queued", 0, _dump(params), params.get("
   |    site_id"),
 99|                params.get("num_servers"), params.get("simulation_hours"
   |    ),
100|                _dump(params.get("algorithms") or []), _now(),
101|            ),
102|        )
103|        conn.commit()
104|    return run_id
105|
106|
107|def update_run(run_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
108|    allowed = {
109|        "status", "progress", "started_at", "finished_at", "results_path
   |    ",
110|        "metadata_path", "summary_path", "summary_json", "error",
111|    }
112|    updates = {key: value for key, value in fields.items() if key in all
   |    owed}
113|    if not updates:
114|        return
115|    for key in ("summary_json",):
116|        if key in updates:
117|            updates[key] = _dump(updates[key])
118|    assignments = ", ".join("%s = ?" % key for key in updates)
119|    with _LOCK, _connect() as conn:
120|        conn.execute(
121|            "UPDATE runs SET %s WHERE run_id = ?" % assignments,
122|            list(updates.values()) + [run_id],
123|        )
124|        conn.commit()
125|
126|
127|def get_run(run_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
128|    with _LOCK, _connect() as conn:
129|        row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE run_id = ?", (run_i
   |    d,)).fetchone()
130|    return _row_to_run(row) if row else None
131|
132|
133|def list_runs(limit: int = 20) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
134|    limit = max(1, min(int(limit), 100))
135|    with _LOCK, _connect() as conn:
136|        rows = conn.execute(
137|            "SELECT * FROM runs ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?", (limi
   |    t,)
138|        ).fetchall()
139|    return [_row_to_run(row) for row in rows]
140|
141|
142|def latest_run() -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
143|    runs = list_runs(1)
144|    return runs[0] if runs else None
145|
146|
147|def run_dir(run_id: str) -> str:
148|    return os.path.join(RUNS_DIR, run_id)
149|
150|
151|def save_results(run_id: str, df: pd.DataFrame, metadata: Dict[str, Any]
   |    ,
152|                 summary: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
153|    folder = run_dir(run_id)
154|    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
155|    results_path = os.path.join(folder, "results.csv")
156|    metadata_path = os.path.join(folder, "metadata.json")
157|    summary_path = os.path.join(folder, "summary.json")
158|    df.to_csv(results_path, index=False)
159|    with open(metadata_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
160|        json.dump(metadata, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, defaul
   |    t=str)
161|    with open(summary_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
162|        json.dump(summary, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default
   |    =str)
163|    update_run(
164|        run_id,
165|        results_path=results_path,
166|        metadata_path=metadata_path,
167|        summary_path=summary_path,
168|        summary_json=summary,
169|    )
170|    return {
171|        "results_path": results_path,
172|        "metadata_path": metadata_path,
173|        "summary_path": summary_path,
174|    }
175|
176|
177|def load_results(run_id: str) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:
178|    record = get_run(run_id)
179|    path = (record or {}).get("results_path")
180|    if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
181|        path = os.path.join(run_dir(run_id), "results.csv")
182|    if not os.path.exists(path):
183|        return None
184|    return pd.read_csv(path)
185|
186|
187|def compare_runs(left_id: str, right_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]
   |    ]:
188|    left = get_run(left_id)
189|    right = get_run(right_id)
190|    if not left or not right:
191|        return None
192|    left_summary = left.get("summary") or {}
193|    right_summary = right.get("summary") or {}
194|    algorithms = sorted(set(left_summary) | set(right_summary))
195|    rows = []
196|    for algorithm in algorithms:
197|        left_metrics = left_summary.get(algorithm) or {}
198|        right_metrics = right_summary.get(algorithm) or {}
199|        item = {"algorithm": algorithm}
200|        for key in ("energy_per_served_load", "service_rate", "avg_pue",
   |     "max_temp", "co2_kg"):
201|            left_value = left_metrics.get(key)
202|            right_value = right_metrics.get(key)
203|            delta = None
204|            percent = None
205|            if isinstance(left_value, (int, float)) and isinstance(right
   |    _value, (int, float)):
206|                delta = round(float(right_value) - float(left_value), 4)
207|                if left_value:
208|                    percent = round((float(right_value) - float(left_val
   |    ue)) / float(left_value) * 100, 2)
209|            item[key] = {
210|                "left": left_value,
211|                "right": right_value,
212|                "delta": delta,
213|                "percent": percent,
214|            }
215|        rows.append(item)
216|    return {
217|        "left": left,
218|        "right": right,
219|        "metrics": rows,
220|        "rule": "right 相对 left 的变化",
221|    }
222|
223|
224|def import_legacy_run() -> Optional[str]:
225|    if list_runs(1):
226|        return None
227|    if not os.path.exists(LEGACY_RESULTS) or not os.path.exists(LEGACY_M
   |    ETADATA):
228|        return None
229|    try:
230|        df = pd.read_csv(LEGACY_RESULTS)
231|        with open(LEGACY_METADATA, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
232|            meta = json.load(handle)
233|    except Exception:
234|        return None
235|    from src.simulation.metrics import summarize
236|
237|    run_id = meta.get("run_id") or (
238|        "legacy-" + datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(LEGACY_RESUL
   |    TS)).strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
239|    )
240|    params = {
241|        "num_servers": meta.get("num_servers") or 10,
242|        "simulation_hours": meta.get("simulation_hours") or 24,
243|        "algorithms": meta.get("algorithms") or list(df["algorithm"].uni
   |    que()),
244|        "site_id": meta.get("site_id"),
245|        "school_name": meta.get("school_name"),
246|    }
247|    create_run(params, origin="legacy", run_id=run_id)
248|    summary = summarize(df)
249|    meta = dict(meta)
250|    meta["run_id"] = run_id
251|    save_results(run_id, df, meta, summary)
252|    update_run(
253|        run_id,
254|        status="success",
255|        progress=100,
256|        finished_at=_now(),
257|    )
258|    return run_id

================================================================================
53. src/services/simulation_insights.py
================================================================================
  1|"""把最近一次仿真结果整理成可回答的时段与温区洞察。"""
  2|
  3|from datetime import datetime
  4|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
  5|
  6|import pandas as pd
  7|
  8|from src.simulation.metrics import safe_number
  9|
 10|SERVICE_FLOOR = 0.90
 11|
 12|TEMP_BANDS = [
 13|    {"label": "低于 45℃", "low": None, "high": 45.0},
 14|    {"label": "45–55℃", "low": 45.0, "high": 55.0},
 15|    {"label": "55–65℃", "low": 55.0, "high": 65.0},
 16|    {"label": "65–75℃", "low": 65.0, "high": 75.0},
 17|    {"label": "75℃ 及以上", "low": 75.0, "high": None},
 18|]
 19|
 20|TEMP_THRESHOLDS = [40, 45, 50, 55, 60, 65, 70, 75, 80]
 21|
 22|
 23|def latest_data_time() -> Optional[str]:
 24|    import os
 25|
 26|    path = os.path.abspath(
 27|        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", "data", "sim
   |    ulation_results.csv")
 28|    )
 29|    if not os.path.exists(path):
 30|        return None
 31|    return datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(path)).strftime("%Y-%
   |    m-%d %H:%M:%S")
 32|
 33|
 34|def _round(value: Any, digits: int = 3) -> Optional[float]:
 35|    number = safe_number(value)
 36|    if number is None:
 37|        return None
 38|    return round(number, digits)
 39|
 40|
 41|def _prepare(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
 42|    frame = df.copy()
 43|    frame["time"] = pd.to_numeric(frame.get("time"), errors="coerce")
 44|    frame = frame.dropna(subset=["time"])
 45|    frame["time"] = frame["time"].astype(int)
 46|
 47|    for name in (
 48|        "total_load", "served_load", "total_energy", "pue", "peak_temper
   |    ature",
 49|        "avg_temperature", "ambient_temperature", "fan_speed", "cooling_
   |    power",
 50|        "assigned", "dropped", "overheated_servers",
 51|    ):
 52|        if name in frame.columns:
 53|            frame[name] = pd.to_numeric(frame[name], errors="coerce")
 54|
 55|    served = frame["served_load"] if "served_load" in frame.columns else
   |     pd.Series(index=frame.index, dtype=float)
 56|    energy = frame["total_energy"] if "total_energy" in frame.columns el
   |    se pd.Series(index=frame.index, dtype=float)
 57|    frame["energy_per_load"] = energy / served.where(served > 0)
 58|
 59|    assigned = frame["assigned"] if "assigned" in frame.columns else pd.
   |    Series(index=frame.index, dtype=float)
 60|    dropped = frame["dropped"] if "dropped" in frame.columns else pd.Ser
   |    ies(index=frame.index, dtype=float)
 61|    offered = assigned + dropped
 62|    frame["hour_service_rate"] = assigned / offered.where(offered > 0)
 63|
 64|    return frame
 65|
 66|
 67|def _aggregate(group: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 68|    return {
 69|        "hours": int(group["time"].nunique()),
 70|        "avg_energy_per_load": _round(group["energy_per_load"].mean(), 3
   |    ),
 71|        "avg_pue": _round(group["pue"].mean(), 3) if "pue" in group else
   |     None,
 72|        "avg_peak_temp": _round(group["peak_temperature"].mean(), 1) if 
   |    "peak_temperature" in group else None,
 73|        "max_peak_temp": _round(group["peak_temperature"].max(), 1) if "
   |    peak_temperature" in group else None,
 74|        "avg_fan_speed": _round(group["fan_speed"].mean(), 2) if "fan_sp
   |    eed" in group else None,
 75|        "avg_cooling_power": _round(group["cooling_power"].mean(), 1) if
   |     "cooling_power" in group else None,
 76|        "avg_service_rate": _round(group["hour_service_rate"].mean(), 4)
   |    ,
 77|        "avg_load": _round(group["total_load"].mean(), 1) if "total_load
   |    " in group else None,
 78|        "avg_served_load": _round(group["served_load"].mean(), 1) if "se
   |    rved_load" in group else None,
 79|    }
 80|
 81|
 82|def _pick_best(records: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], floor: float = SERVIC
   |    E_FLOOR):
 83|    usable = [
 84|        (name, data) for name, data in records.items()
 85|        if data and data.get("avg_energy_per_load") is not None
 86|    ]
 87|    reliable = [
 88|        (name, data) for name, data in usable
 89|        if (data.get("avg_service_rate") or 0) >= floor
 90|    ]
 91|    pool = reliable or usable
 92|    if not pool:
 93|        return None, False
 94|    name, data = min(pool, key=lambda item: item[1]["avg_energy_per_load
   |    "])
 95|    return name, bool(reliable)
 96|
 97|
 98|def _hour_record(row: pd.Series) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 99|    record = {
100|        "hour": int(row["time"]),
101|        "energy_per_load": _round(row.get("energy_per_load"), 3),
102|        "load": _round(row.get("total_load"), 1),
103|        "served_load": _round(row.get("served_load"), 1),
104|        "pue": _round(row.get("pue"), 3),
105|        "avg_temp": _round(row.get("avg_temperature"), 1),
106|        "peak_temp": _round(row.get("peak_temperature"), 1),
107|        "ambient_temp": _round(row.get("ambient_temperature"), 1),
108|        "fan_speed": _round(row.get("fan_speed"), 2),
109|        "cooling_power": _round(row.get("cooling_power"), 1),
110|        "service_rate": _round(row.get("hour_service_rate"), 4),
111|    }
112|    return record
113|
114|
115|def _corr(frame: pd.DataFrame, left: str, right: str) -> Optional[float]
   |    :
116|    if left not in frame.columns or right not in frame.columns:
117|        return None
118|    pairs = frame[[left, right]].dropna()
119|    if len(pairs) < 2:
120|        return None
121|    if pairs[left].nunique() < 2 or pairs[right].nunique() < 2:
122|        return None
123|    value = pairs[left].corr(pairs[right])
124|    return _round(value, 3)
125|
126|
127|def _range(frame: pd.DataFrame, name: str) -> Dict[str, Optional[float]]
   |    :
128|    if name not in frame.columns:
129|        return {"min": None, "max": None}
130|    values = frame[name].dropna()
131|    if values.empty:
132|        return {"min": None, "max": None}
133|    return {"min": _round(values.min(), 2), "max": _round(values.max(), 
   |    2)}
134|
135|
136|def _band_mask(frame: pd.DataFrame, low: Optional[float], high: Optional
   |    [float]):
137|    values = frame.get("peak_temperature")
138|    if values is None:
139|        return pd.Series(False, index=frame.index)
140|    mask = values.notna()
141|    if low is not None:
142|        mask &= values >= low
143|    if high is not None:
144|        mask &= values < high
145|    return mask
146|
147|
148|def build_insights(df: pd.DataFrame, meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = No
   |    ne,
149|                   generated_at: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]
   |    :
150|    meta = meta or {}
151|    if df is None or df.empty or "algorithm" not in df.columns:
152|        return {}
153|
154|    frame = _prepare(df)
155|    if frame.empty:
156|        return {}
157|
158|    frame["algorithm"] = frame["algorithm"].astype(str)
159|    algorithms = [str(item) for item in frame["algorithm"].drop_duplicat
   |    es()]
160|    hours = sorted(int(item) for item in frame["time"].unique())
161|
162|    series_hours = hours
163|    if len(hours) > 72:
164|        step = max(1, len(hours) // 72)
165|        series_hours = hours[::step][:72]
166|
167|    time_series = {
168|        "hour": series_hours,
169|        "sampled": len(series_hours) < len(hours),
170|        "by_algorithm": {},
171|    }
172|    for algorithm in algorithms:
173|        part = frame[frame["algorithm"] == algorithm].set_index("time").
   |    reindex(series_hours)
174|        time_series["by_algorithm"][algorithm] = {
175|            "energy_per_load": [_round(value, 3) for value in part["ener
   |    gy_per_load"]],
176|            "peak_temp": [_round(value, 1) for value in part["peak_tempe
   |    rature"]] if "peak_temperature" in part else [],
177|            "pue": [_round(value, 3) for value in part["pue"]] if "pue" 
   |    in part else [],
178|            "service_rate": [_round(value, 4) for value in part["hour_se
   |    rvice_rate"]],
179|            "served_load": [_round(value, 1) for value in part["served_l
   |    oad"]] if "served_load" in part else [],
180|        }
181|
182|    best_hours: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
183|    for algorithm in algorithms:
184|        rows = frame[frame["algorithm"] == algorithm].dropna(subset=["en
   |    ergy_per_load"])
185|        rows = rows.sort_values("energy_per_load").head(3)
186|        best_hours[algorithm] = [_hour_record(row) for _, row in rows.it
   |    errows()]
187|
188|    overall_rows = frame.dropna(subset=["energy_per_load"])
189|    if not overall_rows.empty:
190|        best_index = overall_rows.groupby("time")["energy_per_load"].idx
   |    min()
191|        overall_rows = overall_rows.loc[best_index]
192|    reliable_rows = overall_rows[
193|        overall_rows["hour_service_rate"].fillna(0) >= SERVICE_FLOOR
194|    ]
195|    if not reliable_rows.empty:
196|        overall_rows = reliable_rows
197|    overall_rows = overall_rows.sort_values("energy_per_load").head(5)
198|    overall_best_hours = []
199|    for _, row in overall_rows.iterrows():
200|        record = _hour_record(row)
201|        record["algorithm"] = str(row["algorithm"])
202|        overall_best_hours.append(record)
203|
204|    safe_rows = frame.dropna(subset=["energy_per_load"])
205|    if "overheated_servers" in safe_rows.columns:
206|        safe_rows = safe_rows[safe_rows["overheated_servers"].fillna(0) 
   |    <= 0]
207|    if "peak_temperature" in safe_rows.columns:
208|        safe_rows = safe_rows[safe_rows["peak_temperature"].fillna(0) < 
   |    70]
209|    safe_best_hours = []
210|    if not safe_rows.empty:
211|        best_index = safe_rows.groupby("time")["energy_per_load"].idxmin
   |    ()
212|        safe_rows = safe_rows.loc[best_index]
213|        safe_reliable = safe_rows[safe_rows["hour_service_rate"].fillna(
   |    0) >= SERVICE_FLOOR]
214|        if not safe_reliable.empty:
215|            safe_rows = safe_reliable
216|        safe_rows = safe_rows.sort_values("energy_per_load").head(5)
217|        for _, row in safe_rows.iterrows():
218|            record = _hour_record(row)
219|            record["algorithm"] = str(row["algorithm"])
220|            safe_best_hours.append(record)
221|
222|    temperature_bands = []
223|    for band in TEMP_BANDS:
224|        subset = frame[_band_mask(frame, band["low"], band["high"])]
225|        by_algorithm = {
226|            algorithm: _aggregate(subset[subset["algorithm"] == algorith
   |    m])
227|            for algorithm in algorithms
228|        }
229|        best, reliable = _pick_best(by_algorithm)
230|        best_rows = subset[subset["algorithm"] == best] if best else sub
   |    set.iloc[0:0]
231|        temperature_bands.append({
232|            "label": band["label"],
233|            "hours": int(best_rows["time"].nunique()) if not best_rows.e
   |    mpty else 0,
234|            "hours_any": int(subset["time"].nunique()) if not subset.emp
   |    ty else 0,
235|            "by_algorithm": by_algorithm,
236|            "best_algorithm": best,
237|            "best_reliable": reliable,
238|        })
239|
240|    temperature_thresholds = []
241|    temperature_thresholds_above = []
242|    for threshold in TEMP_THRESHOLDS:
243|        subset = frame[_band_mask(frame, None, float(threshold))]
244|        by_algorithm = {
245|            algorithm: _aggregate(subset[subset["algorithm"] == algorith
   |    m])
246|            for algorithm in algorithms
247|        }
248|        best, reliable = _pick_best(by_algorithm)
249|        best_rows = subset[subset["algorithm"] == best] if best else sub
   |    set.iloc[0:0]
250|        temperature_thresholds.append({
251|            "threshold_c": threshold,
252|            "hours_below": int(best_rows["time"].nunique()) if not best_
   |    rows.empty else 0,
253|            "hours_any_below": int(subset["time"].nunique()) if not subs
   |    et.empty else 0,
254|            "by_algorithm": by_algorithm,
255|            "best_algorithm": best,
256|            "best_reliable": reliable,
257|        })
258|
259|        hot_subset = frame[_band_mask(frame, float(threshold), None)]
260|        hot_by_algorithm = {
261|            algorithm: _aggregate(hot_subset[hot_subset["algorithm"] == 
   |    algorithm])
262|            for algorithm in algorithms
263|        }
264|        hot_best, hot_reliable = _pick_best(hot_by_algorithm)
265|        hot_rows = hot_subset[hot_subset["algorithm"] == hot_best] if ho
   |    t_best else hot_subset.iloc[0:0]
266|        temperature_thresholds_above.append({
267|            "threshold_c": threshold,
268|            "hours_above": int(hot_rows["time"].nunique()) if not hot_ro
   |    ws.empty else 0,
269|            "hours_any_above": int(hot_subset["time"].nunique()) if not 
   |    hot_subset.empty else 0,
270|            "by_algorithm": hot_by_algorithm,
271|            "best_algorithm": hot_best,
272|            "best_reliable": hot_reliable,
273|        })
274|
275|    effective_rows = reliable_rows if not reliable_rows.empty else overa
   |    ll_rows
276|    overall_records = {
277|        algorithm: _aggregate(frame[frame["algorithm"] == algorithm])
278|        for algorithm in algorithms
279|    }
280|    overall_best, overall_reliable = _pick_best(overall_records, floor=0
   |    .98)
281|
282|    load_peak = frame.loc[frame["total_load"].idxmax()] if "total_load" 
   |    in frame else None
283|    temp_peak = frame.loc[frame["peak_temperature"].idxmax()] if "peak_t
   |    emperature" in frame else None
284|    ambient_min = frame.loc[frame["ambient_temperature"].idxmin()] if "a
   |    mbient_temperature" in frame else None
285|
286|    recommendations = []
287|    for item in temperature_bands:
288|        best = item.get("best_algorithm")
289|        data = item["by_algorithm"].get(best) if best else None
290|        if not data or data.get("avg_energy_per_load") is None:
291|            continue
292|        text = (
293|            "%s（%d 小时）：优先 %s，单位服务负载能耗 %.3f W/负载，平均 PUE %
   |    .3f，"
294|            "服务率 %.2f%%，平均风扇转速 %.2f。"
295|            % (
296|                item["label"], item["hours"], _display_name(best),
297|                data["avg_energy_per_load"], data.get("avg_pue") or 0,
298|                (data.get("avg_service_rate") or 0) * 100,
299|                data.get("avg_fan_speed") or 0,
300|            )
301|        )
302|        if not item.get("best_reliable"):
303|            text += " 该温区服务率偏低，结论只作参考。"
304|        recommendations.append(text)
305|
306|    snapshot = {
307|        "run_id": meta.get("run_id"),
308|        "generated_at": generated_at or latest_data_time(),
309|        "site": meta.get("site_id"),
310|        "school_name": meta.get("school_name"),
311|        "province": meta.get("site_province"),
312|        "emission_region": meta.get("emission_region"),
313|        "num_servers": meta.get("num_servers"),
314|        "simulation_hours": meta.get("simulation_hours"),
315|        "algorithms": algorithms,
316|        "rows": int(len(frame)),
317|        "hours": len(hours),
318|        "controlled": meta.get("controlled"),
319|        "scenario_digest": meta.get("scenario_digest"),
320|        "scenario_source": meta.get("scenario_source"),
321|        "service_floor": SERVICE_FLOOR,
322|    }
323|
324|    return {
325|        "snapshot": snapshot,
326|        "overall_best": overall_best,
327|        "overall_best_reliable": overall_reliable,
328|        "overall_best_hours": overall_best_hours,
329|        "safe_best_hours": safe_best_hours,

334|        "temperature_thresholds_above": temperature_thresholds_above,
335|        "peaks": {

   |    d_peak["algorithm"])} if load_peak is not None else None,
337|            "temp_peak": _hour_record(temp_peak) | {"algorithm": str(tem
   |    p_peak["algorithm"])} if temp_peak is not None else None,
338|            "ambient_min": _hour_record(ambient_min) | {"algorithm": str
   |    (ambient_min["algorithm"])} if ambient_min is not None else None,
339|        },
340|        "relationships": {
341|            "ambient_vs_pue": _corr(frame, "ambient_temperature", "pue")
   |    ,
342|            "ambient_vs_cooling": _corr(frame, "ambient_temperature", "c
   |    ooling_power"),

   |    ature"),

   |    ,

   |    ,

347|        },

   |    器数量前建议重新运行验证。",
356|        "effective_hours": int(effective_rows["time"].nunique()) if not 
   |    effective_rows.empty else 0,
357|    }

360|def _display_name(algorithm: str) -> str:
361|    return {
362|        "greedy": "贪心算法",
363|        "genetic": "遗传算法",
364|        "round_robin": "轮询算法",
365|        "damo_ets": "DAMO-ETS",
366|        "nsga2": "NSGA-II",
367|        "moead": "MOEA-D",
368|    }.get(algorithm, algorithm)
