  1|"""
  2|语音识别服务模块
  3|
  4|提供跨平台语音识别能力:
  5|- 实时语音采集与流式识别
  6|- 多语言/方言支持 (zh-CN, en-US, ja-JP, ko-KR, yue-HK)
  7|- 离线识别回退 (基于Vosk/Sherpa-ONNX)
  8|- 隐私保护 (本地处理优先, 端到端加密)
  9|- 降级策略 (Web Speech API → 离线引擎 → 手动输入)
 10|
 11|参考项目:
 12|- Web Speech API (W3C标准)
 13|- Vosk (离线语音识别, Apache 2.0)
 14|- Sherpa-ONNX (下一代离线识别)
 15|- Whisper (OpenAI, 高质量识别)
 16|
 17|环境变量:
 18|    DCS_SPEECH_ENGINE=web|vosk|sherpa|whisper
 19|    DCS_SPEECH_MODEL_DIR=models/speech
 20|    DCS_SPEECH_SAMPLE_RATE=16000
 21|"""

123|@dataclass
124|class RecognitionResult:
125|    text: str
126|    confidence: float
127|    language: str
128|    is_final: bool
129|    alternatives: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
130|    duration_ms: float = 0.0
131|    engine_used: str = "web_api"
132|    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: time.strftime("%Y-%m-
   |    %dT%H:%M:%S"))
133|
134|
135|@dataclass
136|class SpeechSession:
137|    session_id: str
138|    language: str
139|    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
140|    state: RecognitionState = RecognitionState.IDLE
141|    results: List[RecognitionResult] = field(default_factory=list)
142|    engine: SpeechEngine = SpeechEngine.AUTO
143|    offline_available: bool = False
144|    audio_data_hash: Optional[str] = None
145|    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
146|
147|
148|class SpeechService:
149|    """
150|    语音识别服务
151|
152|    核心设计:
153|    - 优先级: Web Speech API (在线) → 离线引擎 (本地) → 手动输入 (兜底)
154|    - 隐私: 音频数据本地处理，不上传至第三方服务器
155|    - 多语言: 中/英/日/韩/粤/台/德/法 8种语言
156|    """
157|
158|    def __init__(self):
159|        self._sessions: Dict[str, SpeechSession] = {}
160|        self._session_lock = threading.Lock()
161|        self._offline_model = None
162|        self._engine = self._detect_engine()
163|
164|    def _detect_engine(self) -> SpeechEngine:
165|        env_engine = os.environ.get("DCS_SPEECH_ENGINE", "auto")
166|        if env_engine == "auto":
167|            if self._check_vosk():
168|                return SpeechEngine.VOSK
169|            if self._check_sherpa():
170|                return SpeechEngine.SHERPA
171|            return SpeechEngine.WEB_API
172|        engine_map = {
173|            "web": SpeechEngine.WEB_API,
174|            "vosk": SpeechEngine.VOSK,
175|            "sherpa": SpeechEngine.SHERPA,
176|            "whisper": SpeechEngine.WHISPER,
177|        }
178|        return engine_map.get(env_engine, SpeechEngine.AUTO)
179|
180|    def _check_vosk(self) -> bool:
181|        try:
182|            import vosk
183|            return True
184|        except ImportError:
185|            return False
186|
187|    def _check_sherpa(self) -> bool:
188|        try:
189|            import sherpa_onnx
190|            return True
191|        except ImportError:
192|            return False
193|
194|    def get_supported_languages(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
195|        result = {}
196|        for code, info in SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES.items():
197|            result[code] = {
198|                "name": info["name"],
199|                "label": info["label"],
200|                "code": code,
201|                "web_api_code": info["web_api_code"],
202|                "offline_available": info["offline_available"],
203|                "dialects": info.get("dialects", []),
204|            }
205|        return result
206|
207|    def create_session(self, language: str = "zh-CN") -> SpeechSession:
208|        import secrets
209|        if language not in SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES:
210|            language = "zh-CN"
211|
212|        session_id = secrets.token_hex(16)
213|        lang_info = SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES[language]
214|
215|        session = SpeechSession(
216|            session_id=session_id,
217|            language=language,
218|            state=RecognitionState.IDLE,
219|            engine=self._engine,
220|            offline_available=lang_info["offline_available"],
221|            metadata={
222|                "language_name": lang_info["name"],
223|                "web_api_code": lang_info["web_api_code"],
224|                "offline_model": lang_info.get("vosk_model"),
225|            }
226|        )
227|
228|        with self._session_lock:
229|            self._sessions[session_id] = session
230|
231|        logger.info(f"Speech session created: {session_id}, lang={langua
   |    ge}")
232|        return session
233|
234|    def get_session(self, session_id: str) -> Optional[SpeechSession]:
235|        return self._sessions.get(session_id)
236|
237|    def start_listening(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
238|        session = self._sessions.get(session_id)
239|        if session:
240|            session.state = RecognitionState.LISTENING
241|            logger.info(f"Session {session_id}: listening started")
242|            return True
243|        return False
244|
245|    def stop_listening(self, session_id: str) -> bool:
246|        session = self._sessions.get(session_id)
247|        if session:
248|            session.state = RecognitionState.IDLE
249|            logger.info(f"Session {session_id}: listening stopped")
250|            return True
251|        return False
252|
253|    def add_result(self, session_id: str, text: str, confidence: float,
254|                   is_final: bool = False, engine_used: str = "web_api",
255|                   alternatives: List[Dict] = None, duration_ms: float =
   |     0.0) -> Optional[RecognitionResult]:
256|        session = self._sessions.get(session_id)
257|        if not session:
258|            return None
259|
260|        result = RecognitionResult(
261|            text=text,
262|            confidence=confidence,
263|            language=session.language,
264|            is_final=is_final,
265|            alternatives=alternatives or [],
266|            duration_ms=duration_ms,
267|            engine_used=engine_used,
268|        )
269|        session.results.append(result)
270|
271|        if is_final:
272|            session.state = RecognitionState.COMPLETED
273|        else:
274|            session.state = RecognitionState.PROCESSING
275|
276|        return result
277|
278|    def get_all_text(self, session_id: str, final_only: bool = True) -> 
   |    str:
279|        session = self._sessions.get(session_id)
280|        if not session:
281|            return ""
282|
283|        results = session.results
284|        if final_only:
285|            results = [r for r in results if r.is_final]
286|        return " ".join(r.text for r in results if r.text.strip())
287|
288|    def process_audio_offline(self, audio_bytes: bytes, language: str = 
   |    "zh-CN") -> RecognitionResult:
289|        lang_info = SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES.get(language, SUPPORTED_LANGUAGE
   |    S["zh-CN"])
290|        if not lang_info["offline_available"]:
291|            return RecognitionResult(
292|                text="",
293|                confidence=0.0,
294|                language=language,
295|                is_final=True,
296|                engine_used="offline_unavailable",
297|                alternatives=[{"text": "当前语言不支持离线识别，请切换到
   |    在线模式或选择其他语言"}],
298|            )
299|
300|        try:
301|            if self._engine == SpeechEngine.VOSK:
302|                return self._recognize_vosk(audio_bytes, lang_info)
303|            elif self._engine == SpeechEngine.SHERPA:
304|                return self._recognize_sherpa(audio_bytes, lang_info)
305|        except Exception as e:
306|            logger.error(f"Offline recognition error: {e}")
307|
308|        return RecognitionResult(
309|            text="",
310|            confidence=0.0,
311|            language=language,
312|            is_final=True,
313|            engine_used="offline_error",
314|        )
315|
316|    def _recognize_vosk(self, audio_bytes: bytes, lang_info: Dict) -> Re
   |    cognitionResult:
317|        import wave
318|        import io
319|
320|        vosk_model_name = lang_info.get("vosk_model")
321|        if not vosk_model_name:
322|            raise ValueError("No Vosk model for this language")
323|
324|        if self._offline_model is None:
325|            import vosk
326|            model_dir = os.environ.get("DCS_SPEECH_MODEL_DIR", "models/s
   |    peech")
327|            model_path = os.path.join(model_dir, vosk_model_name)
328|            if not os.path.exists(model_path):
329|                raise FileNotFoundError(f"Vosk model not found: {model_p
   |    ath}")
330|            self._offline_model = vosk.Model(model_path)
331|
332|        import vosk
333|        import json
334|
335|        recognizer = vosk.KaldiRecognizer(self._offline_model, 16000)
336|
337|        wav_io = io.BytesIO(audio_bytes)
338|        with wave.open(wav_io, 'rb') as wf:
339|            while True:
340|                data = wf.readframes(4000)
341|                if len(data) == 0:
342|                    break
343|                recognizer.AcceptWaveform(data)
344|
345|        result_json = json.loads(recognizer.FinalResult())
346|        return RecognitionResult(
347|            text=result_json.get("text", ""),
348|            confidence=result_json.get("confidence", 0.0),
349|            language=lang_info["name"],
350|            is_final=True,
351|            engine_used="vosk",
352|            duration_ms=0.0,
353|        )
354|
355|    def _recognize_sherpa(self, audio_bytes: bytes, lang_info: Dict) -> 
   |    RecognitionResult:
356|        import sherpa_onnx
357|        import numpy as np
358|        import io
359|        import wave
360|
361|        wav_io = io.BytesIO(audio_bytes)
362|        with wave.open(wav_io, 'rb') as wf:
363|            frames = wf.readframes(wf.getnframes())
364|            samples = np.frombuffer(frames, dtype=np.int16).astype(np.fl
   |    oat32) / 32768.0
365|
366|        recognizer = sherpa_onnx.OnlineRecognizer.from_pretrained()
367|        recognizer.accept_waveform(16000, samples)
368|        result = recognizer.get_result()
369|
370|        return RecognitionResult(
371|            text=result.text if hasattr(result, 'text') else "",
372|            confidence=0.95,
373|            language=lang_info["name"],
374|            is_final=True,
375|            engine_used="sherpa_onnx",
376|            duration_ms=0.0,
377|        )
378|
379|    def hash_audio(self, audio_bytes: bytes) -> str:
380|        return hashlib.sha256(audio_bytes).hexdigest()
381|
382|    def encrypt_audio(self, audio_bytes: bytes, key: Optional[bytes] = N
   |    one) -> Tuple[bytes, bytes]:
383|        from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
384|        if key is None:
385|            key = Fernet.generate_key()
386|        f = Fernet(key)
387|        encrypted = f.encrypt(audio_bytes)
388|        return encrypted, key
389|
390|    def cleanup_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
391|        with self._session_lock:
392|            if session_id in self._sessions:
393|                del self._sessions[session_id]
394|                logger.info(f"Session cleaned: {session_id}")
395|
396|    def cleanup_expired(self, max_age_sec: float = 1800.0) -> int:
397|        now = time.time()
398|        expired = []
399|        with self._session_lock:
400|            for sid, session in list(self._sessions.items()):
401|                if now - session.created_at > max_age_sec:
402|                    expired.append(sid)
403|            for sid in expired:
404|                del self._sessions[sid]
405|        if expired:
406|            logger.info(f"Cleaned {len(expired)} expired sessions")
407|        return len(expired)
408|
409|
410|_speech_service_instance: Optional[SpeechService] = None
411|
412|def get_speech_service() -> SpeechService:
413|    global _speech_service_instance
414|    if _speech_service_instance is None:
415|        _speech_service_instance = SpeechService()
416|    return _speech_service_instance

55. src/services/user_store.py

  1|import os
  2|import sqlite3
  3|import threading
  4|from datetime import datetime
  5|
  6|from werkzeug.security import check_password_hash, generate_password_has
   |    h
  7|
  8|_SERVICE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
  9|_SRC_DIR = os.path.dirname(_SERVICE_DIR)
 10|_PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.dirname(_SRC_DIR)
 11|_DEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(_PROJECT_ROOT, 'data', 'users.db')
 12|
 13|
 14|class UserStore:
 15|    def __init__(self, db_path=None):
 16|        self.db_path = db_path or _DEFAULT_DB_PATH
 17|        self._init_lock = threading.Lock()
 18|        self._ensure_schema()
 19|
 20|    def _connect(self):
 21|        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path, timeout=10)
 22|        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
 23|        conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
 24|        conn.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
 25|        return conn
 26|
 27|    def _ensure_schema(self):
 28|        with self._init_lock:
 29|            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.db_path), exist_ok=True)
 30|            with self._connect() as conn:
 31|                conn.execute(
 32|                    '''
 33|                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
 34|                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
 35|                        username TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,
 36|                        password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
 37|                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
 38|                        last_login TEXT
 39|                    )
 40|                    '''
 41|                )
 42|                row = conn.execute(
 43|                    'SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ? COLLATE NOC
   |    ASE',
 44|                    ('yumo',)
 45|                ).fetchone()
 46|                if row is None:
 47|                    conn.execute(
 48|                        'INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, cre
   |    ated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
 49|                        ('yumo', generate_password_hash('123456', method
   |    ='pbkdf2:sha256'), self._now())
 50|                    )
 51|
 52|    @staticmethod
 53|    def _now():
 54|        return datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
 55|
 56|    def get_by_username(self, username):
 57|        with self._connect() as conn:
 58|            return conn.execute(
 59|                'SELECT id, username, password_hash, created_at, last_lo
   |    gin '
 60|                'FROM users WHERE username = ? COLLATE NOCASE',
 61|                (username,)
 62|            ).fetchone()
 63|
 64|    def get_by_id(self, user_id):
 65|        with self._connect() as conn:
 66|            return conn.execute(
 67|                'SELECT id, username, created_at, last_login FROM users 
   |    WHERE id = ?',
 68|                (user_id,)
 69|            ).fetchone()
 70|
 71|    def create_user(self, username, password):
 72|        password_hash = generate_password_hash(password, method='pbkdf2:
   |    sha256')
 73|        try:
 74|            with self._connect() as conn:
 75|                cursor = conn.execute(
 76|                    'INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, created
   |    _at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)',
 77|                    (username, password_hash, self._now())
 78|                )
 79|                user_id = cursor.lastrowid
 80|        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
 81|            raise ValueError('用户名已存在') from exc
 82|        return self.get_by_id(user_id)
 83|
 84|    def change_password(self, user_id, current_password, new_password):
 85|        with self._connect() as conn:
 86|            row = conn.execute(
 87|                'SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE id = ?',
 88|                (user_id,)
 89|            ).fetchone()
 90|            if row is None or not check_password_hash(row['password_hash
   |    '], current_password):
 91|                return False
 92|            conn.execute(
 93|                'UPDATE users SET password_hash = ? WHERE id = ?',
 94|                (generate_password_hash(new_password, method='pbkdf2:sha
   |    256'), user_id)
 95|            )
 96|        return True
 97|
 98|    def verify_user(self, username, password):
 99|        row = self.get_by_username(username)
100|        if row is None:
101|            return None
102|        if not check_password_hash(row['password_hash'], password):
103|            return None
104|        with self._connect() as conn:
105|            conn.execute(
106|                'UPDATE users SET last_login = ? WHERE id = ?',
107|                (self._now(), row['id'])
108|            )
109|        return {'id': row['id'], 'username': row['username']}
110|
111|
112|_store = None
113|_store_lock = threading.Lock()
114|
115|
116|def get_user_store():
117|    global _store
118|    if _store is None:
119|        with _store_lock:
120|            if _store is None:
121|                _store = UserStore()
122|    return _store

56. src/services/weight_tuner.py

  1|"""DAMO-ETS 权重自动寻优
  2|
  3|在固定随机种子下枚举若干组代表性权重方案，逐组跑一次仿真并记录
  4|能耗、温度、PUE、服务率与 deadline 违约，最后按写死的择优规则选出一组。
  5|
  6|择优规则：先筛出任务服务率不低于阈值的方案，再在其中取平均能耗最低者；
  7|若没有方案满足服务率阈值，则退化为取服务率最高的一组。
  8|"""
  9|import time
 10|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
 11|
 12|from src.algorithms.damo_ets import DAMOETSScheduler
 13|from src.simulation import DataCenterSimulator
 14|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 15|
 16|logger = setup_logger("weight_tuner")
 17|
 18|SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR = 0.98
 19|SELECTION_RULE = "服务率 >= 98% 的前提下，能耗与温度归一化后等权综合最优"
 20|
 21|DEFAULT_SCALE = {"num_servers": 40, "simulation_hours": 8, "seed": 42}
 22|
 23|CANDIDATES: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
 24|    {"name": "默认权重", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.35, "beta_energ
   |    y": 0.25, "gamma_thermal": 0.20, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.15, "epsil
   |    on_migration": 0.05}},
 25|    {"name": "温度优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.25, "beta_energ
   |    y": 0.20, "gamma_thermal": 0.35, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.15, "epsil
   |    on_migration": 0.05}},
 26|    {"name": "强温度优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.20, "beta_ene
   |    rgy": 0.20, "gamma_thermal": 0.45, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.10, "eps
   |    ilon_migration": 0.05}},
 27|    {"name": "能耗优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.20, "beta_energ
   |    y": 0.45, "gamma_thermal": 0.20, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.10, "epsil
   |    on_migration": 0.05}},
 28|    {"name": "违约优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.50, "beta_energ
   |    y": 0.20, "gamma_thermal": 0.15, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.10, "epsil
   |    on_migration": 0.05}},
 29|    {"name": "均衡方案", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.30, "beta_energ
   |    y": 0.25, "gamma_thermal": 0.25, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.15, "epsil
   |    on_migration": 0.05}},
 30|    {"name": "异构匹配优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.25, "beta_e
   |    nergy": 0.20, "gamma_thermal": 0.20, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.30, "e
   |    psilon_migration": 0.05}},
 31|    {"name": "强能耗优先", "weights": {"alpha_deadline": 0.15, "beta_ene
   |    rgy": 0.55, "gamma_thermal": 0.20, "delta_heterogeneity": 0.05, "eps
   |    ilon_migration": 0.05}},
 32|]
 33|
 34|WEIGHT_KEYS = ["alpha_deadline", "beta_energy", "gamma_thermal", "delta_
   |    heterogeneity", "epsilon_migration"]
 35|
 36|
 37|def _evaluate(weights: Dict[str, Any], num_servers: int, hours: int, see
   |    d: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 38|    simulator = DataCenterSimulator(num_servers=num_servers, simulation_
   |    hours=hours, seed=seed)
 39|    simulator.damo_ets_scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(simulator.servers, c
   |    onfig=dict(weights), seed=seed)
 40|    frame = simulator.run_simulation("damo_ets")
 41|    stats = simulator.get_statistics(frame)["damo_ets"]
 42|    return {
 43|        "avg_energy": round(stats["avg_energy"], 1),
 44|        "max_temp": round(stats["max_temp"], 2),
 45|        "avg_pue": round(stats["avg_pue"], 3),
 46|        "service_rate": round(stats.get("service_rate", 1.0), 4),
 47|        "dropped_tasks": int(stats.get("dropped_tasks", 0)),
 48|        "deadline_violations": int(stats.get("deadline_violations", 0)),
 49|    }
 50|
 51|
 52|def score_row(row: Dict[str, Any], pool: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> float:
 53|    energies = [item["metrics"]["avg_energy"] for item in pool]
 54|    temps = [item["metrics"]["max_temp"] for item in pool]
 55|    e_low, e_high = min(energies), max(energies)
 56|    t_low, t_high = min(temps), max(temps)
 57|    energy = (row["metrics"]["avg_energy"] - e_low) / (e_high - e_low) i
   |    f e_high > e_low else 0.0
 58|    thermal = (row["metrics"]["max_temp"] - t_low) / (t_high - t_low) if
   |     t_high > t_low else 0.0
 59|    return round(0.5 * energy + 0.5 * thermal, 4)
 60|
 61|
 62|def pick_best(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 63|    qualified = [row for row in rows if row["metrics"]["service_rate"] >
   |    = SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR]
 64|    pool = qualified if qualified else rows
 65|    best = min(pool, key=lambda row: score_row(row, pool))
 66|    result = dict(best)
 67|    result["score"] = score_row(best, pool)
 68|    result["qualified"] = bool(qualified)
 69|    return result
 70|
 71|
 72|def run_sweep(num_servers: Optional[int] = None, hours: Optional[int] = 
   |    None,
 73|              seed: Optional[int] = None, candidates: Optional[List[Dict
   |    [str, Any]]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 74|    scale = dict(DEFAULT_SCALE)
 75|    if num_servers:
 76|        scale["num_servers"] = int(num_servers)
 77|    if hours:
 78|        scale["simulation_hours"] = int(hours)
 79|    if seed is not None:
 80|        scale["seed"] = int(seed)
 81|
 82|    plans = candidates or CANDIDATES
 83|    started = time.perf_counter()
 84|    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
 85|    for plan in plans:
 86|        metrics = _evaluate(plan["weights"], scale["num_servers"], scale
   |    ["simulation_hours"], scale["seed"])
 87|        rows.append({"name": plan["name"], "weights": dict(plan["weights
   |    "]), "metrics": metrics})
 88|        logger.info("寻优: %s -> 能耗 %.1f W, 温度 %.2f C, 服务率 %.2f%%
   |    " % (
 89|            plan["name"], metrics["avg_energy"], metrics["max_temp"], me
   |    trics["service_rate"] * 100))
 90|
 91|    best = pick_best(rows)
 92|    baseline = next((row for row in rows if row["name"] == "默认权重"), 
   |    rows[0])
 93|    return {
 94|        "rule": SELECTION_RULE,
 95|        "service_rate_floor": SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR,
 96|        "scale": scale,
 97|        "candidates": len(rows),
 98|        "elapsed_sec": round(time.perf_counter() - started, 2),
 99|        "rows": rows,
100|        "best": best,
101|        "baseline": baseline,
102|    }
103|
104|
105|def diff_weights(weights: Dict[str, Any], base: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[
   |    Dict[str, Any]]:
106|    changes = []
107|    for key in WEIGHT_KEYS:
108|        old = float(base.get(key, 0))
109|        new = float(weights.get(key, 0))
110|        if abs(new - old) >= 0.001:
111|            changes.append({"key": key, "from": old, "to": new, "delta":
   |     round(new - old, 3)})
112|    changes.sort(key=lambda item: abs(item["delta"]), reverse=True)
113|    return changes

58. src/simulation/datacenter_simulator.py - 数据中心仿真器

  1|import inspect
  2|import numpy as np

  4|from dataclasses import dataclass
  5|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
  6|
  7|from src.models import Server, EnergyModel, TemperatureModel, Task
  8|from src.algorithms import (

 19|    SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR,

 29|
 30|DEFAULT_ALGORITHMS = ['greedy', 'genetic', 'round_robin', 'damo_ets']
 31|
 32|
 33|@dataclass
 34|class ServerSnapshot:
 35|    server_id: int
 36|    server_type: str
 37|    max_load: float
 38|    max_temp: float
 39|    base_power: float
 40|    max_power: float
 41|    current_temp: float
 42|    current_load: float
 43|    idle_power_factor: float = 0.6
 44|    dissipation_coefficient: float = 8.0
 45|    thermal_inertia: float = 0.25
 46|
 47|
 48|def _build_servers(
 49|    num_servers: int,
 50|    server_types_cfg: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]],
 51|    idle_power_factor: float = 0.6,
 52|    dissipation_coefficient: float = 8.0,
 53|    thermal_inertia: float = 0.25,
 54|) -> List[Server]:
 55|    """Build a heterogeneous server list using ``server_types`` proporti
   |    ons."""
 56|    thermal = dict(
 57|        idle_power_factor=idle_power_factor,
 58|        dissipation_coefficient=dissipation_coefficient,
 59|        thermal_inertia=thermal_inertia,
 60|    )
 61|    if not server_types_cfg:
 62|        return [Server(server_id=i, **thermal) for i in range(num_server
   |    s)]
 63|
 64|    proportions = [float(t.get("proportion", 0)) for t in server_types_c
   |    fg]
 65|    total = sum(proportions)
 66|    if total <= 0:
 67|        return [Server(server_id=i, **thermal) for i in range(num_server
   |    s)]
 68|
 69|    servers: List[Server] = []
 70|    sid = 0
 71|    allocated = 0
 72|    for idx, t in enumerate(server_types_cfg):
 73|        if idx == len(server_types_cfg) - 1:
 74|            count = num_servers - allocated
 75|        else:
 76|            count = int(round(num_servers * proportions[idx] / total))
 77|            allocated += count
 78|        for _ in range(count):
 79|            if sid >= num_servers:
 80|                break
 81|            servers.append(Server(
 82|                server_id=sid,
 83|                max_load=float(t.get("max_load", 100)),
 84|                max_temp=float(t.get("max_temp", 85)),
 85|                base_power=float(t.get("base_power", 100)),
 86|                max_power=float(t.get("max_power", 300)),
 87|                server_type=str(t.get("name", "standard")),
 88|                **thermal,
 89|            ))
 90|            sid += 1
 91|        if sid >= num_servers:
 92|            break
 93|
 94|    while len(servers) < num_servers:
 95|        servers.append(Server(server_id=len(servers), **thermal))
 96|    return servers
 97|
 98|
 99|class DataCenterSimulator:
100|    """Hour-by-hour data centre simulation with controlled algorithm com
   |    parison."""
101|
102|    def __init__(
103|        self,
104|        num_servers: Optional[int] = None,
105|        simulation_hours: Optional[int] = None,
106|        seed: Optional[int] = None,
107|        config: Optional[Config] = None,
108|        data_source_override: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
109|    ) -> None:
110|        self.logger = setup_logger("datacenter_sim")
111|
112|        cfg_data: Dict[str, Any] = {}
113|        try:
114|            cfg = config or Config()
115|            cfg_data = cfg.to_dict()
116|        except Exception:
117|            cfg_data = {}
118|
119|        if data_source_override:
120|            merged_source = dict(cfg_data.get("data_source", {}) or {})
121|            merged_source.update(data_source_override)
122|            cfg_data["data_source"] = merged_source
123|
124|        sim_cfg = cfg_data.get("simulation", {}) or {}
125|        self.num_servers: int = int(
126|            num_servers if num_servers is not None else sim_cfg.get("num
   |    _servers", 10)
127|        )
128|        self.simulation_hours: int = int(
129|            simulation_hours if simulation_hours is not None
130|            else sim_cfg.get("simulation_hours", 24)
131|        )
132|        if seed is None:
133|            yaml_seed = sim_cfg.get("random_seed")
134|            seed = int(yaml_seed) if yaml_seed is not None else None
135|        self.seed = seed
136|
137|        server_types_cfg = cfg_data.get("server_types")
138|        if not isinstance(server_types_cfg, list):
139|            server_types_cfg = None
140|
141|        task_gen_cfg = cfg_data.get("task_generation", {}) or {}
142|        task_types_cfg = task_gen_cfg.get("task_types")
143|        if not isinstance(task_types_cfg, list):
144|            task_types_cfg = None
145|
146|        self.sla_slack: float = float(sim_cfg.get("sla_slack", 1.5))
147|        completion_cfg = cfg_data.get("task_completion", {}) or {}
148|        self.completion_mode: str = str(completion_cfg.get("mode", "serv
   |    ice_based"))
149|        self.completion_probability: float = float(completion_cfg.get("c
   |    ompletion_probability", 0.3))
150|
151|        cooling_cfg = cfg_data.get("cooling", {}) or {}
152|        self.cooling_power_coefficient: float = float(cooling_cfg.get("p
   |    ower_coefficient", 500))
153|        self.cooling_target_temp: float = float(cooling_cfg.get("target_
   |    temp", 22))
154|
155|        energy_cfg = cfg_data.get("energy", {}) or {}
156|        self.idle_power_factor: float = float(energy_cfg.get("idle_power
   |    _factor", 0.6))
157|        self.dissipation_coefficient: float = float(cooling_cfg.get("dis
   |    sipation_coefficient", 8.0))
158|        self.thermal_inertia: float = float(cooling_cfg.get("thermal_ine
   |    rtia", 0.25))
159|        self.fan_target_temp: float = float(cooling_cfg.get("fan_target_
   |    temp", 35))
160|
161|        self.servers: List[Server] = _build_servers(
162|            self.num_servers,
163|            server_types_cfg,
164|            self.idle_power_factor,
165|            self.dissipation_coefficient,
166|            self.thermal_inertia,
167|        )
168|        self.task_generator: TaskGenerator = TaskGenerator(
169|            base_rate=int(task_gen_cfg.get("base_rate", 10)),
170|            peak_rate=int(task_gen_cfg.get("peak_rate", 50)),
171|            peak_hours=tuple(task_gen_cfg.get("peak_hours", (9, 18))),
172|            task_types=task_types_cfg,
173|            seed=seed,
174|            sla_slack=self.sla_slack,

272|    def build_scenario(self, hours: Optional[int] = None) -> List[List[T
   |    askSpec]]:
273|        """Freeze the task stream for one scenario.
274|
275|        Generated once and replayed by every algorithm, so the compariso
   |    n is
276|        controlled: all schedulers face the identical demand sequence.
277|        """
278|        hours = self.simulation_hours if hours is None else hours
279|        if self.trace_stream:
280|            source = self.trace_stream
281|            self._stream = [list(source[h % len(source)]) for h in range
   |    (hours)]
282|            if hours > len(source):
283|                self.run_metadata["scenario_cycle_hours"] = len(source)
284|        else:
285|            self._stream = self.task_generator.build_stream(hours)
286|        self.scenario_digest = TaskGenerator.stream_digest(self._stream)
287|        self.run_metadata["scenario_digest"] = self.scenario_digest
288|        self.run_metadata["scenario_tasks"] = int(sum(len(h) for h in se
   |    lf._stream))
289|        self.run_metadata["scenario_source"] = "catalog" if self.trace_s
   |    tream else "synthetic"
290|        return self._stream

351|    def _resolve_scheduler(self, algorithm: str) -> BaseScheduler:
352|        if algorithm == 'greedy':
353|            return self.greedy_scheduler
354|        if algorithm == 'genetic':
355|            return self.genetic_scheduler
356|        if algorithm == 'round_robin':
357|            return self.round_robin_scheduler
358|        if algorithm == 'damo_ets':
359|            return self.damo_ets_scheduler
360|        if is_plugin_algorithm(algorithm):
361|            spec = PLUGIN_ALGORITHMS[algorithm]
362|            return PluginSchedulerAdapter(self.servers, str(spec["plugin
   |    "]), spec.get("config"))
363|        raise ValueError(f"Unknown algorithm: {algorithm}")

365|    def _ambient_at(self, hour: int, series: Optional[List[float]] = Non
   |    e) -> float:
366|        series = self.ambient_series if series is None else series
367|        if series:
368|            return float(series[(self.ambient_offset + hour) % len(serie
   |    s)])
369|        return float(15 + 10 * np.sin((hour - 6) * np.pi / 12))

371|    def _release_finished_tasks(self, hour: int, rng: np.random.Generato
   |    r) -> None:
372|        for server in self.servers:
373|            for task in server.tasks[:]:
374|                if self.completion_mode == "service_based":
375|                    task.service_hour(1.0)
376|                    if task.is_service_complete():
377|                        server.remove_task(task)
378|                        task.completed_time = hour
379|                elif rng.random() < self.completion_probability:
380|                    server.remove_task(task)
381|                    task.completed_time = hour

384|    def run_simulation(
385|        self,
386|        algorithm: str = 'greedy',
387|        task_stream: Optional[List[List[TaskSpec]]] = None,
388|    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
389|        self.logger.info(f"Starting simulation with {algorithm} algorith
   |    m")
390|        scheduler = self._resolve_scheduler(algorithm)
391|
392|        if hasattr(scheduler, "reset"):
393|            scheduler.reset()
394|
395|        stream = self._stream if task_stream is None else task_stream
396|        if stream is None:
397|            stream = self.build_scenario()
398|        scenario = self.task_generator.materialize_stream(stream)
399|        supports_hour = 'current_hour' in inspect.signature(scheduler.sc
   |    hedule_tasks).parameters
400|
401|        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed)
402|        self.results = {k: [] for k in self.results}
403|        self._seen_tasks = []
404|
405|        for hour in range(self.simulation_hours):
406|            ambient_temp = self._ambient_at(hour)
407|            if hour < len(scenario):
408|                new_tasks = scenario[hour]
409|            else:
410|                new_tasks = self.task_generator.generate_tasks(hour)
411|            self._seen_tasks.extend(new_tasks)
412|
413|            if supports_hour:
414|                placements = scheduler.schedule_tasks(new_tasks, current
   |    _hour=hour)  # type: ignore[call-arg]
415|            else:
416|                placements = scheduler.schedule_tasks(new_tasks)
417|
418|            assigned = sum(1 for _task, _server in placements if _server
   |     is not None)
419|            dropped = len(placements) - assigned
420|
421|            hottest = max((s.current_temp for s in self.servers), defaul
   |    t=ambient_temp)
422|            fan_speed = self.temp_model.adjust_fan_speed(
423|                self.fan_target_temp, reference_temp=hottest
424|            )
425|            cooling_throughput = fan_speed * self.cooling_power_coeffici
   |    ent
426|            self.temp_model.update_room_temperature(
427|                self.servers, ambient_temp, cooling_throughput
428|            )
429|
430|            for server in self.servers:
431|                server.update_temperature(ambient_temp, fan_speed)
432|
433|            # Measure while the work is still resident: releasing first 
   |    would
434|            # report zero load (and base-only power) for every task that
   |     fits
435|            # inside one hour, which is exactly what a sub-hourly trace 
   |    produces.
436|            total_load = sum(s.current_load for s in self.servers)
437|            served_load = sum(task.load for task, server in placements i
   |    f server is not None)
438|            temperatures = [s.current_temp for s in self.servers] or [am
   |    bient_temp]
439|            avg_temp = float(np.mean(temperatures))
440|            peak_temp = float(np.max(temperatures))
441|            overheated = sum(1 for s in self.servers if s.is_overheated(
   |    ))
442|            cooling_power = self.energy_model.calculate_cooling_energy(
443|                self.servers, ambient_temp, self.cooling_target_temp, ho
   |    ttest
444|            )
445|            total_energy = self.energy_model.calculate_total_energy(
446|                self.servers, ambient_temp, self.cooling_target_temp, ho
   |    ttest
447|            )
448|            pue = self.energy_model.get_pue(
449|                self.servers, ambient_temp, self.cooling_target_temp, ho
   |    ttest
450|            )
451|
452|            self._release_finished_tasks(hour, rng)
453|
454|            self.results['time'].append(hour)
455|            self.results['total_load'].append(total_load)
456|            self.results['avg_temperature'].append(avg_temp)
457|            self.results['peak_temperature'].append(peak_temp)
458|            self.results['overheated_servers'].append(overheated)
459|            self.results['ambient_temperature'].append(ambient_temp)
460|            self.results['total_energy'].append(total_energy)
461|            self.results['pue'].append(pue)
462|            self.results['cooling_power'].append(cooling_power)
463|            self.results['fan_speed'].append(fan_speed)
464|            self.results['algorithm'].append(algorithm)
465|            self.results['deadline_violations'].append(
466|                count_deadline_violations(self._seen_tasks, hour)
467|            )
468|            self.results['assigned'].append(assigned)
469|            self.results['dropped'].append(dropped)
470|            self.results['offered_tasks'].append(len(new_tasks))
471|            self.results['offered_load'].append(sum(t.load for t in new_
   |    tasks))
472|            self.results['served_load'].append(served_load)
473|            self.results['co2_kg'].append(
474|                total_energy * self.emission_factor / 1000.0 if self.emi
   |    ssion_factor else None
475|            )
476|
477|        self.logger.info(f"Simulation completed with {algorithm} algorit
   |    hm")
478|        frame = pd.DataFrame(self.results)
479|        if not self.emission_factor and 'co2_kg' in frame.columns:
480|            frame = frame.drop(columns=['co2_kg'])
481|        return frame
482|
483|    def run_comparison(self, algorithms: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> 
   |    pd.DataFrame:
484|        if algorithms is None:
485|            algorithms = list(DEFAULT_ALGORITHMS)
486|
487|        self.results = {k: [] for k in self.results}
488|        if self._stream is None:
489|            self.build_scenario()
490|
491|        baseline_snapshot = self.create_snapshot()
492|
493|        dfs = []
494|        skipped: List[str] = []
495|        for algo in algorithms:
496|            self.restore_from_snapshot(baseline_snapshot)
497|            try:
498|                df = self.run_simulation(algo)
499|            except Exception as exc:
500|                self.logger.error(f"算法 {algo} 执行失败, 已跳过: {exc}"
   |    )
501|                skipped.append(algo)
502|                self.results = {k: [] for k in self.results}
503|                continue
504|            dfs.append(df)
505|            self.results = {k: [] for k in self.results}
506|
507|        self.skipped_algorithms = skipped
508|        if not dfs:
509|            raise RuntimeError("所有算法均执行失败, 请检查插件依赖与配置
   |    ")
510|
511|        self.raw_results = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)
512|        self.statistics = summarize(self.raw_results)
513|        self.ranking = rank_algorithms(self.statistics)
514|        return self.raw_results
515|
516|    def reset_servers(self) -> None:
517|        self.servers = [Server(i) for i in range(self.num_servers)]
518|        self._update_schedulers()
519|
520|    def get_statistics(self, df: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None) -> Dict[
   |    str, Dict[str, Any]]:
521|        if df is None:
522|            if self.statistics:
523|                return self.statistics
524|            if self.raw_results is not None:
525|                df = self.raw_results
526|            else:
527|                raise ValueError("尚无仿真结果, 请先运行 run_comparison 
   |    或 run_simulation")
528|        return summarize(df)
529|
530|    def get_ranking(self, df: Optional[pd.DataFrame] = None) -> Dict[str
   |    , Any]:
531|        stats = self.get_statistics(df)
532|        self.ranking = rank_algorithms(stats)
533|        return self.ranking
534|
535|    def _first_hour_column(self, df: pd.DataFrame, column: str) -> Dict[
   |    str, Optional[float]]:
536|        values: Dict[str, Optional[float]] = {}
537|        if df is None or df.empty or column not in df.columns:
538|            return values
539|        for algo in df["algorithm"].unique():
540|            algo_df = df[df["algorithm"] == algo].sort_values("time")
541|            values[algo] = safe_number(algo_df.iloc[0][column])
542|        return values
543|
544|    def first_hour_loads(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Optional[f
   |    loat]]:
545|        """Served load at hour 0; differs only by each scheduler's own c
   |    hoices."""
546|        return self._first_hour_column(df, "total_load")
547|
548|    def first_hour_offered(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Optional
   |    [float]]:
549|        """Offered demand at hour 0. Equal values prove a controlled run
   |    ."""
550|        offered = self._first_hour_column(df, "offered_load")
551|        if offered:
552|            return offered
553|        return self.first_hour_loads(df)
554|
555|    def control_signature(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Optional[
   |    float]]:
556|        return self.first_hour_offered(df)
557|
558|    def is_controlled(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> bool:
559|        values = list(self.control_signature(df).values())
560|        return len(values) > 0 and len(set(values)) == 1

 18|class DistributedSimulator:
 19|    def __init__(self, num_servers: int = 100, parallel_workers: int = N
   |    one):
 20|        self.num_servers = num_servers
 21|        self.parallel_workers = parallel_workers or min(8, cpu_count())
 22|        self.servers: List[Server] = []
 23|        self.tasks: List[Task] = []
 24|        self.energy_model = EnergyModel()
 25|        self.temp_model = TemperatureModel()
 26|        
 27|        self._initialize_servers()
 28|
 29|    def _initialize_servers(self):
 30|        server_types = config.server_types
 31|        for i in range(self.num_servers):
 32|            rand = np.random.random()
 33|            cum_prob = 0.0
 34|            server_type = server_types[0]
 35|            
 36|            for st in server_types:
 37|                cum_prob += st['proportion']
 38|                if rand < cum_prob:
 39|                    server_type = st
 40|                    break
 41|            
 42|            self.servers.append(Server(
 43|                server_id=i,
 44|                max_load=server_type['max_load'],
 45|                max_temp=server_type['max_temp'],
 46|                base_power=server_type['base_power'],
 47|                max_power=server_type['max_power'],
 48|                efficiency=server_type.get('efficiency', 0.85)
 49|            ))
 50|
 51|    def _generate_tasks_for_hour(self, hour: int) -> List[Task]:
 52|        tasks = []
 53|        task_types = config.task_generation.get('task_types', [])
 54|        
 55|        base_rate = config.task_generation.get('base_rate', 10)
 56|        peak_rate = config.task_generation.get('peak_rate', 50)
 57|        peak_hours = config.task_generation.get('peak_hours', [9, 18])
 58|        
 59|        if peak_hours[0] <= hour < peak_hours[1]:
 60|            task_count = int(np.random.poisson(peak_rate))
 61|        else:
 62|            task_count = int(np.random.poisson(base_rate))
 63|        
 64|        for i in range(task_count):
 65|            rand = np.random.random()
 66|            cum_prob = 0.0
 67|            task_type = task_types[0] if task_types else None
 68|            
 69|            for tt in task_types:
 70|                cum_prob += tt['proportion']
 71|                if rand < cum_prob:
 72|                    task_type = tt
 73|                    break
 74|            
 75|            if task_type:
 76|                load = np.random.randint(task_type['load_range'][0], tas
   |    k_type['load_range'][1] + 1)
 77|                duration = np.random.randint(task_type['duration_range']
   |    [0], task_type['duration_range'][1] + 1)
 78|                priority = np.random.randint(1, 6)
 79|            else:
 80|                load_range = config.task_generation.get('load_range', [5
   |    , 30])
 81|                load = np.random.randint(load_range[0], load_range[1] + 
   |    1)
 82|                duration = np.random.randint(1, 6)
 83|                priority = np.random.randint(1, 6)
 84|            
 85|            tasks.append(Task(
 86|                task_id=f"task_{hour}_{i}",
 87|                load=load,
 88|                priority=priority,
 89|                deadline=hour + duration,
 90|                created_time=hour
 91|            ))
 92|        
 93|        return tasks
 94|
 95|    def _simulate_single_hour(self, hour: int, server_snapshot: List[Dic
   |    t[str, float]], 
 96|                              pending_tasks: List[Task], algorithm: str)
   |     -> Dict[str, Any]:
 97|        new_tasks = self._generate_tasks_for_hour(hour)
 98|        all_tasks = pending_tasks + new_tasks
 99|        
100|        servers = [Server(
101|            server_id=i,
102|            max_load=server_snapshot[i]['max_load'],
103|            max_temp=server_snapshot[i]['max_temp'],
104|            base_power=server_snapshot[i]['base_power'],
105|            max_power=server_snapshot[i]['max_power'],
106|            current_temp=server_snapshot[i]['current_temp'],
107|            current_load=server_snapshot[i]['current_load']
108|        ) for i in range(len(server_snapshot))]
109|        
110|        if algorithm == 'greedy':
111|            scheduler = GreedyScheduler(servers)
112|            assignments = scheduler.schedule_tasks(all_tasks)
113|        elif algorithm == 'genetic':
114|            scheduler = GeneticAlgorithm(servers, all_tasks)
115|            assignments = scheduler.schedule()
116|        elif algorithm == 'round_robin':
117|            scheduler = RoundRobinScheduler(servers)
118|            assignments = scheduler.schedule_tasks(all_tasks)
119|        else:
120|            assignments = []
121|        
122|        for task, server in assignments:
123|            server.assign_task(task)
124|        
125|        for server in servers:
126|            server.update_temperature()
127|        
128|        completed_tasks = [t for t in all_tasks if t.completed_time == h
   |    our]
129|        remaining_tasks = [t for t in all_tasks if t.completed_time is N
   |    one]
130|        
131|        total_load = sum(s.current_load for s in servers)
132|        avg_temp = sum(s.current_temp for s in servers) / len(servers)
133|        total_energy = sum(self.energy_model.calculate_power(s) for s in
   |     servers)
134|        pue = self.energy_model.calculate_pue(total_energy, avg_temp)
135|        
136|        return {
137|            'hour': hour,
138|            'algorithm': algorithm,
139|            'total_load': total_load,
140|            'avg_temperature': avg_temp,
141|            'total_energy': total_energy,
142|            'pue': pue,
143|            'completed_tasks': len(completed_tasks),
144|            'remaining_tasks': len(remaining_tasks),
145|            'server_snapshot': [{
146|                'max_load': s.max_load,
147|                'max_temp': s.max_temp,
148|                'base_power': s.base_power,
149|                'max_power': s.max_power,
150|                'current_temp': s.current_temp,
151|                'current_load': s.current_load
152|            } for s in servers],
153|            'remaining_tasks_data': [{
154|                'task_id': t.task_id,
155|                'load': t.load,
156|                'priority': t.priority,
157|                'deadline': t.deadline,
158|                'created_time': t.created_time
159|            } for t in remaining_tasks]
160|        }
161|
162|    def run_distributed_simulation(self, hours: int = 24, algorithm: str
   |     = 'greedy') -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
163|        results = []
164|        server_snapshot = [{
165|            'max_load': s.max_load,
166|            'max_temp': s.max_temp,
167|            'base_power': s.base_power,
168|            'max_power': s.max_power,
169|            'current_temp': 25.0,
170|            'current_load': 0.0
171|        } for s in self.servers]
172|        pending_tasks: List[Task] = []
173|        
174|        logger.info(f"Starting distributed simulation with {self.num_ser
   |    vers} servers, {hours} hours, algorithm: {algorithm}")
175|        
176|        for hour in range(hours):
177|            result = self._simulate_single_hour(hour, server_snapshot, p
   |    ending_tasks, algorithm)
178|            results.append(result)
179|            
180|            server_snapshot = result['server_snapshot']
181|            pending_tasks = [Task(**t) for t in result['remaining_tasks_
   |    data']]
182|            
183|            if hour % 6 == 0:
184|                logger.info(f"Completed hour {hour}/{hours}")
185|        
186|        logger.info(f"Distributed simulation completed. Total records: {
   |    len(results)}")
187|        return results
188|
189|    def run_parallel_comparison(self, algorithms: List[str], hours: int 
   |    = 24) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
190|        results = {}
191|        
192|        def run_algorithm(algorithm):
193|            simulator = DistributedSimulator(num_servers=self.num_server
   |    s)
194|            return algorithm, simulator.run_distributed_simulation(hours
   |    =hours, algorithm=algorithm)
195|        
196|        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=min(len(algorithms), self.p
   |    arallel_workers)) as executor:
197|            futures = {executor.submit(run_algorithm, algo): algo for al
   |    go in algorithms}
198|            
199|            for future in as_completed(futures):
200|                algo, algo_results = future.result()
201|                results[algo] = algo_results
202|                logger.info(f"Completed {algo} algorithm")
203|        
204|        return results
205|
206|    def run_large_scale_test(self, server_counts: List[int], hours: int 
   |    = 24) -> Dict[str, Any]:
207|        results = {}
208|        
209|        for count in server_counts:
210|            logger.info(f"Testing with {count} servers")
211|            simulator = DistributedSimulator(num_servers=count)
212|            result = simulator.run_distributed_simulation(hours=hours, a
   |    lgorithm='greedy')
213|            
214|            avg_pue = sum(r['pue'] for r in result) / len(result)
215|            avg_energy = sum(r['total_energy'] for r in result) / len(re
   |    sult)
216|            avg_temp = sum(r['avg_temperature'] for r in result) / len(r
   |    esult)
217|            
218|            results[count] = {
219|                'avg_pue': avg_pue,
220|                'avg_energy': avg_energy,
221|                'avg_temperature': avg_temp,
222|                'total_hours': hours,
223|                'server_count': count
224|            }
225|        
226|        return results

================================================================================
60. src/simulation/metrics.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Shared metric definitions for algorithm comparison.
  2|
  3|Everything that ranks algorithms lives here so that the CLI, the experim
   |    ent
  4|scripts, the Web API and the frontend all apply the same rule:
  5|
  6|    1. drop every algorithm whose service rate is below the floor,
  7|    2. rank the rest by energy per unit of actually served load.
  8|
  9|Ranking by raw energy alone rewards an algorithm for serving less work.
 10|"""
 11|
 12|from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional
 13|
 14|import pandas as pd
 15|
 16|SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR = 0.98
 17|
 18|RANKING_RULE = "服务率 >= %.0f%% 的前提下，按单位服务负载能耗升序排名" % 
   |    (
 19|    SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR * 100.0,
 20|)
 21|
 22|
 23|def safe_number(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
 24|    """Convert to float, mapping NaN/inf/None to None so JSON stays vali
   |    d."""
 25|    try:
 26|        number = float(value)
 27|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
 28|        return None
 29|    if number != number or number in (float("inf"), float("-inf")):
 30|        return None
 31|    return number
 32|
 33|
 34|def count_deadline_violations(tasks: Iterable[Any], last_hour: int) -> i
   |    nt:
 35|    missed = 0
 36|    for task in tasks:
 37|        if task.missed_deadline(last_hour):
 38|            missed += 1
 39|    return missed
 40|
 41|
 42|def _numeric(df: pd.DataFrame, name: str) -> Optional[pd.Series]:
 43|    """Coerce a column to numbers, or return None when it is absent."""
 44|    if name not in df.columns:
 45|        return None
 46|    return pd.to_numeric(df[name], errors="coerce")
 47|
 48|
 49|def _first_present(df: pd.DataFrame, names: Iterable[str]) -> Optional[p
   |    d.Series]:
 50|    for name in names:
 51|        series = _numeric(df, name)
 52|        if series is not None:
 53|            return series
 54|    return None
 55|
 56|
 57|def summarize(df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
 58|    """Per-algorithm summary used by every consumer of the results."""
 59|    stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
 60|    if df is None or df.empty or "algorithm" not in df.columns:
 61|        return stats
 62|
 63|    for algo in df["algorithm"].unique():
 64|        algo_df = df[df["algorithm"] == algo]
 65|        entry: Dict[str, Any] = {"records": int(len(algo_df))}
 66|
 67|        energy = _numeric(algo_df, "total_energy")
 68|        entry["avg_energy"] = safe_number(energy.mean()) if energy is no
   |    t None else None
 69|        entry["total_energy"] = safe_number(energy.sum()) if energy is n
   |    ot None else None
 70|
 71|        pue = _numeric(algo_df, "pue")
 72|        entry["avg_pue"] = safe_number(pue.mean()) if pue is not None el
   |    se None
 73|
 74|        load = _numeric(algo_df, "total_load")
 75|        entry["total_load"] = safe_number(load.sum()) if load is not Non
   |    e else None
 76|
 77|        peak = _first_present(algo_df, ("peak_temperature", "avg_tempera
   |    ture"))
 78|        if peak is None:
 79|            entry["max_temp"] = None
 80|            entry["p95_temp"] = None
 81|        else:
 82|            peak = peak.dropna()
 83|            entry["max_temp"] = safe_number(peak.max()) if len(peak) els
   |    e None
 84|            entry["p95_temp"] = safe_number(peak.quantile(0.95)) if len(
   |    peak) else None
 85|
 86|        avg_temp = _numeric(algo_df, "avg_temperature")
 87|        entry["avg_temp"] = safe_number(avg_temp.mean()) if avg_temp is 
   |    not None else None
 88|
 89|        assigned_series = _numeric(algo_df, "assigned")
 90|        dropped_series = _numeric(algo_df, "dropped")
 91|        assigned = int(assigned_series.sum()) if assigned_series is not 
   |    None else 0
 92|        dropped = int(dropped_series.sum()) if dropped_series is not Non
   |    e else 0
 93|        served = assigned + dropped
 94|        entry["assigned_tasks"] = assigned
 95|        entry["dropped_tasks"] = dropped
 96|        entry["service_rate"] = round(assigned / served, 4) if served el
   |    se 1.0
 97|
 98|        violations = _numeric(algo_df, "deadline_violations")
 99|        entry["deadline_violations"] = int(violations.max()) if violatio
   |    ns is not None else 0
100|        entry["deadline_violation_rate"] = (
101|            round(entry["deadline_violations"] / served, 4) if served el
   |    se 0.0
102|        )
103|
104|        co2 = _numeric(algo_df, "co2_kg")
105|        if co2 is not None:
106|            entry["co2_kg"] = safe_number(co2.sum())
107|
108|        overheated = _numeric(algo_df, "overheated_servers")
109|        if overheated is not None:
110|            entry["overheated_hours"] = int((overheated > 0).sum())
111|
112|        total_energy = entry["total_energy"]
113|        served = _numeric(algo_df, "served_load")
114|        if served is not None:
115|            served_sum = float(served.sum())
116|        else:
117|            served_sum = entry["total_load"] or 0.0
118|        entry["served_load"] = safe_number(served_sum)
119|        if total_energy is not None and served_sum:
120|            entry["energy_per_served_load"] = round(total_energy / serve
   |    d_sum, 4)
121|        else:
122|            entry["energy_per_served_load"] = None
123|
124|        stats[algo] = entry
125|
126|    return stats
127|
128|
129|def rank_algorithms(
130|    stats: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
131|    floor: float = SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR,
132|) -> Dict[str, Any]:
133|    """Apply the documented ranking rule and return the full decision tr
   |    ace."""
134|    eligible: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
135|    excluded: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
136|
137|    for algo, metrics in stats.items():
138|        rate = metrics.get("service_rate")
139|        rate = 1.0 if rate is None else float(rate)
140|        intensity = metrics.get("energy_per_served_load")
141|        record = {
142|            "algorithm": algo,
143|            "service_rate": round(rate, 4),
144|            "energy_per_served_load": intensity,
145|            "max_temp": metrics.get("max_temp"),
146|        }
147|        if rate < floor:
148|            record["reason"] = "服务率低于 %.0f%%，不参与能耗排名" % (flo
   |    or * 100.0)
149|            excluded.append(record)
150|        elif intensity is None:
151|            record["reason"] = "缺少单位服务负载能耗，无法参与排名"
152|            excluded.append(record)
153|        else:
154|            eligible.append(record)
155|
156|    eligible.sort(
157|        key=lambda item: (
158|            item["energy_per_served_load"],
159|            item["max_temp"] if item["max_temp"] is not None else float(
   |    "inf"),
160|            item["algorithm"],
161|        )
162|    )
163|
164|    for position, record in enumerate(eligible, start=1):
165|        record["rank"] = position
166|
167|    best = eligible[0]["algorithm"] if eligible else None
168|    best_metrics = None
169|    if best is not None:
170|        best_metrics = {
171|            "algorithm": best,
172|            "energy_per_served_load": eligible[0]["energy_per_served_loa
   |    d"],
173|            "service_rate": eligible[0]["service_rate"],
174|            "avg_pue": stats[best].get("avg_pue"),
175|            "max_temp": stats[best].get("max_temp"),
176|        }
177|
178|    return {
179|        "rule": RANKING_RULE,
180|        "service_rate_floor": floor,
181|        "eligible": eligible,
182|        "excluded": excluded,
183|        "best": best,
184|        "best_metrics": best_metrics,
185|        "note": (
186|            "没有算法满足服务率门槛，本轮不给出能耗排名"
187|            if not eligible
188|            else "排名基于单位服务负载能耗，已排除服务率不足的算法"
189|        ),
190|    }

61. src/simulation/task_generator.py
================================================================================
  1|import hashlib
  2|import json
  3|import numpy as np
  4|from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
  5|from src.models import Task
  6|
  7|TaskSpec = Tuple[int, int, str, int, int]
  8|
  9|
 10|class TaskGenerator:
 11|    DEFAULT_TASK_TYPES: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
 12|        {"name": "cpu_bound",    "proportion": 0.4, "load_range": [10, 5
   |    0], "duration_range": [1, 5]},
 13|        {"name": "gpu_bound",    "proportion": 0.3, "load_range": [20, 8
   |    0], "duration_range": [2, 10]},
 14|        {"name": "memory_bound", "proportion": 0.2, "load_range": [5, 25
   |    ],  "duration_range": [3, 8]},
 15|        {"name": "io_bound",     "proportion": 0.1, "load_range": [3, 15
   |    ],  "duration_range": [1, 3]},
 16|    ]
 17|
 18|    def __init__(
 19|        self,
 20|        base_rate: int = 10,
 21|        peak_rate: int = 50,
 22|        peak_hours: tuple = (9, 18),
 23|        task_types: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
 24|        seed: Optional[int] = None,
 25|        sla_slack: float = 1.5,
 26|    ) -> None:
 27|        self.base_rate: int = base_rate
 28|        self.peak_rate: int = peak_rate
 29|        self.peak_hours: tuple = peak_hours
 30|        self.task_types: List[Dict[str, Any]] = task_types or self.DEFAU
   |    LT_TASK_TYPES
 31|        self.sla_slack: float = float(sla_slack)
 32|        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
 33|        self.np_rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
 34|
 35|    def get_rate_at_hour(self, hour: int) -> int:
 36|        start, end = self.peak_hours
 37|        if start <= hour < end:
 38|            return self.peak_rate
 39|        return self.base_rate
 40|
 41|    def _sample_workload_class(self) -> str:
 42|        names = [t["name"] for t in self.task_types]
 43|        weights = [float(t.get("proportion", 0)) for t in self.task_type
   |    s]
 44|        total = sum(weights)
 45|        if total <= 0:
 46|            return names[0] if names else "cpu_bound"
 47|        probs = [w / total for w in weights]
 48|        return str(self.rng.choice(names, p=probs))
 49|
 50|    def _range_for(self, workload_class: str, key: str) -> tuple:
 51|        for t in self.task_types:
 52|            if t.get("name") == workload_class:
 53|                rng = t.get(key)
 54|                if isinstance(rng, (list, tuple)) and len(rng) == 2:
 55|                    return int(rng[0]), int(rng[1])
 56|        return 5, 30
 57|
 58|    def _deadline_offset(self, duration: int) -> int:
 59|        """Deadline relative to arrival: duration scaled by the SLA slac
   |    k factor.
 60|
 61|        ``sla_slack=1.0`` means zero queueing tolerance, so any task tha
   |    t is not
 62|        served immediately misses its deadline.
 63|        """
 64|        scaled = int(round(duration * self.sla_slack))
 65|        return max(duration, scaled, 1)
 66|
 67|    def generate_tasks(self, current_time: int) -> List[Task]:
 68|        return [self._materialize(spec, current_time) for spec in self.g
   |    enerate_specs(current_time)]
 69|
 70|    def generate_specs(self, current_time: int) -> List[TaskSpec]:
 71|        hour = current_time % 24
 72|        rate = self.get_rate_at_hour(hour)
 73|        num_tasks = int(self.rng.poisson(rate))
 74|        specs: List[TaskSpec] = []
 75|
 76|        for _ in range(num_tasks):
 77|            workload_class = self._sample_workload_class()
 78|            lo, hi = self._range_for(workload_class, "load_range")
 79|            load = int(self.rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
 80|            priority = int(self.rng.integers(1, 6))
 81|
 82|            d_lo, d_hi = self._range_for(workload_class, "duration_range
   |    ")
 83|            duration = int(self.rng.integers(d_lo, d_hi + 1))
 84|            specs.append(
 85|                (load, priority, workload_class, duration, self._deadlin
   |    e_offset(duration))
 86|            )
 87|
 88|        return specs
 89|
 90|    def build_stream(self, hours: int) -> List[List[TaskSpec]]:
 91|        """Generate the whole scenario once.
 92|
 93|        Every algorithm replays this exact stream, which is what makes t
   |    he
 94|        cross-algorithm comparison controlled instead of four separate r
   |    uns.
 95|        """
 96|        return [self.generate_specs(hour) for hour in range(hours)]
 97|
 98|    @staticmethod
 99|    def _materialize(spec: TaskSpec, arrival_hour: int) -> Task:
100|        load, priority, workload_class, duration, sla_offset = spec
101|        task = Task(
102|            load=load,
103|            priority=priority,
104|            deadline=arrival_hour + sla_offset,
105|            arrival_time=arrival_hour,
106|            workload_class=workload_class,
107|            duration=duration,
108|        )
109|        task.created_time = arrival_hour
110|        return task
111|
112|    def materialize_stream(self, stream: List[List[TaskSpec]]) -> List[L
   |    ist[Task]]:
113|        """Build fresh Task objects for one algorithm run.
114|
115|        Tasks carry mutable state (load on a server, start and completio
   |    n
116|        times), so each run needs its own instances even though the stre
   |    am
117|        values are shared.
118|        """
119|        return [
120|            [self._materialize(spec, hour) for spec in hour_specs]
121|            for hour, hour_specs in enumerate(stream)
122|        ]
123|
124|    @staticmethod
125|    def stream_digest(stream: List[List[TaskSpec]]) -> str:
126|        payload = json.dumps(stream, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii
   |    =True)
127|        return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
128|
129|    def generate_daily_profile(self) -> List[Dict]:
130|        profile = []
131|        for hour in range(24):
132|            rate = self.get_rate_at_hour(hour)
133|            profile.append({
134|                'hour': hour,
135|                'rate': rate,
136|                'is_peak': self.peak_hours[0] <= hour < self.peak_hours[
   |    1]
137|            })
138|        return profile
