63. src/testing/compatibility_tester.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|兼容性测试模块
  3|
  4|支持多维度的兼容性测试:
  5|- 跨Python版本 (3.9/3.10/3.11/3.12)
  6|- 跨操作系统 (openEuler/CentOS/Ubuntu/Windows)
  7|- 依赖版本矩阵
  8|- 硬件平台 (x86_64/arm64)
  9|- API向后兼容
 10|
 11|参考项目: tox, nox, pytest-matrix
 12|"""
 13|
 14|import sys
 15|import platform
 16|import subprocess
 17|import importlib
 18|from importlib.metadata import version, PackageNotFoundError
 19|from packaging.version import parse as parse_version
 20|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
 21|from dataclasses import dataclass
 22|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 23|from src.utils.config import Config
 24|
 25|logger = setup_logger("compatibility_tester")
 26|config = Config()
 27|
 28|@dataclass
 29|class CompatibilityCheck:
 30|    name: str
 31|    dimension: str
 32|    expected: str
 33|    actual: str
 34|    compatible: bool
 35|    details: str = ""
 36|
 37|class CompatibilityTester:
 38|    """
 39|    兼容性测试器
 40|    
 41|    验证系统在不同环境下的兼容性。
 42|    支持矩阵化的兼容性验证。
 43|    """
 44|
 45|    def __init__(self):
 46|        self.checks: List[CompatibilityCheck] = []
 47|
 48|    def check_python_version(self, min_version: str = "3.9", max_version
   |    : str = "3.12") -> CompatibilityCheck:
 49|        current = f"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}"
 50|        compatible = tuple(map(int, current.split('.'))) >= tuple(map(in
   |    t, min_version.split('.')))
 51|        check = CompatibilityCheck(
 52|            name="Python版本检查",
 53|            dimension="python_version",
 54|            expected=f">={min_version}",
 55|            actual=current,
 56|            compatible=compatible
 57|        )
 58|        self.checks.append(check)
 59|        return check
 60|
 61|    def check_os_compatibility(self, supported_os: List[str] = None) -> 
   |    CompatibilityCheck:
 62|        if supported_os is None:
 63|            supported_os = ["Linux", "Windows", "Darwin"]
 64|        current = platform.system()
 65|        compatible = current in supported_os
 66|        check = CompatibilityCheck(
 67|            name="操作系统兼容",
 68|            dimension="os",
 69|            expected=str(supported_os),
 70|            actual=current,
 71|            compatible=compatible
 72|        )
 73|        self.checks.append(check)
 74|        return check
 75|
 76|    def check_platform_arch(self, supported: List[str] = None) -> Compat
   |    ibilityCheck:
 77|        if supported is None:
 78|            supported = ["x86_64", "AMD64", "arm64", "aarch64"]
 79|        current = platform.machine()
 80|        compatible = current.lower() in [s.lower() for s in supported]
 81|        check = CompatibilityCheck(
 82|            name="硬件架构兼容",
 83|            dimension="arch",
 84|            expected=str(supported),
 85|            actual=current,
 86|            compatible=compatible
 87|        )
 88|        self.checks.append(check)
 89|        return check
 90|
 91|    def check_dependency_version(self, package: str, min_version: str, m
   |    ax_version: str = None) -> CompatibilityCheck:
 92|        try:
 93|            current = version(package)
 94|            compatible = parse_version(current) >= parse_version(min_ver
   |    sion)
 95|            if max_version and compatible:
 96|                compatible = parse_version(current) <= parse_version(max
   |    _version)
 97|        except PackageNotFoundError:
 98|            current = "NOT_INSTALLED"
 99|            compatible = False
100|
101|        check = CompatibilityCheck(
102|            name=f"依赖检查: {package}",
103|            dimension="dependency",
104|            expected=f">={min_version}" + (f", <={max_version}" if max_v
   |    ersion else ""),
105|            actual=current,
106|            compatible=compatible
107|        )
108|        self.checks.append(check)
109|        return check
110|
111|    def check_all_dependencies(self, dep_matrix: Dict[str, List[str]] = 
   |    None) -> List[CompatibilityCheck]:
112|        if dep_matrix is None:
113|            dep_matrix = {
114|                'numpy': ['1.24.0'], 'scipy': ['1.10.0'],
115|                'pandas': ['2.0.0'], 'matplotlib': ['3.7.0'],
116|                'flask': ['2.3.0'], 'pyyaml': ['6.0'],
117|                'requests': ['2.31.0'], 'psutil': ['5.9.0']
118|            }
119|        
120|        results = []
121|        for pkg, versions in dep_matrix.items():
122|            min_ver = versions[0]
123|            check = self.check_dependency_version(pkg, min_ver)
124|            results.append(check)
125|        return results
126|
127|    def check_api_compatibility(self, module_name: str, required_functio
   |    ns: List[str]) -> List[CompatibilityCheck]:
128|        results = []
129|        try:
130|            mod = importlib.import_module(module_name)
131|            for func_name in required_functions:
132|                exists = hasattr(mod, func_name)
133|                results.append(CompatibilityCheck(
134|                    name=f"API检查: {module_name}.{func_name}",
135|                    dimension="api",
136|                    expected="exists",
137|                    actual="exists" if exists else "not_found",
138|                    compatible=exists
139|                ))
140|        except ImportError:
141|            results.append(CompatibilityCheck(
142|                name=f"模块导入: {module_name}",
143|                dimension="api",
144|                expected="importable",
145|                actual="not_found",
146|                compatible=False
147|            ))
148|        self.checks.extend(results)
149|        return results
150|
151|    def check_import_all_modules(self) -> List[CompatibilityCheck]:
152|        modules = [
153|            'src.algorithms.greedy_algorithm',
154|            'src.algorithms.genetic_algorithm',
155|            'src.algorithms.round_robin',
156|            'src.models.server',
157|            'src.models.task',
158|            'src.simulation.datacenter_simulator',
159|            'src.plugin.base',
160|            'src.plugin.manager',
161|            'src.plugin.simulation_plugin',
162|            'src.utils.config',
163|            'src.utils.logger',
164|            'src.sdk.client',
165|        ]
166|        
167|        results = []
168|        for mod_name in modules:
169|            try:
170|                importlib.import_module(mod_name)
171|                results.append(CompatibilityCheck(
172|                    name=f"导入: {mod_name}",
173|                    dimension="module_import",
174|                    expected="importable",
175|                    actual="importable",
176|                    compatible=True
177|                ))
178|            except ImportError as e:
179|                results.append(CompatibilityCheck(
180|                    name=f"导入: {mod_name}",
181|                    dimension="module_import",
182|                    expected="importable",
183|                    actual=f"failed: {str(e)[:80]}",
184|                    compatible=False
185|                ))
186|        self.checks.extend(results)
187|        return results
188|
189|    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
190|        total = len(self.checks)
191|        passed = sum(1 for c in self.checks if c.compatible)
192|        failed = total - passed
193|        
194|        by_dimension = {}
195|        for c in self.checks:
196|            if c.dimension not in by_dimension:
197|                by_dimension[c.dimension] = {'total': 0, 'passed': 0}
198|            by_dimension[c.dimension]['total'] += 1
199|            if c.compatible:
200|                by_dimension[c.dimension]['passed'] += 1
201|
202|        return {
203|            'total_checks': total,
204|            'passed': passed,
205|            'failed': failed,
206|            'compatibility_score': passed / max(total, 1) * 100,
207|            'by_dimension': by_dimension
208|        }

================================================================================
64. src/testing/environment_manager.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|环境重置与恢复模块
  3|
  4|实现测试环境的快速重置与恢复机制:
  5|- 状态快照 (Snapshot)
  6|- 快速回滚 (Rollback)
  7|- 自动清理 (Auto-cleanup)
  8|- 环境隔离 (Isolation)
  9|
 10|参考项目: Docker Snapshot, Vagrant, Terraform
 11|"""
 12|
 13|import os
 14|import json
 15|import time
 16|import shutil
 17|import threading
 18|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
 19|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 20|from datetime import datetime
 21|from enum import Enum
 22|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 23|from src.utils.config import Config
 24|
 25|logger = setup_logger("environment_manager")
 26|config = Config()
 27|
 28|class EnvironmentState(Enum):
 29|    CLEAN = "clean"
 30|    INITIALIZED = "initialized"
 31|    DIRTY = "dirty"
 32|    RESTORING = "restoring"
 33|    SNAPSHOTTING = "snapshotting"
 34|
 35|@dataclass
 36|class Snapshot:
 37|    snapshot_id: str
 38|    timestamp: str
 39|    state: Dict[str, Any]
 40|    description: str
 41|    size_bytes: int = 0
 42|
 43|class EnvironmentManager:
 44|    """
 45|    环境管理器
 46|    
 47|    提供测试环境快照/回滚能力:
 48|    1. 创建快照 - 保存当前完整状态
 49|    2. 恢复快照 - 一键回滚到历史状态
 50|    3. 清理环境 - 重置到初始状态
 51|    4. 状态对比 - 比较环境变更
 52|    """
 53|
 54|    def __init__(self, snapshot_dir: str = "test_snapshots"):
 55|        self.snapshot_dir = snapshot_dir
 56|        self.max_snapshots = 10
 57|        self.current_state = EnvironmentState.CLEAN
 58|        self._snapshots: List[Snapshot] = []
 59|        self._state_data: Dict[str, Any] = {}
 60|        self._lock = threading.Lock()
 61|        
 62|        os.makedirs(snapshot_dir, exist_ok=True)
 63|
 64|    def save_state(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
 65|        with self._lock:
 66|            self._state_data[key] = value
 67|            self.current_state = EnvironmentState.DIRTY
 68|
 69|    def get_state(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
 70|        return self._state_data.get(key, default)
 71|
 72|    def create_snapshot(self, description: str = "") -> Snapshot:
 73|        timestamp = datetime.now()
 74|        snapshot_id = f"snapshot_{timestamp.strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f')
   |    }"
 75|        
 76|        self.current_state = EnvironmentState.SNAPSHOTTING
 77|        
 78|        snapshot_data = {
 79|            'state': dict(self._state_data),
 80|            'meta': {
 81|                'description': description,
 82|                'created_at': timestamp.isoformat(),
 83|                'snapshot_id': snapshot_id
 84|            }
 85|        }
 86|
 87|        filepath = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{snapshot_id}.json"
   |    )
 88|        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 89|            json.dump(snapshot_data, f, indent=2, default=str, ensure_as
   |    cii=False)
 90|
 91|        size = os.path.getsize(filepath)
 92|        snapshot = Snapshot(
 93|            snapshot_id=snapshot_id,
 94|            timestamp=timestamp.isoformat(),
 95|            state=dict(self._state_data),
 96|            description=description,
 97|            size_bytes=size
 98|        )
 99|
100|        with self._lock:
101|            self._snapshots.append(snapshot)
102|            if len(self._snapshots) > self.max_snapshots:
103|                oldest = self._snapshots.pop(0)
104|                old_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{oldest.sna
   |    pshot_id}.json")
105|                if os.path.exists(old_path):
106|                    os.remove(old_path)
107|
108|        self.current_state = EnvironmentState.INITIALIZED
109|        logger.info(f"Snapshot created: {snapshot_id}")
110|        return snapshot
111|
112|    def restore_snapshot(self, snapshot_id: str = None) -> bool:
113|        self.current_state = EnvironmentState.RESTORING
114|
115|        if snapshot_id is None and self._snapshots:
116|            snapshot_id = self._snapshots[-1].snapshot_id
117|
118|        filepath = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{snapshot_id}.json"
   |    )
119|        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
120|            logger.error(f"Snapshot not found: {snapshot_id}")
121|            return False
122|
123|        try:
124|            with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
125|                snapshot_data = json.load(f)
126|
127|            with self._lock:
128|                self._state_data = dict(snapshot_data['state'])
129|            
130|            logger.info(f"Restored snapshot: {snapshot_id}")
131|            self.current_state = EnvironmentState.INITIALIZED
132|            return True
133|        except Exception as e:
134|            logger.error(f"Restore failed: {e}")
135|            self.current_state = EnvironmentState.DIRTY
136|            return False
137|
138|    def reset_to_clean(self) -> None:
139|        with self._lock:
140|            self._state_data.clear()
141|        
142|        self.current_state = EnvironmentState.CLEAN
143|        logger.info("Environment reset to clean state")
144|
145|    def cleanup_snapshots(self, keep_last: int = 5) -> int:
146|        removed = 0
147|        with self._lock:
148|            while len(self._snapshots) > keep_last:
149|                oldest = self._snapshots.pop(0)
150|                path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{oldest.snapsho
   |    t_id}.json")
151|                if os.path.exists(path):
152|                    os.remove(path)
153|                    removed += 1
154|        
155|        logger.info(f"Cleaned up {removed} old snapshots")
156|        return removed
157|
158|    def list_snapshots(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
159|        return [{
160|            'id': s.snapshot_id,
161|            'timestamp': s.timestamp,
162|            'description': s.description,
163|            'size_kb': s.size_bytes / 1024
164|        } for s in self._snapshots]
165|
166|    def compare_snapshots(self, id1: str, id2: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
167|        file1 = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{id1}.json")
168|        file2 = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, f"{id2}.json")
169|
170|        if not os.path.exists(file1) or not os.path.exists(file2):
171|            return {'error': 'One or both snapshots not found'}
172|
173|        with open(file1, 'r') as f:
174|            data1 = json.load(f)['state']
175|        with open(file2, 'r') as f:
176|            data2 = json.load(f)['state']
177|
178|        keys1, keys2 = set(data1.keys()), set(data2.keys())
179|        added = keys2 - keys1
180|        removed = keys1 - keys2
181|        common = keys1 & keys2
182|        
183|        changed = [k for k in common if data1[k] != data2[k]]
184|
185|        return {
186|            'added_keys': list(added),
187|            'removed_keys': list(removed),
188|            'changed_keys': changed,
189|            'unchanged_keys': list(common - set(changed))
190|        }

65. src/testing/mock_services.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|仿真模拟测试环境 - Mock服务组件
  3|
  4|提供可配置的模拟服务组件，实现对各类接口和协议的模拟支持:
  5|- 网络拓扑模拟（延迟/带宽/丢包/抖动）
  6|- 硬件配置模拟（CPU/RAM/Disk/NIC）
  7|- 协议Mock（HTTP/gRPC/WebSocket）
  8|- 数据流量模拟（正常/峰值/突发/DDoS）
  9|"""
 10|
 11|import random
 12|import time
 13|import json
 14|import threading
 15|import queue
 16|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Callable, Tuple
 17|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 18|from enum import Enum
 19|from datetime import datetime
 20|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 21|from src.utils.config import Config
 22|
 23|logger = setup_logger("mock_services")
 24|config = Config()
 25|
 26|class NetworkCondition(Enum):
 27|    PERFECT = "perfect"
 28|    NORMAL = "normal"
 29|    DEGRADED = "degraded"
 30|    CONGESTED = "congested"
 31|    DISCONNECTED = "disconnected"
 32|
 33|@dataclass
 34|class NetworkRegion:
 35|    name: str
 36|    latency_ms: float
 37|    bandwidth_mbps: float
 38|    packet_loss: float
 39|    jitter_ms: float
 40|
 41|@dataclass
 42|class HardwareProfile:
 43|    name: str
 44|    cpu_cores: int
 45|    memory_gb: int
 46|    disk_gb: int
 47|    nic_speed_mbps: int
 48|    gpu: Optional[str]
 49|    npu: Optional[str]
 50|    os: str
 51|    cpu_usage: float = 0.0
 52|    memory_usage: float = 0.0
 53|    disk_usage: float = 0.0
 54|
 55|@dataclass
 56|class TrafficStats:
 57|    requests_total: int = 0
 58|    requests_success: int = 0
 59|    requests_failed: int = 0
 60|    bytes_sent: int = 0
 61|    bytes_received: int = 0
 62|    avg_response_ms: float = 0.0
 63|    max_response_ms: float = 0.0
 64|    min_response_ms: float = float('inf')
 65|    error_rate: float = 0.0
 66|
 67|class NetworkSimulator:
 68|    """
 69|    网络拓扑模拟器
 70|    
 71|    模拟Spine-Leaf数据中心网络拓扑:
 72|           [Spine1]       [Spine2]
 73|           /     \       /     \
 74|      [Leaf1]  [Leaf2] [Leaf3] [Leaf4]
 75|         |        |       |       |
 76|      [Server] [Server] [Server] [Server]
 77|    
 78|    参考项目: Mininet, NS-3
 79|    """
 80|
 81|    def __init__(self, regions: List[Dict] = None):
 82|        self.regions: List[NetworkRegion] = []
 83|        self.condition = NetworkCondition.NORMAL
 84|        self._latency_multiplier = 1.0
 85|        self._packet_loss_multiplier = 1.0
 86|        self._blocked_regions: set = set()
 87|        
 88|        if regions:
 89|            for r in regions:
 90|                self.regions.append(NetworkRegion(**r))
 91|        else:
 92|            cfg_regions = config.network.get('regions', []) if hasattr(c
   |    onfig, 'network') else []
 93|            for r in cfg_regions:
 94|                self.regions.append(NetworkRegion(**r))
 95|
 96|    def set_condition(self, condition: NetworkCondition) -> None:
 97|        self.condition = condition
 98|        multipliers = {
 99|            NetworkCondition.PERFECT: (0.5, 0.0),
100|            NetworkCondition.NORMAL: (1.0, 1.0),
101|            NetworkCondition.DEGRADED: (2.0, 5.0),
102|            NetworkCondition.CONGESTED: (5.0, 10.0),
103|            NetworkCondition.DISCONNECTED: (1000.0, 1.0),
104|        }
105|        self._latency_multiplier, self._packet_loss_multiplier = multipl
   |    iers[condition]
106|        logger.info(f"Network condition set to {condition.value}")
107|
108|    def get_latency(self, region_name: str) -> float:
109|        if region_name in self._blocked_regions:
110|            return 9999.0
111|        for r in self.regions:
112|            if r.name == region_name:
113|                base = r.latency_ms + random.gauss(0, r.jitter_ms)
114|                return max(0, base * self._latency_multiplier)
115|        return 1.0 * self._latency_multiplier
116|
117|    def simulates_packet_loss(self, region_name: str) -> bool:
118|        if region_name in self._blocked_regions:
119|            return True
120|        for r in self.regions:
121|            if r.name == region_name:
122|                return random.random() < r.packet_loss * self._packet_lo
   |    ss_multiplier
123|        return False
124|
125|    def get_bandwidth(self, region_name: str) -> float:
126|        for r in self.regions:
127|            if r.name == region_name:
128|                if self.condition == NetworkCondition.CONGESTED:
129|                    return r.bandwidth_mbps * 0.1
130|                return r.bandwidth_mbps
131|        return 1000.0
132|
133|    def block_region(self, region_name: str) -> None:
134|        self._blocked_regions.add(region_name)
135|        logger.warning(f"Region blocked: {region_name}")
136|
137|    def unblock_region(self, region_name: str) -> None:
138|        self._blocked_regions.discard(region_name)
139|
140|    def simulate_transfer(self, data_size_bytes: int, region_name: str) 
   |    -> Tuple[float, bool]:
141|        bw = self.get_bandwidth(region_name) * 1e6 / 8
142|        transfer_time = data_size_bytes / max(bw, 1)
143|        
144|        latency = self.get_latency(region_name) / 1000
145|        total_time = transfer_time + latency * 2
146|        
147|        lost = self.simulates_packet_loss(region_name)
148|        
149|        return total_time, not lost
150|
151|
152|class HardwareProfileSimulator:
153|    """
154|    硬件配置模拟器
155|    
156|    模拟不同硬件平台的计算能力:
157|    - 鲲鹏920 + 昇腾310 (国产化)
158|    - 标准x86 + NVIDIA A100
159|    - 高性能GPU节点 (H100)
160|    - 边缘计算节点
161|    
162|    参考项目: NVIDIA DCGM, Prometheus Node Exporter
163|    """
164|
165|    def __init__(self, profiles: List[Dict] = None):
166|        self.profiles: List[HardwareProfile] = []
167|        if profiles:
168|            for p in profiles:
169|                self.profiles.append(HardwareProfile(**p))
170|        else:
171|            cfg_profiles = config.hardware_profiles if hasattr(config, '
   |    hardware_profiles') else []
172|            for p in cfg_profiles:
173|                self.profiles.append(HardwareProfile(**p))
174|
175|    def get_profile(self, name: str) -> Optional[HardwareProfile]:
176|        for p in self.profiles:
177|            if p.name == name:
178|                return p
179|        return None
180|
181|    def simulate_cpu_load(self, profile: HardwareProfile, load_pct: floa
   |    t) -> float:
182|        profile.cpu_usage = min(100.0, max(0.0, load_pct))
183|        return profile.cpu_cores * (1.0 - profile.cpu_usage / 100.0)
184|
185|    def simulate_memory_usage(self, profile: HardwareProfile, usage_gb: 
   |    float) -> Tuple[float, bool]:
186|        profile.memory_usage = usage_gb
187|        pct = usage_gb / profile.memory_gb * 100.0
188|        return pct, pct < 90.0
189|
190|    def simulate_disk_io(self, profile: HardwareProfile, read_mbps: floa
   |    t, write_mbps: float) -> float:
191|        total = read_mbps + write_mbps
192|        return min(100.0, total / 1000.0 * 100.0)
193|
194|    def estimate_task_capacity(self, profile: HardwareProfile) -> int:
195|        base = profile.cpu_cores * 2
196|        if profile.gpu:
197|            base *= 3
198|        if profile.npu:
199|            base *= 2
200|        return int(base)
201|
202|    def get_all_status(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
203|        return [
204|            {
205|                'name': p.name, 'cpu_usage': p.cpu_usage,
206|                'memory_usage': p.memory_usage, 'disk_usage': p.disk_usa
   |    ge,
207|                'available_cores': p.cpu_cores * (1 - p.cpu_usage / 100)
   |    ,
208|                'available_memory': p.memory_gb * (1 - p.memory_usage / 
   |    100)
209|            } for p in self.profiles
210|        ]

212|
213|class ProtocolMockServer:
214|    """
215|    协议模拟服务器
216|    
217|    模拟各类API接口行为:
218|    - HTTP REST API (正常/延迟/错误响应)
219|    - WebSocket (实时推送)
220|    - 自定义协议
221|    
222|    参考项目: WireMock, Mockoon, Postman Mock Server
223|    """
224|
225|    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "http://localhost:5000"):
226|        self.base_url = base_url
227|        self._mocks: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
228|        self._response_delay = 0.0
229|        self._error_rate = 0.0
230|        self._request_log: List[Dict] = []
231|        self._callbacks: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
232|
233|    def register_mock(self, path: str, method: str = "GET",
234|                      response: Any = None, status_code: int = 200,
235|                      headers: Dict = None, delay_sec: float = 0.0) -> N
   |    one:
236|        key = f"{method}:{path}"
237|        self._mocks[key] = {
238|            'response': response or {},
239|            'status_code': status_code,
240|            'headers': headers or {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
241|            'delay': delay_sec
242|        }
243|
244|    def set_error_injection(self, rate: float) -> None:
245|        self._error_rate = max(0.0, min(1.0, rate))
246|
247|    def set_response_delay(self, delay_sec: float) -> None:
248|        self._response_delay = delay_sec
249|
250|    def mock_request(self, method: str, path: str, data: Any = None) -> 
   |    Dict[str, Any]:
251|        key = f"{method}:{path}"
252|        self._request_log.append({
253|            'method': method, 'path': path, 'data': data,
254|            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
255|        })
256|
257|        if random.random() < self._error_rate:
258|            return {'status_code': 500, 'body': {'error': 'Injected serv
   |    er error'}}
259|
260|        mock = self._mocks.get(key)
261|        if not mock:
262|            return {'status_code': 404, 'body': {'error': f'No mock for 
   |    {key}'}}
263|
264|        if mock['delay'] > 0 or self._response_delay > 0:
265|            time.sleep(max(mock['delay'], self._response_delay))
266|
267|        return {
268|            'status_code': mock['status_code'],
269|            'body': mock['response']() if callable(mock['response']) els
   |    e mock['response'],
270|            'headers': mock['headers']
271|        }
272|
273|    def register_callback(self, path: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
274|        self._callbacks[path] = callback
275|
276|    def get_request_log(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[Dict]:
277|        return self._request_log[-limit:]
278|
279|    def clear_request_log(self) -> None:
280|        self._request_log.clear()
281|
282|
283|class TrafficGenerator:
284|    """
285|    数据流量生成器
286|    
287|    模拟不同负载场景:
288|    - 正常负载（日常运行）
289|    - 潮汐峰值（工作时间高峰）
290|    - 突发流量（瞬时高并发）
291|    - 持续高压（长时间高负载）
292|    - DDoS模拟（攻击流量）
293|    
294|    参考项目: Apache JMeter, Locust, wrk2
295|    """
296|
297|    def __init__(self, patterns: List[Dict] = None):
298|        self.patterns: List[Dict] = patterns or []
299|        self._active = False
300|        self._threads: List[threading.Thread] = []
301|        self.stats = TrafficStats()
302|
303|    def load_config_patterns(self) -> None:
304|        cfg_patterns = config.traffic_patterns if hasattr(config, 'traff
   |    ic_patterns') else []
305|        for p in cfg_patterns:
306|            self.patterns.append(p)
307|
308|    def generate_traffic(self, pattern_name: str, target_api: str,
309|                         request_func: Callable) -> TrafficStats:
310|        pattern = None
311|        for p in self.patterns:
312|            if p['name'] == pattern_name:
313|                pattern = p
314|                break
315|        
316|        if not pattern:
317|            raise ValueError(f"Unknown pattern: {pattern_name}")
318|
319|        rps = pattern['requests_per_sec']
320|        duration = pattern['duration_minutes'] * 60
321|        stats = TrafficStats()
322|        
323|        start_time = time.time()
324|        request_count = 0
325|
326|        while time.time() - start_time < duration:
327|            batch = min(rps, 100)
328|            for _ in range(batch):
329|                req_start = time.time()
330|                try:
331|                    result = request_func()
332|                    stats.requests_total += 1
333|                    if result:
334|                        stats.requests_success += 1
335|                    else:
336|                        stats.requests_failed += 1
337|                except Exception:
338|                    stats.requests_failed += 1
339|                
340|                elapsed = (time.time() - req_start) * 1000
341|                stats.avg_response_ms = (stats.avg_response_ms * request
   |    _count + elapsed) / (request_count + 1)
342|                stats.max_response_ms = max(stats.max_response_ms, elaps
   |    ed)
343|                stats.min_response_ms = min(stats.min_response_ms, elaps
   |    ed)
344|                request_count += 1
345|
346|            time.sleep(1.0 / max(rps / batch, 1))
347|
348|        stats.error_rate = stats.requests_failed / max(stats.requests_to
   |    tal, 1)
349|        self.stats = stats
350|        return stats
351|
352|    def generate_burst(self, num_requests: int, target_api: str,
353|                       request_func: Callable) -> TrafficStats:
354|        stats = TrafficStats()
355|        start = time.time()
356|        
357|        for i in range(num_requests):
358|            req_start = time.time()
359|            try:
360|                request_func()
361|                stats.requests_success += 1
362|            except Exception:
363|                stats.requests_failed += 1
364|            stats.requests_total += 1
365|            
366|            elapsed = (time.time() - req_start) * 1000
367|            stats.max_response_ms = max(stats.max_response_ms, elapsed)
368|            stats.min_response_ms = min(stats.min_response_ms, elapsed)
369|
370|        elapsed_total = (time.time() - start)
371|        if elapsed_total > 0:
372|            stats.avg_response_ms = (elapsed_total / num_requests) * 100
   |    0
373|        stats.error_rate = stats.requests_failed / max(stats.requests_to
   |    tal, 1)
374|        self.stats = stats
375|        return stats

66. src/testing/risk_assessor.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|风险评估模块
  3|
  4|对测试结果进行综合风险评估:
  5|- 漏洞严重性评估 (CVSS)
  6|- 风险矩阵生成
  7|- 影响范围分析
  8|- 修复优先级排序
  9|
 10|参考项目: NIST CVSS Calculator, OWASP Risk Rating
 11|"""
 12|
 13|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
 14|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 15|from enum import Enum
 16|from datetime import datetime
 17|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 18|from src.utils.config import Config
 19|
 20|logger = setup_logger("risk_assessor")
 21|config = Config()
 22|
 23|class RiskLevel(Enum):
 24|    CRITICAL = "critical"
 25|    HIGH = "high"
 26|    MEDIUM = "medium"
 27|    LOW = "low"
 28|    ACCEPTABLE = "acceptable"
 29|
 30|@dataclass
 31|class RiskItem:
 32|    category: str
 33|    description: str
 34|    likelihood: float  # 0.0-1.0
 35|    impact: float      # 0.0-1.0
 36|    risk_score: float   # likelihood * impact
 37|    risk_level: RiskLevel
 38|    affected_components: List[str]
 39|    mitigation: str
 40|
 41|@dataclass
 42|class RiskReport:
 43|    report_id: str
 44|    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isofor
   |    mat())
 45|    overall_risk: RiskLevel = RiskLevel.LOW
 46|    risk_items: List[RiskItem] = field(default_factory=list)
 47|    safety_score: float = 100.0
 48|    recommendations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
 49|
 50|class RiskAssessor:
 51|    """
 52|    风险评估器
 53|    
 54|    综合评估系统安全风险:
 55|    1. 漏洞风险等级评定
 56|    2. 影响范围分析
 57|    3. 风险矩阵可视化
 58|    4. 修复建议排序
 59|    """
 60|
 61|    def __init__(self):
 62|        self.risks: List[RiskItem] = []
 63|        self._cvss_threshold = 7.0
 64|
 65|    def assess_from_security_scan(self, vulnerabilities: List[Any]) -> L
   |    ist[RiskItem]:
 66|        severity_to_likelihood = {
 67|            'critical': 0.9,
 68|            'high': 0.7,
 69|            'medium': 0.4,
 70|            'low': 0.2,
 71|            'info': 0.1
 72|        }
 73|        severity_to_impact = {
 74|            'critical': 0.95,
 75|            'high': 0.8,
 76|            'medium': 0.5,
 77|            'low': 0.3,
 78|            'info': 0.1
 79|        }
 80|
 81|        for vuln in vulnerabilities:
 82|            severity = getattr(vuln, 'severity', None)
 83|            if severity:
 84|                sev_str = severity.value if hasattr(severity, 'value') e
   |    lse str(severity)
 85|                likelihood = severity_to_likelihood.get(sev_str, 0.5)
 86|                impact = severity_to_impact.get(sev_str, 0.5)
 87|            else:
 88|                likelihood = 0.5
 89|                impact = 0.5
 90|
 91|            score = likelihood * impact
 92|            level = self._score_to_level(score)
 93|
 94|            risk = RiskItem(
 95|                category=getattr(vuln, 'scan_type', 'unknown').value if 
   |    hasattr(vuln.scan_type, 'value') else str(getattr(vuln, 'scan_type',
   |     'unknown')),
 96|                description=getattr(vuln, 'title', 'Unknown'),
 97|                likelihood=likelihood,
 98|                impact=impact,
 99|                risk_score=score,
100|                risk_level=level,
101|                affected_components=[getattr(vuln, 'affected_component',
   |     'unknown')],
102|                mitigation=getattr(vuln, 'remediation', 'No mitigation a
   |    vailable')
103|            )
104|            self.risks.append(risk)
105|
106|        return self.risks
107|
108|    def assess_from_stress_test(self, metrics) -> RiskItem:
109|        violations = getattr(metrics, 'threshold_violations', [])
110|        error_rate = 1.0 - getattr(metrics, 'success_rate', 1.0)
111|        peak_cpu = getattr(metrics, 'peak_cpu_pct', 0)
112|
113|        if violations:
114|            likelihood = min(1.0, error_rate * 10 + peak_cpu / 200)
115|            impact = 0.7
116|            score = likelihood * impact
117|            level = self._score_to_level(score)
118|        else:
119|            return RiskItem(
120|                category='performance',
121|                description='系统性能在可接受范围内',
122|                likelihood=0.1, impact=0.2, risk_score=0.02,
123|                risk_level=RiskLevel.ACCEPTABLE,
124|                affected_components=['simulation_engine'],
125|                mitigation='无需处理'
126|            )
127|
128|        risk = RiskItem(
129|            category='performance',
130|            description=f"系统超阈值: {len(violations)}项",
131|            likelihood=likelihood,
132|            impact=impact,
133|            risk_score=score,
134|            risk_level=level,
135|            affected_components=['simulation_engine', 'api_server'],
136|            mitigation='优化算法性能，增加资源分配'
137|        )
138|        self.risks.append(risk)
139|        return risk
140|
141|    def assess_from_compatibility(self, compat_summary: Dict[str, Any]) 
   |    -> RiskItem:
142|        score_pct = compat_summary.get('compatibility_score', 100)
143|
144|        if score_pct >= 95:
145|            level = RiskLevel.ACCEPTABLE
146|            likelihood, impact = 0.05, 0.1
147|        elif score_pct >= 80:
148|            level = RiskLevel.LOW
149|            likelihood, impact = 0.2, 0.3
150|        elif score_pct >= 60:
151|            level = RiskLevel.MEDIUM
152|            likelihood, impact = 0.4, 0.5
153|        else:
154|            level = RiskLevel.HIGH
155|            likelihood, impact = 0.7, 0.8
156|
157|        score = likelihood * impact
158|        risk = RiskItem(
159|            category='compatibility',
160|            description=f"兼容性评分: {score_pct:.1f}%",
161|            likelihood=likelihood,
162|            impact=impact,
163|            risk_score=score,
164|            risk_level=level,
165|            affected_components=['all_modules'],
166|            mitigation='修复兼容性问题，更新依赖版本'
167|        )
168|        self.risks.append(risk)
169|        return risk
170|
171|    def _score_to_level(self, score: float) -> RiskLevel:
172|        if score >= 0.8:
173|            return RiskLevel.CRITICAL
174|        elif score >= 0.6:
175|            return RiskLevel.HIGH
176|        elif score >= 0.3:
177|            return RiskLevel.MEDIUM
178|        elif score >= 0.1:
179|            return RiskLevel.LOW
180|        return RiskLevel.ACCEPTABLE
181|
182|    def generate_report(self) -> RiskReport:
183|        if not self.risks:
184|            return RiskReport(
185|                report_id=f"RISK-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M')}
   |    ",
186|                overall_risk=RiskLevel.LOW,
187|                safety_score=100.0
188|            )
189|
190|        by_level = {level: 0 for level in RiskLevel}
191|        total_score = 0.0
192|
193|        for r in self.risks:
194|            by_level[r.risk_level] += 1
195|            total_score += r.risk_score
196|
197|        if by_level[RiskLevel.CRITICAL] > 0:
198|            overall = RiskLevel.CRITICAL
199|        elif by_level[RiskLevel.HIGH] > 0:
200|            overall = RiskLevel.HIGH
201|        elif by_level[RiskLevel.MEDIUM] > 2:
202|            overall = RiskLevel.MEDIUM
203|        elif by_level[RiskLevel.MEDIUM] > 0:
204|            overall = RiskLevel.LOW
205|        else:
206|            overall = RiskLevel.ACCEPTABLE
207|
208|        safety_score = max(0.0, 100.0 - total_score * 100 / max(len(self
   |    .risks), 1))
209|
210|        recommendations = []
211|        for r in self.risks:
212|            if r.risk_level in (RiskLevel.CRITICAL, RiskLevel.HIGH):
213|                recommendations.insert(0, f"[紧急] {r.description}: {r.m
   |    itigation}")
214|            elif r.risk_level == RiskLevel.MEDIUM:
215|                recommendations.append(f"[建议] {r.description}: {r.miti
   |    gation}")
216|
217|        report = RiskReport(
218|            report_id=f"RISK-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M')}",
219|            overall_risk=overall,
220|            risk_items=self.risks,
221|            safety_score=safety_score,
222|            recommendations=recommendations
223|        )
224|
225|        logger.info(f"Risk report generated: {overall.value}, safety_sco
   |    re={safety_score:.1f}")
226|        return report

68. src/testing/security_scanner.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|安全漏洞扫描与渗透测试模块
  3|
  4|集成安全漏洞扫描和风险评估能力:
  5|- 依赖漏洞检测 (CVE/NVD)
  6|- 端口扫描 (开放端口检测)
  7|- 代码静态分析 (SAST)
  8|- API模糊测试 (Fuzzing)
  9|- 注入攻击测试 (SQL/XSS/Command)
 10|- 认证绕过测试
 11|- OWASP Top 10 检测
 12|
 13|参考项目: Bandit, OWASP ZAP, SQLMap, Snyk
 14|"""
 15|
 16|import os
 17|import re
 18|import json
 19|import hashlib
 20|import socket
 21|import ssl
 22|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
 23|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 24|from enum import Enum
 25|from datetime import datetime
 26|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 27|from src.utils.config import Config
 28|
 29|logger = setup_logger("security_scanner")
 30|config = Config()
 31|
 32|class SeverityLevel(Enum):
 33|    CRITICAL = "critical"
 34|    HIGH = "high"
 35|    MEDIUM = "medium"
 36|    LOW = "low"
 37|    INFO = "info"
 38|
 39|class ScanType(Enum):
 40|    DEPENDENCY = "dependency_check"
 41|    PORT_SCAN = "port_scan"
 42|    SAST = "code_sast"
 43|    FUZZING = "api_fuzzing"
 44|    INJECTION = "injection_test"
 45|    AUTH = "auth_bypass"
 46|
 47|@dataclass
 48|class Vulnerability:
 49|    vuln_id: str
 50|    title: str
 51|    description: str
 52|    severity: SeverityLevel
 53|    cve_id: Optional[str]
 54|    cvss_score: Optional[float]
 55|    affected_component: str
 56|    remediation: str
 57|    scan_type: ScanType
 58|    found_at: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoform
   |    at())
 59|
 60|class SecurityScanner:
 61|    """
 62|    安全扫描器
 63|    
 64|    多维度安全检测:
 65|    1. 依赖检查 - CVE漏洞库匹配
 66|    2. 端口扫描 - 检测未授权开放端口
 67|    3. 代码审计 - 硬编码密钥/不安全函数
 68|    4. API模糊测试 - 异常输入处理
 69|    5. 注入测试 - SQL/XSS/命令注入
 70|    6. 认证测试 - 绕过/弱口令
 71|    """
 72|
 73|    def __init__(self):
 74|        self.vulnerabilities: List[Vulnerability] = []
 75|        self._scanned_files: Set[str] = set()
 76|        self._allowed_ports = set()
 77|        self._blocked_ports = set()
 78|        self._load_port_config()
 79|
 80|    def _load_port_config(self):
 81|        try:
 82|            self._allowed_ports = set(config.security.get('allowed_ports
   |    ', [5000, 8000]))
 83|            self._blocked_ports = set(config.security.get('blocked_ports
   |    ', [22, 3306]))
 84|        except Exception:
 85|            self._allowed_ports = {5000, 8000, 8080}
 86|            self._blocked_ports = {22, 3306, 5432, 6379}
 87|
 88|    def scan_dependencies(self) -> List[Vulnerability]:
 89|        import pkg_resources
 90|        vulns = []
 91|        
 92|        known_vulnerable = {
 93|            'pyyaml': {
 94|                'version': '<6.0.1',
 95|                'cve': 'CVE-2020-14343',
 96|                'cvss': 7.5,
 97|                'desc': 'YAML解析器存在不安全反序列化漏洞'
 98|            },
 99|            'requests': {
100|                'version': '<2.31.0',
101|                'cve': 'CVE-2023-32681',
102|                'cvss': 6.1,
103|                'desc': '代理授权头信息泄漏'
104|            },
105|            'urllib3': {
106|                'version': '<1.26.18',
107|                'cve': 'CVE-2023-45803',
108|                'cvss': 4.2,
109|                'desc': '重定向后授权信息泄漏'
110|            },
111|            'werkzeug': {
112|                'version': '<2.3.8',
113|                'cve': 'CVE-2023-46136',
114|                'cvss': 7.5,
115|                'desc': '多部分表单数据DoS漏洞'
116|            }
117|        }
118|
119|        for dist in pkg_resources.working_set:
120|            pkg_name = dist.project_name.lower()
121|            if pkg_name in known_vulnerable:
122|                info = known_vulnerable[pkg_name]
123|                if pkg_resources.parse_version(dist.version) < pkg_resou
   |    rces.parse_version(info['version'].lstrip('<=').lstrip('<')):
124|                    v = Vulnerability(
125|                        vuln_id=f"DEP-{pkg_name.upper()}",
126|                        title=f"依赖漏洞: {pkg_name}",
127|                        description=info['desc'],
128|                        severity=SeverityLevel.HIGH if info['cvss'] >= 7
   |     else SeverityLevel.MEDIUM,
129|                        cve_id=info['cve'],
130|                        cvss_score=info['cvss'],
131|                        affected_component=f"{pkg_name}=={dist.version}"
   |    ,
132|                        remediation=f"升级至 {pkg_name}>={info['version'
   |    ]}",
133|                        scan_type=ScanType.DEPENDENCY
134|                    )
135|                    vulns.append(v)
136|        
137|        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
138|        logger.info(f"Dependency scan found {len(vulns)} vulnerabilities
   |    ")
139|        return vulns
140|
141|    def scan_ports(self, host: str = 'localhost', ports: List[int] = Non
   |    e) -> List[Vulnerability]:
142|        if ports is None:
143|            ports = list(range(1, 1025))
144|        
145|        vulns = []
146|        open_ports = []
147|
148|        for port in ports:
149|            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
150|            sock.settimeout(0.1)
151|            result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
152|            if result == 0:
153|                open_ports.append(port)
154|            sock.close()
155|
156|        for port in open_ports:
157|            if port in self._blocked_ports:
158|                vulns.append(Vulnerability(
159|                    vuln_id=f"PORT-{port}",
160|                    title=f"敏感端口开放: {port}",
161|                    description=f"端口 {port} 应被防火墙阻止但当前开放",
162|                    severity=SeverityLevel.HIGH,
163|                    cve_id=None, cvss_score=None,
164|                    affected_component=f"{host}:{port}",
165|                    remediation=f"在防火墙中阻止端口 {port}",
166|                    scan_type=ScanType.PORT_SCAN
167|                ))
168|
169|        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
170|        logger.info(f"Port scan: {len(open_ports)} open, {len(vulns)} vu
   |    lnerabilities")
171|        return vulns
172|
173|    def scan_code_sast(self, src_dir: str = 'src') -> List[Vulnerability
   |    ]:
174|        vulns = []
175|        dangerous_patterns = {
176|            'hardcoded_secret': (
177|                r'(?:password|secret|api_key|token|auth)\s*=\s*["\'][^"\
   |    '\\n]{8,}["\']',
178|                SeverityLevel.CRITICAL,
179|                '硬编码密钥检测'
180|            ),
181|            'eval_usage': (
182|                r'\beval\s*\(',
183|                SeverityLevel.HIGH,
184|                '危险的eval()调用'
185|            ),
186|            'exec_usage': (
187|                r'\bexec\s*\(',
188|                SeverityLevel.HIGH,
189|                '危险的exec()调用'
190|            ),
191|            'sql_injection_risk': (
192|                r'(?:execute|cursor\.execute)\s*\(\s*["\'].*%(?:s|d)',
193|                SeverityLevel.HIGH,
194|                '潜在SQL注入风险'
195|            ),
196|            'debug_mode': (
197|                r'debug\s*=\s*True',
198|                SeverityLevel.MEDIUM,
199|                '调试模式开启'
200|            ),
201|            'insecure_deserialization': (
202|                r'\bpickle\.(?:loads|load)\(',
203|                SeverityLevel.HIGH,
204|                '不安全的反序列化'
205|            ),
206|            'ssl_verify_disabled': (
207|                r'verify\s*=\s*False',
208|                SeverityLevel.MEDIUM,
209|                'SSL证书验证禁用'
210|            )
211|        }
212|
213|        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(src_dir):
214|            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ('__pycache__', '.git
   |    ', 'venv', 'node_modules')]
215|            for file in files:
216|                if not file.endswith('.py'):
217|                    continue
218|                filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
219|                if filepath in self._scanned_files:
220|                    continue
221|                self._scanned_files.add(filepath)
222|
223|                try:
224|                    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
225|                        content = f.read()
226|                        for pattern_name, (pattern, severity, title) in 
   |    dangerous_patterns.items():
227|                            matches = re.finditer(pattern, content, re.I
   |    GNORECASE)
228|                            for match in matches:
229|                                line_no = content[:match.start()].count(
   |    '\n') + 1
230|                                vulns.append(Vulnerability(
231|                                    vuln_id=f"SAST-{hashlib.md5(f'{filep
   |    ath}:{line_no}'.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}",
232|                                    title=title,
233|                                    description=f"在 {filepath}:{line_no
   |    } 发现 {pattern_name}",
234|                                    severity=severity,
235|                                    cve_id=None, cvss_score=None,
236|                                    affected_component=f"{filepath}:{lin
   |    e_no}",
237|                                    remediation=f"检查并修复 {filepath} 
   |    第{line_no}行",
238|                                    scan_type=ScanType.SAST
239|                                ))
240|                except Exception as e:
241|                    logger.warning(f"SAST scan failed for {filepath}: {e
   |    }")
242|
243|        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
244|        logger.info(f"SAST scan found {len(vulns)} issues")
245|        return vulns
246|
247|    def test_injection(self, api_endpoint: str, method: str = 'POST') ->
   |     List[Vulnerability]:
248|        import requests
249|        import urllib.parse
250|
251|        vulns = []
252|        payloads = {
253|            'SQL注入': [
254|                "' OR '1'='1",
255|                "1; DROP TABLE simulations",
256|                "' UNION SELECT * FROM users --",
257|                "1' AND '1'='1"
258|            ],
259|            'XSS注入': [
260|                "<script>alert(1)</script>",
261|                "javascript:alert(document.cookie)",
262|                "<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>",
263|                "<body onload=alert(1)>"
264|            ],
265|            '命令注入': [
266|                "; ls -la",
267|                "| cat /etc/passwd",
268|                "$(whoami)",
269|                "`id`"
270|            ],
271|            '路径穿越': [
272|                "../../../etc/passwd",
273|                "..\\..\\..\\windows\\system32",
274|                "/etc/passwd%00.html"
275|            ]
276|        }
277|
278|        for category, payload_list in payloads.items():
279|            for payload in payload_list[:2]:
280|                try:
281|                    if method.upper() == 'GET':
282|                        resp = requests.get(f"{api_endpoint}?input={urll
   |    ib.parse.quote(payload)}", timeout=10)
283|                    else:
284|                        resp = requests.post(api_endpoint,
285|                            json={'data': payload},
286|                            headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'}
   |    ,
287|                            timeout=10
288|                        )
289|
290|                    if resp.status_code >= 500:
291|                        vulns.append(Vulnerability(
292|                            vuln_id=f"INJ-{hashlib.md5(f'{category}:{pay
   |    load}'.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}",
293|                            title=f"{category}漏洞",
294|                            description=f"服务端返回 {resp.status_code}，
   |    替代预期错误",
295|                            severity=SeverityLevel.MEDIUM if 'XSS' in ca
   |    tegory else SeverityLevel.HIGH,
296|                            cve_id=None, cvss_score=None,
297|                            affected_component=api_endpoint,
298|                            remediation=f"对 {category} 类输入实施严格验
   |    证和转义",
299|                            scan_type=ScanType.INJECTION
300|                        ))
301|                except requests.exceptions.ConnectionError:
302|                    pass
303|                except Exception as e:
304|                    logger.warning(f"Injection test failed: {e}")
305|
306|        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
307|        logger.info(f"Injection test found {len(vulns)} vulnerabilities"
   |    )
308|        return vulns
309|
310|    def test_auth_bypass(self, api_endpoint: str) -> List[Vulnerability]
   |    :
311|        import requests
312|        vulns = []
313|
314|        tests = [
315|            ('无认证头', {}),
316|            ('空Authorization', {'Authorization': ''}),
317|            ('无效Bearer', {'Authorization': 'Bearer invalid_token_12345
   |    '}),
318|            ('Basic编码注入', {'Authorization': 'Basic YWRtaW46J29yJzEnP
   |    ScxJw=='}),
319|        ]
320|
321|        for test_name, headers in tests:
322|            try:
323|                resp = requests.get(api_endpoint, headers=headers, timeo
   |    ut=10)
324|                if resp.status_code == 200:
325|                    vulns.append(Vulnerability(
326|                        vuln_id=f"AUTH-{test_name.replace(' ', '_')}",
327|                        title=f"认证绕过: {test_name}",
328|                        description=f"使用 {test_name} 可访问受保护资源"
   |    ,
329|                        severity=SeverityLevel.CRITICAL,
330|                        cve_id=None, cvss_score=9.0,
331|                        affected_component=api_endpoint,
332|                        remediation="实施严格的认证验证",
333|                        scan_type=ScanType.AUTH
334|                    ))
335|            except Exception:
336|                pass
337|
338|        self.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
339|        return vulns
340|
341|    def scan_all(self, src_dir: str = 'src', api_endpoint: str = 'http:/
   |    /localhost:5000/api/v1') -> List[Vulnerability]:
342|        logger.info("Starting full security scan...")
343|        self.scan_dependencies()
344|        self.scan_code_sast(src_dir)
345|        self.scan_ports()
346|        self.test_injection(api_endpoint)
347|        self.test_auth_bypass(f"{api_endpoint}/metrics")
348|        logger.info(f"Full scan complete: {len(self.vulnerabilities)} to
   |    tal vulnerabilities")
349|        return self.vulnerabilities
350|
351|    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
352|        by_severity = {s: 0 for s in SeverityLevel}
353|        by_type = {s: 0 for s in ScanType}
354|
355|        for v in self.vulnerabilities:
356|            by_severity[v.severity] += 1
357|            by_type[v.scan_type] += 1
358|
359|        return {
360|            'total': len(self.vulnerabilities),
361|            'by_severity': {s.value: c for s, c in by_severity.items()},
362|            'by_type': {t.value: c for t, c in by_type.items()},
363|            'critical_count': by_severity[SeverityLevel.CRITICAL],
364|            'high_count': by_severity[SeverityLevel.HIGH],
365|            'needs_immediate_action': by_severity[SeverityLevel.CRITICAL
   |    ] > 0
366|        }

================================================================================
69. src/testing/stress_tester.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|压力测试模块
  3|
  4|模拟不同负载条件下的系统表现:
  5|- CPU压力测试 (多线程计算密集)
  6|- 内存压力测试 (大数据分配)
  7|- 磁盘IO压力测试 (密集读写)
  8|- 网络压力测试 (高并发请求)
  9|- 时间窗口测试 (持续高压)
 10|
 11|参考项目: Apache JMeter, Locust, stress-ng
 12|"""
 13|
 14|import time
 15|import threading
 16|import random
 17|import numpy as np
 18|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Callable, Tuple
 19|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 20|from enum import Enum
 21|from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, ProcessPoolExecutor, 
   |    as_completed
 22|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 23|from src.utils.config import Config
 24|import psutil
 25|
 26|logger = setup_logger("stress_tester")
 27|config = Config()
 28|
 29|@dataclass
 30|class StressMetrics:
 31|    test_name: str
 32|    duration_sec: float
 33|    peak_cpu_pct: float
 34|    avg_cpu_pct: float
 35|    peak_memory_mb: float
 36|    avg_memory_mb: float
 37|    peak_disk_io_mbps: float
 38|    peak_network_mbps: float
 39|    total_requests: int
 40|    success_rate: float
 41|    avg_response_ms: float
 42|    p50_response_ms: float
 43|    p95_response_ms: float
 44|    p99_response_ms: float
 45|    error_count: int
 46|    timeout_count: int
 47|    threshold_violations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
 48|
 49|class StressTester:
 50|    """
 51|    压力测试器
 52|    
 53|    系统性地模拟各种负载场景，评估系统极限:
 54|    1. 阶梯增压 (逐步提高负载找到拐点)
 55|    2. 恒压测试 (长时间保持高负载)
 56|    3. 突发测试 (瞬时高并发)
 57|    4. 混合负载 (多类型任务混合)
 58|    """
 59|
 60|    def __init__(self, target_func: Callable = None):
 61|        self.target_func = target_func
 62|        self.metrics_history: List[StressMetrics] = []
 63|        self.thresholds = self._load_thresholds()
 64|
 65|    def _load_thresholds(self) -> Dict:
 66|        try:
 67|            return config.stress_thresholds
 68|        except Exception:
 69|            return {
 70|                'cpu': {'warning': 70, 'critical': 85, 'max': 95},
 71|                'memory': {'warning': 75, 'critical': 90, 'max': 95},
 72|                'response_time': {'warning_ms': 200, 'critical_ms': 500,
   |     'timeout_ms': 5000},
 73|                'error_rate': {'warning': 0.01, 'critical': 0.05, 'max':
   |     0.10}
 74|            }
 75|
 76|    def _monitor_system(self, duration_sec: float) -> Dict[str, List[flo
   |    at]]:
 77|        cpu_samples = []
 78|        mem_samples = []
 79|        interval = 0.5
 80|        for _ in range(int(duration_sec / interval)):
 81|            cpu_samples.append(psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1))
 82|            mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
 83|            mem_samples.append(mem.used / (1024 * 1024))
 84|        return {'cpu': cpu_samples, 'memory': mem_samples}
 85|
 86|    def run_cpu_stress(self, num_threads: int = 4, duration_sec: int = 3
   |    0) -> StressMetrics:
 87|        logger.info(f"Starting CPU stress test: {num_threads} threads, {
   |    duration_sec}s")
 88|
 89|        def cpu_intensive_work():
 90|            for _ in range(1000000):
 91|                np.random.rand(100, 100).dot(np.random.rand(100, 100))
 92|
 93|        start = time.time()
 94|        responses = []
 95|
 96|        monitor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_system, a
   |    rgs=(duration_sec,))
 97|        monitor_thread.start()
 98|
 99|        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_threads) as executor:
100|            futures = [executor.submit(cpu_intensive_work) for _ in rang
   |    e(num_threads * 10)]
101|            for future in as_completed(futures):
102|                pass
103|
104|        monitor_thread.join(timeout=5)
105|        elapsed = time.time() - start
106|
107|        metrics = StressMetrics(
108|            test_name="CPU压力测试",
109|            duration_sec=elapsed,
110|            peak_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
111|            avg_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
112|            peak_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 * 1024),
113|            avg_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 * 1024),
114|            peak_disk_io_mbps=0, peak_network_mbps=0,
115|            total_requests=num_threads * 10,
116|            success_rate=1.0,
117|            avg_response_ms=0, p50_response_ms=0,
118|            p95_response_ms=0, p99_response_ms=0,
119|            error_count=0, timeout_count=0
120|        )
121|        self.metrics_history.append(metrics)
122|        return metrics
123|
124|    def run_concurrency_stress(self, concurrency_levels: List[int],
125|                               request_func: Callable,
126|                               requests_per_level: int = 100) -> List[St
   |    ressMetrics]:
127|        results = []
128|        for concurrency in concurrency_levels:
129|            logger.info(f"Concurrency stress test: {concurrency} concurr
   |    ent")
130|            responses = []
131|            errors = 0
132|            timeouts = 0
133|            start = time.time()
134|
135|            def worker():
136|                try:
137|                    result = request_func()
138|                    responses.append(time.time())
139|                    return result
140|                except Exception:
141|                    nonlocal errors
142|                    errors += 1
143|                    return None
144|
145|            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=concurrency) as executor
   |    :
146|                futures = [executor.submit(worker) for _ in range(reques
   |    ts_per_level)]
147|                for future in as_completed(futures):
148|                    try:
149|                        future.result(timeout=5)
150|                    except Exception:
151|                        timeouts += 1
152|
153|            elapsed = time.time() - start
154|            if len(responses) >= 2:
155|                latencies = [t - start for t in sorted(responses)]
156|                metrics = StressMetrics(
157|                    test_name=f"并发压力: {concurrency}",
158|                    duration_sec=elapsed,
159|                    peak_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
160|                    avg_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
161|                    peak_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 
   |    * 1024),
162|                    avg_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 *
   |     1024),
163|                    peak_disk_io_mbps=0, peak_network_mbps=0,
164|                    total_requests=requests_per_level,
165|                    success_rate=(requests_per_level - errors) / request
   |    s_per_level,
166|                    avg_response_ms=np.mean(latencies) * 1000,
167|                    p50_response_ms=np.percentile(latencies, 50) * 1000,
168|                    p95_response_ms=np.percentile(latencies, 95) * 1000,
169|                    p99_response_ms=np.percentile(latencies, 99) * 1000,
170|                    error_count=errors, timeout_count=timeouts
171|                )
172|                results.append(metrics)
173|                self.metrics_history.append(metrics)
174|
175|        return results
176|
177|    def run_endurance_test(self, request_func: Callable, duration_minute
   |    s: int = 5) -> StressMetrics:
178|        logger.info(f"Starting endurance test: {duration_minutes} minute
   |    s")
179|        start = time.time()
180|        end = start + duration_minutes * 60
181|        total = 0
182|        errors = 0
183|
184|        while time.time() < end:
185|            try:
186|                request_func()
187|                total += 1
188|            except Exception:
189|                errors += 1
190|
191|        elapsed = time.time() - start
192|        metrics = StressMetrics(
193|            test_name="持续负载测试",
194|            duration_sec=elapsed,
195|            peak_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
196|            avg_cpu_pct=psutil.cpu_percent(),
197|            peak_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 * 1024),
198|            avg_memory_mb=psutil.virtual_memory().used / (1024 * 1024),
199|            peak_disk_io_mbps=0, peak_network_mbps=0,
200|            total_requests=total,
201|            success_rate=(total - errors) / max(total, 1),
202|            avg_response_ms=elapsed / max(total, 1) * 1000,
203|            p50_response_ms=0, p95_response_ms=0, p99_response_ms=0,
204|            error_count=errors, timeout_count=0
205|        )
206|        self.metrics_history.append(metrics)
207|        return metrics
208|
209|    def check_thresholds(self, metrics: StressMetrics) -> List[str]:
210|        violations = []
211|
212|        thresholds = self.thresholds
213|        if metrics.peak_cpu_pct > thresholds.get('cpu', {}).get('critica
   |    l', 85):
214|            violations.append(f"CPU超临界阈值: {metrics.peak_cpu_pct:.1f
   |    }% > {thresholds['cpu']['critical']}%")
215|        elif metrics.peak_cpu_pct > thresholds.get('cpu', {}).get('warni
   |    ng', 70):
216|            violations.append(f"CPU超警告阈值: {metrics.peak_cpu_pct:.1f
   |    }% > {thresholds['cpu']['warning']}%")
217|
218|        if (1.0 - metrics.success_rate) > thresholds.get('error_rate', {
   |    }).get('critical', 0.05):
219|            violations.append(f"错误率超临界阈值: {(1.0-metrics.success_
   |    rate)*100:.1f}%")
220|
221|        if metrics.avg_response_ms > thresholds.get('response_time', {})
   |    .get('critical_ms', 500):
222|            violations.append(f"响应时间超临界阈值: {metrics.avg_respons
   |    e_ms:.0f}ms")
223|
224|        metrics.threshold_violations = violations
225|        return violations
226|
227|    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
228|        if not self.metrics_history:
229|            return {'tests': 0}
230|
231|        all_peak_cpu = [m.peak_cpu_pct for m in self.metrics_history]
232|        all_error_rates = [1.0 - m.success_rate for m in self.metrics_hi
   |    story]
233|
234|        return {
235|            'total_tests': len(self.metrics_history),
236|            'max_cpu_pct': max(all_peak_cpu),
237|            'avg_cpu_pct': np.mean(all_peak_cpu),
238|            'max_error_rate': max(all_error_rates),
239|            'avg_error_rate': np.mean(all_error_rates),
240|            'total_violations': sum(len(m.threshold_violations) for m in
   |     self.metrics_history),
241|            'violations': [m.threshold_violations for m in self.metrics_
   |    history if m.threshold_violations]
242|        }

================================================================================
70. src/testing/test_orchestrator.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|自动化测试引擎
  3|
  4|建立自动化测试流程，支持:
  5|- 兼容性测试 (跨版本/跨平台/跨依赖)
  6|- 压力测试 (CPU/内存/IO/网络/并发)
  7|- 安全渗透测试 (注入/模糊/认证绕过)
  8|- 回归测试
  9|
 10|参考项目: pytest, Selenium Grid, Locust
 11|"""
 12|
 13|import time
 14|import json
 15|import threading
 16|import subprocess
 17|import os
 18|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Callable, Tuple
 19|from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 20|from enum import Enum
 21|from datetime import datetime
 22|from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
 23|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 24|from src.utils.config import Config
 25|
 26|logger = setup_logger("test_orchestrator")
 27|config = Config()
 28|
 29|class TestStatus(Enum):
 30|    PENDING = "pending"
 31|    RUNNING = "running"
 32|    PASSED = "passed"
 33|    FAILED = "failed"
 34|    SKIPPED = "skipped"
 35|    ERROR = "error"
 36|
 37|class TestSeverity(Enum):
 38|    CRITICAL = "critical"
 39|    HIGH = "high"
 40|    MEDIUM = "medium"
 41|    LOW = "low"
 42|
 43|@dataclass
 44|class TestResult:
 45|    test_id: str
 46|    test_name: str
 47|    category: str
 48|    status: TestStatus
 49|    severity: TestSeverity
 50|    duration_sec: float
 51|    message: str = ""
 52|    details: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
 53|    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isofor
   |    mat())
 54|
 55|@dataclass
 56|class TestSuite:
 57|    name: str
 58|    description: str
 59|    tests: List[Callable] = field(default_factory=list)
 60|    setup: Optional[Callable] = None
 61|    teardown: Optional[Callable] = None
 62|    timeout_sec: int = 300
 63|
 64|
 65|class TestOrchestrator:
 66|    """
 67|    测试编排引擎
 68|    
 69|    管理测试套件的执行流程:
 70|    1. 环境准备 (Setup)
 71|    2. 测试执行 (并发执行)
 72|    3. 结果收集 (Result Collection)
 73|    4. 环境清理 (Teardown)
 74|    5. 报告生成 (Report)
 75|    """
 76|
 77|    def __init__(self, parallel_workers: int = 4):
 78|        self.parallel_workers = parallel_workers
 79|        self._suites: Dict[str, TestSuite] = {}
 80|        self._results: List[TestResult] = []
 81|        self._running = False
 82|        self._hooks: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {
 83|            'before_all': [], 'after_all': [],
 84|            'before_each': [], 'after_each': [],
 85|            'on_failure': []
 86|        }
 87|
 88|    def register_suite(self, suite: TestSuite) -> None:
 89|        self._suites[suite.name] = suite
 90|
 91|    def add_hook(self, event: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
 92|        if event in self._hooks:
 93|            self._hooks[event].append(callback)
 94|
 95|    def run_all(self) -> List[TestResult]:
 96|        self._running = True
 97|        self._results = []
 98|
 99|        for hook in self._hooks['before_all']:
100|            hook()
101|
102|        for name, suite in self._suites.items():
103|            suite_results = self._run_suite(suite)
104|            self._results.extend(suite_results)
105|
106|        for hook in self._hooks['after_all']:
107|            hook()
108|
109|        self._running = False
110|        return self._results
111|
112|    def _run_suite(self, suite: TestSuite) -> List[TestResult]:
113|        results = []
114|        logger.info(f"Running suite: {suite.name}")
115|
116|        if suite.setup:
117|            try:
118|                suite.setup()
119|            except Exception as e:
120|                logger.error(f"Suite setup failed: {e}")
121|                return [TestResult(
122|                    test_id=f"{suite.name}_setup",
123|                    test_name="Suite Setup",
124|                    category="setup",
125|                    status=TestStatus.ERROR,
126|                    severity=TestSeverity.CRITICAL,
127|                    duration_sec=0,
128|                    message=str(e)
129|                )]
130|
131|        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.parallel_workers) as ex
   |    ecutor:
132|            futures = {}
133|            for test in suite.tests:
134|                future = executor.submit(self._run_single_test, test, su
   |    ite)
135|                futures[future] = test
136|
137|            for future in as_completed(futures):
138|                try:
139|                    result = future.result(timeout=suite.timeout_sec)
140|                    results.append(result)
141|                except Exception as e:
142|                    test = futures[future]
143|                    results.append(TestResult(
144|                        test_id=getattr(test, '__name__', 'unknown'),
145|                        test_name=getattr(test, '__name__', 'unknown'),
146|                        category=suite.name,
147|                        status=TestStatus.ERROR,
148|                        severity=TestSeverity.HIGH,
149|                        duration_sec=suite.timeout_sec,
150|                        message=str(e)
151|                    ))
152|
153|        if suite.teardown:
154|            try:
155|                suite.teardown()
156|            except Exception as e:
157|                logger.error(f"Suite teardown failed: {e}")
158|
159|        return results
160|
161|    def _run_single_test(self, test_func: Callable, suite: TestSuite) ->
   |     TestResult:
162|        name = getattr(test_func, '__name__', 'unknown')
163|        start = time.time()
164|
165|        for hook in self._hooks['before_each']:
166|            hook()
167|
168|        try:
169|            result = test_func()
170|            status = TestStatus.PASSED if result else TestStatus.FAILED
171|            message = "Test passed" if result else "Test assertion faile
   |    d"
172|        except Exception as e:
173|            status = TestStatus.ERROR
174|            message = str(e)
175|            for hook in self._hooks['on_failure']:
176|                hook(name, str(e))
177|
178|        for hook in self._hooks['after_each']:
179|            hook()
180|
181|        elapsed = time.time() - start
182|        return TestResult(
183|            test_id=name, test_name=name, category=suite.name,
184|            status=status, severity=TestSeverity.MEDIUM,
185|            duration_sec=elapsed, message=message
186|        )
187|
188|    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
189|        total = len(self._results)
190|        passed = sum(1 for r in self._results if r.status == TestStatus.
   |    PASSED)
191|        failed = sum(1 for r in self._results if r.status == TestStatus.
   |    FAILED)
192|        errors = sum(1 for r in self._results if r.status == TestStatus.
   |    ERROR)
193|        skipped = sum(1 for r in self._results if r.status == TestStatus
   |    .SKIPPED)
194|        
195|        return {
196|            'total': total,
197|            'passed': passed,
198|            'failed': failed,
199|            'errors': errors,
200|            'skipped': skipped,
201|            'pass_rate': passed / max(total, 1) * 100,
202|            'total_duration_sec': sum(r.duration_sec for r in self._resu
   |    lts)
203|        }
204|
205|    def get_results_by_severity(self, severity: TestSeverity) -> List[Te
   |    stResult]:
206|        return [r for r in self._results if r.severity == severity]

71. src/testing/test_runner.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|测试环境一键运行器
  3|
  4|集成所有测试模块，提供统一的测试入口:
  5|1. 环境检测 + 初始化
  6|2. 兼容性测试 (跨版本/跨平台)
  7|3. 压力测试 (CPU/内存/并发)
  8|4. 安全扫描 (依赖/端口/SAST/注入)
  9|5. 风险评估 + 报告生成
 10|"""
 11|
 12|import sys
 13|import json
 14|import time
 15|import os
 16|from datetime import datetime
 17|from typing import Dict, Any, List
 18|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 19|from src.utils.config import Config
 20|from src.testing import (
 21|    CompatibilityTester, StressTester, SecurityScanner,
 22|    RiskAssessor, EnvironmentManager
 23|)
 24|
 25|logger = setup_logger("test_runner")
 26|config = Config()
 27|
 28|def run_full_test_suite(output_file: str = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 29|    """
 30|    运行完整测试套件
 31|    
 32|    流程:
 33|    1. 环境初始化 + 快照
 34|    2. 兼容性测试
 35|    3. 压力测试
 36|    4. 安全扫描
 37|    5. 风险评估
 38|    6. 报告生成
 39|    7. 环境清理
 40|    """
 41|    
 42|    start_time = time.time()
 43|    results = {
 44|        'run_id': datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S'),
 45|        'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
 46|        'summary': {},
 47|        'compatibility': {},
 48|        'stress': {},
 49|        'security': {},
 50|        'risk': None,
 51|        'duration_sec': 0
 52|    }
 53|    
 54|    logger.info("=" * 60)
 55|    logger.info("开始仿真模拟测试环境 - 完整测试套件")
 56|    logger.info("=" * 60)
 57|    
 58|    env_mgr = EnvironmentManager()
 59|    env_mgr.save_state('test_start', datetime.now().isoformat())
 60|    env_mgr.create_snapshot("测试执行前快照")
 61|
 62|    try:
 63|        # 1. 兼容性测试
 64|        logger.info("\n[1/5] 兼容性测试...")
 65|        ct = CompatibilityTester()
 66|        ct.check_python_version("3.9", "3.12")
 67|        ct.check_os_compatibility()
 68|        ct.check_platform_arch()
 69|        ct.check_all_dependencies()
 70|        ct.check_import_all_modules()
 71|        results['compatibility'] = ct.get_summary()
 72|        logger.info(f"  兼容性评分: {results['compatibility']['compatibi
   |    lity_score']:.1f}%")
 73|
 74|        # 2. 压力测试
 75|        logger.info("\n[2/5] 压力测试...")
 76|        st = StressTester()
 77|        cpu_metrics = st.run_cpu_stress(num_threads=2, duration_sec=5)
 78|        st.check_thresholds(cpu_metrics)
 79|        results['stress'] = st.get_summary()
 80|        logger.info(f"  最高CPU使用率: {results['stress']['max_cpu_pct']
   |    :.1f}%")
 81|
 82|        # 3. 安全扫描
 83|        logger.info("\n[3/5] 安全扫描...")
 84|        ss = SecurityScanner()
 85|        ss.scan_dependencies()
 86|        ss.scan_code_sast('src')
 87|        ss.scan_ports('localhost', [5000, 5432, 6379, 8080])
 88|        results['security'] = ss.get_summary()
 89|        logger.info(f"  发现漏洞数: {results['security']['total']}")
 90|        logger.info(f"  严重: {results['security']['critical_count']} / 
   |    高: {results['security']['high_count']}")
 91|
 92|        # 4. 风险评估
 93|        logger.info("\n[4/5] 风险评估...")
 94|        ra = RiskAssessor()
 95|        ra.assess_from_security_scan(ss.vulnerabilities)
 96|        ra.assess_from_stress_test(cpu_metrics)
 97|        report = ra.generate_report()
 98|        results['risk'] = {
 99|            'overall_risk': report.overall_risk.value,
100|            'safety_score': report.safety_score,
101|            'recommendations': report.recommendations
102|        }
103|        logger.info(f"  整体风险等级: {report.overall_risk.value}")
104|        logger.info(f"  安全评分: {report.safety_score:.1f}/100")
105|
106|        # 5. 汇总
107|        logger.info("\n[5/5] 生成测试报告...")
108|        results['summary'] = {
109|            'compatibility_score': results['compatibility']['compatibili
   |    ty_score'],
110|            'stress_tests_passed': results['stress']['total_tests'],
111|            'security_vulnerabilities': results['security']['total'],
112|            'critical_vulnerabilities': results['security']['critical_co
   |    unt'],
113|            'risk_level': results['risk']['overall_risk'],
114|            'safety_score': results['risk']['safety_score']
115|        }
116|
117|        results['duration_sec'] = time.time() - start_time
118|        logger.info(f"\n测试完成! 耗时: {results['duration_sec']:.1f}s")
119|
120|        # 生成报告文件
121|        if output_file is None:
122|            os.makedirs('test_reports', exist_ok=True)
123|            output_file = f"test_reports/test_report_{results['run_id']}
   |    .json"
124|
125|        with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
126|            json.dump(results, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, default=
   |    str)
127|
128|        logger.info(f"报告已保存至: {output_file}")
129|
130|    except Exception as e:
131|        logger.error(f"测试执行异常: {e}", exc_info=True)
132|        results['error'] = str(e)
133|
134|    finally:
135|        env_mgr.create_snapshot("测试执行后快照")
136|        env_mgr.cleanup_snapshots(keep_last=5)
137|
138|    _print_report(results)
139|    return results
140|
141|
142|def _print_report(results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
143|    logger.info("=" * 60)
144|    logger.info("  测试环境执行报告")
145|    logger.info("=" * 60)
146|    logger.info(f"  执行ID:      {results['run_id']}")
147|    logger.info(f"  耗时:        {results['duration_sec']:.1f}s")
148|    logger.info(f"  兼容性评分:  {results['compatibility'].get('compatib
   |    ility_score', 0):.1f}%")
149|    logger.info(f"  安全漏洞:    {results['security'].get('total', 0)} (
   |    {results['security'].get('critical_count', 0)} 严重)")
150|    logger.info(f"  风险等级:    {results['risk'].get('overall_risk', 'N
   |    /A')}")
151|    logger.info(f"  安全评分:    {results['risk'].get('safety_score', 0)
   |    :.1f}/100")
152|    logger.info("=" * 60)
153|
154|    if results.get('summary', {}).get('risk_level') == 'critical':
155|        logger.warning("⚠ 发现严重风险！请立即处理以下问题:")
156|        for rec in results['risk'].get('recommendations', []):
157|            logger.warning(f"    - {rec}")
158|    elif results.get('summary', {}).get('safety_score', 100) >= 80:
159|        logger.info("✓ 系统安全状况良好")
160|
161|
162|if __name__ == '__main__':
163|    run_full_test_suite()
