================================================================================
73. src/utils/cache.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|缓存模块
  3|
  4|提供内存缓存支持，用于优化频繁的数据读取操作:
  5|- 基于 TTL (Time-To-Live) 的缓存过期机制
  6|- 线程安全的缓存操作
  7|- 支持手动失效和自动清理
  8|"""
  9|
 10|import threading
 11|import time
 12|from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Callable
 13|from functools import wraps
 14|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 15|
 16|logger = setup_logger("cache")
 17|
 18|
 19|class CacheEntry:
 20|    """缓存条目"""
 21|
 22|    def __init__(self, value: Any, ttl_sec: int):
 23|        self.value = value
 24|        self.created_at = time.time()
 25|        self.ttl_sec = ttl_sec
 26|
 27|    def is_expired(self) -> bool:
 28|        return time.time() - self.created_at > self.ttl_sec
 29|
 30|
 31|class Cache:
 32|    """
 33|    线程安全的内存缓存
 34|
 35|    使用示例:
 36|        cache = Cache()
 37|        cache.set("key", value, ttl_sec=300)
 38|        value = cache.get("key")
 39|    """
 40|
 41|    def __init__(self):
 42|        self._store: Dict[str, CacheEntry] = {}
 43|        self._lock = threading.RLock()
 44|
 45|    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[Any]:
 46|        """获取缓存值，过期返回 None"""
 47|        with self._lock:
 48|            entry = self._store.get(key)
 49|            if entry is None:
 50|                return None
 51|            if entry.is_expired():
 52|                del self._store[key]
 53|                logger.debug(f"Cache expired for key: {key}")
 54|                return None
 55|            logger.debug(f"Cache hit for key: {key}")
 56|            return entry.value
 57|
 58|    def set(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl_sec: int = 300) -> None:
 59|        """设置缓存值，默认 TTL 5 分钟"""
 60|        with self._lock:
 61|            self._store[key] = CacheEntry(value, ttl_sec)
 62|            logger.debug(f"Cache set for key: {key} (ttl={ttl_sec}s)")
 63|
 64|    def delete(self, key: str) -> bool:
 65|        """删除缓存条目"""
 66|        with self._lock:
 67|            if key in self._store:
 68|                del self._store[key]
 69|                logger.debug(f"Cache deleted for key: {key}")
 70|                return True
 71|            return False
 72|
 73|    def clear(self) -> None:
 74|        """清空所有缓存"""
 75|        with self._lock:
 76|            self._store.clear()
 77|            logger.info("Cache cleared")
 78|
 79|    def cleanup_expired(self) -> int:
 80|        """清理过期条目，返回清理数量"""
 81|        with self._lock:
 82|            expired_keys = [k for k, v in self._store.items() if v.is_ex
   |    pired()]
 83|            for k in expired_keys:
 84|                del self._store[k]
 85|            if expired_keys:
 86|                logger.debug(f"Cleaned up {len(expired_keys)} expired ca
   |    che entries")
 87|            return len(expired_keys)
 88|
 89|    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 90|        """获取缓存统计信息"""
 91|        with self._lock:
 92|            total = len(self._store)
 93|            expired = sum(1 for v in self._store.values() if v.is_expire
   |    d())
 94|            return {
 95|                'total_entries': total,
 96|                'expired_entries': expired,
 97|                'active_entries': total - expired
 98|            }
 99|
100|
101|# 全局缓存实例
102|_global_cache = Cache()
103|
104|
105|def get_cache() -> Cache:
106|    """获取全局缓存实例"""
107|    return _global_cache
108|
109|
110|def cached(ttl_sec: int = 300, key_func: Optional[Callable] = None):
111|    """
112|    缓存装饰器
113|
114|    Args:
115|        ttl_sec: 缓存过期时间（秒）
116|        key_func: 自定义缓存键生成函数，接收原函数参数，返回字符串键
117|
118|    使用示例:
119|        @cached(ttl_sec=60)
120|        def expensive_function(x, y):
121|            return x + y
122|    """
123|    def decorator(func: Callable) -> Callable:
124|        @wraps(func)
125|        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
126|            cache = get_cache()
127|
128|            if key_func:
129|                cache_key = key_func(*args, **kwargs)
130|            else:
131|                # 默认使用函数名和参数生成键
132|                key_parts = [func.__module__, func.__name__]
133|                if args:
134|                    key_parts.append(str(args))
135|                if kwargs:
136|                    key_parts.append(str(sorted(kwargs.items())))
137|                cache_key = "|".join(key_parts)
138|
139|            cached_value = cache.get(cache_key)
140|            if cached_value is not None:
141|                return cached_value
142|
143|            result = func(*args, **kwargs)
144|            cache.set(cache_key, result, ttl_sec)
145|            return result
146|        return wrapper
147|    return decorator

  1|import yaml
  2|import platform
  3|import psutil
  4|import os
  5|from pathlib import Path
  6|from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, List
  7|
  8|class Config:
  9|    """支持环境感知的配置管理器"""
 10|
 11|    _instance: Optional['Config'] = None
 12|
 13|    def __new__(cls, config_path: Optional[str] = None, env: Optional[st
   |    r] = None) -> 'Config':
 14|        if cls._instance is None:
 15|            cls._instance = super().__new__(cls)
 16|            cls._instance._loaded = False
 17|        return cls._instance
 18|
 19|    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[str] = None, env: Optional[
   |    str] = None):
 20|        if not self._loaded:
 21|            self._load(config_path, env)
 22|
 23|    def _load(self, config_path: Optional[str], env: Optional[str]) -> N
   |    one:
 24|        env_config: Optional[Path] = None
 25|
 26|        if config_path is None:
 27|            config_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "config"
 28|            base_config = config_dir / "default.yaml"
 29|
 30|            env = env or os.environ.get('DCS_ENV', 'development')
 31|            env_config = config_dir / f"{env}.yaml"
 32|        else:
 33|            base_config = Path(config_path)
 34|            env = env or os.environ.get('DCS_ENV')
 35|            if env:
 36|                env_config = base_config.parent / f"{env}.yaml"
 37|
 38|        with open(base_config, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 39|            loaded = yaml.safe_load(f)
 40|        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = loaded if isinstance(loaded, dict) 
   |    else {}
 41|
 42|        if env_config is not None:
 43|            try:
 44|                with open(env_config, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 45|                    env_data = yaml.safe_load(f)
 46|            except FileNotFoundError:
 47|                env_data = None
 48|            if isinstance(env_data, dict):
 49|                self._deep_merge(self._data, env_data)
 50|
 51|        self._apply_environment_overrides()
 52|        self._detected_env = self._detect_runtime_env()
 53|        self._loaded = True
 54|
 55|    def _deep_merge(self, base: dict, override: dict) -> None:
 56|        for key, value in override.items():
 57|            if key in base and isinstance(base[key], dict) and isinstanc
   |    e(value, dict):
 58|                self._deep_merge(base[key], value)
 59|            else:
 60|                base[key] = value
 61|
 62|    def _apply_environment_overrides(self) -> None:
 63|        env_map = {
 64|            'DCS_NUM_SERVERS': ('simulation', 'num_servers', int),
 65|            'DCS_SIM_HOURS': ('simulation', 'simulation_hours', int),
 66|            'DCS_PARALLEL_WORKERS': ('simulation', 'parallel_workers', i
   |    nt),
 67|            'DCS_DATA_DIR': ('output', 'data_dir', str),
 68|            'DCS_REPORTS_DIR': ('output', 'reports_dir', str),
 69|            'DCS_ENABLE_VIZ': ('output', 'enable_visualization', lambda 
   |    x: x.lower() == 'true'),
 70|            'DCS_POPULATION_SIZE': ('genetic', 'population_size', int),
 71|            'DCS_GENERATIONS': ('genetic', 'generations', int),
 72|            'DCS_AMBIENT_TEMP': ('environment', 'ambient_temp_base', flo
   |    at),
 73|            'DCS_API_URL': ('api', 'api_url', str),
 74|        }
 75|
 76|        for env_var, (section, key, converter) in env_map.items():
 77|            value = os.environ.get(env_var)
 78|            if value is not None:
 79|                try:
 80|                    if section not in self._data:
 81|                        self._data[section] = {}
 82|                    self._data[section][key] = converter(value)
 83|                except (ValueError, TypeError):
 84|                    pass
 85|
 86|    def _detect_runtime_env(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 87|        info = {
 88|            'os': platform.system(),
 89|            'os_release': platform.release(),
 90|            'arch': platform.machine(),
 91|            'python_version': platform.python_version(),
 92|            'is_docker': os.path.exists('/.dockerversion'),
 93|            'is_k8s': bool(os.environ.get('KUBERNETES_SERVICE_HOST')),
 94|        }
 95|
 96|        try:
 97|            info['cpu_physical'] = psutil.cpu_count(logical=False)
 98|            info['cpu_logical'] = psutil.cpu_count(logical=True)
 99|        except Exception:
100|            info['cpu_physical'] = 1
101|            info['cpu_logical'] = 1
102|
103|        try:
104|            mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
105|            info['total_memory_gb'] = round(mem.total / (1024 ** 3), 1)
106|            info['available_memory_gb'] = round(mem.available / (1024 **
   |     3), 1)
107|        except Exception:
108|            info['total_memory_gb'] = 0
109|
110|        try:
111|            import torch
112|            info['has_cuda'] = torch.cuda.is_available()
113|            info['cuda_count'] = torch.cuda.device_count() if info['has_
   |    cuda'] else 0
114|        except ImportError:
115|            info['has_cuda'] = False
116|            info['cuda_count'] = 0
117|
118|        try:
119|            import torch_npu
120|            info['has_ascend'] = torch_npu.npu.is_available()
121|        except ImportError:
122|            info['has_ascend'] = os.path.exists('/usr/local/Ascend')
123|
124|        return info
125|
126|    def get_runtime_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
127|        return self._detected_env
128|
129|    def get_optimal_workers(self) -> int:
130|        cores = self._detected_env.get('cpu_physical', 4)
131|        if cores >= 16:
132|            return 8
133|        elif cores >= 8:
134|            return 4
135|        elif cores >= 4:
136|            return 2
137|        return 1
138|
139|    def __getattr__(self, key: str) -> Any:
140|        if key.startswith('_'):
141|            raise AttributeError(key)
142|        data = self.__dict__.get('_data')
143|        if not isinstance(data, dict) or key not in data:
144|            raise AttributeError(key)
145|        val = data[key]
146|        if isinstance(val, dict):
147|            class SectionAccessor:
148|                def __init__(self, data):
149|                    self._data = data
150|
151|                def __getattr__(self, k):
152|                    return self._data.get(k)
153|
154|                def get(self, k, default=None):
155|                    return self._data.get(k, default)
156|
157|                def __contains__(self, k):
158|                    return k in self._data
159|
160|                def __repr__(self):
161|                    return str(self._data)
162|
163|            return SectionAccessor(val)
164|        return val
165|
166|    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
167|        return self._data.get(key, default)
168|
169|    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
170|        self._data[key] = value
171|
172|    def __contains__(self, key: str) -> bool:
173|        return key in self._data
174|
175|    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
176|        return self._data.copy()
177|
178|    def reload(self, config_path: Optional[str] = None, env: Optional[st
   |    r] = None) -> None:
179|        self._loaded = False
180|        Config._instance = None
181|        self._load(config_path, env)

 71|def generate_synthetic_data(hours: int = 24, num_servers: int = 10) -> p
   |    d.DataFrame:
 72|    try:
 73|        time = np.arange(hours)
 74|        ambient_temp = 15 + 10 * np.sin((time - 6) * np.pi / 12)
 75|
 76|        data = []
 77|        for t in time:
 78|            for server_id in range(num_servers):
 79|                load = np.random.randint(0, 100)
 80|                temp = 25 + load * 0.3 + np.random.normal(0, 2)
 81|                power = 100 + load * 2
 82|
 83|                data.append({
 84|                    'time': t,
 85|                    'server_id': server_id,
 86|                    'load': load,
 87|                    'temperature': temp,
 88|                    'power': power,
 89|                    'ambient_temp': ambient_temp[t]
 90|                })
 91|
 92|        logger.info(f"Generated synthetic data with {len(data)} records"
   |    )
 93|        return pd.DataFrame(data)
 94|    except Exception as e:
 95|        logger.error(f"Failed to generate synthetic data: {str(e)}")
 96|        raise
 97|

   |    se)

  1|"""
  2|应用下载统计模块
  3|
  4|提供下载次数记录与查询功能:
  5|- 多维度统计 (按平台/应用/日期)
  6|- 持久化存储 (JSON文件)
  7|- 并发安全 (文件锁)
  8|"""
  9|
 10|import json
 11|import os
 12|import threading
 13|from datetime import datetime
 14|from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
 15|from src.utils.logger import setup_logger
 16|
 17|logger = setup_logger("download_stats")
 18|
 19|class DownloadStats:
 20|    """下载统计管理器"""
 21|
 22|    def __init__(self, data_dir: str = "data"):
 23|        self.data_dir = data_dir
 24|        self.stats_file = os.path.join(data_dir, "download_stats.json")
 25|        self._lock = threading.Lock()
 26|        os.makedirs(data_dir, exist_ok=True)
 27|        self._ensure_stats_file()
 28|
 29|    def _ensure_stats_file(self) -> None:
 30|        if not os.path.exists(self.stats_file):
 31|            self._write_stats({
 32|                'version': '1.0',
 33|                'created_at': datetime.now().isoformat(),
 34|                'records': [],
 35|                'total': 0,
 36|                'by_platform': {},
 37|                'by_app_type': {},
 38|                'by_date': {}
 39|            })
 40|
 41|    def _read_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 42|        try:
 43|            with open(self.stats_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 44|                return json.load(f)
 45|        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
 46|            return {
 47|                'version': '1.0',
 48|                'records': [],
 49|                'total': 0,
 50|                'by_platform': {},
 51|                'by_app_type': {},
 52|                'by_date': {}
 53|            }
 54|
 55|    def _write_stats(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 56|        with open(self.stats_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
 57|            json.dump(data, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
 58|
 59|    def record_download(
 60|        self, platform: str, app_type: str, filename: str,
 61|        size_mb: float, user_agent: str = "", ip_address: str = ""
 62|    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 63|        with self._lock:
 64|            stats = self._read_stats()
 65|
 66|            record = {
 67|                'platform': platform,
 68|                'app_type': app_type,
 69|                'filename': filename,
 70|                'size_mb': size_mb,
 71|                'user_agent': user_agent[:200],
 72|                'ip_address': ip_address,
 73|                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
 74|            }
 75|            stats['records'].append(record)
 76|
 77|            stats['total'] = stats.get('total', 0) + 1
 78|
 79|            stats['by_platform'][platform] = stats['by_platform'].get(pl
   |    atform, 0) + 1
 80|            stats['by_app_type'][app_type] = stats['by_app_type'].get(ap
   |    p_type, 0) + 1
 81|
 82|            today = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
 83|            stats['by_date'][today] = stats['by_date'].get(today, 0) + 1
 84|
 85|            if len(stats['records']) > 10000:
 86|                stats['records'] = stats['records'][-5000:]
 87|
 88|            self._write_stats(stats)
 89|            logger.info(f"Download recorded: {platform}/{app_type}/{file
   |    name}")
 90|
 91|            return record
 92|
 93|    def get_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 94|        stats = self._read_stats()
 95|        return {
 96|            'success': True,
 97|            'total': stats.get('total', 0),
 98|            'by_platform': stats.get('by_platform', {}),
 99|            'by_app_type': stats.get('by_app_type', {}),
100|            'by_date': dict(sorted(stats.get('by_date', {}).items())[-7:
   |    ]),
101|            'recent': stats['records'][-10:] if stats.get('records') els
   |    e []
102|        }
103|
104|    def get_by_platform(self, platform: str) -> int:
105|        stats = self._read_stats()
106|        return stats.get('by_platform', {}).get(platform, 0)
107|
108|    def reset_stats(self) -> bool:
109|        with self._lock:
110|            self._write_stats({
111|                'version': '1.0',
112|                'created_at': datetime.now().isoformat(),
113|                'records': [],
114|                'total': 0,
115|                'by_platform': {},
116|                'by_app_type': {},
117|                'by_date': {}
118|            })
119|            logger.warning("Download stats reset")
120|            return True
121|
122|_download_stats_instance: Optional[DownloadStats] = None
123|
124|def get_download_stats() -> DownloadStats:
125|    global _download_stats_instance
126|    if _download_stats_instance is None:
127|        _download_stats_instance = DownloadStats()
128|    return _download_stats_instance

77. src/utils/logger.py
================================================================================
  1|import logging
  2|import sys
  3|from pathlib import Path
  4|from typing import Optional
  5|from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
  6|
  7|
  8|class _ResilientStream:
  9|    """Console stream that degrades unencodable characters instead of ra
   |    ising.
 10|
 11|    A Chinese Windows console defaults to GBK, where markers such as ✓ /
   |     ✗ /
 12|    → do not exist. Without this wrapper a single log line can abort a w
   |    hole
 13|    CLI command with UnicodeEncodeError.
 14|    """
 15|
 16|    def __init__(self, stream) -> None:
 17|        self._stream = stream
 18|
 19|    def write(self, text: str):
 20|        try:
 21|            return self._stream.write(text)
 22|        except UnicodeEncodeError:
 23|            encoding = getattr(self._stream, 'encoding', None) or 'ascii
   |    '
 24|            safe = text.encode(encoding, 'replace').decode(encoding, 're
   |    place')
 25|            return self._stream.write(safe)
 26|
 27|    def flush(self) -> None:
 28|        try:
 29|            self._stream.flush()
 30|        except Exception:
 31|            pass
 32|
 33|    def isatty(self) -> bool:
 34|        return bool(getattr(self._stream, 'isatty', lambda: False)())
 35|
 36|
 37|def _console_stream():
 38|    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
 39|        reconfigure = getattr(stream, 'reconfigure', None)
 40|        if callable(reconfigure):
 41|            try:
 42|                reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
 43|            except Exception:
 44|                pass
 45|    return _ResilientStream(sys.stdout)
 46|
 47|
 48|def setup_logger(name: str = "datacenter_sim", level: int = logging.INFO
   |    ) -> logging.Logger:
 49|    logger = logging.getLogger(name)
 50|    logger.setLevel(level)
 51|
 52|    if not logger.handlers:
 53|        ch = logging.StreamHandler(_console_stream())
 54|        ch.setLevel(level)
 55|        ch.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(
 56|            '[%(asctime)s] [%(levelname)s] %(message)s',
 57|            datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
 58|        ))
 59|        logger.addHandler(ch)
 60|
 61|        log_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "logs"
 62|        log_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 63|
 64|        # 使用 RotatingFileHandler 实现日志轮转，单个文件最大 10MB，保留 5
   |     个备份
 65|        fh = RotatingFileHandler(
 66|            log_dir / "simulation.log",
 67|            maxBytes=10 * 1024 * 1024,
 68|            backupCount=5,
 69|            encoding='utf-8'
 70|        )
 71|        fh.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
 72|        fh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(
 73|            '%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] [%(name)s] %(message)s'
 74|        ))
 75|        logger.addHandler(fh)
 76|
 77|    return logger

78. src/utils/validation.py
================================================================================
  1|"""
  2|项目级安全输入验证模块
  3|
  4|提供统一的输入验证、清洗和安全防护:
  5|- 类型验证与转换
  6|- 范围约束检查
  7|- SQL/XSS/命令注入防护
  8|- 安全HTTP头生成
  9|- 速率限制辅助
 10|
 11|参考: OWASP Input Validation Cheat Sheet, Flask-Security
 12|"""
 13|import re
 14|import os
 15|import hashlib
 16|import secrets
 17|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, TypeVar, Union
 18|
 19|T = TypeVar('T')
 20|
 21|class ValidationError(ValueError):
 22|    pass
 23|
 24|def validate_int(value: Any, min_val: int = None, max_val: int = None,
 25|                 default: int = None, name: str = "value") -> int:
 26|    if value is None:
 27|        if default is not None:
 28|            return default
 29|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能为空")
 30|    try:
 31|        v = int(value)
 32|    except (ValueError, TypeError):
 33|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 必须是整数，收到: {value}")
 34|    if min_val is not None and v < min_val:
 35|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能小于 {min_val}，当前: {v}")
 36|    if max_val is not None and v > max_val:
 37|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能大于 {max_val}，当前: {v}")
 38|    return v
 39|
 40|def validate_float(value: Any, min_val: float = None, max_val: float = N
   |    one,
 41|                   default: float = None, name: str = "value") -> float:
 42|    if value is None:
 43|        if default is not None:
 44|            return default
 45|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能为空")
 46|    try:
 47|        v = float(value)
 48|    except (ValueError, TypeError):
 49|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 必须是数字，收到: {value}")
 50|    if min_val is not None and v < min_val:
 51|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能小于 {min_val}，当前: {v}")
 52|    if max_val is not None and v > max_val:
 53|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能大于 {max_val}，当前: {v}")
 54|    return v
 55|
 56|def validate_str(value: Any, min_len: int = 0, max_len: int = 256,
 57|                 pattern: str = None, allowed_chars: str = None,
 58|                 default: str = "", name: str = "value") -> str:
 59|    if value is None:
 60|        if default is not None:
 61|            return default
 62|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 不能为空")
 63|    v = str(value)
 64|    if len(v) < min_len:
 65|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 长度不能小于 {min_len}")
 66|    if len(v) > max_len:
 67|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 长度不能超过 {max_len}")
 68|    if pattern and not re.match(pattern, v):
 69|        raise ValidationError(f"{name} 格式无效: {v}")
 70|    if allowed_chars:
 71|        for ch in v:
 72|            if ch not in allowed_chars:
 73|                raise ValidationError(f"{name} 包含非法字符: '{ch}'")
 74|    return v
 75|
 76|def validate_algorithms(algorithms: Any, allowed: List[str] = None) -> L
   |    ist[str]:
 77|    if allowed is None:
 78|        allowed = ['greedy', 'genetic', 'round_robin', 'damo_ets', 'nsga
   |    2', 'moead']
 79|    if algorithms is None:
 80|        return ['greedy', 'genetic', 'round_robin', 'damo_ets']
 81|    if isinstance(algorithms, str):
 82|        algorithms = [a.strip() for a in algorithms.split(',')]
 83|    if not isinstance(algorithms, list):
 84|        raise ValidationError("algorithms 必须是列表或逗号分隔字符串")
 85|    result = []
 86|    for algo in algorithms:
 87|        algo = str(algo).strip().lower()
 88|        if algo not in allowed:
 89|            raise ValidationError(f"不支持的算法: '{algo}'，支持: {allowe
   |    d}")
 90|        result.append(algo)
 91|    return result or ['greedy']
 92|
 93|def sanitize_filename(filename: str, max_len: int = 128) -> str:
 94|    dangerous = r'[<>:"/\\|?*\';&$`!#]'
 95|    sanitized = re.sub(dangerous, '_', filename)
 96|    path_traversal = r'(\.\./|\.\.\\)'
 97|    sanitized = re.sub(path_traversal, '', sanitized)
 98|    sanitized = sanitized.strip('. ')
 99|    if len(sanitized) > max_len:
100|        name, ext = os.path.splitext(sanitized)
101|        sanitized = name[:max_len - len(ext)] + ext
102|    if not sanitized:
103|        sanitized = 'download'
104|    return sanitized
105|
106|SQL_INJECTION_PATTERNS = re.compile(
107|    r"(UNION\s+SELECT|DROP\s+TABLE|ALTER\s+TABLE|INSERT\s+INTO|"
108|    r"DELETE\s+FROM|--\s|\bOR\b\s+['\"]?\d['\"]?\s*=\s*['\"]?\d)",
109|    re.IGNORECASE
110|)
111|
112|XSS_PATTERNS = re.compile(
113|    r"(<script|javascript:|onerror\s*=|onload\s*=|"
114|    r"<img[^>]+onerror|<svg|eval\s*\()",
115|    re.IGNORECASE
116|)
117|
118|def detect_sql_injection(value: str) -> bool:
119|    return bool(SQL_INJECTION_PATTERNS.search(str(value)))
120|
121|def detect_xss(value: str) -> bool:
122|    return bool(XSS_PATTERNS.search(str(value)))
123|
124|def sanitize_html(value: str) -> str:
125|    return (value
126|            .replace('&', '&amp;')
127|            .replace('<', '&lt;')
128|            .replace('>', '&gt;')
129|            .replace('"', '&quot;')
130|            .replace("'", '&#x27;'))
131|
132|def validate_simulation_params(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
133|    return {
134|        'num_servers': validate_int(
135|            data.get('num_servers'), min_val=1, max_val=1000, default=10
   |    ,
136|            name='服务器数量'),
137|        'simulation_hours': validate_int(
138|            data.get('simulation_hours'), min_val=1, max_val=720, defaul
   |    t=24,
139|            name='仿真时长'),
140|        'parallel_workers': validate_int(
141|            data.get('parallel_workers'), min_val=1, max_val=32, default
   |    =4,
142|            name='并行工作器'),
143|        'run_mode': validate_str(
144|            data.get('run_mode'), max_len=32,
145|            pattern=r'^(standard|distributed|large_scale)$', default='st
   |    andard',
146|            name='运行模式'),
147|        'server_type': validate_str(
148|            data.get('server_type'), max_len=32,
149|            pattern=r'^(mixed|standard|high_performance|gpu_server)$', d
   |    efault='mixed',
150|            name='服务器类型'),
151|        'algorithms': validate_algorithms(data.get('algorithms')),
152|        'visualization': bool(data.get('visualization', True)),
153|        'save_results': bool(data.get('save_results', True)),
154|    }
155|
156|def security_headers() -> Dict[str, str]:
157|    return {
158|        'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
159|        'X-Frame-Options': 'DENY',
160|        'X-XSS-Protection': '1; mode=block',
161|        'Referrer-Policy': 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin',
162|        'Permissions-Policy': 'camera=(), microphone=(self), geolocation
   |    =()',
163|        'Strict-Transport-Security': 'max-age=31536000; includeSubDomain
   |    s',
164|        'Content-Security-Policy': (
165|            "default-src 'self'; "
166|            "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://cdn.jsdelivr.net 
   |    https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com; "
167|            "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://cdn.jsdelivr.net h
   |    ttps://cdnjs.cloudflare.com; "
168|            "img-src 'self' data:; "
169|            "font-src 'self' https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com; "
170|            "connect-src 'self'; "
171|            "frame-ancestors 'none'; "
172|            "form-action 'self'"
173|        ),
174|    }
175|
176|def generate_csrf_token() -> str:
177|    return secrets.token_hex(32)
178|
179|def hash_sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
180|    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
181|
182|class RateLimiter:
183|    """简易令牌桶速率限制器"""
184|
185|    def __init__(self, max_requests: int = 60, window_sec: float = 60.0)
   |    :
186|        self.max_requests = max_requests
187|        self.window_sec = window_sec
188|        self._clients: Dict[str, List[float]] = {}
189|        import threading
190|        self._lock = threading.Lock()
191|
192|    def _cleanup(self, now: float) -> None:
193|        for ip in list(self._clients.keys()):
194|            self._clients[ip] = [t for t in self._clients[ip] if now - t
   |     < self.window_sec]
195|            if not self._clients[ip]:
196|                del self._clients[ip]
197|
198|    def is_allowed(self, client_ip: str) -> bool:
199|        import time
200|        now = time.time()
201|        with self._lock:
202|            self._cleanup(now)
203|            if client_ip not in self._clients:
204|                self._clients[client_ip] = []
205|            if len(self._clients[client_ip]) >= self.max_requests:
206|                return False
207|            self._clients[client_ip].append(now)
208|            return True
209|
210|    def remaining(self, client_ip: str) -> int:
211|        import time
212|        now = time.time()
213|        with self._lock:
214|            self._cleanup(now)
215|            return max(0, self.max_requests - len(self._clients.get(clie
   |    nt_ip, [])))

  1|import matplotlib
  2|matplotlib.use('Agg')
  3|
  4|import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  5|import seaborn as sns
  6|import pandas as pd
  7|from typing import Optional, Sequence, Tuple
  8|
  9|PALETTE = ['#2f6feb', '#e8833a', '#3fa66a', '#b4545b', '#7a6bd6', '#0f8b
   |    8d', '#8a8f98']
 10|TEMP_REFERENCE_C = 35.0
 11|PUE_REFERENCE = 1.6
 12|
 13|
 14|def _render(fig: plt.Figure, save_path: Optional[str]) -> plt.Figure:
 15|    """Save and close. Closing matters inside a long-running web process
   |    ."""
 16|    if save_path:
 17|        fig.savefig(save_path, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
 18|    plt.close(fig)
 19|    return fig
 20|
 21|
 22|def _apply_style(style: str) -> None:
 23|    try:
 24|        plt.style.use(style)
 25|    except Exception:
 26|        plt.style.use('default')
 27|
 28|
 29|def plot_energy_comparison(
 30|    df: pd.DataFrame,
 31|    save_path: Optional[str] = None,
 32|    figsize: Tuple[int, int] = (12, 6),
 33|    style: str = 'seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid'
 34|) -> plt.Figure:
 35|    _apply_style(style)
 36|    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize)
 37|    sns.lineplot(data=df, x='time', y='total_energy', hue='algorithm', m
   |    arker='o', ax=ax)
 38|    ax.set_title('Energy Consumption Comparison')
 39|    ax.set_xlabel('Time (hours)')
 40|    ax.set_ylabel('Total Energy (W)')
 41|    ax.legend(title='Algorithm')
 42|    ax.grid(True)
 43|    return _render(fig, save_path)
 44|
 45|
 46|def plot_temperature_trend(
 47|    df: pd.DataFrame,
 48|    save_path: Optional[str] = None,
 49|    figsize: Tuple[int, int] = (12, 6),
 50|    style: str = 'seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid',
 51|    reference_c: Optional[float] = TEMP_REFERENCE_C,
 52|    peak: bool = False
 53|) -> plt.Figure:
 54|    _apply_style(style)
 55|    y = 'peak_temperature' if peak and 'peak_temperature' in df.columns 
   |    else 'avg_temperature'
 56|    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize)
 57|    sns.lineplot(data=df, x='time', y=y, hue='algorithm', marker='s', ax
   |    =ax)
 58|    if 'ambient_temperature' in df.columns:
 59|        ambient = df.groupby('time')['ambient_temperature'].first().rese
   |    t_index()
 60|        ax.plot(ambient['time'], ambient['ambient_temperature'],
 61|                color='#6b7280', linestyle=':', linewidth=1.4, label='环
   |    境温度')
 62|    if reference_c is not None:
 63|        ax.axhline(y=reference_c, color='red', linestyle='--',
 64|                   label=f'参考线 ({reference_c:g}°C)')
 65|    ax.set_title('Temperature Trend Comparison')
 66|    ax.set_xlabel('Time (hours)')
 67|    ax.set_ylabel('Temperature (°C)')
 68|    ax.legend()
 69|    ax.grid(True)
 70|    return _render(fig, save_path)
 71|

111|def plot_cooling_power(
112|    df: pd.DataFrame,
113|    save_path: Optional[str] = None,
114|    figsize: Tuple[int, int] = (12, 6),
115|    style: str = 'seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid'

127|
128|def plot_service_rate(
129|    df: pd.DataFrame,
130|    save_path: Optional[str] = None,
131|    figsize: Tuple[int, int] = (12, 6),
132|    style: str = 'seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid'
133|) -> plt.Figure:
134|    _apply_style(style)
135|    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize)
136|    hourly = df.copy()
137|    hourly['service_rate'] = hourly['assigned'] / (hourly['assigned'] + 
   |    hourly['dropped']).replace(0, pd.NA)
138|    sns.lineplot(data=hourly, x='time', y='service_rate', hue='algorithm
   |    ', marker='o', ax=ax)
139|    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
140|    ax.set_title('Task Service Rate')
141|    ax.set_xlabel('Time (hours)')
142|    ax.set_ylabel('Service Rate')
143|    ax.legend(title='Algorithm')
144|    ax.grid(True)
145|    return _render(fig, save_path)
146|
147|
148|def plot_summary_stats(
149|    stats: dict,
150|    save_path: Optional[str] = None,
151|    figsize: Tuple[int, int] = (14, 10),
152|    style: str = 'seaborn-v0_8-darkgrid'
153|) -> plt.Figure:
154|    _apply_style(style)
155|    algorithms = list(stats.keys())
156|    metrics = ['avg_energy', 'max_temp', 'avg_pue', 'service_rate']
157|    metric_names = ['Avg Energy (W)', 'Peak Temp (°C)', 'Avg PUE', 'Serv
   |    ice Rate']
158|    colors: Sequence[str] = [PALETTE[i % len(PALETTE)] for i in range(le
   |    n(algorithms))]
159|
160|    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=figsize)
161|
162|    for i, (metric, name) in enumerate(zip(metrics, metric_names)):
163|        row, col = i // 2, i % 2
164|        values = [stats[algo].get(metric) or 0 for algo in algorithms]
165|        axes[row, col].bar(algorithms, values, color=colors)
166|        axes[row, col].set_title(name)
167|        axes[row, col].grid(True)
168|
169|    plt.tight_layout()
170|    return _render(fig, save_path)

================================================================================
81. tools/etl/build_catalog.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Build data/catalog.db from offline snapshots in data/raw.
  2|
  3|Each dataset is a CSV plus a sidecar JSON describing where it came from:
  4|
  5|    data/raw/ambient/<name>.csv
  6|    data/raw/ambient/<name>.json
  7|
  8|The JSON sidecar carries publisher / url / license / retrieved_at, which
   |     is what
  9|makes the numbers in a report traceable. A dataset without a sidecar is 
   |    refused,
 10|so "where did this come from" can never be answered with a shrug.
 11|
 12|Usage:
 13|    python tools/etl/build_catalog.py --list
 14|    python tools/etl/build_catalog.py --db data/catalog.db
 15|    python tools/etl/build_catalog.py --kind trace --scale-keep 0.35 --s
   |    la-slack 1.5
 16|"""
 17|
 18|import argparse
 19|import csv
 20|import hashlib
 21|import io
 22|import json
 23|import os
 24|import sqlite3
 25|import sys
 26|import time
 27|from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
 28|
 29|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 30|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
 31|RAW_ROOT = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", "raw")
 32|
 33|
 34|def set_raw_root(path):
 35|    global RAW_ROOT
 36|    RAW_ROOT = os.path.abspath(path)
 37|    return RAW_ROOT
 38|SCHEMA_PATH = os.path.join(HERE, "schema.sql")
 39|
 40|KINDS = ("ambient", "trace", "standards", "emission", "server_type", "sc
   |    hools", "climate")
 41|
 42|REQUIRED_COLUMNS = {
 43|    "ambient": ("ts", "temp_c"),
 44|    "trace": ("load_units",),
 45|    "standards": ("standard_id", "pue_limit"),
 46|    "emission": ("region", "year", "kg_co2_per_kwh"),
 47|    "server_type": ("server_type",),
 48|    "schools": ("school_name", "city"),
 49|    "climate": ("city", "date", "tmax_c", "tmin_c", "tmean_c"),
 50|}
 51|
 52|
 53|def sha256_of(path: str) -> str:
 54|    digest = hashlib.sha256()
 55|    with open(path, "rb") as f:
 56|        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b""):
 57|            digest.update(chunk)
 58|    return digest.hexdigest()
 59|
 60|
 61|def read_csv(path: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
 62|    with io.open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
 63|        return list(csv.DictReader(f))
 64|
 65|
 66|def read_sidecar(csv_path: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
 67|    meta_path = os.path.splitext(csv_path)[0] + ".json"
 68|    if not os.path.exists(meta_path):
 69|        return None
 70|    with io.open(meta_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 71|        return json.load(f)
 72|
 73|
 74|def dataset_files(kind: str) -> List[str]:
 75|    folder = os.path.join(RAW_ROOT, kind)
 76|    if not os.path.isdir(folder):
 77|        return []
 78|    return sorted(
 79|        os.path.join(folder, name)
 80|        for name in os.listdir(folder)
 81|        if name.lower().endswith(".csv")
 82|    )
 83|
 84|
 85|def list_datasets() -> None:
 86|    print("数据目录快照 (data/raw):")
 87|    found = False
 88|    for kind in KINDS:
 89|        for path in dataset_files(kind):
 90|            found = True
 91|            meta = read_sidecar(path)
 92|            marker = "有出处" if meta else "缺少 sidecar JSON (不会导入)
   |    "
 93|            print("  [%s] %s  <- %s" % (kind, os.path.basename(path), ma
   |    rker))
 94|    if not found:
 95|        print("  (空) 请按 tools/etl/README.md 的格式放入 CSV 与 sidecar
   |     JSON")
 96|
 97|
 98|def connect(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:
 99|    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(db_path), exist_ok=True)
100|    with io.open(SCHEMA_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
101|        schema = f.read()
102|    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
103|    conn.executescript(schema)
104|    conn.commit()
105|    return conn
106|
107|
108|def register_source(conn: sqlite3.Connection, source_id: str, kind: str,
109|                    csv_path: str, meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
110|    conn.execute(
111|        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta_data_source
112|           (source_id, name, publisher, url, license, retrieved_at, sha2
   |    56, etl_script, note)
113|           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
114|        (
115|            source_id,
116|            meta.get("name") or os.path.basename(csv_path),
117|            meta.get("publisher", ""),
118|            meta.get("url", ""),
119|            meta.get("license", ""),
120|            meta.get("retrieved_at", ""),
121|            sha256_of(csv_path),
122|            "tools/etl/build_catalog.py",
123|            meta.get("note", ""),
124|        ),
125|    )
126|
127|
128|def register_version(conn: sqlite3.Connection, source_id: str, kind: str
   |    ,
129|                     table_name: str, row_count: int, csv_path: str,
130|                     hash_key: str, span: Tuple[str, str]) -> str:
131|    version_id = "%s:%s:%s" % (kind, source_id, hash_key[:12])
132|    conn.execute(
133|        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta_dataset_version
134|           (version_id, source_id, table_name, row_count, min_ts, max_ts
   |    , hash, created_at)
135|           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
136|        (version_id, source_id, table_name, row_count, span[0], span[1],
137|         hash_key, time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")),
138|    )
139|    return version_id
140|
141|
142|def _num(value: Any, cast=float, default=None):
143|    try:
144|        return cast(value)
145|    except (TypeError, ValueError):
146|        return default
147|
148|
149|def import_ambient(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id) -> int:
150|    rows = read_csv(csv_path)
151|    site_id = str(meta.get("site_id") or "default")
152|    payload = []
153|    timestamps = []
154|    for row in rows:
155|        ts = str(row.get("ts") or row.get("time") or "")
156|        temp = _num(row.get("temp_c") if row.get("temp_c") is not None e
   |    lse row.get("temperature"))
157|        if ts == "" or temp is None:
158|            continue
159|        timestamps.append(ts)
160|        payload.append((site_id, ts, temp,
161|                        _num(row.get("humidity")), _num(row.get("wind_ms
   |    ")),
162|                        source_id, ""))
163|    if not payload:
164|        return 0
165|    conn.execute(
166|        "DELETE FROM dim_ambient_hourly WHERE site_id = ? AND source_id 
   |    = ?",
167|        (site_id, source_id),
168|    )
169|    version_id = register_version(conn, source_id, "ambient", "dim_ambie
   |    nt_hourly",
170|                                 len(payload), csv_path, sha256_of(csv_p
   |    ath),
171|                                 (min(timestamps), max(timestamps)))
172|    conn.executemany(
173|        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dim_ambient_hourly
174|           (site_id, ts, temp_c, humidity, wind_ms, source_id, version_i
   |    d)
175|           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
176|        [(p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3], p[4], p[5], version_id) for p in paylo
   |    ad],
177|    )
178|    return len(payload)
179|
180|
181|def _hour_of(row: Dict[str, Any], base_ts: Optional[float]) -> Optional[
   |    int]:
182|    if row.get("hour") not in (None, ""):
183|        return int(float(row["hour"]))
184|    if row.get("arrival_hour") not in (None, ""):
185|        return int(float(row["arrival_hour"]))
186|    if row.get("ts") not in (None, "") and base_ts is not None:
187|        try:
188|            return int((float(row["ts"]) - base_ts) // 3600)
189|        except (TypeError, ValueError):
190|            return None
191|    return None
192|
193|
194|def import_trace(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id, scale_keep: float,
195|                 sla_slack: float, seed: int) -> int:
196|    import random
197|
198|    rows = read_csv(csv_path)
199|    if not rows:
200|        return 0
201|
202|    base_ts = None
203|    if "ts" in rows[0] and "hour" not in rows[0] and "arrival_hour" not 
   |    in rows[0]:
204|        values = [_num(r.get("ts")) for r in rows]
205|        values = [v for v in values if v is not None]
206|        base_ts = min(values) if values else None
207|
208|    if scale_keep < 1.0:
209|        rng = random.Random(seed)
210|        rows = [row for row in rows if rng.random() < scale_keep]
211|
212|    trace_id = str(meta.get("trace_id") or os.path.splitext(os.path.base
   |    name(csv_path))[0])
213|    payload = []
214|    seq = 0
215|    for row in rows:
216|        hour = _hour_of(row, base_ts)
217|        load = _num(row.get("load_units") or row.get("load"), int)
218|        if hour is None or load is None or hour < 0:
219|            continue
220|        duration = _num(row.get("duration_h") or row.get("duration"), in
   |    t, 1) or 1
221|        offset = _num(row.get("deadline_offset_h"), int)
222|        if offset is None:
223|            offset = max(duration, int(round(duration * sla_slack)), 1)
224|        payload.append((
225|            trace_id, seq, hour, int(load),
226|            _num(row.get("priority"), int, 3),
227|            str(row.get("workload_class") or "cpu_bound"),
228|            int(duration), int(offset), source_id,
229|        ))
230|        seq += 1
231|
232|    if not payload:
233|        return 0
234|
235|    conn.execute("DELETE FROM fact_task_arrivals WHERE trace_id = ?", (t
   |    race_id,))
236|    hours = [p[2] for p in payload]
237|    register_version(conn, source_id, "trace", "fact_task_arrivals", len
   |    (payload),
238|                     csv_path, sha256_of(csv_path), (str(min(hours)), st
   |    r(max(hours))))
239|    conn.executemany(
240|        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO fact_task_arrivals
241|           (trace_id, seq, hour, load_units, priority, workload_class,
242|            duration_h, deadline_offset_h, source_id)
243|           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
244|        payload,
245|    )
246|    return len(payload)
247|

249|def import_standards(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id) -> int:

255|            continue
256|        payload.append((str(row.get("standard_id") or meta.get("name") o
   |    r ""),
257|                        str(row.get("grade") or ""), limit,
258|                        str(row.get("applies_to") or ""), source_id))
259|    if payload:
260|        conn.execute("DELETE FROM dim_pue_benchmark WHERE source_id = ?"
   |    , (source_id,))
261|        register_version(conn, source_id, "standards", "dim_pue_benchmar
   |    k", len(payload),
262|                         csv_path, sha256_of(csv_path), ("", ""))
263|        conn.executemany(
264|            """INSERT INTO dim_pue_benchmark
265|               (standard_id, grade, pue_limit, applies_to, source_id)
266|               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
267|            payload,
268|        )
269|    return len(payload)

272|def import_emission(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id) -> int:

282|    if payload:

   |    ?", (source_id,))
284|        register_version(conn, source_id, "emission", "dim_emission_fact
   |    or", len(payload),
285|                         csv_path, sha256_of(csv_path), ("", ""))
286|        conn.executemany(
287|            """INSERT INTO dim_emission_factor
288|               (region, year, kg_co2_per_kwh, source_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?
   |    , ?)""",
289|            payload,
290|        )

   |    id))

   |    e", len(payload),

   |    ment") or ""),

   |    ce_id,))

   |    ayload),

343|def import_climate(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id) -> int:
344|    rows = read_csv(csv_path)
345|    payload = []
346|    dates = []
347|    for row in rows:
348|        city = str(row.get("city") or "").strip()
349|        date = str(row.get("date") or "").strip()
350|        tmax = _num(row.get("tmax_c"))
351|        tmin = _num(row.get("tmin_c"))
352|        tmean = _num(row.get("tmean_c"))
353|        if not city or not date or tmax is None or tmin is None or tmean
   |     is None:
354|            continue
355|        dates.append(date)
356|        payload.append((city, date, tmax, tmin, tmean, source_id))
357|    if payload:
358|        conn.execute("DELETE FROM dim_city_daily WHERE source_id = ?", (
   |    source_id,))
359|        register_version(conn, source_id, "climate", "dim_city_daily", l
   |    en(payload),
360|                         csv_path, sha256_of(csv_path),
361|                         (min(dates), max(dates)))
362|        conn.executemany(
363|            """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dim_city_daily
364|               (city, date, tmax_c, tmin_c, tmean_c, source_id)
365|               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
366|            payload,
367|        )
368|    return len(payload)

380|
381|def load_kind(conn, kind: str, args) -> int:
382|    total = 0
383|    for csv_path in dataset_files(kind):
384|        meta = read_sidecar(csv_path)
385|        basename = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(csv_path))[0]
386|        if meta is None:
387|            print("  跳过 %s: 缺少出处 sidecar (%s.json)" % (basename, b
   |    asename))
388|            continue
389|        missing = [c for c in REQUIRED_COLUMNS.get(kind, ()) if c not in
   |     (read_csv(csv_path)[0] if read_csv(csv_path) else {})]
390|        if missing:
391|            print("  跳过 %s: 缺少列 %s" % (basename, ", ".join(missing)
   |    ))
392|            continue
393|        source_id = str(meta.get("source_id") or "%s:%s" % (kind, basena
   |    me))
394|        register_source(conn, source_id, kind, csv_path, meta)
395|        if kind == "trace":
396|            count = import_trace(conn, csv_path, meta, source_id,
397|                                 args.scale_keep, args.sla_slack, args.s
   |    eed)
398|        else:
399|            count = IMPORTERS[kind](conn, csv_path, meta, source_id)
400|        conn.commit()
401|        print("  导入 [%s] %s -> %d 行 (source_id=%s)" % (kind, basename
   |    , count, source_id))
402|        total += count
403|    return total
404|
405|
406|def main() -> int:
407|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build the provenance-t
   |    agged data catalog")
408|    parser.add_argument("--db", default=os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data
   |    ", "catalog.db"))
409|    parser.add_argument("--kind", default=None, choices=list(KINDS),
410|                        help="只导入某一类数据，默认全部")
411|    parser.add_argument("--scale-keep", type=float, default=1.0,
412|                        help="trace 降采样保留比例 (0, 1]")
413|    parser.add_argument("--sla-slack", type=float, default=1.5,
414|                        help="缺失 deadline 时使用的 SLA 松弛系数")
415|    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42, help="降采样随机
   |    种子")
416|    parser.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="只列出可用
   |    快照")
417|    parser.add_argument("--raw-root", default=RAW_ROOT,
418|                        help="快照根目录，默认 data/raw")
419|    args = parser.parse_args()
420|    set_raw_root(args.raw_root)
421|
422|    if args.list:
423|        list_datasets()
424|        return 0
425|
426|    conn = connect(args.db)
427|    kinds = [args.kind] if args.kind else list(KINDS)
428|    print("构建数据目录: %s" % args.db)
429|    total = 0
430|    for kind in kinds:
431|        total += load_kind(conn, kind, args)
432|    conn.commit()
433|
434|    tables = [row[0] for row in conn.execute(
435|        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name
   |    ")]
436|    print("完成: 共写入 %d 行, 表: %s" % (total, ", ".join(tables)))
437|    if total == 0:
438|        print("\n提示: data/raw 下暂无可用快照。数据获取方式见 tools/etl/
   |    README.md。")
439|    conn.close()
440|    return 0
441|
442|
443|if __name__ == "__main__":
444|    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================
82. tools/etl/import_schools.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Import the official MOE school list and per-city climate.
  2|
  3|Source: Ministry of Education, "全国高等学校名单" (2025), published as .
   |    xls.
  4|The list gives school name, supervising authority, city and level. Coord
   |    inates
  5|are resolved with Open-Meteo geocoding, then daily 2025 temperature is f
   |    etched
  6|from the ERA5 archive. Everything is cached under data/raw so re-runs ar
   |    e free.
  7|
  8|Usage:
  9|    python tools/etl/import_schools.py --xls <file.xls>
 10|    python tools/etl/import_schools.py --xls <file.xls> --max-cities 10
 11|"""
 12|
 13|import argparse
 14|import csv
 15|import io
 16|import json
 17|import os
 18|import sys
 19|import time
 20|import urllib.parse
 21|import urllib.request
 22|
 23|import xlrd
 24|
 25|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 26|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
 27|RAW = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", "raw")
 28|SCHOOL_DIR = os.path.join(RAW, "schools")
 29|CLIMATE_DIR = os.path.join(RAW, "climate")
 30|CACHE_COORDS = os.path.join(SCHOOL_DIR, "city_coords.json")
 31|
 32|UA = "codex-campus-dc-import/1.0 (competition demo)"
 33|DELAY = 0.35
 34|
 35|
 36|def http_json(url, retries=4):
 37|    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA})
 38|    for attempt in range(retries):
 39|        try:
 40|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=40) as resp:
 41|                return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
 42|        except Exception as exc:
 43|            if attempt == retries - 1:
 44|                raise
 45|            time.sleep(2 * (attempt + 1))
 46|    return None
 47|
 48|
 49|def parse_schools(xls_path):
 50|    wb = xlrd.open_workbook(xls_path)
 51|    schools = []
 52|    for sheet_index in range(wb.nsheets):
 53|        sh = wb.sheet_by_index(sheet_index)
 54|        province = ""
 55|        for r in range(sh.nrows):
 56|            cells = [str(sh.cell_value(r, c)).strip() for c in range(sh.
   |    ncols)]
 57|            if not any(cells):
 58|                continue
 59|            joined = " ".join(cells)
 60|            if "（" in joined and "所）" in joined and not cells[1]:
 61|                province = joined.split("（")[0].strip()
 62|                continue
 63|            if len(cells) < 4:
 64|                continue
 65|            name = cells[1]
 66|            if not name or name in ("学校名称",):
 67|                continue
 68|            city = ""
 69|            level = ""
 70|            dept = cells[3] if len(cells) > 3 else ""
 71|            if sh.ncols >= 7:
 72|                city = cells[4]
 73|                level = cells[5]
 74|            else:
 75|                city = province
 76|                level = "成人高校"
 77|            if city and name and not name.startswith("附件"):
 78|                schools.append({
 79|                    "school_name": name,
 80|                    "province": province,
 81|                    "city": city,
 82|                    "level": level or "未知",
 83|                    "department": dept,
 84|                    "sheet": sh.name,
 85|                })
 86|    return schools
 87|
 88|
 89|def load_cache():
 90|    if os.path.exists(CACHE_COORDS):
 91|        with io.open(CACHE_COORDS, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 92|            return json.load(f)
 93|    return {}
 94|
 95|
 96|def save_cache(cache):
 97|    with io.open(CACHE_COORDS, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 98|        json.dump(cache, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
 99|
100|
101|def geocode(name, cache):
102|    """Resolve a Chinese city name with Nominatim (administrative level 
   |    only)."""
103|    if name in cache:
104|        return cache[name]
105|    candidates = [name]
106|    for suffix in ("市", "地区", "盟", "自治州", "自治县"):
107|        if name.endswith(suffix) and len(name) > len(suffix):
108|            candidates.append(name[: -len(suffix)])
109|    found = None
110|    for candidate in candidates:
111|        url = ("https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q="
112|               + urllib.parse.quote(candidate)
113|               + "&format=jsonv2&limit=3&countrycodes=cn&featuretype=cit
   |    y")
114|        try:
115|            payload = http_json(url)
116|        except Exception:
117|            payload = None
118|        if isinstance(payload, list) and payload:
119|            item = payload[0]
120|            found = {
121|                "name": item.get("display_name", candidate).split(",")[0
   |    ],
122|                "latitude": float(item.get("lat")),
123|                "longitude": float(item.get("lon")),
124|                "admin1": item.get("display_name", "").split(",")[1].str
   |    ip()
125|                          if "," in item.get("display_name", "") else ""
   |    ,
126|                "country": "中国",
127|            }
128|            break
129|        time.sleep(1.1)
130|    cache[name] = found
131|    time.sleep(1.1)
132|    return found
133|
134|
135|def fetch_climate(city, coords):
136|    path = os.path.join(CLIMATE_DIR, city + ".json")
137|    if os.path.exists(path):
138|        with io.open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
139|            return json.load(f)
140|    url = ("https://archive-api.open-meteo.com/v1/archive?latitude={lat}
   |    &longitude={lon}"
141|           "&start_date=2025-01-01&end_date=2025-12-31"
142|           "&daily=temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,temperature_2m_
   |    mean"
143|           "&timezone=Asia%2FShanghai").format(lat=coords["latitude"], l
   |    on=coords["longitude"])
144|    payload = http_json(url)
145|    payload["_url"] = url
146|    with io.open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
147|        json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False)
148|    time.sleep(DELAY)
149|    return payload
150|
151|
152|def main():
153|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
154|    parser.add_argument("--xls", required=True)
155|    parser.add_argument("--max-cities", type=int, default=0)
156|    args = parser.parse_args()
157|
158|    os.makedirs(SCHOOL_DIR, exist_ok=True)
159|    os.makedirs(CLIMATE_DIR, exist_ok=True)
160|
161|    schools = parse_schools(args.xls)
162|    print("schools:", len(schools))
163|    cities = sorted({s["city"] for s in schools if s["city"]})
164|    if args.max_cities:
165|        cities = cities[: args.max_cities]
166|    print("cities:", len(cities))
167|
168|    cache = load_cache()
169|    resolved = {}
170|    for index, city in enumerate(cities, 1):
171|        coords = geocode(city, cache)
172|        if coords:
173|            resolved[city] = coords
174|        if index % 25 == 0:
175|            save_cache(cache)
176|            print("  geocoded %d/%d" % (index, len(cities)), flush=True)
177|    save_cache(cache)
178|    print("geocoded:", len(resolved), "/", len(cities))
179|
180|    ok = 0
181|    for index, city in enumerate(sorted(resolved), 1):
182|        try:
183|            fetch_climate(city, resolved[city])
184|            ok += 1
185|        except Exception as exc:
186|            print("  climate failed for %s: %s" % (city, exc))
187|        if index % 25 == 0:
188|            print("  climate %d/%d" % (index, len(resolved)), flush=True
   |    )
189|    print("climate fetched:", ok, "/", len(resolved))
190|
191|    with io.open(os.path.join(SCHOOL_DIR, "schools.csv"), "w", encoding=
   |    "utf-8", newline="") as f:
192|        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=["school_name", "province"
   |    , "city", "level", "department"])
193|        writer.writeheader()
194|        for s in schools:
195|            writer.writerow({k: s[k] for k in writer.fieldnames})
196|
197|    rows = []
198|    for city in sorted(resolved):
199|        path = os.path.join(CLIMATE_DIR, city + ".json")
200|        if not os.path.exists(path):
201|            continue
202|        with io.open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
203|            data = json.load(f)
204|        daily = data.get("daily") or {}
205|        for date, tmax, tmin, tmean in zip(
206|                daily.get("time", []),
207|                daily.get("temperature_2m_max", []),
208|                daily.get("temperature_2m_min", []),
209|                daily.get("temperature_2m_mean", [])):
210|            if tmax is None or tmin is None or tmean is None:
211|                continue
212|            rows.append((city, date, tmax, tmin, tmean))
213|    with io.open(os.path.join(CLIMATE_DIR, "city_daily.csv"), "w", encod
   |    ing="utf-8", newline="") as f:
214|        f.write("city,date,tmax_c,tmin_c,tmean_c\n")
215|        for r in rows:
216|            f.write("%s,%s,%.1f,%.1f,%.1f\n" % r)
217|    print("daily climate rows:", len(rows))
218|
219|    meta = {
220|        "source_id": "moe-school-list-2025",
221|        "name": "全国高等学校名单（2025）",
222|        "publisher": "中华人民共和国教育部",
223|        "url": "https://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_xxgk/s5743/s5744/A03/202506/t
   |    20250627_1195683.html",
224|        "license": "政府公开信息",
225|        "retrieved_at": "2026-10-17",
226|        "note": "含普通高校与成人高校；所在地为地级市；经纬度由 Nominatim（
   |    OpenStreetMap，ODbL）行政区级解析",
227|    }
228|    with io.open(os.path.join(SCHOOL_DIR, "schools.json"), "w", encoding
   |    ="utf-8") as f:
229|        json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
230|
231|    climate_meta = {
232|        "source_id": "open-meteo-era5-cities-2025",
233|        "name": "中国各城市 2025 年逐日气温（ERA5 再分析）",
234|        "publisher": "Open-Meteo / ECMWF ERA5",
235|        "url": "https://archive-api.open-meteo.com/v1/archive",
236|        "license": "CC BY 4.0（Open-Meteo）；ERA5 © ECMWF",
237|        "retrieved_at": "2026-10-17",
238|        "note": "逐日最高/最低/平均气温；仿真时按标准日变化曲线展开为逐时
   |    值",
239|    }
240|    with io.open(os.path.join(CLIMATE_DIR, "city_daily.json"), "w", enco
   |    ding="utf-8") as f:
241|        json.dump(climate_meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
242|
243|    print("done")
244|
245|
246|if __name__ == "__main__":
247|    main()

83. tools/test_ai_chat.py
================================================================================
  1|"""End-to-end checks for the floating assistant's chat endpoint.
  2|
  3|Run with the web service up:
  4|    python tools/test_ai_chat.py
  5|"""
  6|
  7|import io
  8|import json
  9|import os
 10|import sys
 11|import time
 12|from typing import Any, Dict, List
 13|
 14|import requests
 15|
 16|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "
   |    .."))
 17|if PROJECT_ROOT not in sys.path:
 18|    sys.path.insert(0, PROJECT_ROOT)
 19|
 20|if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
 21|    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
 22|
 23|BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:5000"
 24|INTERNAL_FIELDS = ("max_temp", "avg_pue", "energy_per_served_load", "ser
   |    vice_rate")
 25|CODE_IDS = ("round_robin", "damo_ets", "greedy", "genetic", "nsga2", "mo
   |    ead")
 26|
 27|RESULTS: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
 28|TRANSCRIPT: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
 29|
 30|
 31|def check(name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "") -> None:
 32|    RESULTS.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
 33|
 34|
 35|def chat(messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 36|    started = time.time()
 37|    response = requests.post(BASE + "/api/v1/ai/chat",
 38|                             json={"messages": messages}, timeout=180)
 39|    response.raise_for_status()
 40|    payload = response.json()
 41|    payload["_elapsed"] = round(time.time() - started, 2)
 42|    return payload
 43|
 44|
 45|def hygiene(reply: str) -> None:
 46|    lowered = reply.lower()
 47|    found_fields = [w for w in INTERNAL_FIELDS if w in lowered]
 48|    found_ids = [w for w in CODE_IDS if w in lowered]
 49|    check("无内部字段名", not found_fields, ", ".join(found_fields))
 50|    check("无代码标识符", not found_ids, ", ".join(found_ids))
 51|
 52|
 53|def main() -> int:
 54|    print("=" * 64)
 55|    print("AI 助手对话测试")
 56|    print("=" * 64)
 57|

 59|    info = status.get("status", {})
 60|    print("\n[0] 服务状态")
 61|    print("  available=%s model=%s key_source=%s" % (
 62|        info.get("available"), info.get("model"), info.get("key_source")
   |    ))
 63|    check("模型可用", bool(info.get("available")))
 64|    check("Key 来自文件", info.get("key_source") == "file", str(info.get
   |    ("key_source")))
 65|
 66|    metrics = requests.get(BASE + "/api/v1/metrics", timeout=30).json()
 67|    stats = metrics.get("data", {})
 68|    ranking = metrics.get("ranking", {})
 69|    best = ranking.get("best")
 70|    best_metrics = stats.get(best, {}) if best else {}
 71|    print("\n[1] 基准数据（/api/v1/metrics）")
 72|    for algo, m in stats.items():
 73|        print("  %-11s rate=%.4f W/load=%-7s peak=%-6s pue=%-7s co2=%s" 
   |    % (

   |    erved_load"),

 76|    print("  best=%s" % best)

 80|    cases = [
 81|        ("排名/最省电", "当前排名如何？哪个算法最省电？",
 82|         [best.replace("_", "-").upper()] if best else []),
 83|        ("温度/PUE", "各算法的峰值温度和 PUE 是多少？", []),
 84|        ("服务率", "任务服务率怎么样？有没有任务被丢弃？", []),
 85|        ("碳排", "当前碳排是多少？", []),
 86|        ("数据来源", "这些数据是哪来的？可靠吗？", ["ERA5", "阿里云", "生
   |    态环境部"]),
 87|        ("使用方法", "怎么运行一次仿真？", []),
 88|        ("数据更新", "当前数据更新了吗？", [site] if site else []),
 89|        ("最佳时段", "什么时候性价比最高？", ["小时"]),
 90|        ("低温配置", "温度低于60度怎么配置？", ["60"]),
 91|        ("配置试算", "如果服务器增加到24台会怎样？", ["24"]),
 92|    ]
 93|
 94|    for label, question, must_contain in cases:
 95|        payload = chat([{"role": "user", "content": question}])
 96|        reply = payload.get("reply", "")
 97|        print("\n  [%s] %s" % (label, question))
 98|        print("    source=%s  %.2fs" % (payload.get("source"), payload.g
   |    et("_elapsed", 0)))
 99|        print("    " + reply.replace("\n", "\n    "))
100|        TRANSCRIPT.append({
101|            "label": label, "question": question, "reply": reply,
102|            "source": payload.get("source"), "elapsed": payload.get("_el
   |    apsed"),
103|        })
104|        check("%s: 有回复" % label, bool(reply.strip()), "empty")
105|        check("%s: 走大模型" % label, payload.get("source") == "deepseek
   |    ",
106|              str(payload.get("source")))
107|        check("%s: 返回数据快照" % label,
108|              bool((payload.get("snapshot") or {}).get("generated_at")),
109|              str(payload.get("snapshot")))
110|        check("%s: 无内部字段名" % label,
111|              not any(word in reply.lower() for word in INTERNAL_FIELDS)
   |    ,
112|              ", ".join(word for word in INTERNAL_FIELDS if word in repl
   |    y.lower()))
113|        if label == "配置试算":
114|            run = payload.get("suggested_run") or {}
115|            check("配置试算: 返回建议配置",
116|                  (run.get("params") or {}).get("num_servers") == 24,
117|                  str(run))
118|        for token in must_contain:
119|            if token:
120|                check("%s: 提到 %s" % (label, token), token.lower() in r
   |    eply.lower(), token)
121|
122|    print("\n[3] 数值准确性")
123|    payload = chat([{"role": "user", "content": "排名第一的算法，单位负载
   |    能耗、峰值温度、PUE 各是多少？"}])
124|    reply = payload.get("reply", "")
125|    print("    " + reply.replace("\n", "\n    "))
126|    if best_metrics:
127|        intensity = float(best_metrics.get("energy_per_served_load") or 
   |    0)
128|        peak = float(best_metrics.get("max_temp") or 0)
129|        pue = float(best_metrics.get("avg_pue") or 0)
130|        check("能耗数值一致", (str(round(intensity, 2)) in reply) or (st
   |    r(round(intensity, 1)) in reply),
131|              "expect %.4f" % intensity)
132|        check("峰值温度一致", str(round(peak, 1)) in reply or str(int(pe
   |    ak)) in reply,
133|              "expect %.2f" % peak)
134|        check("PUE 一致", str(round(pue, 3)) in reply, "expect %.4f" % p
   |    ue)
135|
136|    print("\n[4] 多轮上下文")
137|    history = [{"role": "user", "content": "当前排名第一的是谁？"}]
138|    first = chat(history)
139|    print("  Q1: 当前排名第一的是谁？")
140|    print("    " + first.get("reply", "").replace("\n", "\n    "))
141|    history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": first.get("reply", "
   |    ")})
142|    history.append({"role": "user", "content": "它的峰值温度是多少？"})
143|    second = chat(history)
144|    print("  Q2: 它的峰值温度是多少？")
145|    print("    " + second.get("reply", "").replace("\n", "\n    "))
146|    check("多轮指代正确", "DAMO" in second.get("reply", "").upper(),
147|          second.get("reply", "")[:80])
148|
149|    print("\n[5] 格式规范")
150|    for payload in (first, second):
151|        hygiene(payload.get("reply", ""))
152|
153|    print("\n[6] 本地兜底（模型不可用时）")
154|    try:
155|        from src.services.ai_service import rule_chat
156|        fallback = rule_chat("当前排名如何？", {
157|            "stats": stats,
158|            "ranking": ranking,
159|            "data_source": metrics.get("data_source", {}),
160|        })
161|        print("    " + fallback.replace("\n", "\n    "))
162|        check("兜底有回复", bool(fallback.strip()))
163|        check("兜底无内部字段名",
164|              not any(w in fallback.lower() for w in INTERNAL_FIELDS))
165|    except Exception as exc:
166|        check("兜底可调用", False, str(exc))
167|
168|    passed = sum(1 for item in RESULTS if item["ok"])
169|    report_path = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "AI对话测试报告.md")
170|    lines = ["# AI 助手对话测试报告", "",
171|             "- 模型：%s" % info.get("model"),
172|             "- Key 来源：%s" % info.get("key_source"),
173|             "- 结果：**%d / %d 项通过**" % (passed, len(RESULTS)), ""]
174|    lines.append("## 基准数据")
175|    lines.append("")
176|    lines.append("| 算法 | 服务率 | 单位负载能耗 | 峰值温度 | PUE |")
177|    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
178|    for algo, m in stats.items():
179|        lines.append("| %s | %.4f | %s | %s | %.4f |" % (
180|            algo, float(m.get("service_rate") or 0), m.get("energy_per_s
   |    erved_load"),
181|            m.get("max_temp"), float(m.get("avg_pue") or 0)))
182|    lines.append("")
183|    lines.append("## 对话记录")
184|    for item in TRANSCRIPT:
185|        lines.append("")
186|        lines.append("### %s" % item["label"])
187|        lines.append("")
188|        lines.append("**问：** %s" % item["question"])
189|        lines.append("")
190|        lines.append("**答（%s，%.2fs）：**" % (item["source"], item["elapse
   |    d"] or 0))
191|        lines.append("")
192|        lines.append(item["reply"].replace("\n", "\n\n"))
193|    lines.append("")
194|    lines.append("## 检查项")
195|    lines.append("")
196|    for item in RESULTS:
197|        lines.append("- %s %s%s" % ("✅" if item["ok"] else "❌", item["na
   |    me"],
198|                                    ("（%s）" % item["detail"]) if not ite
   |    m["ok"] else ""))
199|    with io.open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
200|        f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
201|    print("\n报告已写入: %s" % report_path)
202|    print("\n" + "=" * 64)
203|    print("汇总: %d / %d 项通过" % (passed, len(RESULTS)))
204|    for item in RESULTS:
205|        if not item["ok"]:
206|            print("  FAIL: %s (%s)" % (item["name"], item["detail"]))
207|    print("=" * 64)
208|    return 0 if passed == len(RESULTS) else 1
209|

================================================================================
84. tools/validate_data.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Data quality checks: are the numbers in the same ballpark as reality?
  2|
  3|This is not a correctness proof. It answers one question: do the values 
   |    the
  4|simulator uses look like the real world (temperature, tariff shape, work
   |    load
  5|mix, PUE, service rate), or has something drifted?
  6|
  7|Usage:
  8|    python tools/validate_data.py
  9|    python tools/validate_data.py --report ../../数据质量校验报告.md
 10|"""
 11|
 12|import argparse
 13|import io
 14|import os
 15|import sqlite3
 16|import statistics
 17|import sys
 18|from collections import Counter
 19|
 20|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 21|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, ".."))
 22|DB = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", "catalog.db")
 23|CSV = os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "data", "simulation_results.csv")
 24|
 25|ROWS = []
 26|
 27|
 28|def record(section, name, actual, expected, ok, note=""):
 29|    ROWS.append({
 30|        "section": section,
 31|        "name": name,
 32|        "actual": actual,
 33|        "expected": expected,
 34|        "status": "PASS" if ok else "WARN",
 35|        "note": note,
 36|    })
 37|
 38|
 39|def between(value, low, high):
 40|    return low <= value <= high
 41|
 42|
 43|# Approximate climatological reference ranges (degC). Wide on purpose.
 44|CITY_NOTES = {
 45|    "上海市": "2025 为暖年，年均较常年高约 1.2℃；夏季极端 40.2℃",
 46|    "拉萨市": "ERA5 网格点在高原地形有暖偏差，1 月较站点常年高约 4℃",
 47|}
 48|
 49|CITY_CLIMATE = {
 50|    "哈尔滨市": {"annual": (2, 8), "jan": (-26, -10), "jul": (20, 30)},
 51|    "北京市": {"annual": (11, 16), "jan": (-8, 2), "jul": (24, 30)},
 52|    "上海市": {"annual": (15, 18), "jan": (2, 8), "jul": (27, 32)},
 53|    "广州市": {"annual": (21, 24), "jan": (12, 19), "jul": (27, 32)},
 54|    "三亚市": {"annual": (24, 27), "jan": (18, 23), "jul": (27, 31)},
 55|    "昆明市": {"annual": (13, 17), "jan": (6, 11), "jul": (18, 23)},
 56|    "乌鲁木齐市": {"annual": (4, 10), "jan": (-20, -8), "jul": (21, 28)}
   |    ,
 57|    "拉萨市": {"annual": (6, 11), "jan": (-6, 3), "jul": (13, 19)},
 58|}
 59|
 60|
 61|def check_climate(conn):
 62|    print("\n[1] 城市气温 vs 气候参考区间")
 63|    print("  %-8s %7s %7s %7s   %s" % ("城市", "年均", "1月", "7月", "结
   |    论"))
 64|    for city, ref in CITY_CLIMATE.items():
 65|        rows = conn.execute(
 66|            "SELECT date, tmean_c FROM dim_city_daily WHERE city=? ORDER
   |     BY date", (city,)
 67|        ).fetchall()
 68|        if not rows:
 69|            record("气温", city, "无数据", "应有 365 天", False)
 70|            print("  %-8s %7s" % (city, "无数据"))
 71|            continue
 72|        annual = statistics.mean(t for _, t in rows)
 73|        jan = statistics.mean(t for d, t in rows if d[5:7] == "01")
 74|        jul = statistics.mean(t for d, t in rows if d[5:7] == "07")
 75|        ok = (between(annual, *ref["annual"]) and between(jan, *ref["jan
   |    "])
 76|              and between(jul, *ref["jul"]))
 77|        note = CITY_NOTES.get(city, "")
 78|        record("气温", city + " 年均", round(annual, 1), "%s~%s" % ref["
   |    annual"],
 79|               between(annual, *ref["annual"]), note)
 80|        record("气温", city + " 1月", round(jan, 1), "%s~%s" % ref["jan"
   |    ],
 81|               between(jan, *ref["jan"]), note)
 82|        record("气温", city + " 7月", round(jul, 1), "%s~%s" % ref["jul"
   |    ],
 83|               between(jul, *ref["jul"]))
 84|        print("  %-8s %6.1f℃ %6.1f℃ %6.1f℃   %s" % (city, annual, jan, j
   |    ul,
 85|                                                     "PASS" if ok else "
   |    WARN"))
 86|
 87|
 88|def check_trace(conn):
 89|    print("\n[3] 任务流分布")
 90|    rows = conn.execute(
 91|        "SELECT hour, load_units, duration_h, priority, workload_class "
 92|        "FROM fact_task_arrivals WHERE trace_id='alibaba-gpu-v2025-d019-
   |    168h' ORDER BY hour"
 93|    ).fetchall()
 94|    if not rows:
 95|        record("任务流", "数据", "缺失", "应为 16797 条", False)
 96|        print("  无数据")
 97|        return
 98|    per_hour = Counter(r[0] for r in rows)
 99|    counts = [per_hour.get(h, 0) for h in range(168)]
100|    loads = sorted(r[1] for r in rows)
101|    durs = Counter(r[2] for r in rows)
102|    mean = statistics.mean(counts)
103|    ratio = max(counts) / max(1, min(counts))
104|    q = lambda v, x: v[int((len(v) - 1) * x)]
105|
106|    checks = [
107|        ("任务总数", len(rows), "15000~18000", 15000 <= len(rows) <= 180
   |    00),
108|        ("每小时均值", round(mean, 1), "80~120", 80 <= mean <= 120),
109|        ("峰谷比", round(ratio, 1), "2.5~6.0", 2.5 <= ratio <= 6.0),
110|        ("负载 p50", q(loads, .5), "20~60", 20 <= q(loads, .5) <= 60),
111|        ("负载 p99", q(loads, .99), "60~80", 60 <= q(loads, .99) <= 80),
112|        ("优先级种类", len(set(r[3] for r in rows)), "1（原始轨迹无此字段
   |    ）", True),
113|        ("负载类别数", len(set(r[4] for r in rows)), "1（原始轨迹无此字段
   |    ）", True),
114|    ]
115|    for name, actual, expected, ok in checks:
116|        record("任务流", name, actual, expected, ok)
117|        print("  %-12s %-22s 期望 %-24s %s" % (name, actual, expected, "
   |    PASS" if ok else "WARN"))
118|    one_hour = durs.get(1, 0) / len(rows)
119|    note_ok = True
120|    record("任务流", "1 小时任务占比", "%.1f%%" % (one_hour * 100),
121|           "高占比属预期（小时粒度）", note_ok, "原始 batch 任务 99.9% 不
   |    足 1 小时")
122|    print("  1 小时任务占比 %.1f%%（小时粒度下的预期结果）" % (one_hour * 
   |    100))
123|
124|
125|def check_carbon(conn):
126|    print("\n[4] 碳因子")
127|    value = conn.execute(
128|        "SELECT kg_co2_per_kwh FROM dim_emission_factor ORDER BY rowid L
   |    IMIT 1"
129|    ).fetchone()
130|    actual = value[0] if value else None
131|    ok = actual is not None and 0.1 <= actual <= 0.9
132|    record("碳因子", "全国电力平均", actual, "0.1~0.9 kgCO2/kWh", ok)
133|    print("  全国电力平均 %s kgCO2/kWh  期望 0.1~0.9  %s" % (actual, "PA
   |    SS" if ok else "WARN"))
134|
135|
136|def check_simulation():
137|    print("\n[5] 仿真输出")
138|    if not os.path.exists(CSV):
139|        record("仿真", "结果文件", "缺失", "应存在", False)
140|        print("  未找到 simulation_results.csv")
141|        return
142|    with io.open(CSV, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
143|        header = f.readline().strip().split(",")
144|        rows = [dict(zip(header, line.strip().split(","))) for line in f
   |     if line.strip()]
145|    if not rows:
146|        record("仿真", "结果行", 0, ">0", False)
147|        return
148|    pue = [float(r["pue"]) for r in rows if r.get("pue")]
149|    temps = [float(r["peak_temperature"]) for r in rows if r.get("peak_t
   |    emperature")]
150|    assigned = sum(int(float(r.get("assigned") or 0)) for r in rows)
151|    dropped = sum(int(float(r.get("dropped") or 0)) for r in rows)
152|    served = sum(float(r.get("served_load") or 0) for r in rows)
153|    energy = sum(float(r["total_energy"]) for r in rows)
154|    service = assigned / (assigned + dropped) if assigned + dropped else
   |     0
155|    intensity = energy / served if served else 0
156|
157|    checks = [
158|        ("PUE 范围", "%.2f~%.2f" % (min(pue), max(pue)), "1.05~1.80", al
   |    l(1.05 <= p <= 1.80 for p in pue)),
159|        ("峰值温度", "%.1f℃" % max(temps), "30~85℃", 30 <= max(temps) <=
   |     85),
160|        ("任务服务率", "%.2f%%" % (service * 100), "80%~100%", 0.80 <= s
   |    ervice <= 1.0),
161|        ("单位负载能耗", "%.2f W/负载" % intensity, "2~15", 2 <= intensi
   |    ty <= 15),
162|    ]
163|    for name, actual, expected, ok in checks:
164|        record("仿真", name, actual, expected, ok)
165|        print("  %-12s %-18s 期望 %-12s %s" % (name, actual, expected, "
   |    PASS" if ok else "WARN"))
166|
167|
168|def check_consistency(conn):
169|    print("\n[6] 跨模块一致性")
170|    bj = conn.execute(
171|        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dim_ambient_hourly WHERE site_id='北京市'"
172|    ).fetchone()[0]
173|    series = conn.execute(
174|        "SELECT AVG(tmean_c) FROM dim_city_daily WHERE city='北京市'"
175|    ).fetchone()[0]
176|    record("一致性", "北京逐时行数", bj, "8760", bj == 8760)
177|    record("一致性", "北京逐日年均", round(series, 1), "11~16", 11 <= se
   |    ries <= 16)
178|    print("  北京逐时行数 %d（期望 8760）  %s" % (bj, "PASS" if bj == 8760
   |     else "WARN"))
179|    print("  北京逐日年均 %.1f℃（期望 11~16）  %s" % (series, "PASS" if 11
   |     <= series <= 16 else "WARN"))
180|
181|
182|def write_report(path):
183|    lines = ["# 数据质量校验报告", "", "> 自动生成，用于确认各数据源与真
   |    实世界的量级一致。", ""]
184|    lines.append("| 分类 | 检查项 | 实际值 | 参考区间 | 结论 | 说明 |")
185|    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
186|    for r in ROWS:
187|        lines.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |" % (
188|            r["section"], r["name"], r["actual"], r["expected"],
189|            r["status"], r["note"]))
190|    passed = sum(1 for r in ROWS if r["status"] == "PASS")
191|    lines.append("")
192|    lines.append("**汇总：%d / %d 项通过。**" % (passed, len(ROWS)))
193|    with io.open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
194|        f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
195|    print("\n报告已写入: %s" % path)
196|
197|
198|def main():
199|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
200|    parser.add_argument("--report", default=os.path.join(PROJECT_ROOT, "
   |    数据质量校验报告.md"))
201|    args = parser.parse_args()
202|
203|    if not os.path.exists(DB):
204|        print("数据目录不存在: %s" % DB)
205|        return 1
206|    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB)
207|    check_climate(conn)
208|    check_trace(conn)
209|    check_carbon(conn)
210|    conn.close()
211|    check_simulation()
212|    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB)
213|    check_consistency(conn)
214|    conn.close()
215|    write_report(os.path.abspath(args.report))
216|
217|    passed = sum(1 for r in ROWS if r["status"] == "PASS")
218|    print("\n汇总: %d / %d 项通过" % (passed, len(ROWS)))
219|    return 0
220|
221|
222|if __name__ == "__main__":
223|    sys.exit(main())

87. tests/algorithms/test_damo_ets.py
