  1|"""Unit tests for DAMOETSScheduler."""
  2|
  3|import sys
  4|import os
  5|
  6|# Make the project importable when running from the tests directory
  7|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "
   |    ..", ".."))
  8|if PROJECT_ROOT not in sys.path:
  9|    sys.path.insert(0, PROJECT_ROOT)
 10|
 11|from src.algorithms import DAMOETSScheduler, GreedyScheduler
 12|from src.models import Server, Task
 13|
 14|
 15|def _make_servers(count: int = 4) -> list:
 16|    servers = []
 17|    for i in range(count):
 18|        servers.append(Server(
 19|            server_id=i,
 20|            max_load=100,
 21|            max_temp=85,
 22|            base_power=100,
 23|            max_power=300,
 24|            initial_temp=25,
 25|            server_type="standard" if i % 2 == 0 else "gpu_server",
 26|        ))
 27|    return servers
 28|
 29|
 30|def _make_task(load: int = 10, deadline=None, wclass: str = "cpu_bound",
   |     priority: int = 1) -> Task:
 31|    return Task(load=load, priority=priority, deadline=deadline, workloa
   |    d_class=wclass)
 32|
 33|
 35|# Tests
 37|
 38|def test_empty_tasks_returns_empty():
 39|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(_make_servers(), seed=42)
 40|    assert scheduler.schedule_tasks([]) == []
 41|    assert scheduler.get_deadline_violations() == 0
 42|
 43|
 44|def test_edf_ordering():
 45|    """Tasks with earlier deadlines should be assigned first; non-deadli
   |    ne tasks last."""
 46|    servers = _make_servers(4)
 47|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(servers, seed=1)
 48|
 49|    late = _make_task(load=10, deadline=10, wclass="cpu_bound")
 50|    early = _make_task(load=10, deadline=2, wclass="cpu_bound")
 51|    no_dl = _make_task(load=10, deadline=None, wclass="cpu_bound")
 52|
 53|    results = scheduler.schedule_tasks([late, early, no_dl], current_hou
   |    r=0)
 54|    assigned = [t for t, s in results if s is not None]
 55|    assert assigned[0] is early
 56|    assert assigned[1] is late
 57|    # no_dl is processed last (no deadline group), still assigned
 58|    assert no_dl in assigned
 59|
 60|
 61|def test_heterogeneous_match_prefers_gpu_for_gpu_bound():
 62|    """GPU-bound tasks should preferentially route to gpu_server."""
 63|    servers = _make_servers(4)  # 0,2 = standard; 1,3 = gpu_server
 64|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(servers, seed=0)
 65|
 66|    gpu_task = _make_task(load=20, deadline=5, wclass="gpu_bound", prior
   |    ity=5)
 67|    results = scheduler.schedule_tasks([gpu_task], current_hour=0)
 68|    chosen = results[0][1]
 69|    assert chosen is not None
 70|    assert chosen.server_type == "gpu_server"
 71|
 72|
 73|def test_thermal_safety_rejects_overheating_assignment():
 74|    """If all servers would overheat, the task should be unassigned."""
 75|    servers = _make_servers(2)
 76|    for s in servers:
 77|        s.current_temp = 84  # Just below max (85 * 0.95 = 80.75) -> alr
   |    eady unsafe
 78|        s.current_load = 50
 79|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(servers, seed=0)
 80|
 81|    task = _make_task(load=30, deadline=5, wclass="cpu_bound", priority=
   |    5)
 82|    results = scheduler.schedule_tasks([task], current_hour=0)
 83|    assert results[0][1] is None
 84|
 85|
 86|def test_deadline_violation_counter():
 87|    """Tasks whose deadline is already in the past at assignment hour ar
   |    e counted."""
 88|    servers = _make_servers(4)
 89|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(servers, seed=0)
 90|
 91|    # current_hour=10, but the task's deadline is 5 — already missed
 92|    expired = _make_task(load=10, deadline=5, wclass="cpu_bound")
 93|    future = _make_task(load=10, deadline=15, wclass="cpu_bound")
 94|
 95|    results = scheduler.schedule_tasks([expired, future], current_hour=1
   |    0)
 96|    violations = scheduler.get_deadline_violations()
 97|    assert violations == 1
 98|    # Both should be assigned (the counter is about deadline breach, not
   |     assignment failure)
 99|    assert all(server is not None for _, server in results)
100|
101|
102|def test_reproducible_with_seed():
103|    """Same seed + same inputs -> same assignments and same violation co
   |    unt."""
104|    servers_a = _make_servers(4)
105|    servers_b = _make_servers(4)
106|    scheduler_a = DAMOETSScheduler(servers_a, seed=123)
107|    scheduler_b = DAMOETSScheduler(servers_b, seed=123)
108|
109|    def _build_tasks():
110|        return [
111|            _make_task(
112|                load=15,
113|                deadline=i + 3,
114|                wclass="gpu_bound" if i % 2 == 0 else "cpu_bound",
115|            )
116|            for i in range(6)
117|        ]
118|
119|    tasks_a = _build_tasks()
120|    tasks_b = _build_tasks()
121|
122|    res_a = scheduler_a.schedule_tasks(tasks_a, current_hour=0)
123|    res_b = scheduler_b.schedule_tasks(tasks_b, current_hour=0)
124|
125|    # Compare by (deadline, workload_class, server_id) — not task_id (uu
   |    id)
126|    def _signature(results):
127|        return [
128|            (t.deadline, t.workload_class, s.server_id if s else None)
129|            for t, s in results
130|        ]
131|
132|    assert _signature(res_a) == _signature(res_b)
133|    assert scheduler_a.get_deadline_violations() == scheduler_b.get_dead
   |    line_violations()
134|
135|
136|def test_damo_ets_lower_or_equal_energy_than_greedy_under_heavy_deadline
   |    _load():
137|    """DAMO-ETS prioritises deadline-urgency so it should be at least as
   |     energy-efficient
138|    as Greedy when both have enough capacity, measured by avg total assi
   |    gned load per task."""
139|    servers_a = _make_servers(6)
140|    servers_b = _make_servers(6)
141|
142|    damo = DAMOETSScheduler(servers_a, seed=0)
143|    greedy = GreedyScheduler(servers_b)
144|
145|    tasks = []
146|    for i in range(10):
147|        tasks.append(_make_task(
148|            load=20,
149|            deadline=i + 2,
150|            wclass="gpu_bound" if i % 3 == 0 else "cpu_bound",
151|            priority=3,
152|        ))
153|
154|    damo_res = damo.schedule_tasks(tasks, current_hour=0)
155|    greedy_res = greedy.schedule_tasks(tasks)
156|
157|    damo_assigned = sum(1 for _, s in damo_res if s is not None)
158|    greedy_assigned = sum(1 for _, s in greedy_res if s is not None)
159|
160|    # Both should assign the majority; DAMO-ETS should be at least as go
   |    od
161|    assert damo_assigned >= greedy_assigned - 1
162|
163|
164|def test_get_name_returns_damo_ets():
165|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(_make_servers(2), seed=0)
166|    assert scheduler.get_name() == "damo_ets"
167|
168|
169|def test_reset_clears_violation_counter():
170|    scheduler = DAMOETSScheduler(_make_servers(2), seed=0)
171|    expired = _make_task(load=5, deadline=1, wclass="cpu_bound")
172|    scheduler.schedule_tasks([expired], current_hour=5)
173|    assert scheduler.get_deadline_violations() >= 1
174|    scheduler.reset()
175|    assert scheduler.get_deadline_violations() == 0

88. tests/algorithms/test_genetic_reproducibility.py

  1|"""Reproducibility tests for the GeneticScheduler."""
  2|
  3|import sys
  4|import os
  5|
  6|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "
   |    ..", ".."))
  7|if PROJECT_ROOT not in sys.path:
  8|    sys.path.insert(0, PROJECT_ROOT)
  9|
 10|from src.algorithms import GeneticScheduler, GeneticAlgorithm
 11|from src.models import Server, Task
 12|
 13|
 14|def _make_servers(count: int = 6) -> list:
 15|    return [Server(server_id=i) for i in range(count)]
 16|
 17|
 18|def _make_tasks(count: int = 8) -> list:
 19|    return [
 20|        Task(
 21|            load=10 + (i % 3) * 5,
 22|            priority=(i % 5) + 1,
 23|            workload_class="cpu_bound" if i % 2 == 0 else "gpu_bound",
 24|        )
 25|        for i in range(count)
 26|    ]
 27|
 28|
 29|def test_genetic_algorithm_reproducible_with_seed():
 30|    """GeneticAlgorithm.evolve() with the same seed returns the same bes
   |    t assignment."""
 31|    tasks = _make_tasks(8)
 32|    servers_a = _make_servers(6)
 33|    servers_b = _make_servers(6)
 34|
 35|    ga_a = GeneticAlgorithm(servers_a, tasks, seed=42)
 36|    ga_b = GeneticAlgorithm(servers_b, tasks, seed=42)
 37|
 38|    best_a, fit_a = ga_a.evolve()
 39|    best_b, fit_b = ga_b.evolve()
 40|
 41|    assert best_a == best_b, "Best individuals must match with same seed
   |    "
 42|    assert abs(fit_a - fit_b) < 1e-9
 43|
 44|
 45|def test_genetic_scheduler_reproducible_via_full_run():
 46|    """GeneticScheduler.schedule_tasks returns the same set of (task, se
   |    rver_id) pairs."""
 47|    tasks = _make_tasks(8)
 48|    servers_a = _make_servers(6)
 49|    servers_b = _make_servers(6)
 50|
 51|    s1 = GeneticScheduler(servers_a, seed=7)
 52|    s2 = GeneticScheduler(servers_b, seed=7)
 53|
 54|    res1 = s1.schedule_tasks(tasks)
 55|    res2 = s2.schedule_tasks(tasks)
 56|
 57|    pairs1 = [(t.task_id, s.server_id if s else None) for t, s in res1]
 58|    pairs2 = [(t.task_id, s.server_id if s else None) for t, s in res2]
 59|    assert pairs1 == pairs2
 60|
 61|
 62|def test_genetic_different_seed_produces_different_results():
 63|    """Sanity check: different seeds should usually give different resul
   |    ts."""
 64|    tasks = _make_tasks(10)
 65|    s1 = GeneticScheduler(_make_servers(8), seed=1)
 66|    s2 = GeneticScheduler(_make_servers(8), seed=2)
 67|    res1 = s1.schedule_tasks(tasks)
 68|    res2 = s2.schedule_tasks(tasks)
 69|    pairs1 = [(t.task_id, s.server_id if s else None) for t, s in res1]
 70|    pairs2 = [(t.task_id, s.server_id if s else None) for t, s in res2]
 71|    assert pairs1 != pairs2

   |    ..", ".."))

 23|class _StubConfig:
 24|    def __init__(self, data_source):
 25|        self._data = {
 26|            "simulation": {"random_seed": 42, "sla_slack": 1.5},
 27|            "task_generation": {"base_rate": 4, "peak_rate": 10, "peak_h
   |    ours": [9, 18]},
 28|            "task_completion": {"mode": "service_based", "completion_pro
   |    bability": 0.3},
 29|            "cooling": {"power_coefficient": 500, "target_temp": 22, "fa
   |    n_target_temp": 35,
 30|                        "dissipation_coefficient": 8.0, "thermal_inertia
   |    ": 0.25},

   |    5,

   |    : 35},

   |    parallel": False},

 41|
 42|def _write_dataset(raw_root, kind, name, rows, meta):
 43|    folder = os.path.join(raw_root, kind)
 44|    os.makedirs(folder, exist_ok=True)
 45|    csv_path = os.path.join(folder, name + ".csv")
 46|    with io.open(csv_path, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
 47|        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
 48|        writer.writeheader()
 49|        writer.writerows(rows)
 50|    with io.open(os.path.splitext(csv_path)[0] + ".json", "w", encoding=
   |    "utf-8") as f:
 51|        json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False)
 52|    return csv_path
 53|
 54|
 55|def _ambient_rows(hours=48):
 56|    rows = []
 57|    for hour in range(hours):
 58|        ts = "2026-03-01T%02d:00:00" % (hour % 24)
 59|        temp = 26.0 + 8.0 * ((hour % 24) / 24.0)
 60|        rows.append({"ts": ts, "temp_c": round(temp, 2), "humidity": 45,
   |     "wind_ms": 2.0})
 61|    return rows
 62|
 63|
 64|def _build(raw_root, db_path, extra_args=()):
 65|    cmd = [sys.executable, os.path.join("tools", "etl", "build_catalog.p
   |    y"),
 66|           "--raw-root", str(raw_root), "--db", str(db_path)]
 67|    cmd.extend(extra_args)
 68|    return subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=PROJECT_ROOT, capture_output=True, te
   |    xt=True)
 69|

170|        "license": "test-only",

172|        "trace_id": "cluster-x",
173|    })
174|
175|    outcome = _build(str(raw_root), str(db_path), ("--sla-slack", "1.5")
   |    )
176|    assert outcome.returncode == 0, outcome.stderr
177|
178|    catalog = Catalog(str(db_path))
179|    stream = catalog.task_stream("cluster-x")
180|    assert stream is not None
181|    assert len(stream) == 12
182|    assert len(stream[0]) == 5
183|    load, priority, workload_class, duration, offset = stream[0][0]
184|    assert load >= 10 and priority == 3 and duration == 2 and offset >= 
   |    2
185|
186|    simulator = DataCenterSimulator(
187|        num_servers=8, simulation_hours=12, seed=5,
188|        config=_StubConfig({"mode": "catalog", "catalog_db": str(db_path
   |    ),
189|                            "trace_id": "cluster-x"}),
190|    )
191|    assert simulator.trace_stream is not None
192|    df = simulator.run_comparison(algorithms=["greedy", "damo_ets"])
193|    assert simulator.run_metadata["scenario_source"] == "catalog"
194|    assert df["offered_load"].max() > 0

 79|    """测试 execute 在异常情况下的错误码"""

 85|    def test_execute_dependency_missing(self):
 86|        """依赖缺失时 execute 返回 PluginResult.fail(DEPENDENCY_MISSING)
   |    """
 87|        with patch("src.plugins.rl_ppo_scheduler.has_gymnasium", return_
   |    value=False), \
 88|             patch("src.plugins.rl_ppo_scheduler.has_sb3", return_value=
   |    False):
 89|            # 即使 initialize 返回 False，execute 也要返回失败结果而非抛

   |    lse)

 96|    def test_load_model_not_found(self):
 97|        """模型文件不存在时返回 MODEL_NOT_FOUND"""

   |    stent/ppo.zip"})
101|        # execute 推理模式，无 model_path → 失败
102|        result = self.plugin.execute(train=False, tasks=[], servers=[])
103|        # 推理无 tasks 时直接调用 _load_model，model_path 不存在 → MODEL_
   |    NOT_FOUND
104|        # 如果传了空 tasks 列表但有 model_path 仍走 _load_model
105|        self.assertIsInstance(result, PluginResult)

================================================================================
95. tests/simulation/test_controlled_comparison.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Tests for the controlled comparison guarantees.
  2|
  3|These lock in the properties that make the algorithm numbers comparable:
  4|one task stream per scenario, an SLA metric that can actually move, and 
   |    a
  5|ranking rule that refuses to reward an algorithm for serving less work.
  6|"""
  7|
  8|import os
  9|import sys
 10|
 11|PROJECT_ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "
   |    ..", ".."))
 12|if PROJECT_ROOT not in sys.path:
 13|    sys.path.insert(0, PROJECT_ROOT)
 14|
 15|import pandas as pd
 16|
 17|from src.simulation import DataCenterSimulator
 18|from src.simulation.metrics import SERVICE_RATE_FLOOR, rank_algorithms, 
   |    summarize, safe_number
 19|from src.simulation.task_generator import TaskGenerator
 20|
 21|ALGORITHMS = ["greedy", "genetic", "round_robin", "damo_ets"]
 22|
 23|
 24|class _StubConfig:
 25|    """Minimal config object so tests do not depend on the YAML files.""
   |    "
 26|
 27|    def __init__(self, overrides=None):
 28|        self._data = {
 29|            "simulation": {"random_seed": 42, "sla_slack": 1.5},
 30|            "task_generation": {"base_rate": 6, "peak_rate": 20, "peak_h
   |    ours": [9, 18]},
 31|            "task_completion": {"mode": "service_based", "completion_pro
   |    bability": 0.3},
 32|            "cooling": {"power_coefficient": 500, "target_temp": 22, "fa
   |    n_target_temp": 35,
 33|                        "dissipation_coefficient": 8.0, "thermal_inertia
   |    ": 0.25},
 34|            "energy": {"idle_power_factor": 0.6, "part_load_penalty": 0.
   |    5},
 35|            "genetic": {"population_size": 12, "generations": 8, "enable
   |    _parallel": False},
 36|            "greedy": {"temperature_weight": 0.3},
 37|            "data_source": {"mode": "synthetic"},
 38|        }
 39|        if overrides:
 40|            for section, values in overrides.items():
 41|                self._data.setdefault(section, {}).update(values)
 42|
 43|    def to_dict(self):
 44|        return self._data
 45|
 46|
 47|def _simulator(servers=40, hours=8, seed=42, overrides=None):
 48|    return DataCenterSimulator(
 49|        num_servers=servers,
 50|        simulation_hours=hours,
 51|        seed=seed,
 52|        config=_StubConfig(overrides),
 53|    )
 54|
 55|
 56|def test_all_algorithms_see_the_same_offered_demand():
 57|    """The control guarantee is about demand, not about what each algori
   |    thm served."""
 58|    sim = _simulator()
 59|    df = sim.run_comparison(algorithms=ALGORITHMS)
 60|    offered = sim.control_signature(df)
 61|    assert set(offered) == set(ALGORITHMS)
 62|    assert len(set(offered.values())) == 1, offered
 63|    assert sim.is_controlled(df)
 64|
 65|
 66|def test_scenario_stream_is_reproducible():
 67|    first = TaskGenerator(seed=7, sla_slack=1.5).build_stream(6)
 68|    second = TaskGenerator(seed=7, sla_slack=1.5).build_stream(6)
 69|    assert first == second
 70|    assert TaskGenerator.stream_digest(first) == TaskGenerator.stream_di
   |    gest(second)
 71|
 72|
 73|def test_materialised_tasks_are_independent_instances():
 74|    generator = TaskGenerator(seed=3)
 75|    stream = generator.build_stream(3)
 76|    run_a = generator.materialize_stream(stream)
 77|    run_b = generator.materialize_stream(stream)
 78|    assert run_a[0][0] is not run_b[0][0]
 79|    run_a[0][0].completed_time = 99
 80|    assert run_b[0][0].completed_time is None
 81|
 82|
 83|def test_deadline_violations_are_reachable():
 84|    tight = _simulator(servers=12, hours=12, overrides={"simulation": {"
   |    sla_slack": 1.0}})
 85|    df = tight.run_comparison(algorithms=ALGORITHMS)
 86|    stats = tight.get_statistics(df)
 87|    assert any(entry["deadline_violations"] > 0 for entry in stats.value
   |    s())
 88|
 89|    loose = _simulator(servers=60, hours=12, overrides={"simulation": {"
   |    sla_slack": 4.0}})
 90|    df_loose = loose.run_comparison(algorithms=ALGORITHMS)
 91|    loose_stats = loose.get_statistics(df_loose)
 92|    assert all(entry["deadline_violation_rate"] <= 1.0 for entry in loos
   |    e_stats.values())
 93|
 94|
 95|def test_summary_reports_service_rate_and_energy_intensity():
 96|    sim = _simulator(servers=60)
 97|    df = sim.run_comparison(algorithms=["greedy", "round_robin"])
 98|    stats = sim.get_statistics(df)
 99|    for entry in stats.values():
100|        assert 0.0 <= entry["service_rate"] <= 1.0
101|        assert entry["energy_per_served_load"] is not None
102|        assert entry["energy_per_served_load"] > 0
103|        assert entry["assigned_tasks"] + entry["dropped_tasks"] > 0
104|
105|
106|def test_ranking_excludes_algorithms_below_the_service_floor():
107|    stats = {
108|        "honest": {"service_rate": 0.99, "energy_per_served_load": 5.0, 
   |    "max_temp": 40.0},
109|        "slacker": {"service_rate": 0.40, "energy_per_served_load": 3.0,
   |     "max_temp": 35.0},
110|    }
111|    ranking = rank_algorithms(stats)
112|    assert ranking["best"] == "honest"
113|    assert [item["algorithm"] for item in ranking["excluded"]] == ["slac
   |    ker"]
114|    assert "服务率" in ranking["excluded"][0]["reason"]
115|
116|
117|def test_ranking_orders_by_energy_intensity():
118|    stats = {
119|        "a": {"service_rate": 1.0, "energy_per_served_load": 7.0, "max_t
   |    emp": 40.0},
120|        "b": {"service_rate": 1.0, "energy_per_served_load": 5.0, "max_t
   |    emp": 42.0},
121|    }
122|    ranking = rank_algorithms(stats)
123|    assert ranking["best"] == "b"
124|    assert [item["algorithm"] for item in ranking["eligible"]] == ["b", 
   |    "a"]
125|
126|
127|def test_ranking_reports_no_winner_when_nothing_qualifies():
128|    stats = {"a": {"service_rate": 0.5, "energy_per_served_load": 5.0, "
   |    max_temp": 40.0}}
129|    ranking = rank_algorithms(stats)
130|    assert ranking["best"] is None
131|    assert "不给出能耗排名" in ranking["note"]
132|
133|
134|def test_cooling_cost_rises_with_the_hot_aisle():
135|    sim = _simulator(servers=10, hours=4)
136|    for server in sim.servers:
137|        server.current_load = server.max_load
138|    cool = sim.energy_model.calculate_cooling_energy(sim.servers, 25.0, 
   |    22.0, peak_temp=30.0)
139|    hot = sim.energy_model.calculate_cooling_energy(sim.servers, 25.0, 2
   |    2.0, peak_temp=50.0)
140|    assert hot > cool
141|
142|
143|def test_algorithms_differ_in_energy_intensity():
144|    """Scheduling choices must be able to move the energy figure."""
145|    sim = _simulator(servers=24, hours=12)
146|    df = sim.run_comparison(algorithms=["greedy", "round_robin", "damo_e
   |    ts"])
147|    stats = sim.get_statistics(df)
148|    intensities = [
149|        entry["energy_per_served_load"] for entry in stats.values()
150|        if entry["energy_per_served_load"] is not None
151|    ]
152|    assert len(intensities) >= 2
153|    assert len(set(intensities)) > 1, intensities
154|
155|
156|def test_safe_number_maps_non_numbers_to_none():
157|    assert safe_number(float("nan")) is None
158|    assert safe_number(None) is None
159|    assert safe_number("abc") is None
160|    assert safe_number(2.5) == 2.5
161|

163|def test_summarize_tolerates_missing_columns():
164|    df = pd.DataFrame([
165|        {"time": 0, "algorithm": "x", "total_energy": 100.0, "total_load
   |    ": 10.0},
166|        {"time": 1, "algorithm": "x", "total_energy": 120.0, "total_load
   |    ": 12.0},
167|    ])
168|    stats = summarize(df)
169|    assert stats["x"]["avg_energy"] == 110.0
170|    assert stats["x"]["service_rate"] == 1.0
171|    assert stats["x"]["deadline_violations"] == 0
172|
173|
174|def test_pue_responds_to_ambient_temperature():
175|    sim = _simulator(servers=10, hours=6)
176|    warm = sim.energy_model.get_pue(sim.servers, 30.0, 22.0)
177|    cool = sim.energy_model.get_pue(sim.servers, 10.0, 22.0)
178|    assert warm is not None and cool is not None
179|    assert warm > cool

 17|def _context():
 18|    stats = {
 19|        "damo_ets": {
 20|            "service_rate": 0.9912, "energy_per_served_load": 5.2333,
 21|            "max_temp": 54.4, "p95_temp": 48.0, "avg_pue": 1.4638,
 22|            "deadline_violations": 3,
 23|        },
 24|        "greedy": {
 25|            "service_rate": 1.0, "energy_per_served_load": 5.604,
 26|            "max_temp": 56.6, "p95_temp": 50.0, "avg_pue": 1.4509,
 27|            "deadline_violations": 0,
 28|        },
 29|    }
 30|    return {
 31|        "stats": stats,
 32|        "ranking": rank_algorithms(stats),
 33|        "data_source": {"site_id": "北京市", "emission_region": "华北"},
 34|    }

 37|
 38|def _insight_context():
 39|    rows = []
 40|    for algorithm, values in {
 41|        "greedy": [(10.0, 50.0, 1.50), (7.0, 60.0, 1.45), (5.0, 70.0, 1.
   |    40)],
 42|        "damo_ets": [(8.0, 50.0, 1.42), (6.0, 60.0, 1.38), (5.5, 65.0, 1
   |    .35)],
 43|    }.items():
 44|        for hour, (intensity, temperature, pue) in enumerate(values):
 45|            rows.append({
 46|                "time": hour,
 47|                "algorithm": algorithm,
 48|                "total_load": 10.0,
 49|                "served_load": 10.0,
 50|                "total_energy": intensity * 10.0,
 51|                "pue": pue,
 52|                "peak_temperature": temperature,
 53|                "avg_temperature": temperature - 2,
 54|                "ambient_temperature": 25.0,
 55|                "fan_speed": 0.5,
 56|                "cooling_power": 100.0,
 57|                "assigned": 10,
 58|                "dropped": 0,
 59|                "overheated_servers": 0,
 60|            })
 61|    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
 62|    meta = {
 63|        "site_id": "测试市",
 64|        "site_province": "测试省",
 65|        "emission_region": "华北",
 66|        "num_servers": 10,
 67|        "simulation_hours": 3,
 68|        "controlled": True,
 69|    }
 70|    insights = build_insights(df, meta, generated_at="2026-10-18 10:00:0
   |    0")
 71|    stats = summarize(df)
 72|    return {
 73|        "stats": stats,
 74|        "ranking": rank_algorithms(stats),
 75|        "insights": insights,
 76|        "data_source": meta,
 77|    }

236|def test_chat_endpoint_falls_back_when_model_unavailable(monkeypatch):
237|    import src.api.app  # noqa: F401  (registers the blueprint)
238|    from src.api import ai_api
239|    from src.api.app import app
240|
241|    class Broken:
242|        model = "none"
243|        key_source = "none"
244|
245|        def reload_key(self):
246|            pass
247|
248|        def chat_messages(self, *args, **kwargs):
249|            raise RuntimeError("no network")
250|
251|    monkeypatch.setattr(ai_api, "get_ai_service", lambda: Broken())
252|    client = app.test_client()
253|    response = client.post(
254|        "/api/v1/ai/chat",
255|        json={"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "当前排名如何？"}]
   |    },
256|    )
257|    assert response.status_code == 200
258|    payload = response.get_json()
259|    assert payload["success"] is True
260|    assert payload["source"] == "rule"
261|    assert payload["reply"]

97. tests/test_plan_optimizer.py

  1|import time
  2|
  3|from src.services.plan_optimizer import PlanOptimizer, _server_counts
  4|
  5|
  6|def test_server_counts_include_bounds():
  7|    assert _server_counts(10, 10) == [10]
  8|    counts = _server_counts(10, 30)
  9|    assert counts[0] == 10
 10|    assert counts[-1] == 30
 11|    assert len(counts) == len(set(counts))
 12|
 13|
 14|def test_small_global_optimization_runs():
 15|    optimizer = PlanOptimizer()
 16|    job = optimizer.start({
 17|        "server_min": 12,
 18|        "server_max": 16,
 19|        "simulation_hours": 2,
 20|        "algorithms": ["greedy", "round_robin"],
 21|        "target": "pue",
 22|        "service_rate_floor": 0.98,
 23|        "site_id": "北京市",
 24|        "include_weights": False,
 25|    })
 26|    deadline = time.time() + 60
 27|    current = optimizer.snapshot(job["job_id"])
 28|    while current["status"] not in ("success", "failed") and time.time()
   |     < deadline:
 29|        time.sleep(0.2)
 30|        current = optimizer.snapshot(job["job_id"])
 31|    assert current["status"] == "success", current.get("error")
 32|    result = current["result"]
 33|    assert result["top"]
 34|    assert len(result["top"]) <= 3
 35|    assert result["best"]["metrics"]["service_rate"] >= 0.98
 36|    assert all(plan["metrics"].get("avg_pue") is not None for plan in re
   |    sult["top"])

98. tests/test_run_store.py
================================================================================
  1|"""Tests for the persistent run registry."""
  2|
  3|import pandas as pd
  4|
  5|from src.services import run_store
  6|
  7|
  8|def _isolate(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
  9|    monkeypatch.setattr(run_store, "DATA_DIR", str(tmp_path))
 10|    monkeypatch.setattr(run_store, "RUNS_DIR", str(tmp_path / "runs"))
 11|    monkeypatch.setattr(run_store, "DB_PATH", str(tmp_path / "runs.db"))
 12|    monkeypatch.setattr(run_store, "LEGACY_RESULTS", str(tmp_path / "sim
   |    ulation_results.csv"))
 13|    monkeypatch.setattr(run_store, "LEGACY_METADATA", str(tmp_path / "ru
   |    n_metadata.json"))
 14|    run_store._init()
 15|
 16|
 17|def test_run_store_roundtrip(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
 18|    _isolate(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
 19|    run_id = run_store.create_run({
 20|        "num_servers": 6,
 21|        "simulation_hours": 2,
 22|        "algorithms": ["greedy"],
 23|        "site_id": "北京市",
 24|    }, origin="test")
 25|    df = pd.DataFrame([{
 26|        "time": 0,
 27|        "algorithm": "greedy",
 28|        "total_load": 10.0,
 29|        "served_load": 10.0,
 30|        "total_energy": 50.0,
 31|        "pue": 1.4,
 32|        "peak_temperature": 55.0,
 33|        "assigned": 10,
 34|        "dropped": 0,
 35|    }])
 36|    summary = {
 37|        "greedy": {
 38|            "service_rate": 1.0,
 39|            "energy_per_served_load": 5.0,
 40|            "avg_pue": 1.4,
 41|            "max_temp": 55.0,
 42|        }
 43|    }
 44|    run_store.save_results(run_id, df, {"run_id": run_id}, summary)
 45|    run_store.update_run(run_id, status="success", progress=100)
 46|    record = run_store.get_run(run_id)
 47|    assert record["status"] == "success"
 48|    assert record["summary"]["greedy"]["energy_per_served_load"] == 5.0
 49|    assert run_store.list_runs(1)[0]["run_id"] == run_id
 50|    assert run_store.load_results(run_id).shape[0] == 1
 51|
 52|
 53|def test_compare_runs_uses_right_minus_left(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
 54|    _isolate(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
 55|    left = run_store.create_run({"num_servers": 6, "simulation_hours": 2
   |    , "algorithms": ["greedy"]})
 56|    right = run_store.create_run({"num_servers": 24, "simulation_hours":
   |     2, "algorithms": ["greedy"]})
 57|    run_store.update_run(left, summary_json={
 58|        "greedy": {"service_rate": 0.5, "energy_per_served_load": 4.0, "
   |    avg_pue": 1.4, "max_temp": 60.0}
 59|    })
 60|    run_store.update_run(right, summary_json={
 61|        "greedy": {"service_rate": 0.8, "energy_per_served_load": 5.0, "
   |    avg_pue": 1.5, "max_temp": 55.0}
 62|    })
 63|    comparison = run_store.compare_runs(left, right)
 64|    item = comparison["metrics"][0]
 65|    assert item["energy_per_served_load"]["delta"] == 1.0
 66|    assert item["energy_per_served_load"]["percent"] == 25.0
 67|    assert item["service_rate"]["delta"] == 0.3
